

===== FILE: PROJECT_STATE.md =====
# Greater Cairo Atlas — Project State

Last updated: `2026-11-13`

## Current delivery status

PR #28 was merged. The active PHASE 07.10 + 08.0 work is on the session branch; this change set is not merged. The implementation regenerates station geometry from exact `osm_node_id` facts, preserves the lifecycle guards, completes license/verification/URL and atlas UX updates, and requires the full PostGIS seed+feature import integration test before PR #29 is opened. Do not merge PR #29.

| Phase | Scope | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 01–06 | Application, schema, source-backed seed, geometry ingestion, map, exports/import hardening | Complete |
| 07.1–07.9 | Reconciliation, OSM review, station/boundary matching, reports, license and QA iterations | Complete; see checked-in feature reports and research revision notes |
| 07.10 | Exact fact-driven OSM station point regeneration with lifecycle checks and ODbL provenance | Incomplete: two explicit lifecycle conflicts remain excluded; geometry totals miss targets and five station modes remain undetermined |
| 08.0 | Trust/consistency, Arabic labels, map UX, full QA and one review PR | In progress; one unmerged PR #29 required |

## Current canonical dataset counts

Offline seed validation: **417 entities, 2,157 facts, 378 evidence records, 2,510 fact–evidence links**. Current `data/features/features.geojson`: **2,942 features**; `data/features/boundaries.geojson`: **19 boundaries**. These are checked-in source counts, not a claim about the live production database. The production import remains transactional and checksum-gated.

PHASE 07.10 station-group geometry counts (stored OSM station groups with exact seed node facts):

| Line | Target groups / points | Exact-node geometries | Note |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Metro L1 | 35 | 35 | Target met |
| Metro L2 | 20 | 19 | `STOP-AHMED-OMAR-HASHEM` rejected: approved-planned entity, but its exact nodes 9076939018/9076939019 are operational Mohamed Naguib subway stop positions without construction tags |
| Metro L3 | 34 | 32 | `STOP-KIT-KAT` rejected: operational entity’s exact referenced node 9077189602 carries `construction=station`; 10099279486 is retained as an alternate node fact |
| LRT | 12 | 12 groups (13 point features, including a multi-position entity) | Target station-group count met |
| East Monorail | 22 | 22 groups (23 point features, including a multi-position entity) | Target station-group count met |

Across the fact-backed station set, 117 station entities reference 248 OSM node IDs; 115 entities have accepted geometry. No geometry is substituted by name. The two mismatches above remain excluded under lifecycle rules, rather than being forced to meet numeric targets. Five seed station entities have no determinate transit mode from line membership or code prefix and are explicitly reported; the UI does not label them with a generic mode.

## Data and import pipeline

Canonical research inputs are in `data/research/`; adaptation produces `data/seed/{entities.csv,evidence.json,facts.json}`. OSM outputs and exact-match audits are in `data/features/`. Validate with `npm run seed:check`; regenerate licensed geometry from stored source extracts using `scripts/ingest-features.py`; then run the full checks, including `tests/features-postgis.integration.test.ts`, which imports the current full seed and all features into PGlite/PostGIS and validates the production geometry rules. Live database reconciliation must not be claimed until a production import succeeds.

`ci.yml` now runs on every pull request into `main` and every push to `main` (BATCH 12.1), plus manual dispatch. It needs no secrets and no database: the PostGIS integration tests run on in-process PGlite + PostGIS WASM inside `npm test`, not against a service container. `migrate.yml` stays manual-only because it needs real database credentials.

## Revision and phase history

- **rev7:** 342 entities, 1,391 facts, 350 evidence records, 1,505 links; preserved the prior rev6.2 claims and added documented evidence/facts. See `data/research/rev7/CHANGELOG_rev7.md`.
- **rev8:** 415 entities, 2,075 facts, 368 evidence records, 2,427 links. Added and consolidated OSM transit station identities while retaining node IDs as candidate facts; five transit-line count baselines were L1 35, L2 20, L3 34, LRT 12, East Monorail 22. See `data/research/rev8-draft/CHANGELOG_rev8.md`.
- **rev9:** 417 entities, 2,092 facts, 378 evidence records, 2,445 links. Additive status delta from `data/research/rev9-status/` resolved 3 of 17 `unknown` entities (ROAD-AMR-IBN-ALAS, ROAD-REGIONAL-NW-ARC, TRANSIT-SUPERJET) using news evidence only, so no new fact is `verified`; 14 entities remain unknown. See `data/research/rev9-status/ADAPTATION_NOTES.md`.
- **PHASE 07.3–07.5:** conservative station matching, boundary matching, and candidate review; retained ambiguity and unmatched records in `data/features/phase-07-3-station-matching-report.ar.md`, `phase-07-4-boundary-station-report.ar.md`, `phase-07-5-boundary-candidates.ar.md`, and `match-report.json`.
- **PHASE 07.10 current:** 2,942 feature geometries and 19 boundaries; exact station-entity geometry results are L1 35/35, L2 19/20, L3 32/33, LRT 12/12, East Monorail 22/22. Five modes remain undetermined. Rejections and undetermined modes are included in generated reports; PHASE 07.10 remains incomplete.
- **BATCH 12.4 — CAPMAS governorate parentage:** 65 entities whose boundary is a union of CAPMAS units now have an evidence-backed `located_in` relation to their governorate authority entity, derived only from those units' published `adm1_pcode` (never geometry, never name matching). Each relation carries a supporting `located_in` fact citing `EV-CODAB-ADMIN-2026`, kept at `candidate` because no fact-level verification was performed. Current seed: 2,157 facts, 194 relations, 2,510 fact–evidence links.
- **BATCH 12.5 — points outside official boundaries:** 7 point occurrences across 6 entities (`CITY-OBOUR`, `DIST-AIN-SHAMS`, `DIST-AMIRIYA`, `DIST-BAB-SHAREYA`, `DIST-BAHTIM`, `DIST-HELIOPOLIS`) fall outside their own CAPMAS boundary. Reported in `data/features/point-outside-boundary-report.json`; **no point was moved**. `DIST-BAHTIM` lies inside the OSM boundary, so it is recorded as a source disagreement rather than a bad coordinate.
- **BRT (الأتوبيس الترددي) — partial geometry from OSM only:** 14 `highway=busway` ways named «مسار الاتوبيس الترددي BRT» contribute the visible fragment of `TRANSIT-BRT` (1.366 km of mapped line, ODbL). Of the 28 `STOP-BRT-*` entities exactly one has an OSM node that matches clearly (`STOP-BRT-MOSHIR-TANTAWY` → `OSM-NODE-13764384338`); the other 27 stay without geometry until a citable source exists, so the line is drawn as a partial corridor and is labelled as such in researcher mode. Station matching for this line is proximity-exempt (`partial_source_geometry`) because the mapped fragment cannot validate the full corridor.

## Licensing and verification rules

Research facts, evidence, and all non-OSM-derived data: `© Mustafa Aboalazm (مصطفى أبوالعزم), licensed under CC BY 4.0`. OpenStreetMap-derived geometry: `© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0`. Code has no owner-designated license; the data licenses do not grant code permissions. See [`DATA_LICENSE.md`](./DATA_LICENSE.md).

A fact marked `verified` must link to evidence whose `source_type` is `official` and whose URL is non-empty. This is enforced consistently by the seed validator/importer and migration 008’s deferred database trigger. At present, all **331 verified facts** satisfy that requirement. Evidence records lacking URLs are retained as-is and are not promoted to verified.

## Current QA and release constraints

Checks to run before PR #29: `npm run lint`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm test`, `npm run seed:check`, `npm run db:migrate:dry-run`, `npm run build`, and the full PostGIS import integration test. Provide screenshots of the home map, Layers panel, and a selected entity after fly-to. Open one PR #29 from the active branch and leave it open for review.


===== FILE: .github/workflows/ci.yml =====
name: CI

on:
  # BATCH 12.1 — run on every pull request into main and on every push to main.
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
  push:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  verify:
    name: Lint, Typecheck, Test & Build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: "22"
          cache: "npm"

      - name: Set up Python for geometry validation
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.12"

      - name: Install geometry validator
        run: python -m pip install -r requirements-ingest.txt

      - name: Validate committed GeoJSON against PostGIS geometry rules
        run: python scripts/ingest-features.py --check-geojson data/features/features.geojson

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Run ESLint
        run: npm run lint

      - name: Run TypeScript strict typecheck
        run: npm run typecheck

      - name: Run Vitest unit & route tests
        run: npm test

      - name: Verify SQL migrations offline
        run: npm run db:migrate:dry-run

      - name: Verify seed schema & data/seed files offline
        run: npm run seed:check

      - name: Build Next.js production bundle (no DB required)
        run: npm run build


===== FILE: .github/workflows/migrate.yml =====
name: Migrate Database

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: neon-database-migrations
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  migrate:
    name: Apply SQL Migrations to Neon (Manual Override)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - name: Verify DATABASE_URL secret is configured
        env:
          DATABASE_URL: ${{ secrets.DATABASE_URL }}
          DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED: ${{ secrets.DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED }}
        run: |
          set +x
          if [ -z "${DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED:-}" ] && [ -z "${DATABASE_URL:-}" ]; then
            echo "::error::Missing required GitHub Actions secret 'DATABASE_URL' (or 'DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED'). Note: normal deployments run migrations automatically on Vercel via vercel-build."
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "Verified migration database secret is present (value hidden)."

      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: "22"
          cache: "npm"

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Apply pending SQL migrations
        env:
          DATABASE_URL: ${{ secrets.DATABASE_URL }}
          DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED: ${{ secrets.DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED }}
        run: |
          set +x
          npm run db:migrate

-- Migration 000: Enable PostGIS spatial extension
-- Phase 01 (Foundation) — Greater Cairo Atlas
-- Note: Core domain tables (entities, facts, evidence, features) are intentionally deferred to Phase 02.

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS postgis;


===== FILE: db/migrations/001_core_schema.sql =====
-- Migration 001: Core Evidentiary & Spatial Schema
-- Phase 02 — Greater Cairo Atlas
--
-- Core domain tables:
--   1. entities      — Canonical urban/geospatial subjects (stable text code, bilingual names, sector, lifecycle status)
--   2. evidence      — Verifiable provenance records (URL, publisher, source_type, publication/access dates)
--   3. facts         — Multi-valued assertions allowing conflicting claims side by side (no UNIQUE on entity_id + attribute)
--   4. features      — PostGIS geometries in WGS 84 (SRID 4326) with GiST index, OSM lineage, and license (ODbL)
-- Associative table:
--   - fact_evidence  — Many-to-many provenance links between facts and evidence (ON DELETE RESTRICT)

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = NOW();
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. entities
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Lifecycle invariant: `status` defaults to 'unknown' (never 'operational') and is the sole
-- representation of operational state. A 'planned' or 'approved_planned' entity cannot be
-- represented as operational without an explicit `status` update.
CREATE TABLE entities (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name_ar TEXT NOT NULL,
  name_en TEXT NOT NULL,
  entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  sector TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unknown',
  description TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

  CONSTRAINT entities_code_format_chk CHECK (
    code ~ '^[A-Z0-9]+(-[A-Z0-9]+)*$' AND char_length(code) BETWEEN 3 AND 120
  ),
  CONSTRAINT entities_name_ar_nonempty_chk CHECK (btrim(name_ar) <> ''),
  CONSTRAINT entities_name_en_nonempty_chk CHECK (btrim(name_en) <> ''),
  CONSTRAINT entities_entity_type_chk CHECK (
    entity_type IN (
      'city',
      'district',
      'neighborhood',
      'road',
      'transit_line',
      'transit_station',
      'bridge_interchange',
      'utility',
      'public_facility',
      'development_zone',
      'landmark',
      'waterway'
    )
  ),
  CONSTRAINT entities_sector_chk CHECK (
    sector IN ('NORTH', 'EAST', 'SOUTH', 'WEST', 'CENTRAL')
  ),
  CONSTRAINT entities_status_chk CHECK (
    status IN (
      'operational',
      'partial',
      'under_construction',
      'planned',
      'approved_planned',
      'unknown'
    )
  ),
  CONSTRAINT entities_description_nonempty_chk CHECK (
    description IS NULL OR btrim(description) <> ''
  )
);

CREATE INDEX entities_sector_idx ON entities (sector);
CREATE INDEX entities_status_idx ON entities (status);
CREATE INDEX entities_entity_type_idx ON entities (entity_type);

CREATE TRIGGER trg_entities_set_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON entities
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. evidence
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE evidence (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  title TEXT,
  url TEXT NOT NULL,
  publisher TEXT NOT NULL,
  source_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  published_date DATE,
  accessed_date DATE NOT NULL,
  notes TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

  CONSTRAINT evidence_code_format_chk CHECK (
    code ~ '^[A-Z0-9]+(-[A-Z0-9]+)*$' AND char_length(code) BETWEEN 3 AND 120
  ),
  CONSTRAINT evidence_title_nonempty_chk CHECK (
    title IS NULL OR btrim(title) <> ''
  ),
  CONSTRAINT evidence_url_http_chk CHECK (
    url ~ '^https?://[^[:space:]]+$'
  ),
  CONSTRAINT evidence_publisher_nonempty_chk CHECK (btrim(publisher) <> ''),
  CONSTRAINT evidence_source_type_chk CHECK (
    source_type IN ('official', 'news', 'dataset', 'map', 'pdf')
  ),
  CONSTRAINT evidence_notes_nonempty_chk CHECK (
    notes IS NULL OR btrim(notes) <> ''
  )
);

CREATE INDEX evidence_source_type_idx ON evidence (source_type);
CREATE INDEX evidence_publisher_idx ON evidence (publisher);

CREATE TRIGGER trg_evidence_set_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON evidence
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. facts
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Disagreement invariant: NO UNIQUE constraint on (entity_id, attribute).
-- Multiple conflicting facts for the same entity and attribute coexist side by side.
CREATE TABLE facts (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  entity_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES entities(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  attribute TEXT NOT NULL,
  value TEXT NOT NULL,
  unit TEXT,
  valid_as_of DATE,
  verification_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'candidate',
  notes TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

  CONSTRAINT facts_code_format_chk CHECK (
    code ~ '^[A-Z0-9]+(-[A-Z0-9]+)*$' AND char_length(code) BETWEEN 3 AND 120
  ),
  CONSTRAINT facts_attribute_format_chk CHECK (
    attribute ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$' AND char_length(attribute) BETWEEN 2 AND 100
  ),
  CONSTRAINT facts_value_nonempty_chk CHECK (btrim(value) <> ''),
  CONSTRAINT facts_unit_nonempty_chk CHECK (
    unit IS NULL OR btrim(unit) <> ''
  ),
  CONSTRAINT facts_verification_state_chk CHECK (
    verification_state IN (
      'candidate',
      'single_source',
      'corroborated',
      'verified',
      'disputed',
      'rejected',
      'superseded'
    )
  ),
  CONSTRAINT facts_notes_nonempty_chk CHECK (
    notes IS NULL OR btrim(notes) <> ''
  )
);

CREATE INDEX facts_entity_id_idx ON facts (entity_id);
CREATE INDEX facts_entity_attribute_idx ON facts (entity_id, attribute);
CREATE INDEX facts_verification_state_idx ON facts (verification_state);

CREATE TRIGGER trg_facts_set_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON facts
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Associative Link: fact_evidence (many-to-many between facts and evidence)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE fact_evidence (
  fact_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES facts(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  evidence_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES evidence(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (fact_id, evidence_id)
);

CREATE INDEX fact_evidence_evidence_id_idx ON fact_evidence (evidence_id);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. features
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE features (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  entity_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES entities(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  evidence_id UUID REFERENCES evidence(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  geometry geometry(Geometry, 4326) NOT NULL,
  osm_type TEXT,
  osm_id BIGINT,
  osm_version INTEGER,
  license TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ODbL',
  properties JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

  CONSTRAINT features_code_format_chk CHECK (
    code ~ '^[A-Z0-9]+(-[A-Z0-9]+)*$' AND char_length(code) BETWEEN 3 AND 120
  ),
  CONSTRAINT features_geometry_valid_wgs84_chk CHECK (
    ST_SRID(geometry) = 4326 AND NOT ST_IsEmpty(geometry) AND ST_IsValid(geometry)
  ),
  CONSTRAINT features_osm_type_chk CHECK (
    osm_type IS NULL OR osm_type IN ('node', 'way', 'relation')
  ),
  CONSTRAINT features_osm_id_positive_chk CHECK (
    osm_id IS NULL OR osm_id > 0
  ),
  CONSTRAINT features_osm_version_positive_chk CHECK (
    osm_version IS NULL OR osm_version > 0
  ),
  CONSTRAINT features_osm_lineage_complete_chk CHECK (
    (osm_type IS NULL AND osm_id IS NULL AND osm_version IS NULL)
    OR (osm_type IS NOT NULL AND osm_id IS NOT NULL AND osm_version IS NOT NULL)
  ),
  CONSTRAINT features_license_chk CHECK (
    license IN (
      'ODbL',
      'ODbL-1.0',
      'CC-BY-4.0',
      'CC0-1.0',
      'official_gazette',
      'custom_documented'
    )
  ),
  CONSTRAINT features_osm_requires_odbl_chk CHECK (
    osm_type IS NULL OR license IN ('ODbL', 'ODbL-1.0')
  ),
  CONSTRAINT features_properties_object_chk CHECK (
    jsonb_typeof(properties) = 'object'
  )
);

CREATE INDEX features_geometry_gist_idx ON features USING GIST (geometry);
CREATE INDEX features_entity_id_idx ON features (entity_id);
CREATE INDEX features_evidence_id_idx ON features (evidence_id) WHERE evidence_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX features_osm_ref_idx ON features (osm_type, osm_id, osm_version) WHERE osm_type IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_features_set_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON features
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at_timestamp();


===== FILE: db/migrations/002_entity_taxonomy.sql =====
-- Migration 002: Researcher Entity Taxonomy & Secondary Evidence Epistemic Guard
-- Phase 02.1 — Greater Cairo Atlas
--
-- Note: 001_core_schema.sql is never edited because applied migration checksums are immutable.
--
-- Changes:
--   1. Extend `entities_entity_type_chk` to support the full 15-type researcher taxonomy
--      (city, district, neighborhood, road, bridge, interchange, tunnel, transit_line,
--      transit_stop, project, authority, landmark, facility, waterway, utility) while
--      retaining the 4 Phase 02 types (transit_station, bridge_interchange, public_facility,
--      development_zone) for backward compatibility.
--   2. Extend `evidence_source_type_chk` with 'secondary' (Wikipedia, real-estate blogs,
--      aggregators).
--   3. Add DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED constraint triggers enforcing that a fact with
--      `verification_state = 'verified'` must be linked to at least one non-'secondary'
--      evidence record at transaction commit.

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Extend entities.entity_type CHECK constraint
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE entities
  DROP CONSTRAINT entities_entity_type_chk;

ALTER TABLE entities
  ADD CONSTRAINT entities_entity_type_chk CHECK (
    entity_type IN (
      -- Researcher taxonomy (15 canonical types)
      'city',
      'district',
      'neighborhood',
      'road',
      'bridge',
      'interchange',
      'tunnel',
      'transit_line',
      'transit_stop',
      'project',
      'authority',
      'landmark',
      'facility',
      'waterway',
      'utility',
      -- Retained Phase 02 compatibility types
      'transit_station',
      'bridge_interchange',
      'public_facility',
      'development_zone'
    )
  );

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Extend evidence.source_type CHECK constraint with 'secondary'
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE evidence
  DROP CONSTRAINT evidence_source_type_chk;

ALTER TABLE evidence
  ADD CONSTRAINT evidence_source_type_chk CHECK (
    source_type IN (
      'official',
      'news',
      'dataset',
      'map',
      'pdf',
      'secondary'
    )
  );

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Enforce that 'secondary' sources alone can never make a fact 'verified'
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION assert_fact_verified_has_primary_source(p_fact_id UUID)
RETURNS VOID
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_code TEXT;
  v_state TEXT;
BEGIN
  SELECT code, verification_state
    INTO v_code, v_state
    FROM facts
   WHERE id = p_fact_id;

  IF NOT FOUND OR v_state <> 'verified' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
      FROM fact_evidence fe
      JOIN evidence ev ON ev.id = fe.evidence_id
     WHERE fe.fact_id = p_fact_id
       AND ev.source_type <> 'secondary'
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION
      'Fact "%" cannot have verification_state = ''verified'' when backed only by secondary (or missing) evidence; at least one non-secondary source is required.',
      v_code
      USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  END IF;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_facts()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
  PERFORM assert_fact_verified_has_primary_source(NEW.id);
  RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_fact_evidence()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
  PERFORM assert_fact_verified_has_primary_source(OLD.fact_id);
  RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_evidence()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
  r RECORD;
BEGIN
  IF NEW.source_type = 'secondary' THEN
    FOR r IN
      SELECT fact_id FROM fact_evidence WHERE evidence_id = NEW.id
    LOOP
      PERFORM assert_fact_verified_has_primary_source(r.fact_id);
    END LOOP;
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;

CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trg_facts_verified_requires_primary_source
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OF verification_state ON facts
DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_facts();

CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trg_fact_evidence_verified_requires_primary_source
AFTER DELETE OR UPDATE ON fact_evidence
DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_fact_evidence();

CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trg_evidence_secondary_verified_guard
AFTER UPDATE OF source_type ON evidence
DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_evidence();


===== FILE: db/migrations/003_research_seed_extensions.sql =====
-- Migration 003: Research Seed Extensions (REGIONAL Sector, Airport/Rail Types, Aliases & Partial Date Precision)
-- Phase 03 — Greater Cairo Atlas
--
-- Note: 000_enable_postgis.sql, 001_core_schema.sql, and 002_entity_taxonomy.sql are never
-- edited because applied migration checksums are immutable.
--
-- Changes:
--   1. Extend `entities_sector_chk` with 'REGIONAL' for cross-sector corridors, intercity roads,
--      high-speed rail lines, and regional cities.
--   2. Extend `entities_entity_type_chk` with 'airport' and 'rail_station'.
--   3. Add `entities.aliases TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::text[]` with a non-blank element guard.
--   4. Add `facts.valid_as_of_precision` ('day' | 'month' | 'year', default 'day') so partial
--      dates (e.g. 2026-02, 2024-10, 2025) are stored as first-of-period DATE + precision
--      rather than inventing day precision.
--   5. Relax `evidence.url` to allow NULL when a documented non-empty `title` + `publisher`
--      identifies an offline document or named official release awaiting its exact URL,
--      and add `evidence.published_date_precision` ('day' | 'month' | 'year', default 'day').

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Extend entities.sector CHECK constraint with 'REGIONAL'
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE entities
  DROP CONSTRAINT entities_sector_chk;

ALTER TABLE entities
  ADD CONSTRAINT entities_sector_chk CHECK (
    sector IN (
      'NORTH',
      'EAST',
      'SOUTH',
      'WEST',
      'CENTRAL',
      'REGIONAL'
    )
  );

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2. Extend entities.entity_type CHECK constraint with 'airport' and 'rail_station'
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE entities
  DROP CONSTRAINT entities_entity_type_chk;

ALTER TABLE entities
  ADD CONSTRAINT entities_entity_type_chk CHECK (
    entity_type IN (
      -- Researcher taxonomy (17 canonical types)
      'city',
      'district',
      'neighborhood',
      'road',
      'bridge',
      'interchange',
      'tunnel',
      'transit_line',
      'transit_stop',
      'rail_station',
      'airport',
      'project',
      'authority',
      'landmark',
      'facility',
      'waterway',
      'utility',
      -- Retained Phase 02 compatibility types
      'transit_station',
      'bridge_interchange',
      'public_facility',
      'development_zone'
    )
  );

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3. Add entities.aliases TEXT[] if missing
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION text_array_has_no_blank_items(arr TEXT[])
RETURNS BOOLEAN
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
STRICT
AS $$
  SELECT NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
      FROM unnest(arr) AS item
     WHERE item IS NULL OR btrim(item) = ''
  );
$$;

ALTER TABLE entities
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS aliases TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::text[];

ALTER TABLE entities
  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS entities_aliases_nonblank_chk;

ALTER TABLE entities
  ADD CONSTRAINT entities_aliases_nonblank_chk CHECK (
    text_array_has_no_blank_items(aliases)
  );

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4. Add facts.valid_as_of_precision ('day' | 'month' | 'year', default 'day')
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE facts
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS valid_as_of_precision TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'day';

ALTER TABLE facts
  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS facts_valid_as_of_precision_chk;

ALTER TABLE facts
  ADD CONSTRAINT facts_valid_as_of_precision_chk CHECK (
    valid_as_of_precision IN ('day', 'month', 'year')
  );

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 5. Evidence URL nullability for documented offline/pending-URL sources + published_date_precision
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
ALTER TABLE evidence
  ALTER COLUMN url DROP NOT NULL;

ALTER TABLE evidence
  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS evidence_url_http_chk;

ALTER TABLE evidence
  ADD CONSTRAINT evidence_url_http_chk CHECK (
    url IS NULL OR url ~ '^https?://[^[:space:]]+$'
  );

ALTER TABLE evidence
  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS evidence_locator_or_title_chk;

ALTER TABLE evidence
  ADD CONSTRAINT evidence_locator_or_title_chk CHECK (
    url IS NOT NULL OR title IS NOT NULL
  );

ALTER TABLE evidence
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS published_date_precision TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'day';

ALTER TABLE evidence
  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS evidence_published_date_precision_chk;

ALTER TABLE evidence
  ADD CONSTRAINT evidence_published_date_precision_chk CHECK (
    published_date_precision IN ('day', 'month', 'year')
  );


===== FILE: db/migrations/004_feature_bbox_attribution.sql =====
-- Migration 004: Feature provenance and Greater Cairo coverage guard.
-- Earlier applied migrations (000-003) are immutable.
-- Coordinates are WGS84 longitude/latitude; the broad operational envelope
-- covers Greater Cairo and its documented regional transit/road corridors.

ALTER TABLE features
  ADD CONSTRAINT features_greater_cairo_bbox_chk CHECK (
    ST_XMin(Box3D(geometry)) >= 30.5
    AND ST_YMin(Box3D(geometry)) >= 29.2
    AND ST_XMax(Box3D(geometry)) <= 32.1
    AND ST_YMax(Box3D(geometry)) <= 30.8
  );

ALTER TABLE features
  ADD CONSTRAINT features_provenance_metadata_chk CHECK (
    jsonb_typeof(properties -> 'source') = 'string'
    AND btrim(properties ->> 'source') <> ''
    AND jsonb_typeof(properties -> 'attribution') = 'string'
    AND btrim(properties ->> 'attribution') <> ''
    AND jsonb_typeof(properties -> 'retrieval_date') = 'string'
    AND properties ->> 'retrieval_date' ~ '^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$'
  );


===== FILE: db/migrations/005_import_runs.sql =====
-- Migration 005: Checksummed automatic production seed + feature imports.
-- The checksum row is committed in the same transaction as its imported dataset.

CREATE TABLE import_runs (
  dataset_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  checksum TEXT NOT NULL,
  imported_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  CONSTRAINT import_runs_dataset_key_nonempty_chk CHECK (btrim(dataset_key) <> ''),
  CONSTRAINT import_runs_checksum_sha256_chk CHECK (checksum ~ '^[a-f0-9]{64}$')
);


===== FILE: db/migrations/006_road_only_bbox_extension.sql =====
-- Migration 006: Extend the road-only WGS84 envelope without widening non-road features.
-- Existing migration 004 remains immutable. Road geometry accepts [30.5, 29.2, 32.35, 30.9];
-- all other geometry retains the original [30.5, 29.2, 32.1, 30.8] coverage envelope.

ALTER TABLE features DROP CONSTRAINT features_greater_cairo_bbox_chk;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_entity_geometry_bbox()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
  feature_entity_type TEXT;
  west DOUBLE PRECISION := 30.5;
  south DOUBLE PRECISION := 29.2;
  east DOUBLE PRECISION := 32.1;
  north DOUBLE PRECISION := 30.8;
BEGIN
  SELECT entity_type INTO feature_entity_type FROM entities WHERE id = NEW.entity_id;
  IF feature_entity_type = 'road' THEN
    east := 32.35;
    north := 30.9;
  END IF;
  IF ST_XMin(Box3D(NEW.geometry)) < west OR ST_YMin(Box3D(NEW.geometry)) < south
     OR ST_XMax(Box3D(NEW.geometry)) > east OR ST_YMax(Box3D(NEW.geometry)) > north THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'feature geometry outside % WGS84 bbox',
      CASE WHEN feature_entity_type = 'road' THEN 'road-only' ELSE 'Greater Cairo' END
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_features_entity_geometry_bbox
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF entity_id, geometry ON features
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_entity_geometry_bbox();


===== FILE: db/migrations/007_regional_ring_west_bbox.sql =====
-- Migration 007: align the Regional Ring road exception with the validated seed envelope.
-- Migration 006 remains immutable; all non-Regional-Ring rules are unchanged.

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_entity_geometry_bbox()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
  feature_entity_type TEXT;
  feature_entity_code TEXT;
  west DOUBLE PRECISION := 30.5;
  south DOUBLE PRECISION := 29.2;
  east DOUBLE PRECISION := 32.1;
  north DOUBLE PRECISION := 30.8;
BEGIN
  SELECT entity_type, code
    INTO feature_entity_type, feature_entity_code
    FROM entities
   WHERE id = NEW.entity_id;

  IF feature_entity_type = 'road' THEN
    east := 32.35;
    north := 30.9;
    IF feature_entity_code = 'ROAD-REGIONAL-RING' THEN
      west := 30.25;
    END IF;
  END IF;

  IF ST_XMin(Box3D(NEW.geometry)) < west OR ST_YMin(Box3D(NEW.geometry)) < south
     OR ST_XMax(Box3D(NEW.geometry)) > east OR ST_YMax(Box3D(NEW.geometry)) > north THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'feature geometry outside % WGS84 bbox',
      CASE
        WHEN feature_entity_type = 'road' AND feature_entity_code = 'ROAD-REGIONAL-RING' THEN 'Regional Ring road-only'
        WHEN feature_entity_type = 'road' THEN 'road-only'
        ELSE 'Greater Cairo'
      END
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;


===== FILE: db/migrations/008_verified_facts_require_official_url.sql =====
-- Align the database invariant with the documented verification rule: a verified
-- claim must retain at least one linked official evidence record with a URL.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION assert_fact_verified_has_primary_source(p_fact_id UUID)
RETURNS VOID
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_code TEXT;
  v_state TEXT;
BEGIN
  SELECT code, verification_state INTO v_code, v_state
    FROM facts WHERE id = p_fact_id;
  IF NOT FOUND OR v_state <> 'verified' THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;

  IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
      FROM fact_evidence fe
      JOIN evidence ev ON ev.id = fe.evidence_id
     WHERE fe.fact_id = p_fact_id
       AND ev.source_type = 'official'
       AND ev.url IS NOT NULL
       AND btrim(ev.url) <> ''
  ) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION
      'Fact "%" cannot have verification_state = ''verified'' without linked official evidence with a URL.',
      v_code
      USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  END IF;
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_fact_evidence()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP <> 'INSERT' THEN
    PERFORM assert_fact_verified_has_primary_source(OLD.fact_id);
  END IF;
  IF TG_OP <> 'DELETE' THEN
    PERFORM assert_fact_verified_has_primary_source(NEW.fact_id);
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_fact_evidence_verified_requires_primary_source ON fact_evidence;
CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trg_fact_evidence_verified_requires_primary_source
AFTER INSERT OR DELETE OR UPDATE ON fact_evidence
DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_fact_evidence();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_evidence()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
  r RECORD;
BEGIN
  FOR r IN
    SELECT fact_id FROM fact_evidence WHERE evidence_id = NEW.id
  LOOP
    PERFORM assert_fact_verified_has_primary_source(r.fact_id);
  END LOOP;
  RETURN NULL;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_evidence_secondary_verified_guard ON evidence;
CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trg_evidence_secondary_verified_guard
AFTER UPDATE OF source_type, url ON evidence
DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_fn_check_verified_fact_on_evidence();


===== FILE: db/migrations/009_entity_relations.sql =====
-- PHASE 09.0: Explicit, provenance-backed relations between canonical entities.
-- Relations are directional unless the predicate's consumer chooses to display both ends.
CREATE TABLE entity_relations (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  subject_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES entities(code) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  predicate TEXT NOT NULL,
  object_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES entities(code) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  ordinal INTEGER,
  valid_as_of DATE,
  evidence_ref TEXT REFERENCES evidence(code) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  CONSTRAINT entity_relations_predicate_chk CHECK (
    predicate IN ('has_station', 'interchange_with', 'part_of', 'crosses', 'connects', 'located_in', 'operated_by', 'developed_by')
  ),
  CONSTRAINT entity_relations_ordinal_positive_chk CHECK (ordinal IS NULL OR ordinal > 0),
  CONSTRAINT entity_relations_no_self_reference_chk CHECK (subject_code <> object_code),
  CONSTRAINT entity_relations_unique_triplet UNIQUE (subject_code, predicate, object_code)
);

CREATE INDEX entity_relations_subject_predicate_idx ON entity_relations (subject_code, predicate, ordinal);
CREATE INDEX entity_relations_object_predicate_idx ON entity_relations (object_code, predicate);
CREATE INDEX entity_relations_evidence_ref_idx ON entity_relations (evidence_ref) WHERE evidence_ref IS NOT NULL;


===== FILE: db/migrations/010_submissions.sql =====
-- PHASE 09.1: user-submitted suggestions remain separate from canonical facts.
CREATE TABLE submissions (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  entity_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES entities(code) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  attribute TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(attribute) BETWEEN 1 AND 120),
  proposed_value TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(proposed_value) BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
  source_url TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(source_url) BETWEEN 1 AND 2048),
  note TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '' CHECK (char_length(note) <= 1000),
  email TEXT CHECK (email IS NULL OR char_length(email) <= 254),
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'accepted', 'rejected')),
  reviewer_note TEXT CHECK (reviewer_note IS NULL OR char_length(reviewer_note) <= 1000),
  request_fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (request_fingerprint ~ '^[a-f0-9]{64}$'),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX submissions_status_created_idx ON submissions (status, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX submissions_rate_limit_idx ON submissions (request_fingerprint, created_at DESC);


===== FILE: db/migrations/011_admin_review.sql =====
-- BATCH 13: data administration workspace.
--
-- Two principles drive this migration:
--   1. Review actions NEVER delete or overwrite canonical data. Every decision is appended to
--      admin_audit_log with who/when/before/after/reason, so the history is reconstructable.
--   2. `verified` keeps meaning exactly what migration 008 says it means: an official evidence row
--      with a URL. This migration adds a review flag next to the fact, not a second source of truth
--      for verification_state.

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Review state per fact. One row per fact; absence means "not reviewed".
-- This is deliberately separate from facts.verification_state so that a reviewer can say
-- "I looked at this" without silently promoting evidence-free claims to verified.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE fact_reviews (
  fact_code TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES facts(code) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  review_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
    CHECK (review_state IN ('pending', 'approved', 'rejected', 'disputed')),
  -- Rejection and dispute always need a written reason; approval does not.
  reason TEXT CHECK (reason IS NULL OR char_length(reason) BETWEEN 1 AND 2000),
  reviewer TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(reviewer) BETWEEN 1 AND 120),
  reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  CONSTRAINT fact_reviews_reason_required_chk CHECK (
    review_state IN ('pending', 'approved') OR (reason IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(reason)) > 0)
  )
);

CREATE INDEX fact_reviews_state_idx ON fact_reviews (review_state, reviewed_at DESC);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Append-only audit log. There is no UPDATE/DELETE path in the application, and the triggers
-- below enforce that at the database level so a future bug cannot rewrite history.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE admin_audit_log (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  actor TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(actor) BETWEEN 1 AND 120),
  action TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (action IN (
    'fact.approve', 'fact.reject', 'fact.dispute', 'fact.reset',
    'evidence.attach', 'evidence.detach',
    'geometry.replace',
    'import.preview', 'import.commit',
    'submission.accept', 'submission.reject'
  )),
  entity_code TEXT REFERENCES entities(code) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  fact_code TEXT REFERENCES facts(code) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  evidence_code TEXT REFERENCES evidence(code) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  submission_id UUID REFERENCES submissions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  reason TEXT CHECK (reason IS NULL OR char_length(reason) <= 2000),
  -- The full before/after snapshots. Kept as JSONB so a reviewer can see exactly what changed
  -- without joining half the schema.
  before_state JSONB,
  after_state JSONB,
  request_fingerprint TEXT CHECK (request_fingerprint IS NULL OR request_fingerprint ~ '^[a-f0-9]{64}$')
);

CREATE INDEX admin_audit_log_occurred_idx ON admin_audit_log (occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX admin_audit_log_entity_idx ON admin_audit_log (entity_code, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX admin_audit_log_fact_idx ON admin_audit_log (fact_code, occurred_at DESC);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION admin_audit_log_append_only() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  RAISE EXCEPTION 'admin_audit_log is append-only: % is not permitted', TG_OP;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER admin_audit_log_no_update
  BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON admin_audit_log
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION admin_audit_log_append_only();

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Geometry history. Replacing a geometry must never lose the previous one, and a geometry may only
-- come from an uploaded source with a declared licence.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE geometry_history (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  feature_code TEXT NOT NULL,
  entity_code TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES entities(code) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  geometry JSONB NOT NULL,
  source_label TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(source_label) BETWEEN 1 AND 200),
  license TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (license IN ('ODbL-1.0', 'CC-BY-IGO', 'CC-BY-4.0')),
  attribution TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(attribution) BETWEEN 1 AND 500),
  replaced_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  replaced_by TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(replaced_by) BETWEEN 1 AND 120),
  reason TEXT CHECK (reason IS NULL OR char_length(reason) <= 2000)
);

CREATE INDEX geometry_history_entity_idx ON geometry_history (entity_code, replaced_at DESC);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Import previews. A dry run is stored so the person who approves a commit is approving exactly
-- the diff they previewed, not a re-computation of it.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE admin_import_previews (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  created_by TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(created_by) BETWEEN 1 AND 120),
  source_filename TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(source_filename) BETWEEN 1 AND 260),
  source_format TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (source_format IN ('json', 'csv')),
  -- SHA-256 of the uploaded payload, so a commit must present the same bytes.
  payload_checksum TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (payload_checksum ~ '^[a-f0-9]{64}$'),
  summary JSONB NOT NULL,
  diff JSONB NOT NULL,
  committed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  committed_by TEXT,
  CONSTRAINT admin_import_previews_commit_actor_chk CHECK (
    (committed_at IS NULL AND committed_by IS NULL) OR (committed_at IS NOT NULL AND committed_by IS NOT NULL)
  )
);

CREATE INDEX admin_import_previews_created_idx ON admin_import_previews (created_at DESC);

COMMENT ON TABLE fact_reviews IS 'BATCH 13: human review state for a fact. Never overwrites facts.verification_state.';
COMMENT ON TABLE admin_audit_log IS 'BATCH 13: append-only audit trail for every admin mutation.';
COMMENT ON TABLE geometry_history IS 'BATCH 13: previous geometry of every replaced feature, with source and licence.';
COMMENT ON TABLE admin_import_previews IS 'BATCH 13: stored dry-run diffs so a commit approves exactly what was previewed.';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Reversal (no down-migration runner exists; this is the reviewed reverse script).
-- It is safe to run because every object here is new: nothing existing is altered.
--
--   DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS admin_audit_log_no_update ON admin_audit_log;
--   DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS admin_audit_log_append_only();
--   DROP TABLE IF EXISTS admin_import_previews;
--   DROP TABLE IF EXISTS geometry_history;
--   DROP TABLE IF EXISTS admin_audit_log;   -- history is lost; export it first
--   DROP TABLE IF EXISTS fact_reviews;
--
-- Reversing does not touch facts, evidence, entities, features, or submissions, because this
-- migration never modified them.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


===== FILE: scripts/adapt-research-seed.ts =====
import path from "node:path";
import {
  runAdaptResearchSeed,
  type RunAdaptResearchSeedOptions,
} from "../src/lib/atlas/research-seed";
import { SeedValidationError } from "../src/lib/errors";

function parseArgs(argv: string[]): RunAdaptResearchSeedOptions {
  const options: RunAdaptResearchSeedOptions = {
    outDir: path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data/seed"),
    dryRun: false,
  };

  const readFlagValue = (flag: string, next: string | undefined): string => {
    if (!next || next.startsWith("--")) {
      throw new Error(`Missing value after ${flag} flag.`);
    }
    return next;
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const arg = argv[i];
    if (!arg) {
      continue;
    }

    if (arg === "--dry-run" || arg === "--check") {
      options.dryRun = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--rev3") {
      options.revision = 3;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--rev2") {
      options.revision = 2;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--rev1") {
      options.revision = 1;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--rev") {
      const val = Number(readFlagValue("--rev", argv[i + 1]));
      if (!Number.isInteger(val) || val < 1) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid revision number after --rev: "${argv[i + 1]}".`);
      }
      options.revision = val;
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg.startsWith("--rev=")) {
      const val = Number(arg.slice("--rev=".length).trim());
      if (!Number.isInteger(val) || val < 1) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid revision number in ${arg}.`);
      }
      options.revision = val;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--in") {
      options.inputDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), readFlagValue("--in", argv[i + 1]));
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg.startsWith("--in=")) {
      options.inputDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), arg.slice("--in=".length).trim());
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--out") {
      options.outDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), readFlagValue("--out", argv[i + 1]));
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg.startsWith("--out=")) {
      options.outDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), arg.slice("--out=".length).trim());
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--report") {
      options.reportPath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        readFlagValue("--report", argv[i + 1]),
      );
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg.startsWith("--report=")) {
      options.reportPath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        arg.slice("--report=".length).trim(),
      );
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--entities") {
      options.entitiesPath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        readFlagValue("--entities", argv[i + 1]),
      );
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg.startsWith("--entities=")) {
      options.entitiesPath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        arg.slice("--entities=".length).trim(),
      );
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--evidence") {
      options.evidencePath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        readFlagValue("--evidence", argv[i + 1]),
      );
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg.startsWith("--evidence=")) {
      options.evidencePath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        arg.slice("--evidence=".length).trim(),
      );
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--facts") {
      options.factsPath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        readFlagValue("--facts", argv[i + 1]),
      );
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg.startsWith("--facts=")) {
      options.factsPath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        arg.slice("--facts=".length).trim(),
      );
      continue;
    }

    throw new Error(
      `Unknown CLI argument "${arg}". Supported flags: --in <dir> [--rev <n> | --rev1 | --rev2 | --rev3] [--entities <path> --evidence <path> --facts <path>] [--out <dir>] [--report <path>] [--dry-run].`,
    );
  }

  return options;
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  const options = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  const outcome = await runAdaptResearchSeed(options);
  const { report } = outcome;

  if (!outcome.ok) {
    console.error(
      `Research seed adaptation FAILED (${report.selectedRevision}) with ${report.issues.length} validation error(s). Nothing was written to ${outcome.outDir}.`,
    );

    if (report.missingSourceTypeEvidenceRefs.length > 0) {
      console.error(
        `\n[BLOCKER — rev2 required before import] ${report.missingSourceTypeEvidenceRefs.length} evidence record(s) are missing "source_type":\n  ${report.missingSourceTypeEvidenceRefs.join(", ")}`,
      );
    }

    if (report.secondaryOnlyVerifiedFacts.length > 0) {
      console.error(
        `\n[BLOCKER — secondary-only verified facts] ${report.secondaryOnlyVerifiedFacts.length} fact(s) marked "verified" lack a non-secondary source:`,
      );
      for (const item of report.secondaryOnlyVerifiedFacts) {
        console.error(
          `  - ${item.factCode} (${item.entityCode} · ${item.attribute}) -> [${item.evidenceCodes.join(", ")}]`,
        );
      }
    }

    console.error("\nValidation issues:");
    for (const issue of report.issues) {
      console.error(`  - [${issue.path}] ${issue.message}`);
    }
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const modeLabel = outcome.wroteFiles
    ? `Wrote canonical seed files to ${outcome.outDir}`
    : `Dry-run validation passed (no files written)`;

  console.info(
    [
      `${modeLabel} [revision=${report.selectedRevision}, aliasSeparator=${report.detectedAliasSeparator}]:`,
      `  entities: ${report.counts.entities} (${report.counts.entitiesWithAliases} with aliases; ${report.counts.missingStatusDefaultedToUnknown} missing status -> 'unknown')`,
      `  evidence: ${report.counts.evidence} (${report.counts.evidenceMissingUrl} with url=null; ${report.bundleReport?.unresolvedOfficialEvidenceCount ?? 0} unresolved official: [${(report.bundleReport?.unresolvedOfficialEvidenceCodes ?? []).join(", ")}])`,
      `  facts:    ${report.counts.facts} (${report.counts.factEvidenceLinks} fact_evidence links; precisions: day=${report.counts.factDatePrecisions.day}, month=${report.counts.factDatePrecisions.month}, year=${report.counts.factDatePrecisions.year}, null=${report.counts.factDatePrecisions.null})`,
      `  warnings: ${report.bundleReport?.warnings.length ?? 0}`,
    ].join("\n"),
  );
  for (const warning of report.bundleReport?.warnings ?? []) {
    console.warn(`  [WARN ${warning.code}] ${warning.path}: ${warning.message}`);
  }
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  if (error instanceof SeedValidationError) {
    console.error(error.message);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  console.error(
    `Research seed adapter error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
  );
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: scripts/apply-osm-aliases.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Apply documented exact-label aliases to the adapted seed for the stored OSM extract."""
from __future__ import annotations
import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SEED = ROOT / "data/seed/entities.csv"
OVERRIDES = ROOT / "data/research/rev5/osm-alias-overrides.json"


def main() -> None:
    overrides = json.loads(OVERRIDES.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    with SEED.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        headers = reader.fieldnames
        rows = list(reader)
    if not headers or "code" not in headers or "aliases" not in headers:
        raise SystemExit("Canonical entities CSV must include code and aliases columns.")
    by_code = {row["code"]: row for row in rows}
    missing = sorted(set(overrides) - set(by_code))
    if missing:
        raise SystemExit(f"OSM alias overrides reference missing entities: {', '.join(missing)}")
    for code, additions in overrides.items():
        aliases = [part.strip() for part in (by_code[code].get("aliases") or "").split("|") if part.strip()]
        for alias in additions:
            if not isinstance(alias, str) or not alias.strip() or "|" in alias:
                raise SystemExit(f"Invalid OSM alias for {code}.")
            if alias not in aliases:
                aliases.append(alias)
        by_code[code]["aliases"] = "|".join(aliases)
    with SEED.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=headers, lineterminator="\n")
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    print(f"Applied OSM exact-label aliases for {len(overrides)} Metro entities.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


===== FILE: scripts/apply-rev9-status-delta.ts =====
import { readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import {
  adaptResearchSeedSources,
  buildDeterministicFactCode,
} from "../src/lib/atlas/research-seed";
import {
  escapeCsvCell,
  loadAndValidateSeedDir,
  serializeEntitiesCsv,
  validateSeedSources,
} from "../src/lib/atlas/seed";
import type { EntitySeed, EvidenceSeed, FactSeed } from "../src/lib/atlas/schema";

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const RESEARCH_DIR = path.join(ROOT, "data/research/rev9-status");
const SEED_DIR = path.join(ROOT, "data/seed");
const EXPECTED_STATUS_CHANGES = new Map([
  ["ROAD-AMR-IBN-ALAS", "operational"],
  ["ROAD-REGIONAL-NW-ARC", "operational"],
  ["TRANSIT-SUPERJET", "operational"],
]);

interface StatusChange {
  entity_code: string;
  from: string;
  to: string;
  basis: string[];
  caveat?: string;
}

function json(value: unknown): string {
  return `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`;
}

function csvCell(value: string): string {
  return escapeCsvCell(value);
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  const [current, evidenceText, factsText, statusText] = await Promise.all([
    loadAndValidateSeedDir(SEED_DIR),
    readFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "evidence_rev9.json"), "utf8"),
    readFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "facts_rev9.json"), "utf8"),
    readFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "status_changes_rev9.json"), "utf8"),
  ]);
  const incomingEvidence = JSON.parse(evidenceText) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  const incomingFacts = JSON.parse(factsText) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  const statusChanges = JSON.parse(statusText) as StatusChange[];

  if (incomingEvidence.length !== 6 || incomingFacts.length !== 9) {
    throw new Error(`rev9 must contain exactly 6 evidence rows and 9 facts; found ${incomingEvidence.length} and ${incomingFacts.length}.`);
  }
  if (statusChanges.length !== EXPECTED_STATUS_CHANGES.size ||
      statusChanges.some((change) => EXPECTED_STATUS_CHANGES.get(change.entity_code) !== change.to || change.from !== "unknown")) {
    throw new Error("rev9 status changes do not match the three approved unknown → operational transitions.");
  }
  const evidenceRefs = incomingEvidence.map((item) => String(item.evidence_ref ?? ""));
  if (evidenceRefs.some((ref) => !/^EV-R9-\d{3}$/.test(ref)) || new Set(evidenceRefs).size !== 6) {
    throw new Error("All rev9 evidence refs must be unique EV-R9-### identifiers.");
  }
  const oldEvidenceCodes = new Set(current.evidence.map((item) => item.code));
  const collisions = evidenceRefs.filter((ref) => oldEvidenceCodes.has(ref));
  if (collisions.length) {
    const alreadyApplied = collisions.length === evidenceRefs.length &&
      statusChanges.every((change) => current.entities.find((entity) => entity.code === change.entity_code)?.status === change.to) &&
      current.entities.filter((entity) => entity.status === "unknown").length === 14 &&
      current.facts.filter((fact) => fact.evidence_codes.some((ref) => evidenceRefs.includes(ref))).length === 9;
    if (!alreadyApplied) throw new Error(`Rev9 evidence refs collide with canonical evidence: ${collisions.join(", ")}`);
    console.info("Rev9 status delta is already present in the canonical seed; no files changed.");
    return;
  }

  const entitiesByCode = new Map(current.entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  const updatedEntities = current.entities.map((entity) => ({ ...entity }));
  for (const change of statusChanges) {
    const index = updatedEntities.findIndex((entity) => entity.code === change.entity_code);
    const entity = updatedEntities[index];
    if (!entity || entity.status !== change.from || !change.basis.every((ref) => evidenceRefs.includes(ref))) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid status delta for ${change.entity_code}: expected current ${change.from} and valid evidence basis.`);
    }
    updatedEntities[index] = { ...entity, status: change.to as EntitySeed["status"] };
  }

  // Adapt the immutable research-format files using the canonical research-seed adapter.
  // Supplying only the affected entities keeps this additive patch separate from prior facts.
  const researchEntitiesCsv = [
    "entity_code,entity_type,canonical_name_ar,canonical_name_en,aliases,sector_code,status,description",
    ...statusChanges.map((change) => {
      const entity = entitiesByCode.get(change.entity_code)!;
      return [entity.code, entity.entity_type, entity.name_ar, entity.name_en, entity.aliases.join("|"), entity.sector, change.to, entity.description ?? ""].map(csvCell).join(",");
    }),
    "",
  ].join("\n");
  const adapted = adaptResearchSeedSources({
    entitiesCsv: researchEntitiesCsv,
    evidenceJsonText: evidenceText,
    factsJsonText: factsText,
    selectedRevision: "rev9-status-delta",
  });
  if (!adapted.ok || !adapted.bundle || !adapted.serialized) {
    throw new Error(`rev9 adapter validation failed:\n${adapted.report.issues.map((issue) => `${issue.path}: ${issue.message}`).join("\n")}`);
  }
  if (adapted.bundle.evidence.length !== 6 || adapted.bundle.facts.length !== 9) {
    throw new Error("Adapter output did not preserve the expected six evidence records and nine fact rows.");
  }

  // Adapter fact sequence numbers are deterministic within the patch. Advance them beyond
  // existing canonical sequences so the additive merge cannot overwrite earlier claims.
  const existingFactCodes = new Set(current.facts.map((fact) => fact.code));
  const nextSequence = new Map<string, number>();
  const mergedFacts: FactSeed[] = [];
  for (const fact of adapted.bundle.facts) {
    const group = `${fact.entity_code}\u0000${fact.attribute}`;
    const existingForGroup = current.facts.filter((old) => old.entity_code === fact.entity_code && old.attribute === fact.attribute);
    const currentNext = nextSequence.get(group) ?? (existingForGroup.length + 1);
    let sequence = currentNext;
    let code = buildDeterministicFactCode(fact.entity_code, fact.attribute, sequence);
    while (existingFactCodes.has(code)) code = buildDeterministicFactCode(fact.entity_code, fact.attribute, ++sequence);
    nextSequence.set(group, sequence + 1);
    existingFactCodes.add(code);
    mergedFacts.push({ ...fact, code });
  }
  if (mergedFacts.some((fact) => fact.verification_state === "verified")) {
    throw new Error("rev9 includes a verified fact; none has official evidence.");
  }

  const mergedEvidence: EvidenceSeed[] = [...current.evidence, ...adapted.bundle.evidence];
  const allFacts = [...current.facts, ...mergedFacts];
  const validated = validateSeedSources({
    entitiesCsv: serializeEntitiesCsv(updatedEntities),
    evidenceJsonText: json(mergedEvidence),
    factsJsonText: json(allFacts),
  });
  if (validated.entities.filter((entity) => entity.status === "unknown").length !== 14) {
    throw new Error("Expected exactly 14 unknown-status entities after rev9.");
  }
  if (validated.entities.length !== current.entities.length || allFacts.length !== current.facts.length + 9 || mergedEvidence.length !== current.evidence.length + 6) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected non-additive changes in rev9 merge.");
  }

  await Promise.all([
    writeFile(path.join(SEED_DIR, "entities.csv"), serializeEntitiesCsv(updatedEntities), "utf8"),
    writeFile(path.join(SEED_DIR, "evidence.json"), json(mergedEvidence), "utf8"),
    writeFile(path.join(SEED_DIR, "facts.json"), json(allFacts), "utf8"),
    writeFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "evidence_rev9_adapted.json"), adapted.serialized.evidenceJsonText, "utf8"),
    writeFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "facts_rev9_adapted.json"), json(mergedFacts), "utf8"),
    writeFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "ADAPTATION_NOTES.md"), [
      "# rev9 status delta adaptation",
      "",
      "- Raw `README.md`, `evidence_rev9.json`, `facts_rev9.json`, and `status_changes_rev9.json` were copied unchanged from `research/rev9-status`.",
      "- The research-format evidence and facts were converted with `adaptResearchSeedSources`; canonical evidence refs remain in the `EV-R9-###` namespace.",
      "- The three status changes were applied additively to `data/seed/entities.csv`; no other entity status changed.",
      "- Nine adapted facts and six adapted news evidence rows were appended to the canonical seed. Existing facts and evidence were retained unchanged.",
      "- Adapter-generated fact codes were sequenced after existing facts for the same entity/attribute to prevent identifier collisions.",
      "- All rev9 facts retain their supplied `corroborated`/`single_source` verification state; no claim is marked `verified`.",
      "- `maintenance_closure` is displayed only in the existing researcher-only claim/source sections.",
      "",
    ].join("\n"), "utf8"),
  ]);
  console.info(`Applied rev9 status delta: ${validated.entities.length} entities (${validated.entities.filter((entity) => entity.status === "unknown").length} unknown), ${mergedEvidence.length} evidence records, ${allFacts.length} facts.`);
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: scripts/apply-south-batch04.ts =====
import { readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import {
  adaptResearchSeedSources,
  buildDeterministicFactCode,
} from "../src/lib/atlas/research-seed";
import {
  escapeCsvCell,
  loadAndValidateSeedDir,
  serializeEntitiesCsv,
  validateSeedSources,
} from "../src/lib/atlas/seed";
import type { EntitySeed, EvidenceSeed, FactSeed, FactVerificationState } from "../src/lib/atlas/schema";

/**
 * BATCH 15 — merge South sector research batch 04 into the canonical seed.
 *
 * Additive only. Nothing is deleted, no existing status or sector is rewritten, and no coordinate is
 * invented. Every number this script asserts is stated up front so a silent drift fails the run rather
 * than producing a plausible-looking seed.
 */
const ROOT = process.cwd();
const RESEARCH_DIR = path.join(ROOT, "data/research/south-batch04");
const SEED_DIR = path.join(ROOT, "data/seed");

/** The batch as curated. If any of these change, the researcher has to re-approve the numbers. */
const EXPECTED = {
  newEntities: 31,
  facts: 95,
  evidence: 70,
  statusChanges: 3,
  unknownBefore: 14,
  unknownOriginalsAfter: 11,
  totalUnknownAfter: 13,
} as const;

const APPROVED_STATUS_CHANGES = new Map([
  ["BRIDGE-MONIB", "operational"],
  ["ROAD-EASTERN-DESERT", "operational"],
  ["ROAD-MOKATTAM-AXIS", "operational"],
]);

interface StatusChange {
  entity_code: string;
  from: string;
  to: string;
  basis: string[];
  caveat?: string;
}

const json = (value: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`;
const csvCell = (value: string): string => escapeCsvCell(value);

/**
 * The batch's `entities.csv` is in **seed** format (`code,name_ar,…`), while the canonical adapter reads
 * the **research** format (`entity_code,canonical_name_ar,…`). Rather than fork the adapter or special-case
 * its parser, this converts the header/fields with a strict explicit mapping and fails on anything that
 * does not round-trip. The raw file itself is never modified.
 */
function seedCsvToResearchCsv(seedCsv: string, expectedRows: number): string {
  const lines = seedCsv.split(/\r?\n/).filter((line) => line.trim() !== "");
  const header = splitCsvLine(lines[0]!);
  const required = ["code", "name_ar", "name_en", "entity_type", "sector", "status", "aliases", "description"];
  if (header.length !== required.length || required.some((name, index) => header[index] !== name)) {
    throw new Error(`Unexpected entities.csv header: ${header.join(",")}`);
  }
  const rows = lines.slice(1);
  if (rows.length !== expectedRows) throw new Error(`Expected ${expectedRows} entity rows, found ${rows.length}.`);

  // Map by header name, never by position: the seed order is `code,name_ar,name_en,entity_type,sector,status,…`
  // while the research order is `entity_code,entity_type,canonical_name_ar,…`. Copying cells positionally
  // silently shifts every column (which is exactly what produced "Invalid entity_type <Arabic description>").
  const researchHeader = ["entity_code", "entity_type", "canonical_name_ar", "canonical_name_en", "aliases", "sector_code", "status", "description"];
  const sourceKey: Record<string, string> = {
    entity_code: "code",
    entity_type: "entity_type",
    canonical_name_ar: "name_ar",
    canonical_name_en: "name_en",
    aliases: "aliases",
    sector_code: "sector", // research calls the sector column `sector_code`; the seed calls it `sector`
    status: "status",
    description: "description",
  };
  const out = [researchHeader.join(",")];
  for (const line of rows) {
    const cells = splitCsvLine(line);
    if (cells.length !== required.length) {
      throw new Error(`Expected ${required.length} cells in entities.csv row, found ${cells.length}: ${line.slice(0, 60)}…`);
    }
    const byName = new Map(required.map((name, index) => [name, cells[index]!]));
    out.push(researchHeader.map((name) => escapeCsvCell(byName.get(sourceKey[name]!)!)).join(","));
  }
  return out.join("\n") + "\n";
}

/** Minimal RFC-4180 field split: honours double quotes so an embedded comma can never shift columns. */
function splitCsvLine(line: string): string[] {
  const cells: string[] = [];
  let cell = "";
  let quoted = false;
  for (let index = 0; index < line.length; index += 1) {
    const char = line[index]!;
    if (quoted) {
      if (char === '"') {
        if (line[index + 1] === '"') { cell += '"'; index += 1; }
        else quoted = false;
      } else cell += char;
    } else if (char === '"') quoted = true;
    else if (char === ",") { cells.push(cell); cell = ""; }
    else cell += char;
  }
  cells.push(cell);
  return cells;
}

/**
 * `url_check` has no column in the evidence schema, so it is preserved in the human-readable `note`
 * instead of being dropped — losing it would discard the one piece of information that says whether a
 * `verified` claim's link was actually opened.
 */
function foldUrlCheckIntoNote(row: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {
  const { url_check: urlCheck, ...rest } = row;
  if (typeof urlCheck !== "string" || urlCheck === "") return rest;
  const note = typeof rest.note === "string" ? rest.note : "";
  // Idempotent: a note that already carries the marker is not annotated twice.
  if (note.includes("url_check=")) return rest;
  return { ...rest, note: note ? `${note} · url_check=${urlCheck}` : `url_check=${urlCheck}` };
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  const [current, entitiesCsv, evidenceText, factsText, statusText] = await Promise.all([
    loadAndValidateSeedDir(SEED_DIR),
    readFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "entities.csv"), "utf8"),
    readFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "evidence.json"), "utf8"),
    readFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "facts.json"), "utf8"),
    readFile(path.join(RESEARCH_DIR, "status_changes.json"), "utf8"),
  ]);

  const incomingEvidenceRaw = JSON.parse(evidenceText) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  const incomingFactsRaw = JSON.parse(factsText) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  const statusChanges = JSON.parse(statusText) as StatusChange[];

  // ---- guard rails before anything is written -------------------------------------------------
  if (incomingEvidenceRaw.length !== EXPECTED.evidence) {
    throw new Error(`South batch 04 must contain ${EXPECTED.evidence} evidence rows; found ${incomingEvidenceRaw.length}.`);
  }
  if (incomingFactsRaw.length !== EXPECTED.facts) {
    throw new Error(`South batch 04 must contain ${EXPECTED.facts} facts; found ${incomingFactsRaw.length}.`);
  }
  if (statusChanges.length !== EXPECTED.statusChanges) {
    throw new Error(`South batch 04 must contain ${EXPECTED.statusChanges} status changes; found ${statusChanges.length}.`);
  }
  for (const change of statusChanges) {
    if (change.from !== "unknown" || APPROVED_STATUS_CHANGES.get(change.entity_code) !== change.to) {
      throw new Error(`Unapproved status change for ${change.entity_code}: ${change.from} → ${change.to}.`);
    }
  }
  const evidenceRefs = incomingEvidenceRaw.map((row) => String(row.evidence_ref ?? ""));
  if (evidenceRefs.length !== new Set(evidenceRefs).size) throw new Error("Duplicate evidence_ref in the batch.");
  if (evidenceRefs.some((ref) => !/^EV-S4-\d{3}$/.test(ref))) {
    throw new Error("All evidence refs must be unique EV-S4-### identifiers.");
  }

  const existingEntityCodes = new Set(current.entities.map((entity) => entity.code));
  const existingEvidenceCodes = new Set(current.evidence.map((row) => row.code));
  const collisions = evidenceRefs.filter((ref) => existingEvidenceCodes.has(ref));

  const alreadyApplied = collisions.length === evidenceRefs.length &&
    statusChanges.every((change) => current.entities.find((entity) => entity.code === change.entity_code)?.status === change.to);
  if (alreadyApplied) {
    console.info("South batch 04 is already present in the canonical seed; no files changed.");
    return;
  }
  if (collisions.length) {
    throw new Error(`South batch 04 evidence refs collide with canonical evidence: ${collisions.join(", ")}`);
  }

  // Any NEW entity code that already exists is a hard failure, per the brief.
  const researchEntitiesCsv = seedCsvToResearchCsv(entitiesCsv, EXPECTED.newEntities);
  const newCodes = entitiesCsv.split(/\r?\n/).slice(1).filter((line) => line.trim() !== "").map((line) => splitCsvLine(line)[0]!);
  const duplicateNewCodes = newCodes.filter((code, index) => newCodes.indexOf(code) !== index);
  if (duplicateNewCodes.length) throw new Error(`Duplicate codes inside the batch: ${duplicateNewCodes.join(", ")}`);
  const existingNewCodes = newCodes.filter((code) => existingEntityCodes.has(code));
  if (existingNewCodes.length) {
    throw new Error(`These batch entity codes already exist in the seed: ${existingNewCodes.join(", ")}`);
  }

  /**
   * The batch's facts do not only describe its own 31 new entities — several are evidence for the three
   * status changes and for already-seeded SOUTH entities (`ROAD-EASTERN-DESERT`, `TRANSIT-BRT`, …).
   * The adapter validates every `entity_code` against the CSV it is given, so those existing entities
   * are supplied for validation only and are filtered out again below; nothing about them is rewritten
   * here (their one approved field change is the status delta applied further down).
   */
  const newCodeSet = new Set(newCodes);
  const referencedExistingCodes = [
    ...new Set(
      incomingFactsRaw
        .map((row) => String(row.entity_code ?? ""))
        .filter((code) => code !== "" && !newCodeSet.has(code)),
    ),
  ];
  const missingFromSeed = referencedExistingCodes.filter((code) => !existingEntityCodes.has(code));
  if (missingFromSeed.length) {
    throw new Error(`Batch facts reference entities that exist neither in the batch nor in the seed: ${missingFromSeed.join(", ")}`);
  }

  const referenceRows = referencedExistingCodes.map((code) => {
    const entity = current.entities.find((item) => item.code === code)!;
    return [
      entity.code,
      entity.entity_type,
      entity.name_ar,
      entity.name_en,
      entity.aliases.join("|"),
      entity.sector,
      entity.status,
      entity.description ?? "",
    ].map(csvCell).join(",");
  });
  // One header at the top, then the batch rows and the reference rows that share the same columns.
  const adapterEntitiesCsv = [...researchEntitiesCsv.trimEnd().split("\n"), ...referenceRows].join("\n") + "\n";

  // ---- adapt the research-format files through the canonical adapter -------------------------
  const adapted = adaptResearchSeedSources({
    entitiesCsv: adapterEntitiesCsv,
    // Feed the evidence through the adapter after folding url_check into the note; the adapter's key
    // allow-list is strict and would otherwise reject the raw row.
    evidenceJsonText: json(incomingEvidenceRaw.map(foldUrlCheckIntoNote)),
    factsJsonText: factsText,
    selectedRevision: "south-batch04",
  });
  if (!adapted.ok || !adapted.bundle || !adapted.serialized) {
    throw new Error(`South batch 04 adapter validation failed:\n${adapted.report.issues.map((issue) => `${issue.path}: ${issue.message}`).join("\n")}`);
  }
  if (adapted.bundle.facts.length !== EXPECTED.facts ||
      adapted.bundle.evidence.length !== EXPECTED.evidence) {
    throw new Error("Adapter did not preserve the expected fact and evidence counts.");
  }
  const adaptedNewEntities = adapted.bundle.entities.filter((entity) => newCodeSet.has(entity.code));
  if (adaptedNewEntities.length !== EXPECTED.newEntities) {
    throw new Error(`Adapter returned ${adaptedNewEntities.length} new entities; expected ${EXPECTED.newEntities}.`);
  }

  /**
   * Verification states are carried through exactly as supplied. The README and the brief both say only
   * official + url_check=ok rows are `verified`, so this checks that invariant instead of re-deriving it —
   * if a future batch downgrades a row by accident, this fails rather than quietly publishing it.
   */
  // The adapter re-sorts facts (value, unit, evidence, status_as_of, notes) before assigning codes, so
  // `bundle.facts[i]` is NOT `facts.json[i]`. Match on the full identity instead of trusting the index.
  const suppliedByIdentity = new Map(
    incomingFactsRaw.map((row) => [
      JSON.stringify([
        String(row.entity_code ?? ""),
        String(row.attribute_key ?? ""),
        String(row.value ?? ""),
        row.unit === null || row.unit === undefined ? "" : String(row.unit),
        String(row.status_as_of ?? ""),
      ]),
      row,
    ]),
  );
  const evidenceByRef = new Map(incomingEvidenceRaw.map((row) => [String(row.evidence_ref), row]));
  for (const fact of adapted.bundle.facts) {
    const supplied = suppliedByIdentity.get(
      JSON.stringify([
        fact.entity_code,
        fact.attribute,
        String(fact.value),
        fact.unit === null ? "" : String(fact.unit),
        fact.valid_as_of ?? "",
      ]),
    );
    if (!supplied) {
      throw new Error(`Adapted fact ${fact.code} has no matching row in facts.json (identity mismatch).`);
    }
    const suppliedState = String(supplied.verification_state) as FactVerificationState;
    if (fact.verification_state !== suppliedState) {
      throw new Error(`Fact ${fact.code} was adapted to ${fact.verification_state} but supplied as ${suppliedState}.`);
    }
    if (suppliedState === "verified") {
      const evidence = evidenceByRef.get(String(supplied.evidence_ref));
      const qualifies = evidence?.source_type === "official" && evidence?.url_check === "ok" && typeof evidence?.source_url === "string";
      if (!qualifies) {
        throw new Error(`Fact ${fact.code} claims verified but its evidence is not official + url_check=ok with a URL.`);
      }
    }
  }

  // ---- append entities (all SOUTH, exactly as supplied) --------------------------------------
  const adaptedEntities: EntitySeed[] = adaptedNewEntities;
  if (adaptedEntities.some((entity) => entity.sector !== "SOUTH")) {
    throw new Error("Every entity in South batch 04 must be in the SOUTH sector.");
  }
  const mergedEntities: EntitySeed[] = [...current.entities, ...adaptedEntities];

  // ---- apply the three approved status changes additively ------------------------------------
  const changedEntities = mergedEntities.map((entity) => {
    const change = statusChanges.find((item) => item.entity_code === entity.code);
    if (!change) return entity;
    if (entity.status !== change.from) {
      throw new Error(`Cannot apply status change for ${entity.code}: expected ${change.from}, found ${entity.status}.`);
    }
    // Only the status changes; sector, geometry and every other field are untouched.
    return { ...entity, status: change.to as EntitySeed["status"] };
  });

  // ---- append evidence and facts, sequencing fact codes past existing ones -------------------
  const existingFactCodes = new Set(current.facts.map((fact) => fact.code));
  const nextSequence = new Map<string, number>();
  const mergedFacts: FactSeed[] = [];
  for (const fact of adapted.bundle.facts) {
    const group = `${fact.entity_code}\u0000${fact.attribute}`;
