    const existingForGroup = current.facts.filter((old) => old.entity_code === fact.entity_code && old.attribute === fact.attribute);
    const currentNext = nextSequence.get(group) ?? (existingForGroup.length + 1);
    let sequence = currentNext;
    let code = buildDeterministicFactCode(fact.entity_code, fact.attribute, sequence);
    while (existingFactCodes.has(code)) code = buildDeterministicFactCode(fact.entity_code, fact.attribute, ++sequence);
    nextSequence.set(group, sequence + 1);
    existingFactCodes.add(code);
    mergedFacts.push({ ...fact, code });
  }

  const mergedEvidence: EvidenceSeed[] = [...current.evidence, ...adapted.bundle.evidence];
  const allFacts = [...current.facts, ...mergedFacts];

  // ---- validate the whole bundle before writing ---------------------------------------------
  const validated = validateSeedSources({
    entitiesCsv: serializeEntitiesCsv(changedEntities),
    evidenceJsonText: json(mergedEvidence),
    factsJsonText: json(allFacts),
  });

  const unknownOriginal = validated.entities.filter((entity) => entity.status === "unknown" && existingEntityCodes.has(entity.code));
  const unknownNew = validated.entities.filter((entity) => entity.status === "unknown" && !existingEntityCodes.has(entity.code));
  if (unknownOriginal.length !== EXPECTED.unknownOriginalsAfter) {
    throw new Error(`Expected ${EXPECTED.unknownOriginalsAfter} pre-existing unknown-status entities after the merge; found ${unknownOriginal.length}.`);
  }
  if (unknownNew.length !== EXPECTED.totalUnknownAfter - EXPECTED.unknownOriginalsAfter) {
    throw new Error(`Expected ${EXPECTED.totalUnknownAfter - EXPECTED.unknownOriginalsAfter} new unknown-status entities; found ${unknownNew.length}.`);
  }
  if (validated.entities.length !== current.entities.length + EXPECTED.newEntities) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected non-additive entity change.");
  }
  if (allFacts.length !== current.facts.length + EXPECTED.facts || mergedEvidence.length !== current.evidence.length + EXPECTED.evidence) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected non-additive fact or evidence change.");
  }
  // Nothing may be lost: every original entity, fact and evidence code must still be present.
  for (const entity of current.entities) if (!validated.entities.some((row) => row.code === entity.code)) throw new Error(`Entity ${entity.code} disappeared.`);
  for (const fact of current.facts) if (!allFacts.some((row) => row.code === fact.code)) throw new Error(`Fact ${fact.code} disappeared.`);
  for (const evidence of current.evidence) if (!mergedEvidence.some((row) => row.code === evidence.code)) throw new Error(`Evidence ${evidence.code} disappeared.`);

  await Promise.all([
    writeFile(path.join(SEED_DIR, "entities.csv"), serializeEntitiesCsv(changedEntities), "utf8"),
    writeFile(path.join(SEED_DIR, "evidence.json"), json(mergedEvidence), "utf8"),
    writeFile(path.join(SEED_DIR, "facts.json"), json(allFacts), "utf8"),
  ]);

  console.info(`Applied South batch 04: ${validated.entities.length} entities (+${EXPECTED.newEntities}), ${mergedEvidence.length} evidence (+${EXPECTED.evidence}), ${allFacts.length} facts (+${EXPECTED.facts}). Unknown status: ${unknownOriginal.length} pre-existing + ${unknownNew.length} new = ${unknownOriginal.length + unknownNew.length}.`);
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: scripts/area_boundaries.py =====
"""Extract and conservatively match real OSM administrative/place area polygons."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import re
import unicodedata
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from typing import Any

from shapely.geometry import LineString, MultiPolygon, Point, Polygon, box, mapping
from shapely.ops import nearest_points, polygonize, unary_union

from road_gap_fill import haversine_km, normalize as normalize_road_label

BOUNDARY_BBOX = (30.2, 28.8, 32.5, 31.0)
OSM_ATTRIBUTION = "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0"
AREA_TYPES = {"city", "district", "neighborhood", "authority", "governorate"}
PLACE_VALUES = {"city", "town", "suburb", "quarter", "neighbourhood"}
DEFAULT_ADMIN_LEVELS = {
    "city": {"4", "6", "8"},
    "district": {"6", "8", "9", "10"},
    "neighborhood": {"8", "9", "10"},
    "authority": {"4", "6", "8", "9", "10"},
    "governorate": {"4"},
}
AREA_TYPE_ALIASES = {
    "city": {"city"},
    "district": {"district", "neighborhood"},
    "neighborhood": {"neighborhood", "district"},
    "authority": {"authority", "governorate"},
    "governorate": {"governorate", "authority"},
}
GENERIC_AREA_WORDS = {
    "مدينه", "المدينه", "محافظه", "حي", "الحي", "قسم", "القسم", "مركز", "منطقه", "المنطقه",
    "city", "town", "governorate", "district", "neighborhood", "suburb", "quarter",
}


def normalize_area_name(value: str | None) -> str:
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", value or "").casefold()
    text = text.translate(str.maketrans({"أ": "ا", "إ": "ا", "آ": "ا", "ٱ": "ا", "ة": "ه", "ى": "ي"}))
    text = "".join(ch for ch in text if unicodedata.category(ch) not in {"Mn", "Cf"})
    tokens = re.findall(r"[\w]+", text, flags=re.UNICODE)
    tokens = [token for token in tokens if token not in GENERIC_AREA_WORDS]
    return "".join(tokens)


def _coords(geometry: Any) -> list[list[float]]:
    if not isinstance(geometry, list):
        return []
    return [[float(point["lon"]), float(point["lat"])] for point in geometry
            if isinstance(point, dict) and isinstance(point.get("lon"), (int, float))
            and isinstance(point.get("lat"), (int, float))]


def _relation_polygon(element: dict[str, Any]):
    outer_lines: list[LineString] = []
    inner_lines: list[LineString] = []
    for member in element.get("members", []):
        if member.get("type") != "way":
            continue
        coords = _coords(member.get("geometry"))
        if len(coords) < 2:
            continue
        try:
            line = LineString(coords)
        except Exception:
            continue
        (inner_lines if member.get("role") == "inner" else outer_lines).append(line)
    if not outer_lines:
        return None
    try:
        polygons = list(polygonize(unary_union(outer_lines)))
        if not polygons:
            return None
        outer = unary_union(polygons)
        if inner_lines:
            holes = list(polygonize(unary_union(inner_lines)))
            if holes:
                outer = outer.difference(unary_union(holes))
        if outer.geom_type == "GeometryCollection":
            polygons = [part for part in outer.geoms if part.geom_type == "Polygon"]
            outer = unary_union(polygons) if polygons else outer
        return outer
    except Exception:
        return None


def _area_polygon(element: dict[str, Any]):
    if element.get("type") == "relation":
        return _relation_polygon(element)
    coords = _coords(element.get("geometry"))
    if len(coords) < 4 or coords[0] != coords[-1]:
        return None
    try:
        return Polygon(coords)
    except Exception:
        return None


def _labels(tags: dict[str, Any]) -> set[str]:
    values: list[str] = []
    for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en", "official_name", "alt_name", "old_name", "short_name"):
        value = tags.get(key)
        if isinstance(value, str):
            values.extend(part.strip() for part in re.split(r"[;|/]", value) if part.strip())
    return {key for value in values if (key := normalize_area_name(value))}


def _entity_names(entity: dict[str, str]) -> tuple[set[str], set[str]]:
    canonical = {normalize_area_name(entity.get(key)) for key in ("name_ar", "name_en")}
    canonical.discard("")
    aliases_value = entity.get("aliases", "") or ""
    if isinstance(aliases_value, list):
        alias_values = aliases_value
    else:
        alias_values = re.split(r"[|;]", str(aliases_value))
    aliases = {key for value in alias_values if (key := normalize_area_name(value))}
    return canonical, aliases


def _valid_admin_boundary(tags: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    level = str(tags.get("admin_level", ""))
    return tags.get("boundary") == "administrative" and level.isdigit() and 4 <= int(level) <= 10


def _source_priority(kind: str, tags: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
    if kind == "relation" and _valid_admin_boundary(tags):
        return 0
    if kind == "way" and _valid_admin_boundary(tags):
        return 1
    if kind == "relation" and tags.get("place") in PLACE_VALUES:
        return 2
    if tags.get("place") in PLACE_VALUES:
        return 3
    if kind == "relation" and tags.get("landuse") == "residential":
        return 4
    return 5


def _source_kind(tags: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    if _valid_admin_boundary(tags):
        return "administrative"
    if tags.get("landuse") == "residential":
        return "landuse"
    if tags.get("place") in PLACE_VALUES:
        return "place"
    return "administrative"


def _area_km2(geometry) -> float:
    point = geometry.representative_point()
    return geometry.area * 111.32 * 110.574 * max(0.1, abs(math.cos(math.radians(point.y))))


def _plausible_area_range(entity_type: str) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
    return {"district": (0.05, 60.0), "city": (1.0, 800.0)}.get(entity_type)


def _admin_level_priority(entity_type: str, level: str | None) -> int:
    preferred = {
        "city": ["8", "6", "4"],
        "district": ["9", "10", "8", "6"],
        "neighborhood": ["10", "9", "8"],
        "authority": ["4", "6", "8", "9", "10"],
        "governorate": ["4"],
    }.get(entity_type, [])
    try:
        return preferred.index(level) if level in preferred else len(preferred)
    except ValueError:
        return len(preferred)


def _local_bbox(point: list[float], radius_km: float) -> list[float]:
    lon, lat = point
    lon_delta = radius_km / (111.32 * max(0.1, abs(math.cos(math.radians(lat)))))
    lat_delta = radius_km / 110.574
    return [max(BOUNDARY_BBOX[0], lon - lon_delta), max(BOUNDARY_BBOX[1], lat - lat_delta),
            min(BOUNDARY_BBOX[2], lon + lon_delta), min(BOUNDARY_BBOX[3], lat + lat_delta)]


def _area_entity_points(entities: list[dict[str, str]], existing_features: list[dict[str, Any]] | None) -> dict[str, list[list[float]]]:
    allowed = {entity["code"] for entity in entities if entity.get("entity_type") in AREA_TYPES}
    points: dict[str, list[list[float]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for feature in existing_features or []:
        props = feature.get("properties", {})
        code = props.get("entity_code")
        geometry = feature.get("geometry", {})
        if code not in allowed or geometry.get("type") != "Point":
            continue
        coordinates = geometry.get("coordinates")
        if (isinstance(coordinates, list) and len(coordinates) >= 2 and
                all(isinstance(value, (int, float)) for value in coordinates[:2])):
            point = [float(coordinates[0]), float(coordinates[1])]
            if point not in points[code]:
                points[code].append(point)
    return dict(points)


def _point_distance_m(polygon, point: list[float]) -> float:
    probe = Point(point)
    if polygon.covers(probe):
        return 0.0
    nearest = nearest_points(probe, polygon)[1]
    return haversine_km(point, [nearest.x, nearest.y]) * 1000.0


def _name_score(tags: dict[str, Any], entity: dict[str, str]) -> int:
    canonical, aliases = _entity_names(entity)
    primary: set[str] = set()
    secondary: set[str] = set()
    for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en"):
        value = tags.get(key)
        if isinstance(value, str):
            primary.update(normalize_area_name(part) for part in re.split(r"[;|/]", value) if part.strip())
    for key in ("official_name", "alt_name", "old_name", "short_name"):
        value = tags.get(key)
        if isinstance(value, str):
            secondary.update(normalize_area_name(part) for part in re.split(r"[;|/]", value) if part.strip())
    if primary & canonical:
        return 3
    if primary & aliases:
        return 2
    return 1 if (primary | secondary) & (canonical | aliases) else 0


def process_osm_boundaries(data: dict[str, Any], entities: list[dict[str, str]], date: str,
                           rules: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
                           existing_features: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return one unambiguous admin, place, or named-residential polygon per area entity."""
    rules = rules or {}
    area_rules = rules.get("area_entity_rules", {})
    by_code = {entity["code"]: entity for entity in entities if entity.get("entity_type") in AREA_TYPES}
    points_by_code = _area_entity_points(entities, existing_features)
    point_radius_km = {"city": 80.0, "district": 35.0, "neighborhood": 20.0,
                       "authority": 80.0, "governorate": 80.0}
    entity_bboxes: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
    name_index: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    canonical_by_code: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    aliases_by_code: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    for code, entity in by_code.items():
        canonical, aliases = _entity_names(entity)
        canonical_by_code[code], aliases_by_code[code] = canonical, aliases
        for name in canonical | aliases:
            name_index[name].add(code)
        configured_bbox = area_rules.get(code, {}).get("bbox_wgs84")
        if configured_bbox:
            entity_bboxes[code] = list(configured_bbox)
        elif points_by_code.get(code):
            entity_bboxes[code] = _local_bbox(points_by_code[code][0], point_radius_km.get(entity.get("entity_type", ""), 35.0))
        else:
            entity_bboxes[code] = list(BOUNDARY_BBOX)

    candidates_by_entity: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    audit: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    examined = 0
    rejected_without_points: Counter[str] = Counter()
    for element in data.get("elements", []):
        kind = element.get("type")
        tags = element.get("tags", {})
        if kind not in {"way", "relation"} or not isinstance(tags, dict):
            continue
        boundary = _valid_admin_boundary(tags)
        place = tags.get("place") in PLACE_VALUES
        landuse = tags.get("landuse") == "residential" and bool(_labels(tags))
        if not (boundary or place or landuse):
            continue
        examined += 1
        try:
            osm_id = int(element["id"])
            version = int(element.get("version", 0) or 0)
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        labels = _labels(tags)
        label_candidates = set().union(*(name_index.get(label, set()) for label in labels)) if labels else set()
        if not label_candidates:
            continue
        if version < 1:
            audit.append({"osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "outcome": "rejected", "reason": "missing_osm_version",
                          "candidate_entity_codes": sorted(label_candidates)})
            continue
        level = str(tags.get("admin_level", "")) or None
        if boundary and (not level or not level.isdigit() or not 4 <= int(level) <= 10):
            audit.append({"osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "outcome": "rejected", "reason": "admin_level_outside_requested_4_to_10",
                          "candidate_entity_codes": sorted(label_candidates), "admin_level": level})
            continue
        geometry = _area_polygon(element)
        if geometry is None or geometry.is_empty or geometry.geom_type not in {"Polygon", "MultiPolygon"}:
            audit.append({"osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "outcome": "rejected", "reason": "no_real_polygon_geometry",
                          "candidate_entity_codes": sorted(label_candidates)})
            continue
        if not geometry.is_valid:
            geometry = geometry.buffer(0)
        if geometry.is_empty or not geometry.is_valid or geometry.geom_type not in {"Polygon", "MultiPolygon"}:
            audit.append({"osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "outcome": "rejected", "reason": "invalid_polygon",
                          "candidate_entity_codes": sorted(label_candidates)})
            continue

        qualified: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        rejected_codes: dict[str, str] = {}
        for code in sorted(label_candidates):
            entity = by_code[code]
            permitted_levels = area_rules.get(code, {}).get("admin_levels", DEFAULT_ADMIN_LEVELS.get(entity["entity_type"], set()))
            if boundary and level not in set(map(str, permitted_levels)):
                rejected_codes[code] = "admin_level_not_compatible_with_entity_type"
                continue
            if tags.get("place") in {"city", "town"} and entity["entity_type"] not in {"city", "district", "authority", "governorate"}:
                rejected_codes[code] = "place_type_not_compatible_with_area_entity"
                continue
            if tags.get("place") in {"suburb", "quarter", "neighbourhood"} and entity["entity_type"] not in {"city", "district", "neighborhood", "authority", "governorate"}:
                rejected_codes[code] = "place_type_not_compatible_with_area_entity"
                continue
            if not points_by_code.get(code):
                rejected_without_points[code] += 1
                rejected_codes[code] = "entity_has_no_existing_point_geometry_for_boundary_validation"
                continue
            bbox = entity_bboxes[code]
            if not box(*bbox).covers(geometry):
                rejected_codes[code] = "polygon_outside_entity_specific_bbox_guard"
                continue
            distances = [_point_distance_m(geometry, point) for point in points_by_code[code]]
            distance_m = min(distances)
            if distance_m > 1000.0:
                rejected_codes[code] = f"polygon_is_{distance_m:.1f}m_from_existing_entity_point_over_1000m_limit"
                continue
            area_km2 = _area_km2(geometry)
            area_range = _plausible_area_range(entity["entity_type"])
            cairo_governorate_override = code == "CITY-CAIRO" and kind == "relation" and osm_id == 4103336
            if area_range and not cairo_governorate_override and not (area_range[0] <= area_km2 <= area_range[1]):
                rejected_codes[code] = f"polygon_area_{area_km2:.3f}km2_outside_{area_range[0]:g}_to_{area_range[1]:g}km2_{entity['entity_type']}_range"
                continue
            minimum_area = area_rules.get(code, {}).get("minimum_area_km2")
            if minimum_area is not None and area_km2 < float(minimum_area):
                minimum_reason = area_rules.get(code, {}).get("minimum_area_reason", "candidate is a much smaller same-name sub-area")
                rejected_codes[code] = f"polygon_area_{area_km2:.3f}km2_below_entity_minimum_{float(minimum_area):g}km2; {minimum_reason}"
                continue
            if code == "DIST-BAB-SHAREYA" and osm_id == 709300098 and not boundary and area_km2 < 0.2:
                rejected_codes[code] = "place_neighbourhood_polygon_is_block_scale_0.09km2_not_the_whole_qism_district"
                continue
            canonical, aliases = canonical_by_code[code], aliases_by_code[code]
            primary_names = {normalize_area_name(tags.get(key)) for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en") if tags.get(key)}
            name_strength = 3 if primary_names & canonical else 2 if primary_names & aliases else _name_score(tags, entity)
            source_priority = _source_priority(kind, tags)
            qualified.append({"code": code, "geometry": geometry, "tags": tags, "element": element,
                              "distance_m": distance_m, "area_km2": area_km2, "bbox": bbox, "name_score": name_strength,
                              "source_priority": source_priority, "admin_level_priority": _admin_level_priority(entity["entity_type"], level),
                              "boundary_kind": "administrative" if boundary else "place" if place else "landuse",
                              "source_kind": _source_kind(tags)})
        if len(qualified) > 1:
            audit.append({"osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "outcome": "ambiguous",
                          "labels": sorted(labels), "admin_level": level,
                          "candidate_entity_codes": sorted(candidate["code"] for candidate in qualified),
                          "reason": "name, entity bbox and existing-point proximity leave multiple area entities"})
        elif len(qualified) == 1:
            candidates_by_entity[qualified[0]["code"]].append(qualified[0])
            audit.append({"osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "outcome": "candidate",
                          "entity_code": qualified[0]["code"], "admin_level": level,
                          "distance_to_existing_point_m": round(qualified[0]["distance_m"], 1),
                          "source_priority": qualified[0]["source_priority"]})
        elif label_candidates:
            audit.append({"osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "outcome": "rejected",
                          "labels": sorted(labels), "admin_level": level,
                          "candidate_entity_codes": sorted(label_candidates), "rejections": rejected_codes,
                          "reason": "no entity passed admin/place type, point-distance, entity-bbox, or area-size guards"})

    selected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    matched_by_type: Counter[str] = Counter()
    ambiguous_entities: dict[str, Any] = {}
    unmatched_entities: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for code, entity in sorted(by_code.items()):
        candidates = candidates_by_entity.get(code, [])
        if not candidates:
            reason = "no named polygon passed the point/bbox/type filters"
            if not points_by_code.get(code):
                reason = "entity has no existing point geometry to validate a boundary candidate"
            unmatched_entities[code] = {"entity_type": entity["entity_type"], "reason": reason}
            continue
        if code == "CITY-CAIRO":
            best = [item for item in candidates
                    if item["element"].get("type") == "relation" and int(item["element"].get("id", 0)) == 4103336]
        else:
            ranked_by_area = sorted(candidates, key=lambda item: item["area_km2"], reverse=True)
            largest = ranked_by_area[0]
            runner_up_area = ranked_by_area[1]["area_km2"] if len(ranked_by_area) > 1 else 0.0
            if len(ranked_by_area) == 1 or largest["area_km2"] >= max(runner_up_area * 1.5, runner_up_area + 0.1):
                # A clearly larger plausible polygon wins over a same-named block/sub-neighbourhood.
                best = [largest]
            else:
                best_priority = min((item["source_priority"], item["admin_level_priority"], -item["name_score"])
                                    for item in candidates)
                best = [item for item in candidates
                        if (item["source_priority"], item["admin_level_priority"], -item["name_score"]) == best_priority]
        if len(best) != 1:
            ambiguous_entities[code] = {
                "entity_type": entity["entity_type"],
                "candidate_osm_ids": [{"osm_type": item["element"]["type"], "osm_id": int(item["element"]["id"]),
                                       "admin_level": str(item["tags"].get("admin_level", "")) or None,
                                       "distance_to_point_m": round(item["distance_m"], 1),
                                       "priority": item["source_priority"]} for item in best],
                "reason": "equally prioritized OSM boundary candidates; no geometry selected",
            }
            unmatched_entities[code] = {"entity_type": entity["entity_type"], "reason": "ambiguous_boundary_candidates"}
            continue
        chosen = best[0]
        item, tags = chosen["element"], chosen["tags"]
        kind = item["type"]
        level = str(tags.get("admin_level", "")) or None
        osm_id = int(item["id"])
        version = int(item.get("version", 0) or 0)
        feature_code = f"OSM-BOUNDARY-{kind.upper()}-{osm_id}-{code}"
        source_ids = {"osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "osm_version": version}
        props = {
            "role": "boundary", "entity_type": entity["entity_type"],
            "osm_tags": tags, "boundary_kind": chosen["boundary_kind"], "source_kind": chosen["source_kind"],
            "area_km2": round(chosen["area_km2"], 3),
            "display_label_ar": "حدود محافظة القاهرة" if code == "CITY-CAIRO" else None,
            "display_label_en": "Cairo Governorate boundary" if code == "CITY-CAIRO" else None,
            "admin_level": level, "name": tags.get("name"),
            "name_ar": tags.get("name:ar"), "name_en": tags.get("name:en"),
            "source_ids": source_ids, "source": "OpenStreetMap",
            "attribution": OSM_ATTRIBUTION, "license": "ODbL-1.0", "retrieval_date": date,
            "distance_to_existing_point_m": round(chosen["distance_m"], 1),
            "matching_priority": {"source": chosen["source_priority"], "admin_level": chosen["admin_level_priority"], "name": chosen["name_score"]},
        }
        selected.append({"type": "Feature", "id": feature_code, "geometry": mapping(chosen["geometry"]),
                         "properties": {"code": feature_code, "entity_code": code,
                                        "osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "osm_version": version,
                                        "license": "ODbL-1.0", "properties": props}})
        matched_by_type[entity["entity_type"]] += 1
        for lower in candidates:
            if lower is not chosen:
                reason = (f"same-name plausible polygon is smaller ({lower['area_km2']:.3f} km2) than selected "
                          f"candidate ({chosen['area_km2']:.3f} km2); larger plausible polygon preferred"
                          if lower["area_km2"] < chosen["area_km2"] else
                          "higher-priority administrative relation/place source selected")
                audit.append({"osm_type": lower["element"]["type"], "osm_id": int(lower["element"]["id"]),
                              "outcome": "superseded", "entity_code": code,
                              "selected_osm_type": kind, "selected_osm_id": osm_id,
                              "reason": reason})
        audit.append({"osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "outcome": "matched", "entity_code": code,
                      "admin_level": level, "source_kind": chosen["source_kind"],
                      "area_km2": round(chosen["area_km2"], 3),
                      "distance_to_existing_point_m": round(chosen["distance_m"], 1),
                      "matching_priority": props["matching_priority"], "bbox_wgs84": chosen["bbox"]})

    counts_by_type: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for entity_type in sorted({entity["entity_type"] for entity in by_code.values()}):
        total = sum(entity.get("entity_type") == entity_type for entity in by_code.values())
        matched = matched_by_type[entity_type]
        ambiguous = sum(value["entity_type"] == entity_type for value in ambiguous_entities.values())
        counts_by_type[entity_type] = {"entities": total, "matched": matched, "unmatched": total - matched - ambiguous,
                                       "ambiguous": ambiguous}
    return sorted(selected, key=lambda item: item["properties"]["entity_code"]), {
        "matched_count": len(selected), "audit": audit,
        "source": "OpenStreetMap boundary=administrative / place / named landuse=residential polygons",
        "attribution": OSM_ATTRIBUTION, "license": "ODbL-1.0",
        "entity_counts_by_type": counts_by_type,
        "matched_entities": sorted(item["properties"]["entity_code"] for item in selected),
        "unmatched_entities": unmatched_entities,
        "ambiguous_entities": ambiguous_entities,
        "entity_bbox_guards_wgs84": entity_bboxes,
        "existing_point_entity_count": len(points_by_code),
        "source_polygon_elements_examined": examined,
        "candidates_passing_entity_guards": sum(len(values) for values in candidates_by_entity.values()),
    }


def boundary_candidate_review(data: dict[str, Any], entities: list[dict[str, str]],
                              selected_features: list[dict[str, Any]],
                              existing_features: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
                              previous_features: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
                              rules: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
                              boundary_audit: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Rank exact-name candidates and explain every automatic acceptance/rejection."""
    rules = rules or {}
    area_rules = rules.get("area_entity_rules", {})
    by_code = {entity["code"]: entity for entity in entities
               if entity.get("entity_type") in {"city", "district"}}
    points_by_code = _area_entity_points(entities, existing_features)
    name_index: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    for code, entity in by_code.items():
        canonical, aliases = _entity_names(entity)
        for normalized in canonical | aliases:
            name_index[normalized].add(code)
    selected_by_code = {
        item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code"): item
        for item in selected_features
    }
    previous_codes = {item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code") for item in previous_features or []}
    candidates_by_code: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    landuse_ids_by_code: dict[str, set[int]] = defaultdict(set)
    shared_ambiguous_sources: set[tuple[str, int, str]] = set()
    for audit_item in (boundary_audit or {}).get("audit", []):
        if audit_item.get("outcome") == "ambiguous":
            for code in audit_item.get("candidate_entity_codes", []):
                shared_ambiguous_sources.add((str(audit_item.get("osm_type")), int(audit_item.get("osm_id", 0)), code))

    for element in data.get("elements", []):
        kind = element.get("type")
        tags = element.get("tags", {})
        if kind not in {"way", "relation"} or not isinstance(tags, dict):
            continue
        raw_admin = tags.get("boundary") == "administrative"
        is_admin = _valid_admin_boundary(tags)
        is_place = tags.get("place") in PLACE_VALUES
        is_landuse = tags.get("landuse") == "residential" and bool(_labels(tags))
        if not (raw_admin or is_place or is_landuse):
            continue
        labels = _labels(tags)
        matched_codes = set().union(*(name_index.get(label, set()) for label in labels)) if labels else set()
        if not matched_codes:
            continue
        geometry = _area_polygon(element)
        if geometry is not None and (not geometry.is_valid):
            geometry = geometry.buffer(0)
        valid_geometry = bool(geometry is not None and not geometry.is_empty and geometry.is_valid and
                              geometry.geom_type in {"Polygon", "MultiPolygon"})
        area_km2 = _area_km2(geometry) if valid_geometry else None
        source_kind = _source_kind(tags)
        osm_id = int(element.get("id", 0) or 0)
        version = int(element.get("version", 0) or 0)
        if is_landuse:
            for code in matched_codes:
                landuse_ids_by_code[code].add(osm_id)
        for code in matched_codes:
            entity = by_code[code]
            points = points_by_code.get(code, [])
            distance_m = min((_point_distance_m(geometry, point) for point in points), default=None) if valid_geometry else None
            reasons: list[str] = []
            if raw_admin and not is_admin and not is_place and not is_landuse:
                reasons.append("boundary=administrative has no valid admin_level 4–10 and no supported place/landuse tag")
            if (kind, osm_id, code) in shared_ambiguous_sources:
                reasons.append("the same polygon name matches multiple seed entities; cross-entity assignment is ambiguous")
            if version < 1:
                reasons.append("OSM version missing or invalid")
            if not valid_geometry:
                reasons.append("polygon geometry missing, invalid, or not polygonal")
            if not points:
                reasons.append("entity has no existing point geometry; proximity guard cannot be checked")
            elif valid_geometry:
                configured_bbox = area_rules.get(code, {}).get("bbox_wgs84")
                bbox = list(configured_bbox) if configured_bbox else _local_bbox(
                    points[0], {"city": 80.0, "district": 35.0}.get(entity["entity_type"], 35.0))
                if not box(*bbox).covers(geometry):
                    reasons.append("polygon falls outside entity-specific bbox guard")
                if distance_m is not None and distance_m > 1000:
                    reasons.append(f"polygon is {distance_m:.0f} m from entity point; exceeds 1 km point-to-polygon guard")
                if distance_m is not None and distance_m > 2000:
                    reasons.append("candidate also lies beyond the 2 km candidate search radius")
            area_range = _plausible_area_range(entity["entity_type"])
            cairo_override = code == "CITY-CAIRO" and kind == "relation" and osm_id == 4103336
            if area_km2 is not None and area_range and not cairo_override and not (area_range[0] <= area_km2 <= area_range[1]):
                reasons.append(f"area {area_km2:.3f} km² is outside {entity['entity_type']} range {area_range[0]:g}–{area_range[1]:g} km²")
            minimum_area = area_rules.get(code, {}).get("minimum_area_km2")
            if area_km2 is not None and minimum_area is not None and area_km2 < float(minimum_area):
                minimum_reason = area_rules.get(code, {}).get("minimum_area_reason", "candidate is a much smaller same-name sub-area")
                reasons.append(f"area {area_km2:.3f} km² is below entity-specific minimum {float(minimum_area):g} km²: {minimum_reason}")
            if code == "DIST-BAB-SHAREYA" and osm_id == 709300098 and not is_admin and (area_km2 or 0) < 0.2:
                reasons.append("rejected: 0.09 km² place=neighbourhood is a block, not the full Bab al-Shaariya qism district")
            chosen = selected_by_code.get(code)
            if chosen and chosen.get("properties", {}).get("osm_id") == osm_id and chosen.get("properties", {}).get("osm_type") == kind:
                if cairo_override:
                    decision = "accepted by explicit CITY-CAIRO governorate override; relation 4103336 is labeled as Cairo Governorate"
                    reasons = []
                else:
                    decision = "accepted: unique/selected plausible exact-name candidate within point guard"
                    reasons = []
            elif chosen:
                selected_area = chosen.get("properties", {}).get("properties", {}).get("area_km2")
                if area_km2 is not None and isinstance(selected_area, (int, float)) and area_km2 < selected_area:
                    reasons.append(f"superseded by larger plausible same-name polygon ({selected_area:.3f} km²)")
                else:
                    reasons.append(f"not selected; another candidate won the source/uniqueness policy (OSM {chosen.get('properties', {}).get('osm_type')}/{chosen.get('properties', {}).get('osm_id')})")
                decision = "; ".join(dict.fromkeys(reasons))
            elif not reasons:
                reasons.append("not auto-accepted: multiple equally plausible exact-name candidates remain; ambiguity is not guessed")
                decision = reasons[0]
            else:
                decision = "; ".join(dict.fromkeys(reasons))
            names = {key: tags.get(key) for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en", "alt_name", "old_name") if tags.get(key)}
            candidates_by_code[code].append({
                "osm_type": kind, "osm_id": osm_id, "osm_version": version,
                "names": names, "normalized_names": sorted(labels), "area_km2": round(area_km2, 3) if area_km2 is not None else None,
                "distance_to_entity_point_m": round(distance_m, 1) if distance_m is not None else None,
                "place": tags.get("place"), "admin_level": tags.get("admin_level"),
                "boundary": tags.get("boundary"), "landuse": tags.get("landuse"),
                "source_kind": source_kind, "decision": decision,
                "rejected": bool(reasons),
            })

    previous_by_type: Counter[str] = Counter()
    selected_by_type: Counter[str] = Counter()
    for item in previous_features or []:
        code = item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code")
        if code in by_code:
            previous_by_type[by_code[code]["entity_type"]] += 1
    for code in selected_by_code:
        if code in by_code:
            selected_by_type[by_code[code]["entity_type"]] += 1
    entities_report: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for code, entity in sorted(by_code.items()):
        chosen = selected_by_code.get(code)
        all_candidates = candidates_by_code.get(code, [])
        all_candidates.sort(key=lambda item: (
            item["distance_to_entity_point_m"] is None,
            item["distance_to_entity_point_m"] if item["distance_to_entity_point_m"] is not None else 0,
            -(item["area_km2"] or 0), item["source_kind"], item["osm_type"], item["osm_id"]))
        if chosen:
            result = "matched"
            reason = ("explicit governorate relation override; show as Cairo Governorate" if code == "CITY-CAIRO"
                      else "automatically matched after name, distance, area, and source-priority guards")
        elif code in {item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code") for item in selected_features}:
            result, reason = "ambiguous", "multiple plausible candidates; no geometry selected"
        elif not all_candidates:
            result, reason = "unmatched", "no exact normalized-name candidate polygon exists in the 07.4 extract"
        elif not points_by_code.get(code):
            result, reason = "unmatched", "no existing entity point, so the 1 km proximity guard cannot be verified"
        else:
            eligible = [item for item in all_candidates if not item["rejected"]]
            result, reason = ("unmatched", "no candidate passed every automatic acceptance guard") if not eligible else (
                "ambiguous", "more than one plausible candidate; no geometry selected")
        selected_candidate = next((candidate for candidate in all_candidates if chosen and
                                   candidate["osm_id"] == chosen.get("properties", {}).get("osm_id") and
                                   candidate["osm_type"] == chosen.get("properties", {}).get("osm_type")), None)
        entities_report[code] = {
            "entity_type": entity["entity_type"], "name_ar": entity.get("name_ar"), "name_en": entity.get("name_en"),
            "entity_point": points_by_code.get(code, [None])[0], "status": result, "summary_reason": reason,
            "candidate_count": len(all_candidates), "top_candidates": all_candidates[:3],
            "selected_candidate": selected_candidate,
            "matched_osm_id": chosen.get("properties", {}).get("osm_id") if chosen else None,
        }
    example_codes = {"CITY-REHAB": "الرحاب", "CITY-MADINATY": "مدينتي", "DIST-NARGES": "النرجس", "DIST-DEGLA": "دجلة"}
    landuse_examples = {}
    for code, label in example_codes.items():
        ids = sorted(landuse_ids_by_code.get(code, set()))
        landuse_examples[code] = {"label": label, "named_residential_landuse_present": bool(ids),
                                  "osm_ids": ids,
                                  "status": "present in 07.4 extract" if ids else "needs extract for named landuse=residential polygon"}
    counts = {}
    for entity_type in ("city", "district"):
        total = sum(1 for entity in by_code.values() if entity["entity_type"] == entity_type)
        counts[entity_type] = {"entities": total, "matched_before": previous_by_type[entity_type],
                               "matched_after": selected_by_type[entity_type],
                               "unmatched_after": total - selected_by_type[entity_type]}
    return {"counts_by_type": counts, "entities": entities_report,
            "landuse_examples": landuse_examples,
            "existing_boundary_codes_before": sorted(previous_codes),
            "existing_boundary_codes_after": sorted(code for code in selected_by_code if code in by_code),
            "normalization": "NFKC/casefold; Arabic أ/إ/آ/ٱ→ا, ة→ه, ى→ي; remove حي/قسم/مدينة/منطقة/District/City and generic area words",
            "acceptance": "exact normalized canonical/alias match; point covered or <=1km away (search radius <=2km); city 1-800km2, district 0.05-60km2; larger plausible same-name polygon can supersede a block; no guess on ties"}


===== FILE: scripts/build-rev8-transit-draft.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build a non-seed rev8 research draft for OSM-derived transit and ENR stations."""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
import re
import unicodedata
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REV7 = ROOT / "data/research/rev7"
OUT = ROOT / "data/research/rev8-draft"
REPORT = ROOT / "data/research/osm-stations/stations_from_osm.json"
ACTIVE_LINES = {
    "TRANSIT-METRO-L1", "TRANSIT-METRO-L2", "TRANSIT-METRO-L3", "TRANSIT-LRT", "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST",
}
OFFICIAL_COUNTS = {
    "TRANSIT-METRO-L1": (35, "EV-140"), "TRANSIT-METRO-L2": (20, "EV-143"),
    "TRANSIT-METRO-L3": (34, "EV-312"), "TRANSIT-LRT": (12, "EV-073"),
    "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST": (22, "EV-050"),
}
ENTITY_FIELDS = ["entity_code", "entity_type", "canonical_name_ar", "canonical_name_en", "aliases", "sector_code", "status", "description"]


def norm(value: Any) -> str:
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", str(value or "")).casefold()
    text = "".join(char for char in text if not unicodedata.combining(char)).replace("ـ", "")
    return re.sub(r"[^\w]+", "", text, flags=re.UNICODE)


def is_arabic(value: str) -> bool:
    return bool(re.search(r"[\u0600-\u06ff]", value))


def is_latin(value: str) -> bool:
    return bool(re.search(r"[A-Za-z]", value))


def load_raw_node_tags() -> dict[int, dict[str, Any]]:
    tags: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for path in sorted((ROOT / "data/raw").glob("*.json*")):
        opener = __import__("gzip").open if path.suffix == ".gz" else open
        with opener(path, "rt", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            payload = json.load(stream)
        for element in payload.get("elements", []):
            if element.get("type") == "node":
                node_id = int(element["id"])
                incoming = element.get("tags", {}) or {}
                if len(incoming) >= len(tags.get(node_id, {})):
                    tags[node_id] = incoming
    return tags


def node_status(node_ids: list[int], tags_by_node: dict[int, dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    tags = [tags_by_node.get(int(node_id), {}) for node_id in node_ids]
    if any(t.get("railway") == "construction" or t.get("construction:railway") for t in tags):
        return "under_construction"
    if any(t.get("railway") == "disused" or t.get("disused:railway") or t.get("disused") for t in tags):
        return "unknown"
    return "operational"


def localized_names(station: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, str]:
    raw_name = str(station.get("name") or "").strip()
    name_ar = str(station.get("name_ar") or "").strip()
    name_en = str(station.get("name_en") or "").strip()
    if not name_ar and raw_name and is_arabic(raw_name):
        name_ar = raw_name
    if not name_en and raw_name and is_latin(raw_name):
        name_en = raw_name
    return name_ar, name_en


def add_fact(facts: list[dict[str, Any]], code: str, attribute: str, value: Any, evidence: list[str], unit: str | None = None) -> None:
    facts.append({
        "entity_code": code,
        "attribute_key": attribute,
        "value": value,
        "unit": unit,
        "status_as_of": "2026-11-14",
        "verification_state": "candidate",
        "evidence_ref": evidence if len(evidence) != 1 else evidence[0],
    })


def main() -> None:
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with (REV7 / "entities_rev7.csv").open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as stream:
        entities = list(csv.DictReader(stream))
    with (REV7 / "facts_rev7.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        facts = json.load(stream)
    with (REV7 / "evidence_rev7.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        evidence = json.load(stream)
    with REPORT.open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        report = json.load(stream)

    existing_codes = {row["entity_code"] for row in entities}
    existing_type_by_code = {row["entity_code"]: row["entity_type"] for row in entities}
    existing_max_evidence = max(int(row["evidence_ref"].removeprefix("EV-")) for row in evidence)
    first_new_evidence = max(existing_max_evidence + 1, 351)
    relation_evidence: dict[int, str] = {}
    relation_info: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}

    line_reports = {row["line_code"]: row for row in report["lines"]}
    for line_code in sorted(ACTIVE_LINES):
        line = line_reports.get(line_code)
        if not line:
            continue
        for relation in line["relations"]:
            relation_info[int(relation["relation_id"])] = {
                "line_code": line_code,
                "title": relation.get("route_name_en") or relation.get("route_name") or line_code,
                "stations": relation["ordered_stations"],
            }
    for relation in report.get("enr_route_relations", []):
        relation_info[int(relation["relation_id"])] = {
            "line_code": "ENR",
            "title": relation.get("route_name_en") or relation.get("route_name") or "Egyptian National Railways train route",
            "stations": relation["ordered_stations"],
        }

    new_evidence = []
    for offset, (relation_id, info) in enumerate(sorted(relation_info.items())):
        code = f"EV-{first_new_evidence + offset:03d}"
        relation_evidence[relation_id] = code
        new_evidence.append({
            "evidence_ref": code,
            "source_url": f"https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/{relation_id}",
            "publisher": "OpenStreetMap contributors",
            "title": f"OpenStreetMap route relation {relation_id}: {info['title']}",
            "note": f"Stored OSM route relation; route membership/order source for {info['line_code']}.",
            "source_type": "dataset",
            "published_date": None,
            "accessed_date": "2026-11-14",
        })
    evidence.extend(new_evidence)

    # Create one station record per canonical OSM station group; old STOP codes are retained.
    station_groups = [
        station for station in report["stations"]
        if set(station.get("line_codes", [])) & ACTIVE_LINES
    ]
    selected_group_codes: dict[int, str] = {}
    group_by_node: dict[int, int] = {}
    new_entities = []
    tags_by_node = load_raw_node_tags()
    for index, station in enumerate(station_groups):
        group_id = int(station["osm_node_id"])
        node_ids = sorted({int(node) for node in station.get("osm_node_ids", [group_id])})
        for node_id in node_ids:
            group_by_node[node_id] = group_id
        eligible = [
            row["entity_code"] for row in station.get("existing_entities", [])
            if row["entity_code"] in existing_codes
            and existing_type_by_code.get(row["entity_code"]) in {"transit_stop", "rail_station"}
        ]
        if eligible:
            code = sorted(eligible)[0]
        else:
            code = f"STOP-OSM-{group_id}"
            if code in existing_codes:
                raise ValueError(f"Generated STOP code already exists: {code}")
            name_ar, name_en = localized_names(station)
            lines = set(station.get("line_codes", [])) & ACTIVE_LINES
            status = node_status(node_ids, tags_by_node) if lines else "unknown"
            sector = "EAST" if lines & {"TRANSIT-LRT", "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST"} and not lines & {"TRANSIT-METRO-L1", "TRANSIT-METRO-L2", "TRANSIT-METRO-L3"} else "CENTRAL"
            new_entities.append({
                "entity_code": code,
                "entity_type": "transit_stop",
                "canonical_name_ar": name_ar,
                "canonical_name_en": name_en,
                "aliases": "",
                "sector_code": sector,
                "status": status,
                "description": "OSM-derived research draft; not promoted to seed; official verification pending.",
            })
            existing_codes.add(code)
        selected_group_codes[group_id] = code

    entities.extend(new_entities)

    # Add one ENR station entity per named route=train stop in the source relations.
    rail_by_node: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    rail_memberships: dict[int, list[tuple[int, int]]] = defaultdict(list)
    rail_names = [row for row in entities if row["entity_type"] == "rail_station"]
    rail_name_keys = {
        row["entity_code"]: {norm(row.get(field, "")) for field in ("canonical_name_ar", "canonical_name_en", "aliases") if norm(row.get(field, ""))}
        for row in rail_names
    }
    for relation_id, info in sorted(relation_info.items()):
        if info["line_code"] != "ENR":
            continue
        for station in info["stations"]:
            node_id = int(station["osm_node_id"])
            current = rail_by_node.get(node_id)
            if current is None or (station.get("osm_node_version") or 0) >= (current.get("osm_node_version") or 0):
                rail_by_node[node_id] = station
            rail_memberships[node_id].append((relation_id, int(station.get("line_order", 0))))
    rail_entity_code: dict[int, str] = {}
    new_rail_entities = []
    for node_id, station in sorted(rail_by_node.items()):
        names = {norm(station.get(key)) for key in ("name", "name_ar", "name_en") if norm(station.get(key))}
        matches = [code for code, name_set in rail_name_keys.items() if names & name_set]
        if len(matches) > 1:
            raise ValueError(f"Ambiguous existing RAIL match for OSM node {node_id}: {matches}")
        if matches:
            code = matches[0]
        else:
            code = f"RAIL-OSM-{node_id}"
            if code in existing_codes:
                raise ValueError(f"Generated RAIL code already exists: {code}")
            name_ar, name_en = localized_names(station)
            new_rail_entities.append({
                "entity_code": code, "entity_type": "rail_station",
                "canonical_name_ar": name_ar, "canonical_name_en": name_en,
                "aliases": "", "sector_code": "CENTRAL",
                "status": node_status([node_id], tags_by_node),
                "description": "OSM-derived ENR route station research draft; not promoted to seed; official verification pending.",
            })
            existing_codes.add(code)
        rail_entity_code[node_id] = code
    entities.extend(new_rail_entities)

    new_facts = []
    # Map relation members to the canonical group and retain each relation's order.
    group_relations: dict[int, set[int]] = defaultdict(set)
    for relation_id, info in sorted(relation_info.items()):
        if info["line_code"] == "ENR":
            continue
        ordered_group_ids = []
        seen = set()
        for stop in info["stations"]:
            node_id = int(stop["osm_node_id"])
            group_id = group_by_node.get(node_id)
            if group_id is None:
                continue
            group_relations[group_id].add(relation_id)
            if group_id not in seen:
                ordered_group_ids.append(group_id)
                seen.add(group_id)
        # Keep the first occurrence in source member order after grouping stop/platform copies.
        for order, group_id in enumerate(ordered_group_ids, start=1):
            add_fact(new_facts, selected_group_codes[group_id], "line_order", order,
                     [relation_evidence[relation_id]], f"stop position in OSM relation {relation_id}")

    node_relation_refs: dict[tuple[int, int], set[int]] = defaultdict(set)
    for relation_id, info in sorted(relation_info.items()):
        for station in info["stations"]:
            node_relation_refs[(int(station["osm_node_id"]), relation_id)].add(relation_id)

    for station in station_groups:
        group_id = int(station["osm_node_id"])
        code = selected_group_codes[group_id]
        lines = sorted(set(station.get("line_codes", [])) & ACTIVE_LINES)
        relation_ids = sorted(group_relations.get(group_id, set()))
        if not relation_ids:
            relation_ids = sorted(int(value) for value in station.get("source_relation_ids", []) if int(value) in relation_evidence)
        evidence_refs = [relation_evidence[value] for value in relation_ids]
        for line_code in lines:
            line_relations = [rid for rid in relation_ids if relation_info[rid]["line_code"] == line_code]
            line_evidence = [relation_evidence[rid] for rid in line_relations] or evidence_refs
            add_fact(new_facts, code, "line_membership", line_code, line_evidence)
        for node_id in sorted({int(node) for node in station.get("osm_node_ids", [group_id])}):
            refs = [relation_evidence[rid] for rid in relation_ids if (node_id, rid) in node_relation_refs]
            add_fact(new_facts, code, "osm_node_id", node_id, refs or evidence_refs, "OSM node ID")

    for node_id, station in sorted(rail_by_node.items()):
        code = rail_entity_code[node_id]
        memberships = sorted(set(rail_memberships[node_id]))
        for relation_id in sorted({rid for rid, _ in memberships}):
            relation_label = relation_info[relation_id]["title"]
            add_fact(new_facts, code, "line_membership", relation_label,
                     [relation_evidence[relation_id]], f"OSM route=train relation {relation_id}")
        for relation_id, order in memberships:
            add_fact(new_facts, code, "line_order", order,
                     [relation_evidence[relation_id]], f"stop position in OSM relation {relation_id}")
        refs = sorted({relation_evidence[relation_id] for relation_id, _ in memberships})
        add_fact(new_facts, code, "osm_node_id", node_id, refs, "OSM node ID")
    facts.extend(new_facts)

    entities.sort(key=lambda row: row["entity_code"])
    with (OUT / "entities_rev8.csv").open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=ENTITY_FIELDS, lineterminator="\r\n")
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(entities)
    (OUT / "facts_rev8.json").write_text(json.dumps(facts, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (OUT / "evidence_rev8.json").write_text(json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    lines = [
        "# rev8 transit and ENR station draft — research only",
        "",
        "This is an additive research draft based on rev7 and the OSM extract dated 2026-11-14. This is not a seed import and does not modify `data/seed/`.",
        "New OSM facts remain `candidate`; none are marked verified because station-name text from the official Cairo Governorate pages was not stored for this work. Consequently, only relation-specific OSM dataset evidence is attached to the new station facts.",
        "",
        "## OSM vs official reference counts",
        "",
        "| Line | OSM unique stations | Official reference | Difference (OSM − official) | OSM shortfall | Official evidence |",
        "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|",
    ]
    for line_code in sorted(ACTIVE_LINES):
        line = line_reports[line_code]
        official_count, official_ref = OFFICIAL_COUNTS[line_code]
        difference = int(line["osm_station_count"]) - official_count
        lines.append(f"| {line_code} | {line['osm_station_count']} | {official_count} | {difference:+d} | {max(0, -difference)} | {official_ref} |")
    lines.extend([
        "",
        "No numeric station-count shortfall appears on these five lines. An exact name-by-name omission list cannot be established from the stored official references, which provide counts but not a complete official stop list; no missing station names are guessed.",
        "",
        "## Draft additions and mapping",
        "",
        f"- New STOP entities: {len(new_entities)}; existing station groups mapped to retained STOP codes: {len(station_groups) - len(new_entities)}.",
        f"- Named ENR route=train station nodes: {len(rail_by_node)}; new RAIL entities: {len(new_rail_entities)}; existing RAIL codes retained: {len(rail_by_node) - len(new_rail_entities)}.",
        f"- Added facts: {len(new_facts)} (line membership, relation-specific order, and OSM node ID); all are candidate.",
        f"- New dataset evidence: {len(new_evidence)} records, {new_evidence[0]['evidence_ref'] if new_evidence else 'none'}–{new_evidence[-1]['evidence_ref'] if new_evidence else 'none'}; one record per OSM relation. EV-350 is intentionally skipped to honor the requested EV-351 starting code.",
        "- Existing STOP/RAIL codes are reused when OSM node or normalized name mapping is unambiguous; interchanges stay one entity with a line-membership fact for each matched line.",
        f"- No official Cairo Governorate station evidence was added: exact page text was not stored, so each new station is supported only by OSM. {sum(1 for row in new_entities + new_rail_entities if not row['canonical_name_ar'] or not row['canonical_name_en'])} new entities lack one localized OSM name tag; no translations were invented.",
        "",
        "## Relation evidence and sequencing",
        "",
        "Every new line-membership/order/node-ID fact cites one or more `dataset` evidence records whose URL is the specific OSM relation. Order facts retain relation-specific order, including direction variants and L3 branches; relation IDs are included in the unit to disambiguate direction-specific indices.",
        "",
        "The existing rev7 files are included as the base snapshot in all three rev8 data files. New records are appended; they have not been adapted or imported into the production seed.",
        "",
    ])
    (OUT / "CHANGELOG_rev8.md").write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({
        "entities_total": len(entities), "new_stop_entities": len(new_entities),
        "new_rail_entities": len(new_rail_entities), "facts_total": len(facts),
        "new_facts": len(new_facts), "evidence_total": len(evidence),
        "new_relation_evidence": len(new_evidence), "rail_named_station_nodes": len(rail_by_node),
        "output": str(OUT.relative_to(ROOT)),
    }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


===== FILE: scripts/check-env.ts =====
import { loadEnvConfig } from "@next/env";
import { getRuntimeEnv, requireDatabaseEnv } from "../src/lib/env";
import { AppError } from "../src/lib/errors";
import { logger } from "../src/lib/logger";

function main(): void {
  loadEnvConfig(process.cwd());

  const runtimeEnv = getRuntimeEnv();
  requireDatabaseEnv();

  logger.info("Environment validation passed", {
    nodeEnv: runtimeEnv.NODE_ENV,
    databaseConfigured: true,
  });
}

try {
  main();
} catch (error: unknown) {
  if (error instanceof AppError) {
    logger.error(error.message, {
      code: error.code,
      ...(error.issues ? { issues: error.issues } : {}),
    });
  } else {
    logger.error("Unexpected environment validation failure", { error });
  }
  process.exit(1);
}


===== FILE: scripts/derive-entity-relations.ts =====
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { gunzipSync } from "node:zlib";
import { loadAndValidateSeedDir } from "../src/lib/atlas/seed";
import { deriveEntityRelations, deriveGovernorateRelations, type CodabGovernorateRelation, type OsmRouteRelation } from "../src/lib/atlas/entity-relations";

async function main() {
  const root = process.cwd();
  const bundle = await loadAndValidateSeedDir(path.join(root, "data/seed"));
  const osmPath = path.join(root, "data/raw/cairo-osm-rail-2026-11-14.json.gz");
  const osmData = JSON.parse(gunzipSync(await readFile(osmPath)).toString("utf8")) as { elements: OsmRouteRelation[] };
  const result = deriveEntityRelations(bundle.entities, bundle.facts, osmData.elements.filter((element) => element.type === "relation"));

  // BATCH 12.4 — governorate parentage derived from the CAPMAS adm1 codes already recorded on each
  // entity's boundary units. Nothing is guessed: only entities whose boundary file has one governorate
  // get a link, and only to governorate entities that exist in the seed.
  const codabRelations = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(root, "data/features/codab-relations.json"), "utf8")) as CodabGovernorateRelation[];
  const governorateResult = deriveGovernorateRelations(codabRelations, bundle.entities);
  const seen = new Set(result.relations.map((relation) => `${relation.subject_code}\u0000${relation.predicate}\u0000${relation.object_code}`));
  const merged = [...result.relations];
  for (const relation of governorateResult.relations) {
    const key = `${relation.subject_code}\u0000${relation.predicate}\u0000${relation.object_code}`;
    if (!seen.has(key)) { merged.push(relation); seen.add(key); }
  }
  merged.sort((a, b) => a.subject_code.localeCompare(b.subject_code) || a.predicate.localeCompare(b.predicate) || a.object_code.localeCompare(b.object_code));

  /**
   * BATCH 12.4 — every relation must be traceable to a fact, so the governorate derivation also emits
   * the supporting `located_in` fact per entity, citing the same CAPMAS evidence row. Existing facts
   * are never modified: a fact is only added when the entity does not already carry it.
   */
  const factsPath = path.join(root, "data/seed/facts.json");
  const existingFacts = JSON.parse(await readFile(factsPath, "utf8")) as typeof bundle.facts;
  const factKeys = new Set(existingFacts.map((fact) => `${fact.entity_code}\u0000${fact.attribute}\u0000${fact.value}`));
  const factsAdded: typeof bundle.facts = [];
  for (const relation of governorateResult.relations) {
    const key = `${relation.subject_code}\u0000${relation.predicate}\u0000${relation.object_code}`;
    if (factKeys.has(key)) continue;
    factKeys.add(key);
    factsAdded.push({
      code: `F-${relation.subject_code}-LOCATED-IN-${relation.object_code}`,
      entity_code: relation.subject_code,
      attribute: "located_in",
      value: relation.object_code,
      unit: null,
      valid_as_of: null,
      valid_as_of_precision: "day",
      verification_state: "candidate",
      evidence_codes: [relation.evidence_ref!],
      notes: "Derived from the published CAPMAS COD-AB adm1 pcode of this entity's own boundary units; no geometry was used.",
    });
  }
  if (factsAdded.length > 0) {
    const mergedFacts = [...existingFacts, ...factsAdded];
    mergedFacts.sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code));
    await writeFile(factsPath, `${JSON.stringify(mergedFacts, null, 2)}\n`, "utf8");
  }

  await writeFile(path.join(root, "data/seed/relations.json"), `${JSON.stringify(merged, null, 2)}\n`, "utf8");
  const report = {
    derived_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    relations_count: merged.length,
    by_predicate: Object.fromEntries([...new Set(merged.map((relation) => relation.predicate))].sort().map((predicate) =>
      [predicate, merged.filter((relation) => relation.predicate === predicate).length])),
    without_evidence_count: merged.filter((relation) => !relation.evidence_ref).length,
    governorate_relations_derived: governorateResult.relations.length,
    governorate_relations_unresolved: governorateResult.unresolved,
    governorate_supporting_facts_added: factsAdded.length,
    osm_route_relations_checked: result.osmRouteRelationsChecked,
    osm_route_relations_matched_to_fact_backed_lines: result.osmRouteRelationsMatched,
    osm_route_relation_matched_ids: result.osmRouteRelationMatchedIds,
    osm_route_relations_unmatched_count: result.osmRouteRelationsUnmatched.length,
    osm_route_relations_unmatched: result.osmRouteRelationsUnmatched,
    relation_ordinals_filled_from_osm_route_order: result.osmOrdinalsAdded,
    unresolved_count: result.unresolved.length,
    unresolved: result.unresolved,
  };
  const reportPath = path.join(root, "data/research/entity-relations/derivation-report.json");
  await mkdir(path.dirname(reportPath), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(reportPath, `${JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)}\n`, "utf8");
  console.info(JSON.stringify({ relations_count: report.relations_count, by_predicate: report.by_predicate,
    without_evidence_count: report.without_evidence_count, osm_route_relations_checked: report.osm_route_relations_checked,
    osm_route_relations_matched_to_fact_backed_lines: report.osm_route_relations_matched_to_fact_backed_lines,
    osm_route_relations_unmatched_count: report.osm_route_relations_unmatched_count,
    relation_ordinals_filled_from_osm_route_order: report.relation_ordinals_filled_from_osm_route_order,
    unresolved_count: report.unresolved_count }, null, 2));
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
  process.exitCode = 1;
});


===== FILE: scripts/derive-transit-stations.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Derive source-backed transit stations and (when present) rail context from stored OSM extracts.

No seed entities are created or modified. Relation member order is preserved per OSM relation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import gzip
import json
import math
import re
import sys
import unicodedata
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RAW_DIR = ROOT / "data/raw"
OUT_DIR = ROOT / "data/research/osm-stations"
PUBLIC_DIR = ROOT / "public"
BBOX = (30.25, 29.2, 32.4, 30.95)  # west, south, east, north
RAIL_EXTRACT = RAW_DIR / "cairo-osm-rail-2026-11-14.json.gz"

LINE_DEFS = {
    "TRANSIT-METRO-L1": {"mode": "metro", "label_en": "Cairo Metro Line 1", "label_ar": "مترو القاهرة — الخط الأول", "status": "operational", "official_count": 35, "official_count_evidence": "EV-140"},
    "TRANSIT-METRO-L2": {"mode": "metro", "label_en": "Cairo Metro Line 2", "label_ar": "مترو القاهرة — الخط الثاني", "status": "operational", "official_count": 20, "official_count_evidence": "EV-143"},
    "TRANSIT-METRO-L3": {"mode": "metro", "label_en": "Cairo Metro Line 3", "label_ar": "مترو القاهرة — الخط الثالث", "status": "operational", "official_count": 34, "official_count_evidence": "EV-312"},
    "TRANSIT-LRT": {"mode": "lrt", "label_en": "Cairo Light Rail Transit", "label_ar": "القطار الكهربائي الخفيف", "status": "operational", "official_count": 12, "official_count_evidence": "EV-073"},
    "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST": {"mode": "monorail_east", "label_en": "East Nile Monorail", "label_ar": "مونوريل شرق النيل", "status": "operational", "official_count": 22, "official_count_evidence": "EV-050"},
    "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-WEST": {"mode": "monorail_west", "label_en": "West Nile Monorail", "label_ar": "مونوريل غرب النيل", "status": "under_construction", "official_count": None},
    "TRANSIT-METRO-L4": {"mode": "metro_l4", "label_en": "Cairo Metro Line 4", "label_ar": "مترو القاهرة — الخط الرابع", "status": "under_construction", "official_count": None},
    "TRANSIT-HSR-L1": {"mode": "hsr", "label_en": "High-Speed Rail", "label_ar": "القطار الكهربائي السريع", "status": "under_construction", "official_count": None},
}
LINE_COLORS = {
    "TRANSIT-METRO-L1": "#c8202f", "TRANSIT-METRO-L2": "#e2b527", "TRANSIT-METRO-L3": "#08783e",
    "TRANSIT-METRO-L4": "#7355a5", "TRANSIT-LRT": "#b74831", "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST": "#174d9b",
    "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-WEST": "#527da8", "TRANSIT-BRT": "#2477a8", "TRANSIT-ENR": "#925c2c",
    "TRANSIT-HSR-L1": "#343434",
}


def norm(value: Any) -> str:
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", str(value or "")).casefold()
    text = "".join(char for char in text if not unicodedata.combining(char))
    text = text.replace("ـ", "")
    return re.sub(r"[^\w]+", "", text, flags=re.UNICODE)


def station_match_keys(value: Any) -> set[str]:
    key = norm(value)
    if not key:
        return set()
    keys = {key}
    for prefix in ("محطة", "station", "metrostation", "metrostn"):
        if key.startswith(prefix) and len(key) > len(prefix):
            keys.add(key[len(prefix):])
    for candidate in list(keys):
        for article in ("ال", "al", "el"):
            if candidate.startswith(article) and len(candidate) > len(article) + 2:
                keys.add(candidate[len(article):])
    return keys


def in_bbox(lon: float, lat: float) -> bool:
    west, south, east, north = BBOX
    return west <= lon <= east and south <= lat <= north


def load_extract(filename: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    opener = gzip.open if filename.suffix == ".gz" else open
    with opener(filename, "rt", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        value = json.load(stream)
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or not isinstance(value.get("elements"), list):
        raise ValueError(f"OSM extract must contain an elements array: {filename}")
    return value


def iter_existing_extracts() -> list[Path]:
    # The dedicated rail extract contains the route=subway relations needed to
    # refresh the L1/L2/L3 ordered station sequences as well as rail context.
    return sorted(path for path in RAW_DIR.glob("*.json*") if path.is_file())


def more_complete(old: dict[str, Any] | None, new: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if old is None:
        return new
    old_version, new_version = int(old.get("version", 0) or 0), int(new.get("version", 0) or 0)
    old_score = (old_version, len(old.get("tags", {})), len(old.get("members", [])), len(old.get("geometry", [])))
    new_score = (new_version, len(new.get("tags", {})), len(new.get("members", [])), len(new.get("geometry", [])))
    return new if new_score >= old_score else old


def line_for_relation(tags: dict[str, str]) -> str | None:
    route = tags.get("route", "")
    ref = tags.get("ref", "").strip().upper()
    name = " ".join((tags.get("name", ""), tags.get("name:en", ""), tags.get("name:ar", ""))).casefold()
    if route == "subway" and ref in {"1", "2", "3", "4"}:
        return f"TRANSIT-METRO-L{ref}"
    if route in {"train", "light_rail"} and ref == "LRT":
        return "TRANSIT-LRT"
    if route == "monorail" and ("east nile" in name or "شرق النيل" in name):
        return "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST"
    if route == "monorail" and ("west nile" in name or "غرب النيل" in name):
        return "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-WEST"
    # HSR and other construction routes require explicit OSM construction tagging.
    construction = any("construction" in key.casefold() or value.casefold() in {"construction", "under_construction"}
                       for key, value in tags.items())
    if construction and route in {"train", "subway"}:
        if ref == "4":
            return "TRANSIT-METRO-L4"
        if "high speed" in name or "hsr" in name or "سريع" in name or "القطار الكهربائي السريع" in name:
            return "TRANSIT-HSR-L1"
    return None


def is_under_construction(tags: dict[str, str]) -> bool:
    return any("construction" in key.casefold() or value.casefold() in {"construction", "under_construction"}
               for key, value in tags.items())


def station_from_node(node_id: int, node: dict[str, Any], role: str, relation_id: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    tags = node.get("tags", {}) or {}
    lat, lon = node.get("lat"), node.get("lon")
    if lat is None or lon is None or not in_bbox(float(lon), float(lat)):
        return None
    return {
        "osm_node_id": int(node_id),
        "osm_node_version": int(node["version"]) if node.get("version") is not None else None,
        "name": tags.get("name"),
        "name_ar": tags.get("name:ar"),
        "name_en": tags.get("name:en"),
        "longitude": float(lon), "latitude": float(lat),
        "member_role": role,
        "source_relation_ids": [int(relation_id)] if relation_id is not None else [],
        "osm_tags": tags,
    }


def load_seed_station_names() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    path = ROOT / "data/seed/entities.csv"
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as stream:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(stream))
    result = []
    for row in rows:
        if row.get("entity_type") not in {"transit_stop", "rail_station"} and not row.get("code", "").startswith(("STOP-", "RAIL-")):
            continue
        names = [row.get("name_ar", ""), row.get("name_en", ""), row.get("code", "")]
        names.extend(part.strip() for part in (row.get("aliases", "") or "").split("|") if part.strip())
        result.append({"code": row["code"], "names": sorted({key for name in names for key in station_match_keys(name)})})
    return result


def load_feature_node_entity_map() -> dict[int, set[str]]:
    path = ROOT / "data/features/features.geojson"
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    features = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("features", [])
    result: dict[int, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    for feature in features:
        props = feature.get("properties", {})
        entity_code = props.get("entity_code")
        code = props.get("code", feature.get("id", ""))
        match = re.fullmatch(r"OSM-NODE-(\d+)", str(code))
        if match and entity_code:
            result[int(match.group(1))].add(entity_code)
    return result


def resolve_entity_codes(station: dict[str, Any], seed_names: list[dict[str, Any]], direct: dict[int, set[str]]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    line_codes = set(station.get("line_codes", []))

    def compatible(code: str) -> bool:
        specific = {
            "STOP-M1-": "TRANSIT-METRO-L1", "STOP-M2-": "TRANSIT-METRO-L2", "STOP-M3-": "TRANSIT-METRO-L3",
            "STOP-LRT-": "TRANSIT-LRT", "STOP-MRE-": "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST", "STOP-MRW-": "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-WEST",
        }
        required = next((line for prefix, line in specific.items() if code.startswith(prefix)), None)
        return required is None or required in line_codes

    node_id = station["osm_node_id"]
    if direct.get(node_id):
        matched = [code for code in sorted(direct[node_id]) if compatible(code)]
        if matched:
            return [{"entity_code": code, "matched_by": "OSM node id"} for code in matched]
    candidates = {name_key for key in ("name", "name_ar", "name_en") for name_key in station_match_keys(station.get(key))}
    matches = [{"entity_code": row["code"], "matched_by": "exact normalized name"}
               for row in seed_names if compatible(row["code"]) and candidates.intersection(row["names"])]
    return matches


def distance_meters(left: dict[str, Any], right: dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    radius = 6_371_000.0
    lat1, lat2 = math.radians(left["latitude"]), math.radians(right["latitude"])
    dlat = lat2 - lat1
    dlon = math.radians(right["longitude"] - left["longitude"])
    value = math.sin(dlat / 2) ** 2 + math.cos(lat1) * math.cos(lat2) * math.sin(dlon / 2) ** 2
    return radius * 2 * math.asin(math.sqrt(value))


def station_name_keys(station: dict[str, Any]) -> set[str]:
    return {norm(station.get(field)) for field in ("name", "name_ar", "name_en", "int_name", "alt_name") if norm(station.get(field))}


def station_representative_quality(station: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
    tags = station.get("osm_tags", {}) or {}
    construction = any("construction" in key.casefold() or value.casefold() in {"construction", "under_construction"}
                       for key, value in tags.items())
    if construction:
        return 3
    if tags.get("railway") == "station" or tags.get("public_transport") == "station":
        return 0
    if station.get("member_role") in {"stop", "platform"}:
        return 1
    return 2


def canonical_station_groups(stations: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Collapse reverse-relation copies, retaining every contributing OSM node reference."""
    groups: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for station in sorted(stations, key=lambda item: (item["primary_mode"], station_representative_quality(item), item["osm_node_id"])):
        names = station_name_keys(station)
        target = None
        for group in groups:
            group_names = station_name_keys(group)
            same_node = station["osm_node_id"] in group.get("osm_node_ids", [])
            close = distance_meters(group, station) <= 350
            same_name = bool(names.intersection(group_names))
            same_line = bool(set(station["line_codes"]).intersection(group["line_codes"]))
            unnamed_near_copy = (not names or not group_names) and same_line and distance_meters(group, station) <= 75
            if same_node or (close and same_name) or unnamed_near_copy:
                target = group
                break
        if target is None:
            target = dict(station)
            target["osm_node_ids"] = [station["osm_node_id"]]
            target["osm_node_versions"] = [station.get("osm_node_version")]
            target["source_relation_ids"] = list(station["source_relation_ids"])
            target["line_codes"] = list(station["line_codes"])
            target["modes"] = list(station["modes"])
            target["existing_entities"] = list(station["existing_entities"])
            groups.append(target)
            continue
        for key in ("name", "name_ar", "name_en"):
            if not target.get(key) and station.get(key):
                target[key] = station[key]
        for field, values in (("osm_node_ids", [station["osm_node_id"]]), ("osm_node_versions", [station.get("osm_node_version")]),
                              ("source_relation_ids", station["source_relation_ids"]), ("line_codes", station["line_codes"]),
                              ("modes", station["modes"])):
            target[field] = sorted(set(target.get(field, []) + values), key=lambda value: (value is None, value))
        existing = {item["entity_code"] for item in target["existing_entities"]}
        target["existing_entities"].extend(item for item in station["existing_entities"] if item["entity_code"] not in existing)
    for group in groups:
        group["line_codes"] = sorted(set(group["line_codes"]))
        group["modes"] = sorted(set(group["modes"]))
        group["interchange"] = len(group["line_codes"]) > 1
        group["status"] = "under_construction" if any(LINE_DEFS.get(code, {}).get("status") == "under_construction" for code in group["line_codes"]) and not any(LINE_DEFS.get(code, {}).get("status") == "operational" for code in group["line_codes"]) else "operational"
    return groups


def append_station(target: dict[str, Any], incoming: dict[str, Any], line_code: str | None) -> None:
    old_version = target.get("osm_node_version")
    new_version = incoming.get("osm_node_version")
    if new_version is not None and (old_version is None or new_version >= old_version):
        for key in ("osm_node_version", "name", "name_ar", "name_en", "longitude", "latitude", "osm_tags"):
            if incoming.get(key) not in (None, ""):
                target[key] = incoming[key]
    if line_code and line_code not in target["line_codes"]:
        target["line_codes"].append(line_code)
    for relation_id in incoming.get("source_relation_ids", []):
