        if relation_id not in target["source_relation_ids"]:
            target["source_relation_ids"].append(relation_id)
    if incoming.get("member_role") == "stop" or target.get("member_role") not in {"stop", "platform"}:
        target["member_role"] = incoming.get("member_role")


def create_station_outputs() -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    node_elements: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    relations: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    sources = iter_existing_extracts()
    railway_station_counts_by_source: dict[str, int] = {}
    for source in sources:
        payload = load_extract(source)
        railway_station_counts_by_source[str(source.relative_to(ROOT))] = len({int(element["id"]) for element in payload["elements"] if element.get("type") == "node" and (element.get("tags", {}) or {}).get("railway") == "station"})
        for element in payload["elements"]:
            if element.get("type") == "node":
                node_id = int(element["id"])
                node_elements[node_id] = more_complete(node_elements.get(node_id), element)
            elif element.get("type") == "relation":
                relation_id = int(element["id"])
                relations[relation_id] = more_complete(relations.get(relation_id), element)

    relation_groups: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    all_stations: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    route_sequences: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    enr_relation_groups: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for relation_id, relation in sorted(relations.items()):
        tags = relation.get("tags", {}) or {}
        if tags.get("route") == "train" and tags.get("ref", "").upper() != "LRT":
            ordered_enr_stations = []
            for member in relation.get("members", []):
                if member.get("type") != "node" or member.get("role") not in {"stop", "platform"}:
                    continue
                node_id = int(member["ref"])
                node = node_elements.get(node_id)
                if node is None and member.get("lat") is not None and member.get("lon") is not None:
                    node = {"id": node_id, "lat": member["lat"], "lon": member["lon"]}
                if node is None:
                    continue
                station = station_from_node(node_id, node, member.get("role", ""), relation_id)
                if station is not None and any(station.get(key) for key in ("name", "name_ar", "name_en")):
                    station["line_order"] = len(ordered_enr_stations) + 1
                    ordered_enr_stations.append(station)
            if ordered_enr_stations:
                enr_relation_groups.append({
                    "relation_id": relation_id, "relation_version": relation.get("version"),
                    "route_name": tags.get("name"), "route_name_en": tags.get("name:en"),
                    "route_name_ar": tags.get("name:ar"), "route_ref": tags.get("ref"),
                    "ordered_stations": ordered_enr_stations,
                })
        line_code = line_for_relation(tags)
        if not line_code:
            continue
        if line_code not in LINE_DEFS:
            continue
        definition = LINE_DEFS[line_code]
        # Planned route geometries are only included if the OSM relation itself explicitly says construction.
        if definition["status"] == "under_construction" and not is_under_construction(tags):
            continue
        stops: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for member in relation.get("members", []):
            role = member.get("role", "")
            if member.get("type") != "node" or role not in {"stop", "platform"}:
                continue
            node_id = int(member["ref"])
            node = node_elements.get(node_id)
            if node is None and member.get("lat") is not None and member.get("lon") is not None:
                node = {"id": node_id, "lat": member["lat"], "lon": member["lon"]}
            if node is None:
                continue
            station = station_from_node(node_id, node, role, relation_id)
            if station is None:
                continue
            stops.append(station)
            if node_id not in all_stations:
                all_stations[node_id] = {**station, "line_codes": [], "source_relation_ids": [], "existing_entities": []}
            append_station(all_stations[node_id], station, line_code)
        if not stops:
            continue
        relation_groups[line_code].append({
            "relation_id": relation_id,
            "relation_version": relation.get("version"),
            "route_name": tags.get("name"),
            "route_name_en": tags.get("name:en"),
            "route_name_ar": tags.get("name:ar"),
            "route_ref": tags.get("ref"),
            "route": tags.get("route"),
            "status": definition["status"],
            "ordered_stations": stops,
        })
        route_sequences.append({"line_code": line_code, "relation_id": relation_id, "ordered_node_ids": [s["osm_node_id"] for s in stops]})

    # Supplement route stop-positions with the nearby operational station object when OSM
    # exposes one separately (e.g. Kit Kat: the route members are construction-tagged stop
    # positions, while node/10099279487 is the operational railway=station object).
    for node_id, node in node_elements.items():
        tags = node.get("tags", {}) or {}
        if tags.get("railway") != "station" or tags.get("subway") != "yes" or is_under_construction(tags):
            continue
        station = station_from_node(node_id, node, "station")
        if station is None or node_id in all_stations:
            continue
        names = station_name_keys(station)
        nearby_lines: set[str] = set()
        for route_station in all_stations.values():
            if not route_station.get("line_codes") or not names.intersection(station_name_keys(route_station)):
                continue
            if distance_meters(station, route_station) <= 100:
                nearby_lines.update(route_station["line_codes"])
        if not nearby_lines:
            continue
        station.update({"line_codes": sorted(nearby_lines), "source_relation_ids": [], "existing_entities": [],
                        "station_location_method": "nearby operational railway=station node matched by exact station name and <=100m to route stop-position"})
        all_stations[node_id] = station

    # Keep unassigned railway station/halt nodes as research context only; only
    # route=train relation membership establishes an ENR route assignment.
    rail_nodes = []
    for node_id, node in node_elements.items():
        tags = node.get("tags", {}) or {}
        if tags.get("railway") not in {"station", "halt"} or tags.get("subway") == "yes" or tags.get("station") == "subway":
            continue
        station = station_from_node(node_id, node, "railway_station")
        if station is not None:
            rail_nodes.append(station)
    seed_names = load_seed_station_names()
    direct_matches = load_feature_node_entity_map()
    for station in all_stations.values():
        station["existing_entities"] = resolve_entity_codes(station, seed_names, direct_matches)
        station["line_codes"] = sorted(set(station["line_codes"]))
        station["source_relation_ids"] = sorted(set(station["source_relation_ids"]))
        station["modes"] = sorted({LINE_DEFS.get(code, {}).get("mode", "enr") for code in station["line_codes"]})
        station["interchange"] = len(station["line_codes"]) > 1
        station["status"] = "under_construction" if any(LINE_DEFS.get(code, {}).get("status") == "under_construction" for code in station["line_codes"]) and not any(LINE_DEFS.get(code, {}).get("status") == "operational" for code in station["line_codes"]) else "operational"
        station["primary_mode"] = next((mode for mode in ("metro", "lrt", "monorail_east", "monorail_west", "brt", "enr", "hsr", "metro_l4") if mode in station["modes"]), "enr")
        station["primary_line_code"] = min(station["line_codes"], key=lambda code: (list(LINE_DEFS).index(code) if code in LINE_DEFS else len(LINE_DEFS), code)) if station["line_codes"] else None

    # Merge reverse-direction relation copies into display stations while preserving each route's original ordered nodes.
    display_stations = canonical_station_groups(list(all_stations.values()))
    for station in display_stations:
        station["primary_mode"] = next((mode for mode in ("metro", "lrt", "monorail_east", "monorail_west", "brt", "enr", "hsr", "metro_l4") if mode in station["modes"]), "enr")
        station["primary_line_code"] = station["line_codes"][0] if station["line_codes"] else None

    # Source node tags used in generated records are intentionally limited to relevant transit keys.
    relevant_tags = {"railway", "public_transport", "subway", "station", "train", "name", "name:ar", "name:en", "alt_name", "int_name", "network", "operator", "gtfs:stop_id", "construction", "construction:railway"}
    for station in all_stations.values():
        station["osm_tags"] = {key: value for key, value in station.get("osm_tags", {}).items() if key in relevant_tags}
        station["primary_mode"] = next((mode for mode in ("metro", "lrt", "monorail_east", "monorail_west", "brt", "enr", "hsr", "metro_l4") if mode in station["modes"]), "enr")
        station["primary_line_code"] = station["line_codes"][0] if station["line_codes"] else None

    lines = []
    for line_code, definition in LINE_DEFS.items():
        group = relation_groups.get(line_code, [])
        # Leave construction lines with no explicit OSM relation out of this derived list.
        if not group and definition["status"] == "under_construction":
            continue
        line_stations = [station for station in display_stations if line_code in station["line_codes"]]
        unique_ids = sorted({node_id for station in line_stations for node_id in station.get("osm_node_ids", [])})
        lines.append({
            "line_code": line_code, **definition,
            "relation_count": len(group), "osm_station_count": len(line_stations), "source_node_reference_count": len(unique_ids),
            "official_count_comparison": ({"official_count": definition["official_count"], "osm_count": len(line_stations), "difference": len(line_stations) - definition["official_count"]} if definition["official_count"] is not None else None),
            "official_count_shortfall": max(0, definition["official_count"] - len(line_stations)) if definition["official_count"] is not None else None,
            "relations": group,
            "stations": line_stations,
        })

    stations_sorted = sorted(display_stations, key=lambda station: (station["primary_mode"], station["line_codes"], station["osm_node_id"]))
    for feature_index, station in enumerate(stations_sorted):
        station["feature_id"] = f"OSM-NODE-{station['osm_node_id']}"
    official_railway_node_count = sum(1 for node in node_elements.values() if (node.get("tags", {}) or {}).get("railway") == "station")
    report = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "title": "OSM-sourced Cairo transit station research",
        "bbox_wgs84": list(BBOX),
        "source_files": [str(path.relative_to(ROOT)) for path in sources],
        "rail_extract_available": RAIL_EXTRACT.exists(),
        "railway_station_nodes_in_stored_extracts": official_railway_node_count,
        "railway_station_nodes_by_source": railway_station_counts_by_source,
        "railway_station_or_halt_nodes_in_bbox": len(rail_nodes),
        "line_count": len(lines),
        "station_feature_count": len(stations_sorted),
        "interchange_station_count": sum(bool(station["interchange"]) for station in stations_sorted),
        "existing_seed_stop_entity_count": sum(1 for row in seed_names),
        "existing_seed_stop_entities_mapped": sorted({entry["entity_code"] for station in stations_sorted for entry in station["existing_entities"]}),
        "existing_seed_stop_entities_unmatched": sorted({row["code"] for row in seed_names} - {entry["entity_code"] for station in stations_sorted for entry in station["existing_entities"]}),
        "lines": lines,
        "stations": stations_sorted,
        "unassigned_railway_stations": rail_nodes,
        "enr_route_relations": enr_relation_groups,
        "enr_named_station_node_count": len({station["osm_node_id"] for route in enr_relation_groups for station in route["ordered_stations"]}),
        "route_sequences": route_sequences,
    }
    OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT_DIR / "stations_from_osm.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (OUT_DIR / "stations_from_osm.ar.md").write_text(render_arabic_report(report), encoding="utf-8")
    transit_geojson = {
        "type": "FeatureCollection",
        "metadata": {"source": "OpenStreetMap route relations and railway station nodes", "license": "ODbL-1.0", "retrieval_date": "2026-11-14", "source_file": "data/research/osm-stations/stations_from_osm.json"},
        "features": [station_feature(station) for station in stations_sorted],
    }
    PUBLIC_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (PUBLIC_DIR / "transit-stations.geojson").write_text(json.dumps(transit_geojson, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return report, {"nodes": node_elements, "relations": relations}


def station_feature(station: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    line_codes = station["line_codes"]
    mode = station["primary_mode"]
    # Unmatched OSM stations remain visible as source-context features; only known seed codes can open an entity panel.
    entity_codes = sorted({entry["entity_code"] for entry in station["existing_entities"]})
    return {
        "type": "Feature", "id": station["feature_id"],
        "geometry": {"type": "Point", "coordinates": [station["longitude"], station["latitude"]]},
        "properties": {
            "osm_node_id": station["osm_node_id"], "osm_node_version": station["osm_node_version"],
            "osm_node_ids": station.get("osm_node_ids", [station["osm_node_id"]]),
            "name": station.get("name"), "name_ar": station.get("name_ar"), "name_en": station.get("name_en"),
            "mode": mode, "modes": station["modes"], "line_codes": line_codes,
            "line_color": LINE_COLORS.get(station.get("primary_line_code"), "#677780"),
            "primary_line_code": station.get("primary_line_code"),
            "status": station["status"], "interchange": station["interchange"],
            "entity_codes": entity_codes, "license": "ODbL-1.0",
            "attribution": "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0",
            "source_relation_ids": station["source_relation_ids"],
            "station_location_method": station.get("station_location_method", "OSM route-relation station node"),
        },
    }


def render_arabic_report(report: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    lines = [
        "# محطات النقل المستخرجة من علاقات OSM",
        "",
        "هذه قائمة بحثية مستخرجة آليًا من أعضاء علاقات OSM المرتبة (الدور `stop` أو `platform`) ومن عقد المحطات المسجلة. لم تُنشأ كيانات جديدة ولم تُضف محطات إلى seed. اختلافات الأسماء أو العلاقات المتعاكسة محفوظة كما وردت في المصدر.",
        "",
        f"- نطاق الاستخراج WGS84: `{report['bbox_wgs84']}` (غرب، جنوب، شرق، شمال).",
        f"- ملفات OSM المخزنة: {len(report['source_files'])}؛ عقد `railway=station` الفريدة عبرها: {report['railway_station_nodes_in_stored_extracts']}؛ في لقطة الحدود الأحدث: {report['railway_station_nodes_by_source'].get('data/raw/cairo-osm-boundaries-2026-11-14.json.gz', 'غير متاح')} (التقرير السابق ذكر 175)؛ محطات/مواقف سكك داخل النطاق: {report['railway_station_or_halt_nodes_in_bbox']}.",
        f"- محطات فريدة مرسومة بعد دمج تكرار العقد: {report['station_feature_count']}؛ محطات تبادلية حسب عقدة/اسم مطابق: {report['interchange_station_count']}.",
        "- ترخيص هندسة OSM: ODbL 1.0. `node_version` غير متاح لبعض أعضاء العلاقات الذين لم ترد عقدهم منفصلة في اللقطات، ولا يُستنتج أو يُخترع.",
        "",
    ]
    for line in report["lines"]:
        official = line.get("official_count_comparison")
        official_ref = line.get("official_count_evidence")
        official_note = f"المرجع الرسمي المدخل: {official['official_count']} ({official_ref})؛ OSM: {official['osm_count']} (الفارق {official['difference']:+d})" if official else "لا يوجد عدد رسمي واحد مدخل للمقارنة؛ العدد أدناه هو ما ظهر في OSM فقط."
        shortfall = line.get("official_count_shortfall")
        gap_note = f"النقص العددي مقارنة بالمرجع الرسمي: {shortfall}." if shortfall is not None else "لم يُقدّم للخط عدد رسمي مرجعي."
        lines.extend([f"## {line['label_ar']} (`{line['line_code']}`)", "", f"- الحالة: `{line['status']}`؛ علاقات OSM: {line['relation_count']}؛ محطات OSM الفريدة: **{line['osm_station_count']}**. {official_note} {gap_note}", ""])
        if not line["relations"]:
            lines.extend(["لا توجد علاقة مطابقة في ملفات OSM المخزنة.", ""])
            continue
        for route in line["relations"]:
            lines.extend([f"### العلاقة `{route['relation_id']}` — {route.get('route_name_en') or route.get('route_name') or 'بلا اسم إنجليزي'}", "", "| الترتيب | اسم OSM | الاسم العربي | الاسم الإنجليزي | عقدة OSM | الإصدار | الإحداثيات (lon, lat) | تبادل |", "|---:|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|"])
            for index, stop in enumerate(route["ordered_stations"], 1):
                interchange = "نعم" if any(item["osm_node_id"] == stop["osm_node_id"] and item["interchange"] for item in report["stations"]) else "لا"
                lines.append(f"| {index} | {stop.get('name') or '—'} | {stop.get('name_ar') or '—'} | {stop.get('name_en') or '—'} | {stop['osm_node_id']} | {stop['osm_node_version'] if stop['osm_node_version'] is not None else 'غير متاح'} | {stop['longitude']:.6f}, {stop['latitude']:.6f} | {interchange} |")
            lines.append("")
    lines.extend(["## مطابقة كيانات المحطات الموجودة في seed", "", f"طابقت القائمة المستخرجة {len(report['existing_seed_stop_entities_mapped'])} كيانًا قائمًا من كيانات `STOP-*` و`RAIL-*` بالاسم المطابق أو بمعرّف عقدة OSM. لا تُنشأ كيانات بديلة.", "", "| كود الكيان القائم |", "|---|"])
    lines.extend(f"| `{code}` |" for code in report["existing_seed_stop_entities_mapped"])
    lines.extend(["", "### كيانات seed غير المطابقة", ""])
    lines.extend(f"- `{code}`" for code in report["existing_seed_stop_entities_unmatched"])
    lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def clip_segment(a: tuple[float, float], b: tuple[float, float]) -> tuple[tuple[float, float], tuple[float, float]] | None:
    # Liang-Barsky clipping; input/output coordinates are x=lon,y=lat.
    west, south, east, north = BBOX
    dx, dy = b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]
    p = (-dx, dx, -dy, dy)
    q = (a[0] - west, east - a[0], a[1] - south, north - a[1])
    u0, u1 = 0.0, 1.0
    for pi, qi in zip(p, q):
        if pi == 0:
            if qi < 0:
                return None
            continue
        ratio = qi / pi
        if pi < 0:
            u0 = max(u0, ratio)
        else:
            u1 = min(u1, ratio)
        if u0 > u1:
            return None
    return ((a[0] + u0 * dx, a[1] + u0 * dy), (a[0] + u1 * dx, a[1] + u1 * dy))


def simplify_coordinates(coords: list[list[float]], tolerance_m: float) -> list[list[float]]:
    """Ramer-Douglas-Peucker simplification in local metric coordinates."""
    if len(coords) <= 2 or tolerance_m <= 0:
        return coords
    mean_lat = math.radians(sum(point[1] for point in coords) / len(coords))
    x_scale = 111_320.0 * math.cos(mean_lat)
    y_scale = 110_574.0
    points = [(point[0] * x_scale, point[1] * y_scale) for point in coords]
    keep = {0, len(coords) - 1}
    stack = [(0, len(coords) - 1)]
    tolerance_squared = tolerance_m * tolerance_m
    while stack:
        start, end = stack.pop()
        ax, ay = points[start]
        bx, by = points[end]
        dx, dy = bx - ax, by - ay
        denom = dx * dx + dy * dy
        max_distance = -1.0
        max_index = -1
        for index in range(start + 1, end):
            px, py = points[index]
            if denom == 0:
                distance = (px - ax) ** 2 + (py - ay) ** 2
            else:
                fraction = max(0.0, min(1.0, ((px - ax) * dx + (py - ay) * dy) / denom))
                distance = (px - (ax + fraction * dx)) ** 2 + (py - (ay + fraction * dy)) ** 2
            if distance > max_distance:
                max_distance, max_index = distance, index
        if max_index >= 0 and max_distance > tolerance_squared:
            keep.add(max_index)
            stack.extend(((start, max_index), (max_index, end)))
    return [coords[index] for index in sorted(keep)]


def derive_rail_context(payload: dict[str, Any], simplify_tolerance_m: float = 100.0, include_station_nodes: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
    elements = payload["elements"]
    nodes: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    ways: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    relations: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for element in elements:
        kind = element.get("type")
        if kind == "node":
            nodes[int(element["id"])] = more_complete(nodes.get(int(element["id"])), element)
        elif kind == "way":
            ways[int(element["id"])] = more_complete(ways.get(int(element["id"])), element)
        elif kind == "relation" and (element.get("tags", {}) or {}).get("route") == "train":
            relations.append(element)
    entity_rows = {}
    with (ROOT / "data/seed/entities.csv").open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as stream:
        entity_rows = {row["code"]: row for row in csv.DictReader(stream)
                       if row.get("entity_type") == "transit_line" and row["code"].startswith(("TRANSIT-ENR", "TRANSIT-SUEZ-TRAIN"))}
    entity_norms: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for code, row in entity_rows.items():
        entity_norms[code] = [norm(row.get("name_en")), norm(row.get("name_ar")), *[norm(x) for x in row.get("aliases", "").split("|")]]

    features = []
    for way_id, way in sorted(ways.items()):
        tags = way.get("tags", {}) or {}
        railway = tags.get("railway")
        construction_type = tags.get("construction") or tags.get("construction:railway")
        if railway not in {"rail", "narrow_gauge", "construction", "disused", "preserved"}:
            continue
        if railway == "construction" and construction_type not in {None, "rail", "narrow_gauge", "light_rail", "subway"}:
            continue
        rail_status = "disused" if railway == "disused" else "preserved" if railway == "preserved" else "under_construction" if railway == "construction" or tags.get("construction:railway") in {"rail", "narrow_gauge"} else "operational"
        coords = []
        for point in way.get("geometry", []) or []:
            if point.get("lon") is not None and point.get("lat") is not None:
                coords.append((float(point["lon"]), float(point["lat"])))
        if not coords:
            for node_id in way.get("nodes", []):
                point = nodes.get(int(node_id), {})
                if point.get("lon") is not None and point.get("lat") is not None:
                    coords.append((float(point["lon"]), float(point["lat"])))
        fragments: list[list[list[float]]] = []
        current: list[list[float]] = []
        for first, second in zip(coords, coords[1:]):
            clipped = clip_segment(first, second)
            if clipped is None:
                if len(current) > 1:
                    fragments.append(current)
                current = []
                continue
            left, right = clipped
            if current and current[-1] == [left[0], left[1]]:
                current.append([right[0], right[1]])
            else:
                if len(current) > 1:
                    fragments.append(current)
                current = [[left[0], left[1]], [right[0], right[1]]]
        if len(current) > 1:
            fragments.append(current)
        fragments = [simplify_coordinates(fragment, simplify_tolerance_m) for fragment in fragments]
        fragments = [fragment for fragment in fragments if len(fragment) > 1]
        if not fragments:
            continue
        code = None
        for relation in relations:
            if any(member.get("type") == "way" and int(member.get("ref", 0)) == way_id for member in relation.get("members", [])):
                tags_r = relation.get("tags", {}) or {}
                candidate = norm(tags_r.get("name:en") or tags_r.get("name"))
                code = next((line_code for line_code, names in entity_norms.items() if candidate and candidate in names), None)
                if code:
                    break
        geometries = [{"type": "LineString", "coordinates": fragment} for fragment in fragments]
        geometry = geometries[0] if len(geometries) == 1 else {"type": "MultiLineString", "coordinates": fragments}
        features.append({"type": "Feature", "id": f"OSM-WAY-{way_id}", "geometry": geometry, "properties": {
            "osm_type": "way", "osm_id": way_id, "osm_version": way.get("version"), "railway": railway,
            "construction": tags.get("construction"), "name": tags.get("name"), "name_en": tags.get("name:en"),
            "entity_code": code, "mode": "enr", "rail_status": rail_status, "license": "ODbL-1.0", "attribution": "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0",
        }})
    for node_id, node in sorted(nodes.items()):
        if not include_station_nodes:
            continue
        tags = node.get("tags", {}) or {}
        if tags.get("railway") not in {"station", "halt"} or tags.get("train") != "yes":
            continue
        if node.get("lon") is None or node.get("lat") is None or not in_bbox(float(node["lon"]), float(node["lat"])):
            continue
        features.append({"type": "Feature", "id": f"OSM-NODE-{node_id}", "geometry": {"type": "Point", "coordinates": [float(node["lon"]), float(node["lat"])]}, "properties": {
            "osm_type": "node", "osm_id": node_id, "osm_version": node.get("version"), "railway": tags.get("railway"),
            "name": tags.get("name"), "name_ar": tags.get("name:ar"), "name_en": tags.get("name:en"),
            "entity_code": None, "mode": "enr", "rail_status": "disused" if tags.get("railway") == "disused" or tags.get("disused:railway") or tags.get("disused") else "under_construction" if tags.get("railway") == "construction" or tags.get("construction:railway") else "operational", "license": "ODbL-1.0", "attribution": "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0",
        }})
    features.sort(key=lambda feature: (feature["geometry"]["type"] == "Point", feature["id"]))
    return {"type": "FeatureCollection", "metadata": {"source": str(RAIL_EXTRACT.relative_to(ROOT)), "license": "ODbL-1.0", "attribution": "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0", "bbox_wgs84": list(BBOX), "feature_count": len(features), "simplification_tolerance_m": simplify_tolerance_m}, "features": features}


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--rail-extract", type=Path, default=RAIL_EXTRACT, help="optional attached OSM rail extract (JSON or .json.gz)")
    parser.add_argument("--stations-only", action="store_true", help="do not write rail-network.geojson")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    report, _ = create_station_outputs()
    if not args.stations_only:
        rail_source = str(args.rail_extract.relative_to(ROOT)) if args.rail_extract.is_relative_to(ROOT) else args.rail_extract.name
        if args.rail_extract.exists():
            payload = load_extract(args.rail_extract)
            rail = derive_rail_context(payload, simplify_tolerance_m=100.0, include_station_nodes=True)
            detail = derive_rail_context(payload, simplify_tolerance_m=12.0, include_station_nodes=False)
            rail["metadata"]["source"] = rail_source
            detail["metadata"]["source"] = rail_source
            available = len(rail["features"]) > 0
            (PUBLIC_DIR / "rail-network-detail.geojson").write_text(json.dumps(detail, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
            print(f"Rail context ready: {len(rail['features'])} generalized features and {len(detail['features'])} high-zoom ways from {args.rail_extract}")
        else:
            rail = {"type": "FeatureCollection", "metadata": {"source": rail_source, "license": "ODbL-1.0", "bbox_wgs84": list(BBOX), "feature_count": 0}, "features": []}
            available = False
            print(f"Rail extract not attached yet; pipeline ready for {args.rail_extract.relative_to(ROOT) if args.rail_extract.is_relative_to(ROOT) else args.rail_extract}")
        (PUBLIC_DIR / "rail-network.geojson").write_text(json.dumps(rail, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (PUBLIC_DIR / "rail-network-status.json").write_text(json.dumps({"available": available, "feature_count": len(rail["features"]), "detail_feature_count": len(detail["features"]) if args.rail_extract.exists() else 0, "source": rail_source}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({
        "station_features": report["station_feature_count"],
        "interchanges": report["interchange_station_count"],
        "railway_station_nodes_in_extracts": report["railway_station_nodes_in_stored_extracts"],
        "seed_stop_entities_mapped": len(report["existing_seed_stop_entities_mapped"]),
        "seed_stop_entities_unmatched": len(report["existing_seed_stop_entities_unmatched"]),
        "lines": [{"code": line["line_code"], "relations": line["relation_count"], "stations": line["osm_station_count"], "source_node_refs": line["source_node_reference_count"]} for line in report["lines"]],
    }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== FILE: scripts/import-codab-boundaries.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Idempotently import the reviewed CAPMAS COD-AB boundary extract.

Requires Shapely (also used by scripts/area_boundaries.py). The supplied source
files are read-only; only deterministic derived GeoJSON/relation artifacts and
one seed evidence row are written.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
import re
import sys
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from shapely.geometry import GeometryCollection, MultiPolygon, Polygon, box, mapping, shape
from shapely.ops import unary_union

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RAW = ROOT / "data/raw/cairo-codab-adm2-adm3-2026-11-15.geojson.gz"
LINKS = ROOT / "data/research/codab-boundaries/entity_links.json"
README = ROOT / "data/research/codab-boundaries/README.md"
ENTITIES = ROOT / "data/seed/entities.csv"
EVIDENCE = ROOT / "data/seed/evidence.json"
ADMIN_UNITS = ROOT / "data/features/codab-admin-units.geojson"
ENTITY_BOUNDARIES = ROOT / "data/features/codab-entity-boundaries.geojson"
RELATIONS = ROOT / "data/features/codab-relations.json"
IMPORT_REPORT = ROOT / "data/features/codab-import-report.json"
EVIDENCE_CODE = "EV-CODAB-ADMIN-2026"
SOURCE = "CAPMAS COD-AB 2017 (reviewed 2024)"
ATTRIBUTION = "حدود إدارية © CAPMAS (الجهاز المركزي للتعبئة العامة والإحصاء) عبر OCHA/HDX، بترخيص CC BY-IGO"
BBOX = (30.5, 29.2, 32.1, 30.8)


def sha256(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def source_hashes() -> tuple[str, str]:
    text = README.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    match = re.search(r"json sha256 ([0-9a-f]{64})", text)
    gz_match = re.search(r"gz sha256 ([0-9a-f]{64})", text)
    if not match or not gz_match:
        raise ValueError("README must record both compressed and expanded JSON SHA-256 checksums")
    return gz_match.group(1), match.group(1)


def polygonal_only(geometry: Any):
    if isinstance(geometry, Polygon):
        return geometry
    if isinstance(geometry, MultiPolygon):
        return geometry
    if isinstance(geometry, GeometryCollection) or hasattr(geometry, "geoms"):
        parts = [part for child in geometry.geoms for part in [polygonal_only(child)] if part is not None and not part.is_empty]
        return unary_union(parts) if parts else None
    return None


def read_inputs() -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any], list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, str]]:
    gz_bytes = RAW.read_bytes()
    expected_gz, expected_json = source_hashes()
    if sha256(gz_bytes) != expected_gz:
        raise ValueError(f"Compressed source checksum mismatch for {RAW.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    json_bytes = gzip.decompress(gz_bytes)
    if sha256(json_bytes) != expected_json:
        raise ValueError("Expanded GeoJSON SHA-256 does not match the provenance README")
    geojson = json.loads(json_bytes)
    links = json.loads(LINKS.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    with ENTITIES.open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as stream:
        entities = list(csv.DictReader(stream))
    return geojson, links, entities, links["adm2_display_name_fixes"]


def fmt_area(geometry) -> float:
    latitude = geometry.representative_point().y
    return round(abs(geometry.area) * 111.32 * 110.574 * max(0.1, abs(__import__("math").cos(__import__("math").radians(latitude)))), 4)


def build_outputs(geojson: dict[str, Any], links: dict[str, Any], entities: list[dict[str, Any]], fixes: dict[str, str]):
    exclusions = links["exclude_pcodes"]
    curated_links = links["links"]
    bad_pairs: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    pcode_to_entities: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    for link in curated_links:
        if link["relation"] == "boundary_equals_union":
            for pcode in link["pcodes"]:
                pcode_to_entities[pcode].append(link["entity_code"])
        for rejected in links["rejected_false_matches"]:
            entity_code, separator, rejected_pcode = rejected.partition("->")
            if separator and entity_code == link["entity_code"]:
                bad_pairs[entity_code].update(token for token in re.split(r"[/, ]+", rejected_pcode) if token)
        if set(link["pcodes"]) & set(exclusions):
            raise ValueError(f"Curated link uses an explicitly excluded pcode: {link['entity_code']}")
    for link in curated_links:
        if link["relation"] == "boundary_equals_union" and set(link["pcodes"]) & bad_pairs[link["entity_code"]]:
            raise ValueError(f"Curated boundary link collides with a rejected false match: {link['entity_code']}")

    clip_box = box(*BBOX)
    features: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    clipped_count = 0
    outside: list[str] = []
    excluded: list[str] = []
    raw_by_pcode: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for raw in geojson["features"]:
        p = raw["properties"]
        level = int(p["level"])
        pcode = p["adm2_pcode"] if level == 2 else p["adm3_pcode"]
        if pcode in exclusions:
            excluded.append(pcode)
            continue
        source_geometry = shape(raw["geometry"])
        raw_by_pcode[pcode] = {"raw": raw, "geometry": source_geometry}
        if not source_geometry.intersects(clip_box):
            outside.append(pcode)
            continue
        geometry = source_geometry.intersection(clip_box)
        if not source_geometry.equals(geometry):
            clipped_count += 1
        geometry = polygonal_only(geometry)
        if geometry is None or geometry.is_empty:
            outside.append(pcode)
            continue
        if not geometry.is_valid:
            geometry = polygonal_only(geometry.buffer(0))
        if geometry is None or geometry.is_empty or not geometry.is_valid:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid clipped polygon for {pcode}")
        parent_pcode = p["adm1_pcode"] if level == 2 else p["adm2_pcode"]
        parent_name_ar = p["adm1_ar"] if level == 2 else p["adm2_ar"]
        parent_name_en = p.get("adm1_en") if level == 2 else p.get("adm2_en")
        name_ar = fixes.get(pcode, p["adm2_ar"] if level == 2 else p["adm3_ar"])
        name_en = p.get("adm2_en") if level == 2 else None
        features.append({
            "type": "Feature", "id": f"CAPMAS-ADM{level}-{pcode}", "geometry": mapping(geometry),
            "properties": {
                "code": f"CAPMAS-ADM{level}-{pcode}", "pcode": pcode, "level": level,
                "name_ar": name_ar, "name_en": name_en,
                "adm1_pcode": p["adm1_pcode"], "adm1_name_ar": p["adm1_ar"], "adm1_name_en": p.get("adm1_en"),
                "parent_pcodes": [parent_pcode], "parent_name_ar": parent_name_ar, "parent_name_en": parent_name_en,
                "area_sqkm": fmt_area(geometry), "valid_on": p.get("valid_on"),
                "entity_codes": sorted(set(pcode_to_entities.get(pcode, []))),
                "license": "CC BY-IGO", "source": SOURCE, "evidence_code": EVIDENCE_CODE,
                "attribution": ATTRIBUTION,
            },
        })
    features.sort(key=lambda f: (f["properties"]["level"], f["properties"]["pcode"]))

    entity_by_code = {entity["code"]: entity for entity in entities}
    entity_features = []
    boundary_links = [item for item in curated_links if item["relation"] == "boundary_equals_union"]
    for link in boundary_links:
        code = link["entity_code"]
        missing = [p for p in link["pcodes"] if p not in raw_by_pcode or p not in {f["properties"]["pcode"] for f in features}]
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"Linked entity {code} has no in-extent official geometry for {missing}")
        geom = unary_union([shape(next(f["geometry"] for f in features if f["properties"]["pcode"] == p)) for p in link["pcodes"]])
        if not geom.is_valid:
            geom = geom.buffer(0)
        if not geom.is_valid or geom.is_empty:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid official union boundary for {code}")
        entity = entity_by_code.get(code)
        if not entity:
            raise ValueError(f"Curated link references unknown entity {code}")
        entity_features.append({
            "type": "Feature", "id": f"CAPMAS-ENTITY-{code}", "geometry": mapping(geom),
            "properties": {
                "code": f"CAPMAS-ENTITY-{code}", "entity_code": code,
                "name_ar": entity["name_ar"], "name_en": entity["name_en"],
                "name_ar_units": link.get("names_ar", []), "pcodes": link["pcodes"],
                "level": 2, "license": "CC BY-IGO", "source": SOURCE,
                "evidence_code": EVIDENCE_CODE, "attribution": ATTRIBUTION,
                "area_sqkm": round(sum(next(f["properties"]["area_sqkm"] for f in features if f["properties"]["pcode"] == p) for p in link["pcodes"]), 4),
            },
        })
    entity_features.sort(key=lambda f: f["properties"]["entity_code"])

    # BATCH 12.4 — governorate parentage straight from the CAPMAS adm1 fields of the very units each
    # entity boundary is built from. No geometry is consulted and nothing is inferred beyond the
    # published adm1_pcode/adm1_ar of those units, so this is evidence-backed, not a spatial guess.
    adm1_by_pcode = {}
    for feature in features:
        props = feature["properties"]
        adm1_by_pcode[props["pcode"]] = (props["adm1_pcode"], props["adm1_name_ar"], props["adm1_name_en"])

    relations = []
    for link in boundary_links:
        parents = {adm1_by_pcode[pcode] for pcode in link["pcodes"]}
        if len(parents) != 1:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Entity {link['entity_code']} spans more than one governorate in CAPMAS adm1: {sorted(parents)}; "
                "refusing to derive a single parent governorate"
            )
        adm1_pcode, adm1_ar, adm1_en = parents.pop()
        entity = entity_by_code.get(link["entity_code"])
        relations.append({
            "entity_code": link["entity_code"], "relation": "governorate",
            "adm1_pcode": adm1_pcode, "adm1_name_ar": adm1_ar, "adm1_name_en": adm1_en,
            "pcodes": link["pcodes"],
            "evidence_code": EVIDENCE_CODE, "source": SOURCE, "license": "CC BY-IGO",
        })

    curated_located_within = [link for link in curated_links if link["relation"] == "located_within"]
    for link in curated_located_within:
        relations.append({
            "entity_code": link["entity_code"], "relation": "located_within",
            "pcodes": link["pcodes"], "names_ar": link["names_ar"],
            "evidence_code": EVIDENCE_CODE, "source": SOURCE, "license": "CC BY-IGO",
        })
    relations.sort(key=lambda relation: relation["entity_code"])
    return features, entity_features, relations, {
        "input_features": len(geojson["features"]), "excluded_features": len(set(excluded)),
        "out_of_extent_features": len(outside), "clipped_features": clipped_count,
        "imported_features": len(features), "entity_union_boundaries": len(entity_features),
        "located_within_relations": len(curated_located_within), "governorate_relations": len(relations) - len(curated_located_within), "excluded_pcodes": sorted(set(excluded)),
        "out_of_extent_pcodes": sorted(outside),
    }


def ensure_evidence() -> None:
    evidence = json.loads(EVIDENCE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    expected = {
        "code": EVIDENCE_CODE, "title": "Egypt – Subnational Administrative Boundaries (COD-AB)",
        "url": "https://data.humdata.org/dataset/cod-ab-egy", "publisher": "CAPMAS / OCHA HDX",
        "source_type": "official", "published_date": None, "published_date_precision": "day",
        "accessed_date": "2026-11-15",
        "notes": "License: CC BY-IGO. Administrative boundaries © CAPMAS, via OCHA/HDX (CC BY-IGO). Source created 2017 and reviewed 2024.",
    }
    found = [item for item in evidence if item.get("code") == EVIDENCE_CODE]
    if len(found) > 1:
        raise ValueError(f"Evidence code {EVIDENCE_CODE} occurs more than once; expected exactly one dataset record")
    if found and found[0] != expected:
        raise ValueError(f"Evidence code {EVIDENCE_CODE} exists but its details differ from the curated import source")
    if not found:
        evidence.append(expected)
        EVIDENCE.write_text(json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def write_or_check(path: Path, value: Any, check: bool) -> None:
    text = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n"
    if check:
        if not path.exists() or path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") != text:
            raise ValueError(f"Generated file is stale: {path.relative_to(ROOT)} (run this importer)")
    else:
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="Verify generated artifacts without writing")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    geojson, links, entities, fixes = read_inputs()
    units, unions, relations, report = build_outputs(geojson, links, entities, fixes)
    collection = {"type": "FeatureCollection", "name": "CAPMAS COD-AB administrative units for Greater Cairo", "metadata": {
        "source": SOURCE, "evidence_code": EVIDENCE_CODE, "license": "CC BY-IGO", "attribution": ATTRIBUTION,
        "bbox_wgs84": list(BBOX), "source_json_sha256": source_hashes()[1],
    }, "features": units}
    entity_collection = {"type": "FeatureCollection", "name": "Curated official entity boundary unions", "metadata": collection["metadata"], "features": unions}
    if args.check:
        write_or_check(ADMIN_UNITS, collection, True)
        write_or_check(ENTITY_BOUNDARIES, entity_collection, True)
        write_or_check(RELATIONS, relations, True)
        write_or_check(IMPORT_REPORT, report, True)
        evidence_rows = [x for x in json.loads(EVIDENCE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if x.get("code") == EVIDENCE_CODE]
        if len(evidence_rows) != 1:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected exactly one seed evidence row {EVIDENCE_CODE}; found {len(evidence_rows)}")
    else:
        ensure_evidence()
        write_or_check(ADMIN_UNITS, collection, False)
        write_or_check(ENTITY_BOUNDARIES, entity_collection, False)
        write_or_check(RELATIONS, relations, False)
        write_or_check(IMPORT_REPORT, report, False)
    print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    print("Excluded or skipped pcode list is recorded in this report; no non-curated entity matches are generated.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        raise SystemExit(main())
    except Exception as error:
        print(f"COD-AB import failed: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)


===== FILE: scripts/import-seed.ts =====
import path from "node:path";
import { loadEnvConfig } from "@next/env";
import { runSeedImport } from "../src/lib/atlas/seed";
import { AppError, SeedValidationError } from "../src/lib/errors";
import { logger } from "../src/lib/logger";

interface ParsedCliArgs {
  seedDir: string;
  featuresPath: string | null | undefined;
  dryRun: boolean;
}

function parseArgs(argv: string[]): ParsedCliArgs {
  let seedDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data/seed");
  let featuresPath: string | null | undefined;
  let featuresExplicit = false;
  let dryRun = false;

  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const arg = argv[i];
    if (arg === "--dry-run" || arg === "--check") {
      dryRun = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--features") {
      const next = argv[i + 1];
      if (!next || next.startsWith("--")) {
        throw new Error("Missing GeoJSON path after --features flag.");
      }
      featuresPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), next);
      featuresExplicit = true;
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--no-features") {
      featuresPath = null;
      featuresExplicit = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg === "--dir") {
      const next = argv[i + 1];
      if (!next || next.startsWith("--")) {
        throw new Error("Missing directory path after --dir flag.");
      }
      seedDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), next);
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (arg?.startsWith("--dir=")) {
      const dirVal = arg.slice("--dir=".length).trim();
      if (!dirVal) {
        throw new Error("Empty directory path in --dir=<path>.");
      }
      seedDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), dirVal);
      continue;
    }
    throw new Error(
      `Unknown CLI argument "${arg}". Supported flags: [--dir <path>] [--dry-run].`,
    );
  }

  return {
    seedDir,
    featuresPath: featuresExplicit
      ? featuresPath ?? null
      : seedDir === path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "seed")
        ? path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "features", "features.geojson")
        : null,
    dryRun,
  };
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  loadEnvConfig(process.cwd());

  const { seedDir, featuresPath, dryRun } = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  const summary = await runSeedImport({ seedDir, featuresPath, dryRun });

  if (summary.dryRun) {
    console.info(
      `Seed validation passed (${summary.seedDir}): ${summary.entitiesCount} entities, ${summary.evidenceCount} evidence items, ${summary.factsCount} facts (${summary.factEvidenceLinksCount} fact-evidence links), ${summary.featuresCount} features, ${summary.report.warnings.length} warning(s).`,
    );
    const sync = summary.importSync;
    console.info(sync.databasePreviewAvailable
      ? `Reconciliation preview: would delete ${sync.featuresDeletedCount ?? 0} stale features and ${sync.factEvidenceLinksDeletedCount ?? 0} stale fact-evidence links; retaining ${sync.entitiesNotInSeedCount ?? 0} entities, ${sync.factsNotInSeedCount ?? 0} facts, ${sync.evidenceNotInSeedCount ?? 0} evidence records absent from the seed.`
      : "Reconciliation deletion counts were not queried (no database preview connection configured). No data was changed.");
    for (const warning of summary.report.warnings) {
      console.warn(`  [WARN ${warning.code}] ${warning.path}: ${warning.message}`);
    }
    return;
  }

  console.info(
    `Seed import complete (${summary.seedDir}): upserted ${summary.entitiesCount} entities, ${summary.evidenceCount} evidence items, ${summary.factsCount} facts (${summary.factEvidenceLinksCount} fact-evidence links), ${summary.featuresCount} features; deleted ${summary.importSync.featuresDeletedCount ?? 0} stale features and ${summary.importSync.factEvidenceLinksDeletedCount ?? 0} stale fact-evidence links; retained ${summary.importSync.entitiesNotInSeedCount ?? 0} out-of-seed entities, ${summary.importSync.factsNotInSeedCount ?? 0} facts, ${summary.importSync.evidenceNotInSeedCount ?? 0} evidence records.`,
  );
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  if (error instanceof SeedValidationError) {
    console.error(error.message);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  if (error instanceof AppError) {
    logger.error(error.message, {
      code: error.code,
      ...(error.issues ? { issues: error.issues } : {}),
    });
    process.exit(1);
  }
  logger.error("Unhandled seed import failure", { error });
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: scripts/ingest-features.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build provenance-preserving GeoJSON features from an explicitly licensed GTFS zip and/or OSM.

This is a local ingestion command only; it is never called from a request handler. No entity is
created. Matches require exact, Unicode-normalized equality against an existing bilingual name or
alias and a compatible entity type. Ambiguous/unmatched source objects are reported, not guessed.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import gzip
import json
import math
import re
import shapely
import sys
import unicodedata
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
import zipfile
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from shapely import make_valid
from shapely.geometry import GeometryCollection, MultiPolygon, Point, Polygon, box, mapping, shape
from shapely.validation import explain_validity
from shapely.ops import nearest_points, unary_union
from shapely.geometry.base import BaseGeometry

from area_boundaries import boundary_candidate_review, process_osm_boundaries
from station_matching import match_osm_station_nodes
from station_fact_geometry import match_station_fact_nodes
from road_gap_fill import ROAD_HIGHWAYS, build_road_graph, connect_seed_components, filter_graph_for_entity, haversine_km, normalize as normalize_road_label

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
ENTITIES = ROOT / "data/seed/entities.csv"
OUT = ROOT / "data/features"
# Conservative Greater Cairo envelope [west, south, east, north], WGS84.
BBOX = (30.5, 29.2, 32.1, 30.8)
ROAD_BBOX = (30.5, 29.2, 32.35, 30.9)
OSM_ATTRIBUTION = "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0"
TFC_ATTRIBUTION = "Transport for Cairo (TfC), GTFS for Public Road-Transport, CC BY 4.0"
OVERPASS_URL = "https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter"
TFC_PORTAL_URL = "https://portal.transport-data.org/@tfc"


def normalize(value: str | None) -> str:
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", value or "").casefold()
    return re.sub(r"[^\w]+", "", text, flags=re.UNICODE)


def load_entities() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    with ENTITIES.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    for row in rows:
        row["aliases"] = row.get("aliases", "") or ""
    return rows


def load_station_node_facts() -> tuple[dict[str, set[int]], dict[str, set[str]]]:
    """Read exact OSM node identity and explicit line-membership facts, excluding superseded claims."""
    facts_path = ROOT / "data/seed/facts.json"
    facts = json.loads(facts_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    node_ids: dict[str, set[int]] = defaultdict(set)
    line_memberships: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    for fact in facts:
        code = fact.get("entity_code")
        if not isinstance(code, str) or fact.get("verification_state") == "superseded":
            continue
        if fact.get("attribute") == "osm_node_id":
            try:
                node_ids[code].add(int(fact.get("value")))
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                continue
        elif fact.get("attribute") == "line_membership" and isinstance(fact.get("value"), str):
            line_memberships[code].update(re.findall(r"TRANSIT-[A-Z0-9-]+", fact["value"]))
    return dict(node_ids), dict(line_memberships)


def names(row: dict[str, str]) -> set[str]:
    candidates = [row.get("name_ar", ""), row.get("name_en", "")]
    candidates.extend((row.get("aliases") or "").split("|"))
    return {value for raw in candidates if (value := normalize(raw))}


def matcher(entities: list[dict[str, str]], allowed_types: set[str]):
    index: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    by_code = {row["code"]: row for row in entities}
    for row in entities:
        if row["entity_type"] in allowed_types:
            for name in names(row):
                index[(row["entity_type"], name)].add(row["code"])
    return index, by_code


def exact_match(index: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]], entity_type: str, values: list[str]):
    matches: set[str] = set()
    for value in values:
        key = normalize(value)
        if key:
            matches.update(index.get((entity_type, key), set()))
    return sorted(matches)


def in_bbox(geometry: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return geometry_within_bbox(geometry, BBOX)


def geometry_within_bbox(geometry: dict[str, Any], bbox_wgs84: list[float] | tuple[float, ...] | None) -> bool:
    """Check every coordinate against a [west, south, east, north] WGS84 envelope."""
    if bbox_wgs84 is None:
        return True
    if len(bbox_wgs84) != 4:
        return False
    west, south, east, north = bbox_wgs84

    def positions(value: Any):
        if isinstance(value, list) and len(value) >= 2 and all(isinstance(x, (int, float)) for x in value[:2]):
            yield value
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            for item in value:
                yield from positions(item)

    points = list(positions(geometry.get("coordinates", [])))
    return bool(points) and all(west <= point[0] <= east and south <= point[1] <= north for point in points)


def road_bbox_for_entity(entity_code: str, rules: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> list[float] | tuple[float, ...]:
    rule = (rules or {}).get("entity_rules", {}).get(entity_code, {})
    return rule.get("road_bbox_wgs84", rule.get("bbox_wgs84", ROAD_BBOX))


def road_graph_bbox(rules: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
    bboxes = [ROAD_BBOX]
    for rule in (rules or {}).get("entity_rules", {}).values():
        bbox = rule.get("road_bbox_wgs84")
        if isinstance(bbox, list) and len(bbox) == 4:
            bboxes.append(bbox)
    return (min(bbox[0] for bbox in bboxes), min(bbox[1] for bbox in bboxes),
            max(bbox[2] for bbox in bboxes), max(bbox[3] for bbox in bboxes))


def polygonal_parts(geometry: Any) -> list[Polygon]:
    if isinstance(geometry, Polygon):
        return [geometry]
    if isinstance(geometry, MultiPolygon):
        return list(geometry.geoms)
    if isinstance(geometry, GeometryCollection):
        return [polygon for part in geometry.geoms for polygon in polygonal_parts(part)]
    return []


def validate_feature_geometries(features: list[dict[str, Any]], *, repair: bool,
                                rules: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, Any]]:
    """Apply the PostGIS feature constraint plus the importer's WGS84 bbox/lifecycle assumptions."""
    cairo_bbox = box(BBOX[0], BBOX[1], BBOX[2], BBOX[3])
    accepted: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    repairs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    dropped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def reject(item: dict[str, Any], reason: str, validity_reason: str | None = None) -> None:
        props = item.get("properties", {})
        nested = props.get("properties", {}) if isinstance(props, dict) else {}
        dropped.append({
            "feature_code": props.get("code"),
            "entity_code": props.get("entity_code"),
            "osm_type": props.get("osm_type"),
            "osm_id": props.get("osm_id"),
            "geometry_type": (item.get("geometry") or {}).get("type") if isinstance(item.get("geometry"), dict) else None,
            "category": "invalid geometry",
            "reason": reason,
            "validity_reason": validity_reason,
        })

    for item in features:
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            dropped.append({"feature_code":None,"entity_code":None,"osm_type":None,"osm_id":None,"geometry_type":None,"category":"invalid geometry","reason":"feature_not_object","validity_reason":None})
            continue
        props = item.get("properties", {})
        if not isinstance(props, dict) or props.get("srid") != 4326:
            reject(item, "srid_must_equal_4326")
            continue
        try:
            geom = shape(item["geometry"])
        except Exception as exc:
            reject(item, f"geometry_parse_error: {exc}")
            continue
        if geom.is_empty:
            reject(item, "empty_geometry")
            continue

        validity_reason = explain_validity(geom)
        if not geom.is_valid:
            original_type = geom.geom_type
            if repair and original_type in {"Polygon", "MultiPolygon"}:
                try:
                    repaired = make_valid(geom)
                    polygons = [part for part in polygonal_parts(repaired) if not part.is_empty]
                    if polygons:
                        candidate = polygons[0] if len(polygons) == 1 else MultiPolygon(polygons)
                        if not candidate.is_valid:
                            candidate = make_valid(candidate)
                            polygons = [part for part in polygonal_parts(candidate) if not part.is_empty]
                            candidate = polygons[0] if len(polygons) == 1 else MultiPolygon(polygons) if polygons else GeometryCollection()
                        if not candidate.is_empty and candidate.is_valid:
                            geom = candidate
                            item["geometry"] = mapping(geom)
                            repairs.append({
                                "feature_code": props.get("code"),
                                "entity_code": props.get("entity_code"),
                                "osm_type": props.get("osm_type"),
                                "osm_id": props.get("osm_id"),
                                "geometry_type_before": original_type,
                                "geometry_type_after": geom.geom_type,
                                "validity_reason_before": validity_reason,
                                "repair": "shapely.make_valid; retained polygonal components only",
                            })
                        else:
                            reject(item, "make_valid_did_not_produce_valid_polygonal_geometry", explain_validity(candidate))
                            continue
                    else:
                        reject(item, "make_valid_produced_no_polygonal_components", validity_reason)
                        continue
                except Exception as exc:
                    reject(item, f"make_valid_failed: {exc}", validity_reason)
                    continue
            else:
                reject(item, "invalid_geometry", validity_reason)
                continue

        # GEOS allows zero-length line strings as valid; reject these before PostGIS import.
        if geom.geom_type in {"LineString", "MultiLineString"}:
            lines = list(geom.geoms) if geom.geom_type == "MultiLineString" else [geom]
            if any(line.is_empty or line.length <= 0 or len(set(line.coords)) < 2 for line in lines):
                reject(item, "degenerate_line", explain_validity(geom))
                continue
        if geom.is_empty:
            reject(item, "empty_geometry_after_repair")
            continue
        nested = props.get("properties", {}) if isinstance(props, dict) else {}
        road_geometry = isinstance(nested, dict) and nested.get("entity_type") == "road"
        entity_code = props.get("entity_code")
        entity_road_bbox = ((rules or {}).get("entity_rules", {}).get(entity_code, {}).get("road_bbox_wgs84")
                            if road_geometry else None)
        accepted_bbox = box(*(entity_road_bbox or (ROAD_BBOX if road_geometry else BBOX)))
        if not accepted_bbox.covers(geom):
            reject(item, "geometry_outside_road_bbox" if road_geometry else "geometry_outside_greater_cairo_bbox")
            continue
        accepted.append(item)

    return accepted, {"repairs": repairs, "dropped": dropped}


def feature(code: str, entity: dict[str, str], geometry: dict[str, Any], license_id: str,
            attribution: str, source: str, source_ids: dict[str, Any], retrieval_date: str,
            extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "type": "Feature", "id": code, "geometry": geometry,
        "properties": {
            "code": code, "entity_code": entity["code"], "evidence_code": None,
            "srid": 4326, "license": license_id,
            "osm_type": source_ids.get("osm_type"), "osm_id": source_ids.get("osm_id"),
            "osm_version": source_ids.get("osm_version"),
            "properties": {
                "source": source, "source_ids": source_ids,
                "retrieval_date": retrieval_date, "attribution": attribution,
                **(extra or {}),
            },
        },
    }


def add_candidate(report: dict[str, Any], kind: str, source_id: str, source_name: str,
                  outcome: str, candidates: list[str], reason: str | None = None):
    if kind == "transit_station":
        kind = "transit_stop"
    report["by_type"][kind][outcome] += 1
    record = {"source_id": source_id, "source_name": source_name,
              "entity_type": kind, "candidate_entity_codes": candidates,
              "outcome": outcome, "reason": reason or outcome}
    report.setdefault("_outcomes", []).append(record)
    if outcome != "matched":
        report["unmatched" if outcome == "unmatched" else "ambiguous"].append(record.copy())


def process_gtfs(zip_path: Path, entities: list[dict[str, str]], date: str,
                 license_arg: str | None, license_evidence_url: str | None,
                 report: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if license_arg != "CC-BY-4.0":
        raise ValueError("GTFS license was not verified as CC-BY-4.0. Pass --gtfs-license CC-BY-4.0 only for the permitted Transport Data Commons TfC release. NC data is never accepted.")
    if not license_evidence_url or not license_evidence_url.startswith("https://portal.transport-data.org/"):
        raise ValueError("Provide the exact Transport Data Commons dataset/license page using --gtfs-license-evidence-url; the general portal home page is not sufficient.")
    idx, by_code = matcher(entities, {"transit_line", "transit_stop", "transit_station", "rail_station"})
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path) as zf:
        available = {Path(n).name: n for n in zf.namelist() if not n.endswith("/")}
        def table(filename: str):
            entry = available.get(filename)
            if not entry:
                return []
            with zf.open(entry) as f:
                return list(csv.DictReader(line.decode("utf-8-sig") for line in f))
        routes = table("routes.txt")
        trips = table("trips.txt")
        shape_routes: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        route_by_id = {r.get("route_id", ""): r for r in routes}
        for trip in trips:
            if trip.get("shape_id") and trip.get("route_id"):
                shape_routes[trip["shape_id"]].add(trip["route_id"])
        points: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float, float]]] = defaultdict(list)
        for row in table("shapes.txt"):
            try:
                points[row["shape_id"]].append((int(row["shape_pt_sequence"]), float(row["shape_pt_lon"]), float(row["shape_pt_lat"])))
            except (KeyError, ValueError):
                continue
        for shape_id, sequence in sorted(points.items()):
            route_rows = [route_by_id[rid] for rid in sorted(shape_routes.get(shape_id, set())) if rid in route_by_id]
            labels = [x for r in route_rows for x in (r.get("route_long_name", ""), r.get("route_short_name", "")) if x]
            matches = sorted({code for kind in ("transit_line",) for code in exact_match(idx, kind, labels)})
            source_id = f"shape:{shape_id}"
            if len(matches) != 1:
                add_candidate(report, "transit_line", source_id, " | ".join(labels), "ambiguous" if matches else "unmatched", matches,
                              "GTFS route names did not identify exactly one existing transit_line")
                continue
            entity = by_code[matches[0]]
            if entity["status"] in {"planned", "approved_planned", "under_construction"}:
                add_candidate(report, "transit_line", source_id, " | ".join(labels), "unmatched", [], "planned entity has no evidenced official alignment in this source")
                continue
            coords = [[lon, lat] for _, lon, lat in sorted(sequence)]
            geom = {"type": "LineString", "coordinates": coords}
            if len(coords) < 2 or not in_bbox(geom):
                add_candidate(report, "transit_line", source_id, " | ".join(labels), "unmatched", matches, "geometry is invalid or outside the Greater Cairo bbox")
                continue
            code = "GTFS-SHAPE-" + re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9-]+", "-", shape_id.upper()).strip("-")[:80]
            out.append(feature(code, entity, geom, "CC-BY-4.0", TFC_ATTRIBUTION, "Transport for Cairo GTFS", {"gtfs_shape_id": shape_id, "gtfs_route_ids": sorted(shape_routes.get(shape_id, set())), "license_evidence_url": license_evidence_url}, date, {"entity_type": "transit_line"}))
            add_candidate(report, "transit_line", source_id, " | ".join(labels), "matched", matches)
        for row in table("stops.txt"):
            sid = row.get("stop_id", "")
            try:
                lon, lat = float(row["stop_lon"]), float(row["stop_lat"])
            except (KeyError, ValueError):
                continue
            geom = {"type": "Point", "coordinates": [lon, lat]}
            label = row.get("stop_name", "")
            matches = []
            for kind in ("transit_stop", "transit_station", "rail_station"):
                matches.extend(exact_match(idx, kind, [label]))
            matches = sorted(set(matches))
            if len(matches) != 1 or not in_bbox(geom):
                add_candidate(report, "transit_stop", f"stop:{sid}", label, "ambiguous" if matches else "unmatched", matches,
                              "GTFS stop name did not identify exactly one existing stop/station" if matches else "no exact existing stop/station name or alias")
                continue
            entity = by_code[matches[0]]
            if entity["status"] in {"planned", "approved_planned", "under_construction"}:
                add_candidate(report, "transit_stop", f"stop:{sid}", label, "unmatched", [], "planned stop lacks official alignment provenance")
                continue
            code = "GTFS-STOP-" + re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9-]+", "-", sid.upper()).strip("-")[:80]
            out.append(feature(code, entity, geom, "CC-BY-4.0", TFC_ATTRIBUTION, "Transport for Cairo GTFS", {"gtfs_stop_id": sid, "license_evidence_url": license_evidence_url}, date, {"entity_type": entity["entity_type"], "stop_name": label}))
            add_candidate(report, "transit_stop", f"stop:{sid}", label, "matched", matches)
    return out


def overpass_query() -> str:
    w, s, e, n = BBOX
    box = f"{s},{w},{n},{e}"
    return f"""[out:json][timeout:180];(
      way[highway~\"^(motorway|trunk|primary|secondary)$\"][name]({box});
      way[highway~\"^(motorway_link|trunk_link|primary_link|secondary_link)$\"]({box});
      relation[route~\"^(train|subway|light_rail|monorail|bus)$\"]({box});
      way[railway~\"^(subway|light_rail|monorail)$\"][name]({box});
      node[public_transport~\"^(stop_position|platform|station)$\"]({box});
      node[railway~\"^(station|halt)$\"]({box});
      way[railway~\"^(station|platform)$\"]({box});
    );out meta geom;"""


def process_osm(data: dict[str, Any], entities: list[dict[str, str]], date: str,
                report: dict[str, Any], rules: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    idx, by_code = matcher(entities, {"road", "transit_line", "transit_stop", "transit_station", "rail_station"})
    entity_rules = (rules or {}).get("entity_rules", {})
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item in data.get("elements", []):
        tags = item.get("tags", {})
        etype, match_type, geom = None, None, None
        if item["type"] == "node" and (tags.get("public_transport") == "station" or tags.get("railway") in {"station", "halt", "construction"}):
            # Station nodes need the strict name/mode/lifecycle/line matcher below.
            continue
        elif item["type"] == "way" and tags.get("railway") in {"subway", "light_rail", "monorail"}:
            etype, match_type = "transit_line", "transit_line"
            coords = [[p["lon"], p["lat"]] for p in item.get("geometry", []) if "lon" in p and "lat" in p]
            geom = {"type": "LineString", "coordinates": coords}
        elif item["type"] == "relation" and tags.get("route") in {"train", "subway", "light_rail", "monorail", "bus"}:
            etype, match_type = "transit_line", "transit_line"
            lines = []
            for member in item.get("members", []):
                coords = [[p["lon"], p["lat"]] for p in member.get("geometry", []) if "lon" in p and "lat" in p]
                if len(coords) >= 2:
                    lines.append(coords)
            geom = {"type": "MultiLineString", "coordinates": lines}
        if not etype or not match_type or not isinstance(geom, dict):
            continue
        labels = [tags.get(k, "") for k in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en", "ref") if tags.get(k)]
        matches = exact_match(idx, etype, labels)
        if etype == "transit_line":
            normalized_labels = {normalize(label) for label in labels if normalize(label)}
            for code, line_rule in (rules or {}).get("line_entity_rules", {}).items():
                aliases = {normalize(str(alias)) for alias in line_rule.get("match_aliases", []) if normalize(str(alias))}
                if aliases & normalized_labels and code in by_code and by_code[code].get("entity_type") == "transit_line":
                    matches = sorted(set(matches) | {code})
        ident = f"{item['type']}:{item['id']}"
        if len(matches) == 1:
            rule = entity_rules.get(matches[0], {})
            if rule.get("enabled") is False:
                add_candidate(report, match_type, ident, " | ".join(labels), "ambiguous", matches,
                              rule.get("reason", "entity disabled by match rule"))
                continue
            if not geometry_within_bbox(geom, rule.get("bbox_wgs84")):
                add_candidate(report, match_type, ident, " | ".join(labels), "ambiguous", matches,
                              "geometry falls outside the entity-specific match-rule bbox")
                continue
        if len(matches) != 1:
            add_candidate(report, match_type, ident, " | ".join(labels), "ambiguous" if matches else "unmatched", matches,
                          "OSM names did not identify exactly one compatible existing entity")
            continue
        entity = by_code[matches[0]]
        if entity["status"] in {"planned", "approved_planned", "under_construction"}:
            add_candidate(report, match_type, ident, " | ".join(labels), "unmatched", [], "planned entity geometry requires official alignment evidence")
            continue
        if not in_bbox(geom):
            add_candidate(report, match_type, ident, " | ".join(labels), "unmatched", matches, "geometry is invalid or not wholly inside the Greater Cairo bbox")
            continue
        version = int(item.get("version", 0))
        if version < 1:
            add_candidate(report, match_type, ident, " | ".join(labels), "unmatched", matches, "OSM version metadata is missing")
            continue
        code = f"OSM-{item['type'].upper()}-{int(item['id'])}"
        props = {"osm_type": item["type"], "osm_id": int(item["id"]), "osm_version": version}
        out.append(feature(code, entity, geom, "ODbL-1.0", OSM_ATTRIBUTION, "OpenStreetMap", props, date,
                           {"entity_type": etype, "osm_tags": tags}))
        add_candidate(report, match_type, ident, " | ".join(labels), "matched", matches)
    return out


BRT_LINE_CODE = "TRANSIT-BRT"
BRT_BUSWAY_NAME_TOKENS = ("brt", "ترددي")
BRT_MAX_STATION_LINE_DISTANCE_M = 600.0


def _is_brt_busway(tags: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """A way is BRT alignment only when the source explicitly tags highway=busway and names it BRT."""
    if str(tags.get("highway", "")).casefold() != "busway":
        return False
    if str(tags.get("construction", "")).casefold() or str(tags.get("proposed", "")).casefold():
        return False
    names = " ".join(str(tags.get(key, "")) for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en", "ref"))
    folded = names.casefold()
    return any(token in folded for token in BRT_BUSWAY_NAME_TOKENS)


def _busway_linestring(element: dict[str, Any]):
    geometry = element.get("geometry") or []
    coordinates = [[float(point["lon"]), float(point["lat"])] for point in geometry
                   if isinstance(point, dict) and "lon" in point and "lat" in point]
    if len(coordinates) < 2 or len({(lon, lat) for lon, lat in coordinates}) < 2:
        return None, coordinates
    return shapely.geometry.LineString(coordinates), coordinates


def process_brt_osm(data: dict[str, Any], entities: list[dict[str, str]], date: str,
                    report: dict[str, Any], rules: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Attach OSM ``highway=busway`` BRT alignment to the existing ``TRANSIT-BRT`` entity only.

    The source is explicitly partial: only ways that carry the BRT name token become line geometry,
    every accepted way keeps its own OSM type/id/version and ODbL attribution, and no connector,
    simplification or station position is invented. Stations are never created here; stations whose
    OSM node name matches a seeded STOP-BRT-* entity are handled by the station matcher.
    """
    by_code = {entity["code"]: entity for entity in entities}
    entity = by_code.get(BRT_LINE_CODE)
    audit: dict[str, Any] = {
        "entity_code": BRT_LINE_CODE,
        "source_policy": ("OSM highway=busway ways whose name carries a BRT/ترددي token; "
                          "no synthesized connectors, no name-only station inference"),
        "accepted_osm_way_ids": [],
        "rejected": [],
        "geometry_status": "absent",
        "geometry_completeness_note": ("BRT geometry is partial (OSM): only mapped busway ways are "
                                       "drawn. Unmapped sections stay without geometry until a cited "
                                       "source exists."),
    }
    report["brt_osm_geometry"] = audit
    if entity is None:
        audit["rejected"].append({"reason": "TRANSIT-BRT entity is not present in the seed"})
        return []
    if entity.get("status") in {"planned", "approved_planned", "under_construction"}:
        audit["rejected"].append({"reason": f"entity status {entity.get('status')} requires official alignment evidence"})
        return []

    matched_features: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for element in data.get("elements", []):
        if element.get("type") != "way":
            continue
        tags = element.get("tags") or {}
        if str(tags.get("highway", "")).casefold() != "busway":
            continue
        osm_id = int(element.get("id", 0) or 0)
        version = int(element.get("version", 0) or 0)
        if not _is_brt_busway(tags):
            audit["rejected"].append({"osm_type": "way", "osm_id": osm_id,
                                      "reason": "busway way carries no BRT/ترددي name token"})
            continue
        if version < 1:
            audit["rejected"].append({"osm_type": "way", "osm_id": osm_id,
                                      "reason": "OSM version metadata is missing"})
            continue
        line, coordinates = _busway_linestring(element)
        if line is None:
            audit["rejected"].append({"osm_type": "way", "osm_id": osm_id,
                                      "reason": "busway way has fewer than two distinct positions"})
            continue
        if not in_bbox({"type": "LineString", "coordinates": coordinates}):
            audit["rejected"].append({"osm_type": "way", "osm_id": osm_id,
                                      "reason": "busway geometry falls outside the Greater Cairo bbox"})
            continue
        code = f"OSM-WAY-{osm_id}"
        matched_features.append(feature(
            code, entity, {"type": "LineString", "coordinates": coordinates}, "ODbL-1.0",
            OSM_ATTRIBUTION, "OpenStreetMap", {"osm_type": "way", "osm_id": osm_id, "osm_version": version},
            date, {"entity_type": "transit_line", "osm_tags": tags,
                   "match_method": "OSM highway=busway with an explicit BRT name token; no name-only inference",
                   "geometry_completeness": "partial_source_geometry"},
        ))
        audit["accepted_osm_way_ids"].append(osm_id)
        add_candidate(report, "transit_line", f"way:{osm_id}", " | ".join(
            str(tags.get(key)) for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en") if tags.get(key)),
            "matched", [BRT_LINE_CODE], "OSM highway=busway BRT alignment")

    audit["accepted_way_count"] = len(matched_features)
    if matched_features:
        audit["geometry_status"] = "partial_from_osm"
        merged = unary_union([shape(item["geometry"]) for item in matched_features])
        audit["geometry_type"] = merged.geom_type
        audit["line_length_km_osm"] = round(sum(haversine_km(a, b)
                                                for part in lineal_parts(merged)
                                                for a, b in zip(list(part.coords), list(part.coords)[1:])), 3)
        audit["accepted_osm_way_ids"].sort()
    return matched_features


def brt_station_candidate_audit(osm_data: dict[str, Any], entities: list[dict[str, str]],
                                station_features: list[dict[str, Any]],
                                station_node_ids_by_entity: dict[str, set[int]],
                                source_node_audit: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Report which STOP-BRT-* entities are matched to OSM BRT station nodes and why the rest are not.

    Nothing is inferred: a station node becomes geometry only through the general station matcher
    (exact normalised name or configured alias plus compatible mode), never from a BRT look-alike name.
    """
    by_code = {entity["code"]: entity for entity in entities}
    brt_codes = sorted(code for code in by_code if code.startswith("STOP-BRT-"))
    matched = sorted({str(item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code")) for item in station_features
                      if str(item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code", "")).startswith("STOP-BRT-")})
    audit_by_node = {int(item.get("osm_id", 0) or 0): item for item in (source_node_audit or [])
