        before_counts[item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code", "")] += 1
    after_counts = defaultdict(int)
    for item in features: after_counts[item["properties"]["entity_code"]] += 1
    station_audit = report.get("station_matching")
    if station_audit:
        for code, station_entity in station_audit.get("station_entities", {}).items():
            station_entity["feature_count_before"] = before_counts[code]
            station_entity["feature_count_after"] = after_counts[code]
        for summary in station_audit.get("by_line", {}).values():
            entity_codes = set(summary.get("matched_entity_codes", [])) | set(summary.get("unmatched_entities", []))
            summary["station_feature_count_before"] = sum(before_counts[code] for code in entity_codes)
            summary["station_feature_count_after"] = sum(after_counts[code] for code in entity_codes)
        station_status_counts = Counter(entity.get("status", "unknown") for entity in entities
                                        if entity.get("entity_type") in {"transit_stop", "rail_station"}
                                        and entity.get("code", "").startswith(("STOP-", "RAIL-")))
        station_audit["seed_station_entity_count"] = len(station_audit.get("station_entities", {}))
        station_audit["seed_status_counts"] = dict(sorted(station_status_counts.items()))
        station_audit["station_features_before"] = sum(before_counts[code] for code in station_audit.get("station_entities", {}))
        station_audit["station_features_after"] = sum(after_counts[code] for code in station_audit.get("station_entities", {}))
        station_audit["station_entities_with_geometry_before"] = sum(before_counts[code] > 0 for code in station_audit.get("station_entities", {}))
        station_audit["station_entities_with_geometry_after"] = sum(after_counts[code] > 0 for code in station_audit.get("station_entities", {}))
        station_audit["station_entities_matched"] = sum(item.get("matched_count", 0) > 0 for item in station_audit.get("station_entities", {}).values())
        fact_line_entities: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        for code, line_codes in station_lines_by_entity.items():
            if code not in entity_by_code or entity_by_code[code].get("entity_type") not in {"transit_stop", "rail_station"}:
                continue
            for line_code in line_codes:
                if line_code.startswith("TRANSIT-"):
                    fact_line_entities[line_code].add(code)
        point_entity_codes = {item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code") for item in features
                              if item.get("geometry", {}).get("type") == "Point"}
        per_line_geometry: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        exact_by_line = (station_node_fact_audit or {}).get("by_line", {})
        for line_code, codes_for_line in sorted(fact_line_entities.items()):
            with_geometry = sorted(codes_for_line & point_entity_codes)
            exact_line = exact_by_line.get(line_code, {})
            missing_codes = sorted(codes_for_line - set(with_geometry))
            point_count = sum(sum(1 for item in features
                                  if item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code") == code
                                  and item.get("geometry", {}).get("type") == "Point")
                              for code in codes_for_line)
            per_line_geometry[line_code] = {
                "station_entities": len(codes_for_line),
                "station_entities_with_geometry": len(with_geometry),
                "station_entities_without_geometry": missing_codes,
                "point_feature_count": point_count,
                "osm_station_groups": exact_line.get("station_group_count", 0),
                "osm_station_groups_with_geometry": exact_line.get("matched_group_count", 0),
                "lifecycle_or_geometry_rejected_groups": exact_line.get("rejected_groups", []),
            }
            summary = station_audit.setdefault("by_line", {}).setdefault(line_code, {})
            summary.update({
                "station_entities": len(codes_for_line), "matched_entities": len(with_geometry),
                "matched_entity_codes": with_geometry, "unmatched_entities": missing_codes,
                "station_feature_count_after": point_count,
                "station_group_count": exact_line.get("station_group_count", 0),
                "station_groups_with_geometry": exact_line.get("matched_group_count", 0),
            })
        station_audit["station_entities_with_geometry_by_line"] = per_line_geometry
        report["station_entities_with_geometry_by_line"] = per_line_geometry
    report["feature_counts_before_after_by_entity"] = {e["code"]:{"before":before_counts[e["code"]],"after":after_counts[e["code"]]} for e in entities}
    report["entities_with_geometry"] = {"count":sum(v>0 for v in after_counts.values()),"total_entities":len(entities),"entity_codes":sorted(after_counts)}
    def entity_extent(items: list[dict[str, Any]], entity_code: str) -> list[float] | None:
        geometries = []
        for feature_item in items:
            if feature_item.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code") != entity_code:
                continue
            try:
                geometries.append(shape(feature_item["geometry"]))
            except Exception:
                continue
        return list(unary_union(geometries).bounds) if geometries else None
    scope_codes = ["ROAD-AHMED-ORABI", "ROAD-AMR-IBN-ALAS", "ROAD-MOHAMED-NAGUIB",
                   "ROAD-ASSAR-AXIS", "ROAD-AIN-SOKHNA", "ROAD-CORNICHE"]
    report["road_scope_extent_review"] = {code: {
        "before_wgs84": entity_extent(previous_features, code),
        "after_wgs84": entity_extent(features, code),
        "before_feature_count": before_counts[code], "after_feature_count": after_counts[code],
    } for code in scope_codes}
    report["source_results"] = source_results
    report["source_failures"] = failures
    report["area_boundaries"] = boundary_manifest
    report["boundary_match_counts_by_type"] = boundary_manifest.get("entity_counts_by_type", {})
    report["road_bbox_overrides_by_entity"] = {
        code: rule["road_bbox_wgs84"] for code, rule in rules.get("entity_rules", {}).items()
        if isinstance(rule, dict) and rule.get("road_bbox_wgs84")
    }
    report["bbox_wgs84"] = list(BBOX)
    report["road_bbox_wgs84"] = list(ROAD_BBOX)
    # Quantify the road-only envelope experiment against the previous common envelope.
    old_road_area = box(*BBOX)
    extended_road_area = box(*ROAD_BBOX)
    north_only_area = box(ROAD_BBOX[0], ROAD_BBOX[1], ROAD_BBOX[2], ROAD_BBOX[3]).difference(
        box(ROAD_BBOX[0], ROAD_BBOX[1], ROAD_BBOX[2], BBOX[3]))
    extension_features = 0
    extension_length_km = 0.0
    north_features = 0
    north_length_km = 0.0
    for feature_item in features:
        props = feature_item.get("properties", {})
        metadata = props.get("properties", {}) if isinstance(props, dict) else {}
        if not isinstance(metadata, dict) or metadata.get("entity_type") != "road":
            continue
        try:
            geom = shape(feature_item["geometry"])
            delta = geom.intersection(extended_road_area).difference(old_road_area)
            delta_parts = lineal_parts(delta)
            delta_length = sum(haversine_km(a, b) for part in delta_parts for a, b in zip(list(part.coords), list(part.coords)[1:]))
            if delta_length > 0:
                extension_features += 1
                extension_length_km += delta_length
            north_delta = geom.intersection(north_only_area)
            north_parts = lineal_parts(north_delta)
            north_length = sum(haversine_km(a, b) for part in north_parts for a, b in zip(list(part.coords), list(part.coords)[1:]))
            if north_length > 0:
                north_features += 1
                north_length_km += north_length
        except Exception:
            continue
    report["road_bbox_extension_impact"] = {
        "previous_bbox_wgs84": list(BBOX), "road_bbox_wgs84": list(ROAD_BBOX),
        "road_features_with_geometry_in_new_area": extension_features,
        "road_length_km_in_new_area": round(extension_length_km, 3),
        "north_extension_only": {"south_edge": BBOX[3], "north_edge": ROAD_BBOX[3],
                                  "features": north_features, "length_km": round(north_length_km, 3)},
        "dropped_fragments_under_300m": len(report.get("dropped_road_fragments_under_300m", [])),
    }
    report["features_written"] = feature_count
    report["features_by_entity"] = {code: sum(1 for f in features if f["properties"]["entity_code"] == code) for code in sorted({f["properties"]["entity_code"] for f in features})}
    report["road_gap_fill_totals"] = {
        "gaps_before": sum(item.get("gaps_before", 0) for item in report["road_gap_fill_by_entity"]),
        "gaps_after": sum(item.get("gaps_after", 0) for item in report["road_gap_fill_by_entity"]),
        "gaps_closed": sum(item.get("gaps_closed", 0) for item in report["road_gap_fill_by_entity"]),
        "topology_gaps": len(report["road_topology_gaps"]),
        "network_gaps": sum(gap.get("gap_type") == "network_gap" for gap in report["road_gaps"]),
        "source_clipped_edges": sum(gap.get("gap_type") == "source_clipped_edge" for gap in report["road_gaps"]),
        "km_closed": round(sum(item.get("km_closed", 0.0) for item in report["road_gap_fill_by_entity"]), 3),
    }
    report["road_network_context"] = road_network_manifest
    report["entity_page_map_geometries"] = entity_map_manifest
    report["rejected_road_segments"] = list({(g["source_id"],g["entity_code"]):g for g in report.get("rejected_road_segments",[])}.values())
    report["ملخص_05_5"] = {
        "فجوات_كل_طريق_قبل_وبعد_والكيلومترات_المغلقة": [{
            "رمز_الكيان": item["entity_code"], "الاسم_العربي": item.get("entity_name_ar"),
            "الاسم_الإنجليزي": item.get("entity_name_en"), "الفجوات_قبل": item.get("gaps_before", 0),
            "الفجوات_بعد": item.get("gaps_after", 0), "الفجوات_المغلقة": item.get("gaps_closed", 0),
            "الكيلومترات_المغلقة": item.get("km_closed", 0.0), "الفجوات_المتبقية_مع_الإحداثيات": item.get("unresolved_gaps", []),
        } for item in report["road_gap_fill_by_entity"]],
        "إجمالي_ميزات_الكيانات_بعد_التحديث": feature_count,
        "الكيانات_المطابقة_بعلاقات_طريق_OSM": report["road_route_entities"],
        "عدد_ميزات_شبكة_السياق_غير_الكيانية": road_network_manifest.get("feature_count", 0),
    }
    report["ملخص_05_4"] = {
        "عدد_الميزات_لكل_كيان_قبل_وبعد": report["feature_counts_before_after_by_entity"],
        "الكيانات_ذات_الهندسة_من_إجمالي_342": {"العدد":report["entities_with_geometry"]["count"],"الإجمالي":len(entities)},
        "فجوات_الطرق_المتبقية_مع_نقاط_النهاية": report["road_gaps"],
        "الطرق_المرفوضة_دون_اختلاق_وصلات": report["rejected_road_segments"],
        "العناصر_غير_المطابقة": report["unmatched"],
        "العناصر_الملتبسة": report["ambiguous"],
        "الهندسات_التي_تم_إصلاحها": {"العدد":len(report["geometry_repairs"]),"حسب_الكيان":dict(Counter(x["entity_code"] for x in report["geometry_repairs"])),"التفاصيل":report["geometry_repairs"]},
        "الهندسات_التي_تم_إسقاطها": {"العدد":len(report["invalid_geometry"]),"حسب_الكيان":dict(Counter(x["entity_code"] for x in report["invalid_geometry"])),"التفاصيل":report["invalid_geometry"]},
    }
    report["retrieval_date"] = date
    arabic_lines = [
        "# تقرير PHASE 05.5 — استمرارية هندسة الطرق",
        "",
        f"تاريخ التشغيل: {date}. المصدر: مستخلصات OpenStreetMap المخزنة (الأول وextract2 وroads-full)، بترخيص ODbL-1.0. لم تُنشأ وصلات هندسية اصطناعية.",
        "",
        f"- إجمالي ميزات الكيانات بعد التحديث: **{feature_count}** (شبكة السياق غير محتسبة ضمنها).",
        f"- إجمالي الفجوات: **{report['road_gap_fill_totals']['gaps_before']}** قبل، **{report['road_gap_fill_totals']['gaps_after']}** بعد؛ أُغلقت **{report['road_gap_fill_totals']['gaps_closed']}** بمسارات OSM حقيقية بطول **{report['road_gap_fill_totals']['km_closed']:.3f} كم**. من المتبقي **{report['road_gap_fill_totals']['topology_gaps']}** فجوة طوبولوجية (`osm_topology_gap`, أقل من 25 م ولا تُملأ) و**{report['road_gap_fill_totals']['network_gaps']}** فجوات شبكة.",
        ("- أُغلقت حافة الإقليمي الغربية عند `(30.5148, 29.7646)` بطرق OSM جديدة تمتد غرب lon 30.5؛ أُزيل تصنيف المصدر المقطوع." if report.get("regional_ring_west_patch", {}).get("status") == "closed_by_new_osm_extract" else "- حافة الإقليمي الغربية عند `(30.5148, 29.7646)` ما زالت `source_clipped_edge`: `source clipped at lon 30.5`؛ لم تُحسب فجوة شبكة."),
        f"- ميزات شبكة السياق المنفصلة: {road_network_manifest.get('feature_count', 0)} مبسطة للمستويات المنخفضة و{road_network_manifest.get('detail_feature_count', 0)} للتكبير العالي.",
        "",
        "## نتيجة كل طريق",
        "",
        "| الطريق | قبل | بعد | أُغلقت | كم مغلقة | طوبولوجية <25م | شبكة |",
        "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|",
    ]
    for item in report["road_gap_fill_by_entity"]:
        arabic_lines.append(
            f"| {item.get('entity_name_ar') or item['entity_code']} (`{item['entity_code']}`) | {item.get('gaps_before', 0)} | {item.get('gaps_after', 0)} | {item.get('gaps_closed', 0)} | {item.get('km_closed', 0.0):.3f} | {sum(gap.get('gap_type') == 'osm_topology_gap' for gap in item.get('unresolved_gaps', []))} | {sum(gap.get('gap_type') == 'network_gap' for gap in item.get('unresolved_gaps', []))} |"
        )
    arabic_lines.extend(["", "## مراجعة نطاق المحاور ومكونات الطرق", ""])
    for code, review in report.get("road_scope_extent_review", {}).items():
        arabic_lines.append(f"- `{code}`: النطاق قبل `{review.get('before_wgs84')}` وبعد `{review.get('after_wgs84')}`؛ الميزات {review.get('before_feature_count')} ← {review.get('after_feature_count')}.")
    for item in report.get("urban_axis_component_filter", []):
        dropped_ids = sorted({way_id for component in item.get("components_dropped", []) for way_id in component.get("way_ids", [])})
        arabic_lines.append(f"- تصفية `{item['entity_code']}`: أُبقي أكبر مكوّن والمكونات ضمن {item['max_component_distance_km']:.1f} كم؛ أُسقطت {len(item.get('components_dropped', []))} مكونات غير متصلة، way IDs: `{dropped_ids}`.")
    if not report.get("urban_axis_component_filter"):
        arabic_lines.append("- لا توجد محاور حضرية عليها قاعدة تصفية المكونات في هذه الدورة.")
    arabic_lines.extend(["", "## الكيانات التي استخدمت علاقات OSM من النوع route=road", ""])
    if report["road_route_relations"]:
        for relation in report["road_route_relations"]:
            arabic_lines.append(
                f"- `{relation['entity_code']}` — relation/{relation['relation_id']}؛ {relation.get('name') or 'بلا اسم'}؛ ref: {relation.get('ref') or '—'}؛ {len(relation.get('member_way_ids', []))} مقطعًا عضوًا."
            )
    else:
        arabic_lines.append("- لا توجد علاقات مطابقة في المستخلصات.")
    arabic_lines.extend(["", "## الفجوات المتبقية وإحداثيات نهاياتها", ""])
    remaining = [(item, gap) for item in report["road_gap_fill_by_entity"] for gap in item.get("unresolved_gaps", [])]
    if remaining:
        for item, gap in remaining:
            if gap.get("gap_type") == "source_clipped_edge":
                arabic_lines.append(f"- `{item['entity_code']}` (`source_clipped_edge`): الطرف عند `{gap['coordinate_wgs84']}`؛ `source clipped at lon {gap['source_bbox_west_lon']}`؛ لذا هذه ليست فجوة شبكة؛ المستخلص الإضافي مطلوب.")
                continue
            a = gap.get("endpoint_a", {}).get("coordinates")
            b = gap.get("endpoint_b", {}).get("coordinates")
            reason = "فجوة طوبولوجية بين نهايتي طريق بلا عقدة OSM مشتركة؛ لم تُملأ" if gap.get("gap_type") == "osm_topology_gap" else f"لا يوجد مسار طرق مسموح ضمن حد {gap.get('max_graph_path_km', 0):.3f} كم"
            arabic_lines.append(f"- `{item['entity_code']}` ({gap.get('gap_type', 'network_gap')}): `{a}` ↔ `{b}`؛ المسافة المستقيمة {gap.get('straight_line_km', 0):.3f} كم؛ {reason}.")
    else:
        arabic_lines.append("- لا توجد فجوات متبقية بين مكونات الطرق المطابقة.")
    arabic_report_path = outdir / "phase-05-5-road-gap-report.ar.md"
    arabic_report_path.write_text("\n".join(arabic_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    report["arabic_road_gap_report"] = str(arabic_report_path.relative_to(ROOT))
    station_audit = report.get("station_matching")
    if station_audit:
        station_lines = [
            "# تقرير PHASE 07.4 — الحدود والمحطات ومراجعة الطريق الإقليمي",

            f"- ميزات نقاط المحطات: {station_audit.get('station_features_before', 0)} قبل → {station_audit.get('station_features_after', 0)} بعد؛ كيانات ذات هندسة: {station_audit.get('station_entities_with_geometry_before', 0)} → {station_audit.get('station_entities_with_geometry_after', 0)}.",

             f"- بقيت الحافة عند `(30.5148, 29.7646)` مقطوعة عند lon 30.5؛ لم تُسجّل كفجوة شبكة. النطاق الخاص `{west_patch.get('road_bbox_wgs84')}` لم يوفر اتصالًا مؤكدًا.")])

                                  if gap.get("entity_code") == "ROAD-REGIONAL-RING" and gap.get("gap_type") == "osm_topology_gap"]

    (outdir / "match-report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    raw_archives = []
    for basename in ("osm-overpass-2026-11-14.json", "cairo-osm-overpass-2026-11-14-extract2.json",
                     "cairo-osm-overpass-2026-11-14-roads-full.json", "cairo-osm-boundaries-2026-11-14.json"):
        archive = ROOT / "data/raw" / (basename + ".gz")
        raw_archives.append({"source_file": basename, "file": str(archive.relative_to(ROOT)),
                             "sha256_original_json": sha256_uncompressed_json(archive) if archive.exists() else None,
                             "sha256_gzip": hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest() if archive.exists() else None,
                             "license": "ODbL-1.0"})
    manifest = {
        "title": "Greater Cairo Atlas geometry ingestion manifest", "retrieval_date": date,
        "bbox_wgs84": list(BBOX), "road_bbox_wgs84": list(ROAD_BBOX), "feature_count": feature_count,

        "matching": "Station points use normalized Arabic/English names and seed aliases, explicit station/railway modes or route-relation membership, and a 600 m matched-line guard where line geometry exists; ambiguous same-name nodes are reported rather than assigned. Road route relations are primary where exact name/ref matches exist. Gaps use real motorway/trunk/primary/secondary ways. Real *_link ways may be used only as shared-node paths joining components of the same entity within the configured distance; other named ways still require the same entity name/alias/ref. Sub-25 m endpoint pairs without a shared OSM node are reported as osm_topology_gap and are never snapped or filled. No synthetic connectors or entity creation.",
        "road_gap_fill": {"max_path_km_default": (rules or {}).get("road_gap_fill", {}).get("max_path_km", 3.0),

        "arabic_phase_07_4_report": report.get("arabic_phase_07_4_report"),
        "lifecycle_policy": "Operational/unknown station nodes require exact normalized seed/configured names and compatible mode/route evidence. Planned/under-construction station points require explicit OSM construction tags, matched non-planned official line geometry within 600 m, and compatible lifecycle; planned line geometry is never used. BRT geometry matches only existing STOP-BRT entities from bus=yes/route evidence. No station entity is inferred.",
        "geometry_policy": "WGS84 / SRID 4326; non-empty, GEOS-valid geometries are validated and repaired only when polygonal. Non-road features use bbox_wgs84; roads use their entity-specific road_bbox_wgs84 override when configured, otherwise the shared road envelope. The Regional Ring west patch is accepted only for ROAD-REGIONAL-RING. Every output geometry is checked with Shapely is_valid/explain_validity; degenerate lines and unrepaired/otherwise invalid geometries are dropped.",
        "geometry_validation": {"validator":f"Shapely {shapely.__version__}","db_constraint":"ST_SRID(geometry)=4326 AND NOT ST_IsEmpty(geometry) AND ST_IsValid(geometry)","features_repaired_count":len(report["geometry_repairs"]),"features_dropped_count":len(report["invalid_geometry"]),"repairs":report["geometry_repairs"],"dropped":report["invalid_geometry"]},
        "osm_query_coverage": {
            "preserved_extracts": "Three prior owner-supplied extracts (first extract, extract2, roads-full) plus the new boundaries/stations/road-patch extract are deduplicated by OSM type/id, preferring current versions and most complete geometry. Route graph connectivity uses shared OSM node IDs, never coordinate snapping.",
            "new_extract": {
                "file": "data/raw/cairo-osm-boundaries-2026-11-14.json.gz",
                "timestamp_osm_base": boundary_manifest.get("timestamp_osm_base"),
                "elements": boundary_manifest.get("elements_received"),
                "license": "ODbL-1.0",
                "sha256_original_json": boundary_manifest.get("sha256_original_json"),
                "sha256_gzip": boundary_manifest.get("sha256_gzip"),
                "output": "out meta geom",
                "selectors": {
                    "administrative_relations": {"filter": "boundary=administrative AND admin_level in 4..10", "bbox_s_w_n_e": [29.2, 30.3, 30.95, 32.4]},
                    "place_relations_and_ways": {"filter": "place=city|town|suburb|quarter|neighbourhood", "bbox_s_w_n_e": [29.2, 30.3, 30.95, 32.4]},
                    "regional_ring_highway_ways": {"filter": "highway=motorway|trunk|primary|motorway_link|trunk_link|primary_link", "bbox_s_w_n_e": [29.5, 30.25, 30.15, 30.55]},
                    "regional_ring_route_relations": {"filter": "route=road AND ref=515"},
                    "transit_relations": {"filter": "route=monorail|bus|light_rail", "bbox_s_w_n_e": [29.7, 30.4, 30.7, 32.0]},
                    "station_and_bus_nodes": {"filter": "station=monorail|light_rail; monorail=yes; railway=station|halt|stop; public_transport=station; construction~station; bus=yes with BRT/ترددي names", "bbox_s_w_n_e": [29.7, 30.4, 30.7, 32.0]}
                },
                "reported_contents": {
                    "place_neighbourhood_ways": 5569, "place_city_ways": 16, "place_town_ways": 11, "place_suburb_ways": 9,
                    "administrative_relations_by_level": {"4": 14, "7": 2, "8": 2, "9": 6, "10": 1},
                    "monorail_route_relations": 4, "railway_station_nodes": 175, "railway_stop_nodes": 276,
                    "public_transport_station_nodes": 85, "railway_halt_nodes": 19, "railway_construction_nodes": 16
                },
                "decoded_source_inventory": boundary_manifest.get("extract_inventory_actual", {}),
                "count_note": "reported_contents preserves the fetcher's supplied summary verbatim; decoded_source_inventory records independent counts over this stored 6,851-element file. Differences are retained transparently rather than silently reconciled."
            },
            "geometry_acceptance": {"general_road_bbox_wgs84": list(ROAD_BBOX),
                                     "entity_specific_road_bbox_overrides": report.get("road_bbox_overrides_by_entity", {}),
                                     "dropped_disconnected_fragments_under_m": 300,
                                     "regional_ring_west_patch": report.get("regional_ring_west_patch")}
        },
        "match_rules_file": "match-rules.json",
        "raw_extracts": raw_archives
    }
    (outdir / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"features_written": feature_count, "by_type": report["by_type"], "candidate_entities": report["candidate_entities"], "source_failures": failures}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    if failures:
        print("One or more sources were unavailable/rejected. No unsupported geometry was fabricated.", file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


===== FILE: scripts/migrate.ts =====
import { loadEnvConfig } from "@next/env";
import { runMigrations } from "../src/lib/db/migrate";
import { AppError } from "../src/lib/errors";
import { logger } from "../src/lib/logger";

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  loadEnvConfig(process.cwd());

  const args = new Set(process.argv.slice(2));
  const dryRun = args.has("--dry-run") || args.has("--check");

  const summary = await runMigrations({ dryRun });

  if (summary.dryRun) {
    console.info(
      `Dry-run complete: verified ${summary.discovered.length} migration file(s).`,
    );
    for (const migration of summary.discovered) {
      console.info(
        `  - ${migration.filename} (sha256:${migration.checksum.slice(0, 12)})`,
      );
    }
    return;
  }

  console.info(
    `Migrations complete: ${summary.appliedNow.length} applied, ${summary.alreadyApplied.length} already up to date.`,
  );
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  if (error instanceof AppError) {
    logger.error(error.message, {
      code: error.code,
      ...(error.issues ? { issues: error.issues } : {}),
    });
  } else {
    logger.error("Unhandled migration runner failure", { error });
  }
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: scripts/point_outside_boundary_report.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Report entities whose recorded point lies outside their own official boundary.

BATCH 12.5 — CITY-OBOUR's atlas point sits outside its CAPMAS polygon. This script reports every
such entity instead of moving anything: the point is what the source says, and silently relocating
it would fabricate a location. Output: data/features/point-outside-boundary-report.json

Requires Shapely.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections import Counter
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from shapely.geometry import Point, shape
from shapely.ops import unary_union

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
ENTITY_BOUNDARIES = ROOT / "data/features/codab-entity-boundaries.geojson"
OSM_BOUNDARIES = ROOT / "data/features/boundaries.geojson"
MAP_GEOMETRIES = ROOT / "data/derived/entity-map-geometries.json"
FEATURES = ROOT / "data/features/features.geojson"
REPORT = ROOT / "data/features/point-outside-boundary-report.json"

# Points are compared against a boundary that was rounded to 1e-5 degrees (~1 m) when it was imported,
# and against source coordinates that are often deliberately coarse. A point this close to the edge is
# recorded as "on_edge" rather than "outside", because calling it outside would overstate the finding.
EDGE_TOLERANCE_DEGREES = 1e-6

# Reported as data quality, not as a location claim: these coordinates are exact round numbers, which
# usually means a placeholder rather than a surveyed position.
ROUND_COORDINATE_TOLERANCE = 1e-7


def point_list(geometry: dict[str, Any] | None) -> list[tuple[float, float]]:
    if not geometry:
        return []
    kind = geometry.get("type")
    if kind == "Point":
        coordinates = geometry.get("coordinates")
        if isinstance(coordinates, list) and len(coordinates) >= 2:
            return [(float(coordinates[0]), float(coordinates[1]))]
    if kind == "MultiPoint":
        return [(float(item[0]), float(item[1])) for item in geometry.get("coordinates", []) if len(item) >= 2]
    return []


def is_round(coordinate: tuple[float, float]) -> bool:
    return all(abs(value - round(value, 3)) <= ROUND_COORDINATE_TOLERANCE for value in coordinate)


def main() -> int:
    boundary_source = json.loads(ENTITY_BOUNDARIES.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    map_geometries = json.loads(MAP_GEOMETRIES.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["geometries"]

    official: dict[str, list[Any]] = {}
    metadata: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for feature in boundary_source["features"]:
        properties = feature["properties"]
        code = properties["entity_code"]
        official.setdefault(code, []).append(shape(feature["geometry"]))
        metadata[code] = {
            "name_ar": properties.get("name_ar"),
            "name_en": properties.get("name_en"),
            "pcodes": properties.get("pcodes"),
            "evidence_code": properties.get("evidence_code"),
            "source": properties.get("source"),
            "license": properties.get("license"),
        }

    # A second, independent boundary source: OSM boundaries already paired to an entity.
    osm: dict[str, list[Any]] = {}
    if OSM_BOUNDARIES.exists():
        for feature in json.loads(OSM_BOUNDARIES.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["features"]:
            code = feature.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code")
            if code and feature.get("geometry"):
                osm.setdefault(code, []).append(shape(feature["geometry"]))

    # Fall back to the drawn point features for any boundary entity with no derived map point, so the
    # report covers every entity that has both a point and a boundary.
    feature_points: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]] = {}
    for feature in json.loads(FEATURES.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["features"]:
        geometry = feature.get("geometry")
        if geometry and geometry.get("type") == "Point":
            feature_points.setdefault(feature["properties"]["entity_code"], []).append(
                (float(geometry["coordinates"][0]), float(geometry["coordinates"][1]))
            )

    findings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    checked = 0
    for code in sorted(official):
        shapely_boundary = unary_union(official[code])
        points = point_list((map_geometries.get(code) or {}).get("geometry"))
        if not points:
            points = feature_points.get(code, [])
        if not points:
            continue
        checked += 1
        for coordinate in points:
            point = Point(coordinate)
            if shapely_boundary.buffer(EDGE_TOLERANCE_DEGREES).contains(point):
                continue
            state = "outside" if not shapely_boundary.buffer(1e-6).contains(point) else "on_edge"
            inside_osm = None
            if code in osm:
                inside_osm = any(candidate.buffer(EDGE_TOLERANCE_DEGREES).contains(point) for candidate in osm[code])
            findings.append({
                "entity_code": code,
                **metadata[code],
                "point": [coordinate[0], coordinate[1]],
                "state": state,
                "distance_outside_m": round(shapely_boundary.exterior.distance(point) * 111_320, 1)
                if shapely_boundary.geom_type == "Polygon" else None,
                "inside_osm_boundary": inside_osm,
                "round_coordinate": is_round(coordinate),
                "evidence_code": metadata[code]["evidence_code"],
            })

    by_entity: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    for finding in findings:
        by_entity.setdefault(finding["entity_code"], []).append(finding)

    report = {
        "generated_on": date.today().isoformat(),
        "description": (
            "Entities whose recorded point falls outside the official (CAPMAS COD-AB) boundary of the same "
            "entity. Points are NOT moved: each row records what the source says so a human can review it. "
            "A row with inside_osm_boundary=true means the point is consistent with the OSM boundary but not "
            "with the official one, i.e. a source disagreement rather than a bad coordinate."
        ),
        "method": {
            "boundary_source": "data/features/codab-entity-boundaries.geojson",
            "osm_boundary_source": "data/features/boundaries.geojson",
            "point_source": "data/derived/entity-map-geometries.json (falls back to data/features/features.geojson)",
            "edge_tolerance_degrees": EDGE_TOLERANCE_DEGREES,
            "point_in_polygon": "shapely contains() against the union of the entity's official units",
        },
        "license": "boundaries © CAPMAS via OCHA/HDX CC BY-IGO; points © OpenStreetMap contributors ODbL where OSM-derived",
        "counts": {
            "entities_with_official_boundary": len(official),
            "entities_checked": checked,
            "entities_with_findings": len(by_entity),
            "point_findings": len(findings),
            "round_coordinate_findings": sum(1 for row in findings if row["round_coordinate"]),
            "explained_by_osm_boundary": sum(1 for row in findings if row["inside_osm_boundary"] is True),
        },
        "findings": findings,
    }
    REPORT.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"report": str(REPORT.relative_to(ROOT)), **report["counts"]}, indent=2))
    for code, rows in sorted(by_entity.items()):
        print(f"  {code:28} {len(rows)} point(s) · {rows[0]['name_ar']}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


===== FILE: scripts/prepare-static-assets.mjs =====
import { copyFile, mkdir, stat } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
const publicDir = path.join(root, "public");
const fontDir = path.join(root, "src", "app", "fonts");

const assets = [
  ["node_modules/maplibre-gl/dist/maplibre-gl-worker.mjs", "public/maplibre-gl-worker.mjs"],
  ["node_modules/@fontsource/ibm-plex-sans-arabic/LICENSE", "public/IBM-Plex-OFL.txt"],
  ...[400, 500, 600, 700].flatMap((weight) => [
    [`node_modules/@fontsource/ibm-plex-sans-arabic/files/ibm-plex-sans-arabic-arabic-${weight}-normal.woff2`, `src/app/fonts/ibm-plex-sans-arabic-${weight}.woff2`],
    [`node_modules/@fontsource/ibm-plex-sans-arabic/files/ibm-plex-sans-arabic-latin-${weight}-normal.woff2`, `src/app/fonts/ibm-plex-sans-latin-${weight}.woff2`],
  ]),
  ...[400, 500].map((weight) => [
    `node_modules/@fontsource/ibm-plex-mono/files/ibm-plex-mono-latin-${weight}-normal.woff2`,
    `src/app/fonts/ibm-plex-mono-${weight}.woff2`,
  ]),
];

await mkdir(publicDir, { recursive: true });
await mkdir(fontDir, { recursive: true });

for (const [sourceRelative, destinationRelative] of assets) {
  const source = path.join(root, sourceRelative);
  const destination = path.join(root, destinationRelative);
  const sourceStat = await stat(source).catch(() => null);
  if (!sourceStat?.isFile() || sourceStat.size === 0) {
    throw new Error(`Required self-hosted asset is missing or empty: ${sourceRelative}`);
  }
  await copyFile(source, destination);
}

console.info(`Prepared ${assets.length} self-hosted map/font assets.`);


===== FILE: scripts/road_gap_fill.py =====
"""Stdlib-only OSM road topology and conservative gap-fill path search."""
from __future__ import annotations

import heapq
import math
import re
import unicodedata
from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Any

ROAD_HIGHWAYS = {
    "motorway", "trunk", "primary", "secondary",
    "motorway_link", "trunk_link", "primary_link", "secondary_link", "tertiary",
}
LINK_HIGHWAYS = {highway for highway in ROAD_HIGHWAYS if highway.endswith("_link")}
TOPOLOGY_GAP_THRESHOLD_KM = 0.025


def normalize(value: str | None) -> str:
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", value or "").casefold()
    text = "".join(char for char in text if unicodedata.category(char) not in {"Mn", "Cf"})
    text = text.translate(str.maketrans({"أ": "ا", "إ": "ا", "آ": "ا", "ٱ": "ا", "ى": "ي", "ئ": "ي", "ؤ": "و", "ة": "ه"}))
    tokens = re.findall(r"[\w]+", text, flags=re.UNICODE)
    # Drop common road-type prefixes/suffixes but retain substantive names.
    road_words = {"طريق", "شارع", "محور", "محورال", "كوبري", "جسر", "road", "street", "axis", "highway", "route"}
    while tokens and tokens[0] in road_words:
        tokens.pop(0)
    while tokens and tokens[-1] in road_words:
        tokens.pop()
    return "".join(tokens)


def way_labels(tags: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[set[str], set[str]]:
    names: set[str] = set()
    refs: set[str] = set()
    for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en", "official_name", "alt_name", "old_name"):
        value = tags.get(key)
        if isinstance(value, str):
            names.update(normalize(part.strip()) for part in value.split(";") if normalize(part.strip()))
    for key in ("ref", "old_ref"):
        value = tags.get(key)
        if isinstance(value, str):
            refs.update(normalize(part.strip()) for part in value.split(";") if normalize(part.strip()))
    return names, refs


def allowed_for_entity(tags: dict[str, Any], entity_names: set[str], entity_refs: set[str]) -> bool:
    """Allow only unnamed ways, matching aliases/names, or ways with the entity's exact ref."""
    names, refs = way_labels(tags)
    if not names and not refs:
        return True
    return bool(names & entity_names or refs & entity_refs)


def haversine_km(a: tuple[float, float] | list[float], b: tuple[float, float] | list[float]) -> float:
    lon1, lat1 = map(math.radians, a)
    lon2, lat2 = map(math.radians, b)
    dlon, dlat = lon2 - lon1, lat2 - lat1
    h = math.sin(dlat / 2) ** 2 + math.cos(lat1) * math.cos(lat2) * math.sin(dlon / 2) ** 2
    return 6371.0088 * 2 * math.asin(math.sqrt(h))


def build_road_graph(elements: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, Any]], dict[int, list[tuple[int, int, float]]]]:
    """Build a real-way graph from shared OSM node IDs and geodesic way-segment lengths."""
    ways: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for item in elements:
        if item.get("type") != "way" or item.get("tags", {}).get("highway") not in ROAD_HIGHWAYS:
            continue
        nodes, geometry = item.get("nodes"), item.get("geometry")
        if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not isinstance(geometry, list) or len(nodes) < 2 or len(nodes) != len(geometry):
            continue
        try:
            normalized_geometry = [(float(point["lon"]), float(point["lat"])) for point in geometry]
            normalized_nodes = [int(node) for node in nodes]
            version = int(item.get("version", 0) or 0)
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        if version < 1:
            continue
        way_id = int(item["id"])
        candidate = {**item, "nodes": normalized_nodes, "_coordinates": normalized_geometry}
        previous = ways.get(way_id)
        # Input extracts overlap. Prefer the newest version, then the most complete geometry.
        if previous is None or (version, len(normalized_nodes)) > (int(previous["version"]), len(previous["nodes"])):
            ways[way_id] = candidate

    adjacency: dict[int, list[tuple[int, int, float]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for way_id, way in ways.items():
        for index, (start, end) in enumerate(zip(way["nodes"], way["nodes"][1:])):
            if start == end:
                continue
            length = haversine_km(way["_coordinates"][index], way["_coordinates"][index + 1])
            adjacency[start].append((end, way_id, length))
            adjacency[end].append((start, way_id, length))
    return ways, dict(adjacency)


def filter_graph_for_entity(
    ways: dict[int, dict[str, Any]],
    adjacency: dict[int, list[tuple[int, int, float]]],
    entity_names: set[str],
    entity_refs: set[str],
    relation_member_way_ids: set[int] | None = None,
    allow_interchange_links: bool = False,
    allow_bridges: bool = False,
) -> dict[int, list[tuple[int, int, float]]]:
    """Filter per entity; optional real OSM *_link ways can connect that entity's components."""
    relation_member_way_ids = relation_member_way_ids or set()
    allowed_ids = {
        way_id for way_id, way in ways.items()
        if way_id in relation_member_way_ids
        or allowed_for_entity(way.get("tags", {}), entity_names, entity_refs)
        or (allow_interchange_links and way.get("tags", {}).get("highway") in LINK_HIGHWAYS)
        or (allow_bridges and way.get("tags", {}).get("bridge") == "yes")
    }
    return {
        node: [edge for edge in edges if edge[1] in allowed_ids]
        for node, edges in adjacency.items()
        if any(edge[1] in allowed_ids for edge in edges)
    }


def _components(seed_way_ids: set[int], ways: dict[int, dict[str, Any]]) -> list[set[int]]:
    parent: dict[int, int] = {}

    def find(value: int) -> int:
        parent.setdefault(value, value)
        if parent[value] != value:
            parent[value] = find(parent[value])
        return parent[value]

    def union(left: int, right: int) -> None:
        a, b = find(left), find(right)
        if a != b:
            parent[max(a, b)] = min(a, b)

    node_owner: dict[int, int] = {}
    valid_seeds = sorted(way_id for way_id in seed_way_ids if way_id in ways)
    for way_id in valid_seeds:
        find(way_id)
        for node in ways[way_id]["nodes"]:
            if node in node_owner:
                union(way_id, node_owner[node])
            else:
                node_owner[node] = way_id
    groups: dict[int, set[int]] = defaultdict(set)
    for way_id in valid_seeds:
        groups[find(way_id)].add(way_id)
    return [groups[key] for key in sorted(groups)]


def _component_nodes(component: set[int], ways: dict[int, dict[str, Any]]) -> set[int]:
    return {node for way_id in component for node in ways[way_id]["nodes"]}


def _component_endpoints(component: set[int], ways: dict[int, dict[str, Any]]) -> set[int]:
    """Return matched-way endpoints only; internal vertices are not gap endpoints."""
    endpoints: set[int] = set()
    for way_id in component:
        nodes = ways[way_id]["nodes"]
        if nodes:
            endpoints.update((nodes[0], nodes[-1]))
    return endpoints


def _node_coordinate(node_id: int, ways: dict[int, dict[str, Any]]) -> list[float] | None:
    for way in ways.values():
        for index, node in enumerate(way["nodes"]):
            if node == node_id:
                return list(way["_coordinates"][index])
    return None


def _shortest_path(
    adjacency: dict[int, list[tuple[int, int, float]]],
    start_nodes: set[int],
    target_component_by_node: dict[int, int],
    max_km: float,
) -> tuple[float, int, int, list[int]] | None:
    distances: dict[int, float] = {}
    parents: dict[int, tuple[int, int]] = {}
    queue: list[tuple[float, int]] = []
    for node in sorted(start_nodes):
        distances[node] = 0.0
        heapq.heappush(queue, (0.0, node))
    while queue:
        distance, node = heapq.heappop(queue)
        if distance != distances.get(node) or distance > max_km:
            continue
        if node in target_component_by_node:
            path_ways: list[int] = []
            current = node
            while current in parents:
                previous, way_id = parents[current]
                path_ways.append(way_id)
                current = previous
            path_ways.reverse()
            return distance, current, node, path_ways
        for neighbor, way_id, edge_km in adjacency.get(node, []):
            next_distance = distance + edge_km
            if next_distance > max_km or next_distance >= distances.get(neighbor, float("inf")):
                continue
            distances[neighbor] = next_distance
            parents[neighbor] = (node, way_id)
            heapq.heappush(queue, (next_distance, neighbor))
    return None


def connect_seed_components(
    *,
    entity_code: str,
    seed_way_ids: set[int],
    ways: dict[int, dict[str, Any]],
    adjacency: dict[int, list[tuple[int, int, float]]],
    max_km: float,
    topology_gap_threshold_km: float = TOPOLOGY_GAP_THRESHOLD_KM,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Connect source way components by shortest eligible OSM paths, never by synthetic edges."""
    components = _components(seed_way_ids, ways)
    initial_components = len(components)
    paths: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    filled: set[int] = set()
    node_coordinates = {
        node: coordinate
        for way in ways.values()
        for node, coordinate in zip(way["nodes"], way["_coordinates"])
    }
    if initial_components > 1 and max_km > 0:
        selected_seed = set(seed_way_ids)
        while len(components) > 1:
            best: tuple[float, int, int, list[int], int, int] | None = None
            component_node_sets = [_component_nodes(component, ways) for component in components]
            component_endpoint_sets = [_component_endpoints(component, ways) for component in components]
            for source_index, source_nodes in enumerate(component_node_sets):
                targets: dict[int, int] = {}
                for index, node_set in enumerate(component_node_sets):
                    if index == source_index:
                        continue
                    endpoint_distance = min((
                        haversine_km(node_coordinates[left], node_coordinates[right])
                        for left in component_endpoint_sets[source_index]
                        for right in component_endpoint_sets[index]
                        if left in node_coordinates and right in node_coordinates
                    ), default=float("inf"))
                    # A tiny endpoint mismatch is topology-only. Do not bridge it with a
                    # nearby real way; preserve the disconnected OSM node IDs for review.
                    if endpoint_distance <= topology_gap_threshold_km:
                        continue
                    targets.update((node, index) for node in node_set)
                if not source_nodes or not targets:
                    continue
                result = _shortest_path(adjacency, source_nodes, targets, max_km)
                if result is None:
                    continue
                distance, source_node, target_node, path_way_ids = result
                target_index = targets[target_node]
                candidate = (distance, source_index, target_index, path_way_ids, source_node, target_node)
                if best is None or candidate[:3] < best[:3]:
                    best = candidate
            if best is None:
                break
            distance, source_index, target_index, path_way_ids, source_node, target_node = best
            added_ids = sorted(set(path_way_ids) - selected_seed)
            selected_seed.update(path_way_ids)
            filled.update(added_ids)
            paths.append({
                "entity_code": entity_code,
                "from_node": source_node,
                "to_node": target_node,
                "from_coordinate": node_coordinates.get(source_node),
                "to_coordinate": node_coordinates.get(target_node),
                "path_length_km": round(distance, 3),
                "way_ids": sorted(set(path_way_ids)),
                "filled_way_ids": added_ids,
                "interchange_link_way_ids": sorted(
                    way_id for way_id in set(path_way_ids)
                    if ways.get(way_id, {}).get("tags", {}).get("highway") in LINK_HIGHWAYS
                ),
                "method": "gap_fill_graph",
            })
            components = _components(selected_seed, ways)

    unresolved: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    if len(components) > 1:
        component_endpoint_sets = [_component_endpoints(component, ways) for component in components]
        pair_nearest: dict[tuple[int, int], tuple[float, int, int]] = {}
        for left_index, left_nodes in enumerate(component_endpoint_sets):
            for right_index in range(left_index + 1, len(component_node_sets)):
                nearest: tuple[float, int, int] | None = None
                for left in sorted(left_nodes):
                    left_coord = node_coordinates.get(left)
                    if left_coord is None:
                        continue
                    for right in sorted(component_endpoint_sets[right_index]):
                        right_coord = node_coordinates.get(right)
                        if right_coord is None:
                            continue
                        candidate = (haversine_km(left_coord, right_coord), left, right)
                        if nearest is None or candidate < nearest:
                            nearest = candidate
                if nearest is not None:
                    pair_nearest[(left_index, right_index)] = nearest
        # A minimum spanning tree reports exactly components_after - 1 unresolved joins,
        # with endpoint coordinates and no invented geometry.
        connected_components = {0}
        while len(connected_components) < len(components):
            best: tuple[float, int, int, int, int] | None = None
            for (left_index, right_index), (distance, left, right) in pair_nearest.items():
                if (left_index in connected_components) == (right_index in connected_components):
                    continue
                candidate = (distance, left_index, right_index, left, right)
                if best is None or candidate < best:
                    best = candidate
            if best is None:
                break
            distance, left_index, right_index, left, right = best
            connected_components.update((left_index, right_index))
            gap_type = "osm_topology_gap" if distance <= topology_gap_threshold_km else "network_gap"
            unresolved.append({
                "entity_code": entity_code,
                "gap_type": gap_type,
                "component_a": left_index,
                "component_b": right_index,
                "endpoint_a": {"node_id": left, "coordinates": node_coordinates.get(left)},
                "endpoint_b": {"node_id": right, "coordinates": node_coordinates.get(right)},
                "straight_line_km": round(distance, 3),
                "max_graph_path_km": max_km,
                "reason": "osm_topology_gap" if gap_type == "osm_topology_gap" else "no eligible real-way graph path within configured maximum distance",
            })

    return {
        "entity_code": entity_code,
        "components_before": initial_components,
        "gaps_before": max(0, initial_components - 1),
        "components_after": len(components),
        "gaps_after": max(0, len(components) - 1),
        "gaps_closed": max(0, initial_components - len(components)),
        "km_closed": round(sum(item["path_length_km"] for item in paths), 3),
        "filled_way_ids": sorted(filled),
        "paths": paths,
        "unresolved_gaps": unresolved,
    }


===== FILE: scripts/south_batch04_osm_candidates.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Audit historical local OSM name candidates without changing the supplied query.

Candidates are NOT accepted matches: their semantics, geometry and location need review.
The supplied Overpass response and query are deliberately not touched.
"""
import csv
import gzip
import json
from pathlib import Path

from road_gap_fill import normalize, way_labels

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BBOX = (31.15, 29.75, 31.45, 30.08)  # west, south, east, north


def normalized(value):
    # Reuse road normalization, extending it to the project prefix.
    value = value.strip()
    if value.startswith('مشروع '):
        value = value[len('مشروع '):]
    return normalize(value)


def bbox_status(element):
    points = []
    if 'lon' in element and 'lat' in element:
        points.append((element['lon'], element['lat']))
    for p in element.get('geometry', []):
        if isinstance(p, dict) and 'lon' in p and 'lat' in p:
            points.append((p['lon'], p['lat']))
    for member in element.get('members', []):
        for p in member.get('geometry', []):
            if isinstance(p, dict) and 'lon' in p and 'lat' in p:
                points.append((p['lon'], p['lat']))
    if not points:
        return 'unknown'
    w, s, e, n = BBOX
    return 'inside' if all(w <= x <= e and s <= y <= n for x, y in points) else 'outside_or_crosses'


def main():
    with (ROOT / 'data/research/south-batch04/entities.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig', newline='') as f:
        entities = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    paths = sorted(path for path in (ROOT / 'data/raw').glob('*json.gz')
                   if not path.name.startswith('cairo-osm-south-batch04-'))
    candidates = {row['code']: {} for row in entities}
    for path in paths:
        with gzip.open(path, 'rt', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            document = json.load(f)
        for element in document.get('elements', []):
            tags = element.get('tags', {})
            labels, _ = way_labels(tags)
            # way_labels uses the same road normalization; also handle project prefix.
            for key in ('name', 'name:ar', 'name:en', 'alt_name', 'official_name', 'old_name'):
                for part in str(tags.get(key, '')).split(';'):
                    if normalized(part):
                        labels.add(normalized(part))
            if not labels:
                continue
            for row in entities:
                names = [row['name_ar'], row['name_en'], *row['aliases'].split('|')]
                overlap = sorted(labels & {normalized(name) for name in names if normalized(name)})
                if not overlap:
                    continue
                ident = f"{element['type']}/{element['id']}"
                entry = candidates[row['code']].setdefault(ident, {'osm_id': ident, 'tags': tags, 'bbox': bbox_status(element), 'sources': [], 'matched_names': overlap})
                if path.name not in entry['sources']:
                    entry['sources'].append(path.name)
                if entry['bbox'] == 'unknown':
                    entry['bbox'] = bbox_status(element)
    report = {'scope': 'local extracts only; preliminary candidates, not accepted geometry', 'bbox_wgs84': list(BBOX), 'entities': [
        {'code': row['code'], 'candidates': list(candidates[row['code']].values())} for row in entities
    ]}
    out = ROOT / 'data/research/south-batch04/local_geometry_candidates.json'
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
    print(f"Audited {len(paths)} files, {len(entities)} entities; {sum(bool(x) for x in candidates.values())} have name candidates. No geometry accepted.")


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


===== FILE: scripts/station_fact_geometry.py =====
"""Create station points from the exact OSM node IDs recorded in canonical seed facts."""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Any

from station_matching import (
    _has_explicit_station_mode, _is_construction, _is_station_node, _station_source_quality,
    entity_line_scope, line_mode, station_modes,
)

BBOX = (30.5, 29.2, 32.1, 30.8)
OSM_LICENSE = "ODbL-1.0"
OSM_ATTRIBUTION = "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0"


def match_station_fact_nodes(
    osm_data: dict[str, Any],
    entities: list[dict[str, str]],
    node_ids_by_entity: dict[str, set[int]],
    line_memberships_by_entity: dict[str, set[str]],
    retrieval_date: str,
    station_groups: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, Any]]:
    """Use fact-linked node IDs as exact identity; never substitute a similar name's geometry.

    When a grouped station extract is available, one representative (the group's primary
    ``osm_node_id``) produces a point for that physical station position. All group IDs must
    already be in the seed facts. Fact-linked rail stations not present in the group report
    receive one deterministic exact-node point as a fallback.
    """
    by_code = {entity["code"]: entity for entity in entities}
    refs: dict[int, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    for code, node_ids in node_ids_by_entity.items():
        if code not in by_code or by_code[code].get("entity_type") not in {"transit_stop", "rail_station"}:
            continue
        for node_id in node_ids:
            refs[int(node_id)].add(code)

    elements_by_node: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for element in osm_data.get("elements", []):
        if element.get("type") != "node" or element.get("id") is None:
            continue
        node_id = int(element["id"])
        previous = elements_by_node.get(node_id)
        if previous is None or int(element.get("version", 0) or 0) >= int(previous.get("version", 0) or 0):
            elements_by_node[node_id] = element

    requests: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    requested_groups: set[tuple[str, int]] = set()
    ambiguous_ids: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for group in station_groups or []:
        try:
            node_id = int(group["osm_node_id"])
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        code_options = sorted(refs.get(node_id, set()))
        if len(code_options) != 1:
            if len(code_options) > 1:
                ambiguous_ids.append({"osm_node_id": node_id, "entity_codes": code_options,
                                     "reason": "one exact node ID is assigned to multiple seed station entities"})
            continue
        code = code_options[0]
        requested_groups.add((code, node_id))
        requests.append({"entity_code": code, "osm_node_id": node_id,
                         "line_codes": sorted(set(group.get("line_codes", []))),
                         "group_id": group.get("feature_id") or f"OSM-NODE-{node_id}",
                         "request_kind": "station_group_primary"})

    # ENR route=train stations and other fact-backed stations outside the five-line station
    # group report still receive a direct point; choose the best exact source node deterministically.
    fallback_codes: list[str] = []
    for code in sorted(node_ids_by_entity):
        if code not in by_code or by_code[code].get("entity_type") not in {"transit_stop", "rail_station"}:
            continue
        if any(request["entity_code"] == code for request in requests):
            continue
        fallback_codes.append(code)
        candidates = [elements_by_node[node_id] for node_id in node_ids_by_entity[code] if node_id in elements_by_node]
        if candidates:
            candidates.sort(key=lambda item: (
                _station_source_quality(item.get("tags", {}) or {}),
                int(item.get("version", 0) or 0),
                -int(item.get("id", 0) or 0),
            ), reverse=True)
            node_id = int(candidates[0]["id"])
            requests.append({"entity_code": code, "osm_node_id": node_id,
                             "line_codes": sorted(line_memberships_by_entity.get(code, set())),
                             "group_id": f"OSM-NODE-{node_id}",
                             "request_kind": "fact_node_fallback"})

    # IDs which have no primary station-group mapping and no fallback are reported, not guessed.
    features: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    matched: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    rejected: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    missing_nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    request_seen: set[tuple[str, int]] = set()

    for request in requests:
        code = request["entity_code"]
        node_id = int(request["osm_node_id"])
        key = (code, node_id)
        if key in request_seen:
            continue
        request_seen.add(key)
        entity = by_code[code]
        element = elements_by_node.get(node_id)
        if element is None:
            missing_nodes.append({**request, "reason": "fact-linked node is absent from the stored OSM extracts"})
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": "fact-linked node is absent from the stored OSM extracts"})
            continue
        tags = element.get("tags", {}) or {}
        status = entity.get("status", "unknown")
        allowed_modes, expected_lines = entity_line_scope(entity)
        source_modes = station_modes(tags, set())
        explicit_mode = _has_explicit_station_mode(tags, set())
        seed_lines = set(line_memberships_by_entity.get(code, set()))
        request_lines = set(request.get("line_codes", []))
        if seed_lines and request_lines and not seed_lines.intersection(request_lines):
            reason = "stored station-group line conflicts with the entity's line_membership facts"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        constrained_lines = request_lines or seed_lines or (expected_lines or set())
        allowed_modes = set(allowed_modes) | {mode for line in constrained_lines if (mode := line_mode(line)) is not None}
        if not allowed_modes:
            reason = "seed entity and exact station line facts provide no supported transport mode"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        if expected_lines and constrained_lines and not expected_lines.intersection(constrained_lines):
            reason = "stored line membership conflicts with the entity code's existing station-line guard"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        if explicit_mode and source_modes and not source_modes.intersection(allowed_modes):
            reason = f"exact OSM node mode {sorted(source_modes)} conflicts with seeded mode scope {sorted(allowed_modes)}"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        line_modes = {line_mode(line) for line in constrained_lines}
        line_modes.discard(None)
        if explicit_mode and source_modes and line_modes and not source_modes.intersection(line_modes):
            reason = f"exact OSM node mode {sorted(source_modes)} conflicts with line membership modes {sorted(line_modes)}"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        construction = _is_construction(tags)
        if status in {"operational", "unknown"} and construction:
            reason = "OSM marks this exact node as construction while the seed entity is operational/unknown"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        if status in {"under_construction", "planned", "approved_planned"}:
            if not construction:
                reason = "seed lifecycle is planned/under-construction but this exact OSM node has no construction tag"
                rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
                continue
            reason = "planned/construction feature requires the existing lifecycle alignment checks; no exact-node exception applied"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        if tags.get("railway") in {"disused", "abandoned", "razed"} or any(tags.get(key) for key in ("disused:railway", "abandoned:railway")):
            reason = "OSM marks this exact node as disused/abandoned"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        if not _is_station_node(element):
            reason = "fact-linked OSM object is not a tagged station/platform/stop node"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        lon, lat = element.get("lon"), element.get("lat")
        version = int(element.get("version", 0) or 0)
        if not isinstance(lon, (int, float)) or not isinstance(lat, (int, float)) or not (
            BBOX[0] <= float(lon) <= BBOX[2] and BBOX[1] <= float(lat) <= BBOX[3]
        ):
            reason = "exact OSM node lacks a valid point inside the station study bbox"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        if version < 1:
            reason = "exact OSM station node has no positive version"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue
        if code not in refs.get(node_id, set()):
            # Defensive assertion; grouped reports may select only fact-linked representative IDs.
            reason = "selected station-group node ID is not attached to this entity by an osm_node_id fact"
            rejected[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})
            continue

        line_codes = request.get("line_codes") or sorted(line_memberships_by_entity.get(code, set()))
        feature_code = f"OSM-NODE-{node_id}"
        extra = {
            "entity_type": entity["entity_type"],
            "osm_tags": tags,
            "match_method": "exact osm_node_id fact; no name-based substitution",
            "matched_line_codes": line_codes,
            "station_node_source_quality": _station_source_quality(tags),
            "source_station_modes": sorted({
                mode for mode, keys in (("subway", ("subway",)), ("light_rail", ("light_rail",)),
                                        ("monorail", ("monorail",)), ("train", ("train",)))
                if any(tags.get(key) == "yes" for key in keys)
            }),
        }
        item = {
            "type": "Feature", "id": feature_code,
            "geometry": {"type": "Point", "coordinates": [float(lon), float(lat)]},
            "properties": {
                "code": feature_code, "entity_code": code, "evidence_code": None,
                "srid": 4326, "license": OSM_LICENSE,
                "osm_type": "node", "osm_id": node_id, "osm_version": version,
                "properties": {
                    "source": "OpenStreetMap",
                    "source_ids": {"osm_type": "node", "osm_id": node_id, "osm_version": version},
                    "retrieval_date": retrieval_date, "attribution": OSM_ATTRIBUTION,
                    **extra,
                },
            },
        }
        features.append(item)
        matched[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "osm_version": version, "group_id": request["group_id"],
                              "line_codes": line_codes, "coordinates": [float(lon), float(lat)],
                              "request_kind": request["request_kind"]})

    unrepresented_ids = []
    for code, node_ids in node_ids_by_entity.items():
        selected = {record["osm_id"] for record in matched.get(code, [])}
        for node_id in sorted(node_ids - selected):
            if (code, node_id) not in request_seen:
                unrepresented_ids.append({"entity_code": code, "osm_node_id": node_id,
                                          "reason": "not selected as a station-group primary node; retained as an alternate node fact"})

    by_line: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for request in requests:
        for line_code in request.get("line_codes", []):
            summary = by_line.setdefault(line_code, {"station_group_count": 0, "matched_group_count": 0,
                                                      "matched_entity_codes": [], "matched_feature_count": 0,
                                                      "rejected_groups": []})
            summary["station_group_count"] += 1
            code = request["entity_code"]
            if any(value["group_id"] == request["group_id"] for value in matched.get(code, [])):
                summary["matched_group_count"] += 1
                summary["matched_entity_codes"].append(code)
                summary["matched_feature_count"] += 1
            else:
                summary["rejected_groups"].append({"entity_code": code, "osm_node_id": request["osm_node_id"]})
    for summary in by_line.values():
        summary["matched_entity_codes"] = sorted(set(summary["matched_entity_codes"]))
        summary["matched_entity_count"] = len(summary["matched_entity_codes"])

    def known_station_mode(code: str) -> str | None:
        memberships = line_memberships_by_entity.get(code, set())
        keys = ("METRO", "LRT", "MONORAIL-EAST", "MONORAIL-WEST", "BRT", "HSR", "ENR", "SUEZ-TRAIN")
        for key in keys:
            if any(key in line for line in memberships):
                return key.casefold().replace("-", "_")
        if code == "STOP-CENTRAL-CAPITAL":
            return "lrt_hsr"
        if code.startswith(("STOP-M1-", "STOP-M2-", "STOP-M3-", "STOP-METRO-")):
            return "metro"
        if code.startswith("STOP-LRT-"):
            return "lrt"
        if code.startswith("STOP-MRE-") or "MONORAIL-MOSHIR" in code:
            return "monorail_east"
        if code.startswith("STOP-MRW-"):
            return "monorail_west"
        if code.startswith("STOP-BRT-"):
            return "brt"
        if code.startswith("STOP-HSR-"):
            return "hsr"
        if code.startswith("RAIL-"):
            return "enr"
        return None

    mode_undetermined = sorted(
        code for code, entity in by_code.items()
        if entity.get("entity_type") in {"transit_stop", "rail_station"}
        and known_station_mode(code) is None
    )
    audit = {
        "method": "exact osm_node_id fact to exact stored OSM node; group primary IDs from the stored station report; no fuzzy fallback", 
        "stations_with_undetermined_mode": mode_undetermined,
        "source_station_entities_with_node_facts": len(node_ids_by_entity),
        "source_node_ids_in_facts": sum(len(ids) for ids in node_ids_by_entity.values()),
        "station_groups_requested": len(requests),
        "station_groups_matched": sum(len(items) for items in matched.values()),
        "station_entities_with_geometry": len(matched),
        "matched_features": len(features),
        "lifecycle_or_geometry_rejections": {code: values for code, values in sorted(rejected.items())},
        "missing_source_nodes": missing_nodes,
        "unrepresented_alternate_node_ids": unrepresented_ids,
        "ambiguous_node_ids": ambiguous_ids,
        "station_entities": {code: {"matched_osm_nodes": values,
                                      "matched_osm_node_ids": [item["osm_id"] for item in values],
                                      "status": by_code[code].get("status")}
                             for code, values in sorted(matched.items())},
        "by_line": by_line,
    }
    return features, audit


===== FILE: scripts/station_matching.py =====
"""Conservative OSM station-node matching against seeded transit-stop entities."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import re
import unicodedata
from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Any

from shapely import transform as shapely_transform
from shapely.geometry import Point, shape


SUPPORTED_MODES = {"subway", "light_rail", "monorail", "train", "bus"}
DESCRIPTOR_TOKENS = {
    "محطه", "مترو", "مونوريل", "station", "stations", "metro", "lrt", "monorail",
    "light", "rail", "line", "interchange", "stop", "brt", "ترددي", "السريع",
}
ARABIC_TRANSLATION = str.maketrans({"أ": "ا", "إ": "ا", "آ": "ا", "ة": "ه", "ى": "ي"})


def normalize_station_name(value: str | None) -> str:
    """Normalize Arabic letter variants and remove generic station/mode descriptors."""
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", value or "").translate(ARABIC_TRANSLATION).casefold()
    text = "".join(char for char in text if not unicodedata.combining(char) and char != "ـ")
    tokens = re.findall(r"[\w]+", text, flags=re.UNICODE)
    return "".join(token for token in tokens if token not in DESCRIPTOR_TOKENS)


def _split_labels(value: Any) -> list[str]:
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        return []
    return [label.strip() for label in re.split(r"[;|/\n]+", value) if label.strip()]


def _labels_for_entity(entity: dict[str, str]) -> set[str]:
    values = [entity.get("name_ar", ""), entity.get("name_en", "")]
    values.extend(_split_labels(entity.get("aliases", "")))
    return {key for value in values if (key := normalize_station_name(value))}


def _osm_labels(tags: dict[str, Any]) -> set[str]:
    values: list[str] = []
    for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en", "alt_name", "old_name"):
        values.extend(_split_labels(tags.get(key)))
    return {normalized for value in values if (normalized := normalize_station_name(value))}


def _name_match_score(tags: dict[str, Any], entity: dict[str, str]) -> int:
    canonical = {normalize_station_name(entity.get(key)) for key in ("name_ar", "name_en")}
    canonical.discard("")
    aliases = {normalize_station_name(value) for value in _split_labels(entity.get("aliases", ""))}
    aliases.discard("")
    primary = {normalize_station_name(value) for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en")
               for value in _split_labels(tags.get(key))}
    primary.discard("")
    secondary = {normalize_station_name(value) for key in ("alt_name", "old_name")
                 for value in _split_labels(tags.get(key))}
    secondary.discard("")
    if primary & canonical:
        return 3
    if primary & aliases:
        return 2
    return 1 if (primary | secondary) & (canonical | aliases) else 0


def station_modes(tags: dict[str, Any], relation_modes: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    """Read explicit station/rail tags first; use route relation membership only as fallback."""
    modes: set[str] = set()
    station = str(tags.get("station", "")).casefold()
    railway = str(tags.get("railway", "")).casefold()
    if station == "subway" or tags.get("subway") == "yes" or railway == "subway":
        modes.add("subway")
    if station in {"light_rail", "light rail"} or tags.get("light_rail") == "yes" or railway == "light_rail":
        modes.add("light_rail")
    if station == "monorail" or tags.get("monorail") == "yes" or railway == "monorail":
        modes.add("monorail")
    if station == "train":
        modes.add("train")
    if station in {"bus", "bus_station"} or tags.get("bus") == "yes":
        modes.add("bus")
    if modes:
        return modes
    if relation_modes & SUPPORTED_MODES:
        return relation_modes & SUPPORTED_MODES
    if railway in {"station", "halt"}:
        return {"train"}
    return set()


def _has_explicit_station_mode(tags: dict[str, Any], relation_modes: set[str]) -> bool:
    station = str(tags.get("station", "")).casefold()
    railway = str(tags.get("railway", "")).casefold()
    return (bool(relation_modes & SUPPORTED_MODES) or station in SUPPORTED_MODES or
            railway in {"subway", "light_rail", "monorail"} or
            any(tags.get(key) == "yes" for key in ("subway", "light_rail", "monorail", "bus")))


def entity_line_scope(entity: dict[str, str]) -> tuple[set[str], set[str] | None]:
    """Return allowed transport modes and known matched-line constraints for an entity."""
    code = entity["code"]
    prefix_to_line = {
        "STOP-M1-": ("subway", "TRANSIT-METRO-L1"),
        "STOP-M2-": ("subway", "TRANSIT-METRO-L2"),
        "STOP-M3-": ("subway", "TRANSIT-METRO-L3"),
        "STOP-LRT-": ("light_rail", "TRANSIT-LRT"),
        "STOP-MRE-": ("monorail", "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST"),
        "STOP-MONORAIL-": ("monorail", "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST"),
        "STOP-MRW-": ("monorail", "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-WEST"),
        "STOP-HSR-": ("train", None),
    }
    for prefix, (mode, line) in prefix_to_line.items():
        if code.startswith(prefix):
            if line:
                return {mode}, {line}
            line_id = "TRANSIT-HSR-L1" if "SPHINX" in code else "TRANSIT-HSR-L2" if "GIZA" in code else "TRANSIT-HSR-L3"
            return {mode}, {line_id}
    if code == "STOP-SADAT":
        return {"subway"}, {"TRANSIT-METRO-L1", "TRANSIT-METRO-L2"}
    if code == "STOP-KIT-KAT":
        return {"subway"}, {"TRANSIT-METRO-L3"}
    if code == "STOP-DEMERDASH":
        return {"subway"}, {"TRANSIT-METRO-L1"}
    if code in {"STOP-ABBASSIA", "STOP-CAIRO-STADIUM"}:
        return {"subway"}, {"TRANSIT-METRO-L3"}
    if code == "STOP-ADLY-MANSOUR":
        return {"subway", "light_rail", "monorail"}, {"TRANSIT-METRO-L3", "TRANSIT-LRT", "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST"}
    if code == "STOP-ARTS-CULTURE-CITY":
        return {"light_rail", "monorail"}, {"TRANSIT-LRT", "TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST"}
    if code.startswith("STOP-BRT-"):
        return {"bus"}, {"TRANSIT-BRT"}
    if code.startswith("RAIL-"):
        return {"train"}, None
    return SUPPORTED_MODES, None


def line_mode(line_code: str) -> str | None:
    if line_code.startswith("TRANSIT-METRO-"):
        return "subway"
    if line_code == "TRANSIT-LRT":
        return "light_rail"
    if line_code.startswith("TRANSIT-MONORAIL-"):
        return "monorail"
    if line_code == "TRANSIT-BRT":
        return "bus"
    if line_code.startswith(("TRANSIT-HSR-", "TRANSIT-SUEZ-TRAIN")):
        return "train"
    return None


def _point_line_distance_m(lon: float, lat: float, geometry) -> float:
    """Project a local point/line neighborhood to meters for the 600 m alignment check."""
    cos_lat = max(0.01, abs(math.cos(math.radians(lat))))
    projected = shapely_transform(geometry, lambda coords: __import__("numpy").column_stack(
        ((coords[:, 0] - lon) * 111320.0 * cos_lat, (coords[:, 1] - lat) * 110574.0)))
    return Point(0.0, 0.0).distance(projected)


def _station_source_quality(tags: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
    if tags.get("public_transport") == "station" or tags.get("railway") == "station":
        return 3
    if tags.get("railway") == "halt" or tags.get("public_transport") == "stop_position":
        return 2
    return 1


def _is_construction(tags: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return str(tags.get("construction", "")).casefold() in {"yes", "station", "railway"} or \
        str(tags.get("railway", "")).casefold() == "construction"


def _is_station_node(element: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    if element.get("type") != "node":
        return False
    tags = element.get("tags")
    return isinstance(tags, dict) and (tags.get("public_transport") in {"station", "stop_position", "platform"} or
        tags.get("railway") in {"station", "halt", "stop", "construction"} or tags.get("bus") == "yes")


def match_osm_station_nodes(
    osm_data: dict[str, Any],
    entities: list[dict[str, str]],
    line_features: list[dict[str, Any]],
    retrieval_date: str,
    match_rules: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, Any]]:
    """Match versioned OSM station nodes to seeded entities without inferring ambiguous links."""
    station_entities = [e for e in entities if e.get("entity_type") in {"transit_stop", "rail_station"}
                        and e.get("code", "").startswith(("STOP-", "RAIL-"))]
    name_index: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    by_code = {e["code"]: e for e in station_entities}
    station_entity_rules = (match_rules or {}).get("station_entity_rules", {})
    for entity in station_entities:
        names = _labels_for_entity(entity)
        for alias in station_entity_rules.get(entity["code"], {}).get("match_aliases", []):
            normalized = normalize_station_name(alias)
            if normalized:
                names.add(normalized)
        for name in names:
            name_index[name].add(entity["code"])

    partial_source_line_codes = {
        str(feature.get("properties", {}).get("entity_code"))
        for feature in line_features
        if feature.get("properties", {}).get("properties", {}).get("geometry_completeness") == "partial_source_geometry"
    }
    lines: dict[str, list[Any]] = defaultdict(list)
    relation_line_codes: dict[int, str] = {}
    for feature in line_features:
        properties = feature.get("properties", {})
        metadata = properties.get("properties", {})
        line_code = properties.get("entity_code")
        if metadata.get("entity_type") != "transit_line" or not isinstance(line_code, str):
            continue
        # A partial source alignment (e.g. the mapped-only OSM BRT busway) cannot prove a station is
        # off-line: it must not be used to reject a station that is otherwise name/mode matched.
