        if metadata.get("geometry_completeness") == "partial_source_geometry":
            continue
        try:
            lines[line_code].append(shape(feature["geometry"]))
        except Exception:
            continue
        if properties.get("osm_type") == "relation" and isinstance(properties.get("osm_id"), int):
            relation_line_codes[properties["osm_id"]] = line_code
    line_entities = {entity["code"]: entity for entity in entities if entity.get("entity_type") == "transit_line"}
    planned_line_codes = {code for code, entity in line_entities.items()
                          if entity.get("status") in {"planned", "approved_planned"}}
    line_unions = {}
    for code, geometries in lines.items():
        try:
            from shapely.ops import unary_union
            line_unions[code] = unary_union(geometries)
        except Exception:
            continue

    relation_modes_by_node: dict[int, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    relation_lines_by_node: dict[int, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    relation_lineage: dict[int, list[int]] = defaultdict(list)
    for relation in osm_data.get("elements", []):
        if relation.get("type") != "relation":
            continue
        mode = str(relation.get("tags", {}).get("route", "")).casefold()
        if mode not in SUPPORTED_MODES:
            continue
        line_code = relation_line_codes.get(int(relation.get("id", 0) or 0))
        for member in relation.get("members", []):
            if member.get("type") != "node" or not isinstance(member.get("ref"), int):
                continue
            node_id = member["ref"]
            relation_modes_by_node[node_id].add(mode)
            if line_code:
                relation_lines_by_node[node_id].add(line_code)
                relation_lineage[node_id].append(int(relation["id"]))

    candidates_by_entity: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    rejections_by_entity: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    output: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    node_audit: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    matching_node_ids: set[int] = set()
    ambiguous_node_ids: set[int] = set()
    rejected_node_ids: set[int] = set()
    examined = 0

    for element in osm_data.get("elements", []):
        if not _is_station_node(element):
            continue
        examined += 1
        tags = element.get("tags", {})
        normalized_labels = _osm_labels(tags)
        name_candidates = set().union(*(name_index.get(label, set()) for label in normalized_labels)) if normalized_labels else set()
        if not name_candidates:
            continue
        node_id = int(element.get("id", 0) or 0)
        relation_modes = relation_modes_by_node.get(node_id, set())
        modes = station_modes(tags, relation_modes)
        construction = _is_construction(tags)
        explicit_mode = _has_explicit_station_mode(tags, relation_modes)
        generic_station = not explicit_mode and _is_station_node(element)
        rejected: dict[str, str] = {}
        qualified: list[str] = []
        qualified_line_codes: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        qualified_modes: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
        coords = [element.get("lon"), element.get("lat")]
        point_valid = all(isinstance(value, (int, float)) for value in coords)
        for code in sorted(name_candidates):
            entity = by_code[code]
            allowed_modes, expected_lines = entity_line_scope(entity)
            if not allowed_modes:
                rejected[code] = "seed line is outside the supported subway/light_rail/monorail/train node scope"
                continue
            if entity.get("status") in {"operational", "unknown"}:
                if construction:
                    rejected[code] = "OSM marks station as construction but seed status is operational/unknown"
                    continue
            elif entity.get("status") in {"under_construction", "planned", "approved_planned"}:
                if not construction:
                    rejected[code] = "planned/construction entity has no explicit OSM construction tag"
                    continue
            else:
                rejected[code] = f"unsupported station lifecycle status: {entity.get('status')}"
                continue
            if not point_valid or not (30.5 <= float(coords[0]) <= 32.1 and 29.2 <= float(coords[1]) <= 30.8):
                rejected[code] = "station node lacks a valid WGS84 point inside the station study bbox"
                continue
            member_lines = relation_lines_by_node.get(node_id, set())
            if expected_lines is not None and member_lines and not member_lines.intersection(expected_lines):
                rejected[code] = "OSM route-relation membership identifies a different line"
                continue
            if expected_lines is not None:
                candidate_lines = [line for line in expected_lines if line_mode(line) is None or line_mode(line) in allowed_modes]
            else:
                candidate_lines = [line for line in line_unions if line_mode(line) in allowed_modes]
            if explicit_mode and modes:
                candidate_lines = [line for line in candidate_lines if line_mode(line) in modes]
            candidate_lines = [line for line in candidate_lines if line not in planned_line_codes]
            check_lines = [line for line in candidate_lines if line in line_unions]
            distances = {line: _point_line_distance_m(float(coords[0]), float(coords[1]), line_unions[line])
                         for line in check_lines}
            near_lines = [line for line, distance in distances.items() if distance <= 600.0]
            # Stations on a partial source alignment inherit that line's mode but never a line-distance verdict.
            partial_candidate_lines = [line for line in candidate_lines if line in partial_source_line_codes]
            inferred_modes = {line_mode(line) for line in (near_lines or partial_candidate_lines)
                              if line_mode(line) in allowed_modes}
            mode_fit = (bool(inferred_modes) or allowed_modes == {"train"}) if generic_station else bool(modes & allowed_modes)
            if not mode_fit:
                rejected[code] = "OSM station type does not fit the seeded line; no compatible matched-line geometry supports the generic railway-station tags"
                continue
            if check_lines and not near_lines and not (set(candidate_lines) & partial_source_line_codes):
                nearest_distance = min(distances.values())
                rejected[code] = f"station is {nearest_distance:.1f} m from the matched line geometry (limit 600 m)"
                continue
            if entity.get("status") in {"under_construction", "planned", "approved_planned"} and not near_lines:
                rejected[code] = "construction station is not on an available matched official line alignment within 600 m"
                continue
            effective_modes = inferred_modes if generic_station and inferred_modes else modes & allowed_modes
            matched_lines = sorted(near_lines or (member_lines & set(candidate_lines)) or candidate_lines)
            qualified.append(code)
            qualified_line_codes[code] = matched_lines
            qualified_modes[code] = {mode for mode in effective_modes if mode}
        for rejected_code, reason in rejected.items():
            rejections_by_entity[rejected_code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": reason})

        if len(qualified) == 1:
            code = qualified[0]
            entity = by_code[code]
            version = int(element.get("version", 0) or 0)
            if version < 1:
                rejected[code] = "OSM station node has no positive version"
                rejections_by_entity[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "reason": rejected[code]})
                rejected_node_ids.add(node_id)
                node_audit.append({"osm_id": node_id, "outcome": "unmatched", "candidate_entity_codes": [code],
                                   "source_labels": sorted(normalized_labels), "station_modes": sorted(modes),
                                   "reason": rejected[code]})
                continue
            matched_lines = qualified_line_codes.get(code, [])
            matched_modes = qualified_modes.get(code, set())
            code_feature = f"OSM-NODE-{node_id}"
            configured_aliases = station_entity_rules.get(code, {}).get("match_aliases", [])
            aliases_used = sorted(alias for alias in configured_aliases
                                  if normalize_station_name(alias) in normalized_labels)
            extra = {
                "entity_type": entity["entity_type"], "osm_tags": tags,
                "match_method": "exact normalized seed/configured station name + compatible OSM mode/route membership + matched-line proximity",
                "configured_name_aliases_used": aliases_used,
                "matched_line_codes": matched_lines,
                "station_node_source_quality": _station_source_quality(tags),
                "route_relation_ids": sorted(set(relation_lineage.get(node_id, []))),
                "source_station_modes": sorted(modes), "matched_line_modes": sorted(matched_modes),
            }
            output.append({
                "type": "Feature", "id": code_feature,
                "geometry": {"type": "Point", "coordinates": [float(coords[0]), float(coords[1])]},
                "properties": {
                    "code": code_feature, "entity_code": code, "evidence_code": None, "srid": 4326,
                    "license": "ODbL-1.0", "osm_type": "node", "osm_id": node_id, "osm_version": version,
                    "properties": {
                        "source": "OpenStreetMap",
                        "source_ids": {"osm_type": "node", "osm_id": node_id, "osm_version": version},
                        "retrieval_date": retrieval_date, "attribution": "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0",
                        **extra,
                    },
                },
            })
            candidates_by_entity[code].append({"osm_id": node_id, "version": version, "labels": sorted(normalized_labels),
                                                "line_codes": matched_lines, "coordinates": [float(coords[0]), float(coords[1])],
                                                "name_match_score": _name_match_score(tags, entity),
                                                "source_quality": _station_source_quality(tags), "construction": construction})
            matching_node_ids.add(node_id)
            node_audit.append({"osm_id": node_id, "outcome": "matched", "candidate_entity_codes": [code],
                               "source_labels": sorted(normalized_labels), "station_modes": sorted(modes),
                               "matched_line_codes": matched_lines})
        elif len(qualified) > 1:
            ambiguous_node_ids.add(node_id)
            for code in qualified:
                rejections_by_entity[code].append({"osm_id": node_id,
                    "reason": "source node remains ambiguous across multiple seeded station entity codes after filters"})
            node_audit.append({"osm_id": node_id, "outcome": "ambiguous", "candidate_entity_codes": qualified,
                               "source_labels": sorted(normalized_labels), "station_modes": sorted(modes),
                               "reason": "multiple seeded station entities remain compatible after name, mode, lifecycle and line checks"})
        else:
            rejected_node_ids.add(node_id)
            node_audit.append({"osm_id": node_id, "outcome": "unmatched", "candidate_entity_codes": sorted(name_candidates),
                               "source_labels": sorted(normalized_labels), "station_modes": sorted(modes),
                               "rejections": rejected})

    # One entity can have multiple same-named OSM objects on different lines/at distant locations.
    # Keep the higher-confidence primary-name match; if equally strong candidates are separated by
    # more than one station-scale radius, report ambiguity instead of choosing an arbitrary node.
    selected_node_ids: set[int] = set()
    entity_conflicts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    audit_by_node = {item["osm_id"]: item for item in node_audit}
    for code, values in candidates_by_entity.items():
        highest_score = max(value["name_match_score"] for value in values)
        top_values = [value for value in values if value["name_match_score"] == highest_score]
        lower_values = [value for value in values if value["name_match_score"] < highest_score]
        for value in lower_values:
            node_id = value["osm_id"]
            rejected_node_ids.add(node_id)
            rejections_by_entity[code].append({"osm_id": node_id,
                "reason": "a stronger OSM primary-name match to the seeded canonical name resolves this alias-only candidate"})
            record = audit_by_node.get(node_id)
            if record:
                record.update({"outcome": "unmatched", "reason": "lower-confidence alias candidate; a stronger canonical-name match exists"})
        higher_quality_values = [value for value in top_values if value["source_quality"] == 3]
        lower_quality_values = [value for value in top_values if value["source_quality"] < 3]
        duplicate_values = []
        for value in lower_quality_values:
            lon1, lat1 = value["coordinates"]
            for primary in higher_quality_values:
                lon2, lat2 = primary["coordinates"]
                dlat, dlon = math.radians(lat2-lat1), math.radians(lon2-lon1)
                h = math.sin(dlat/2)**2 + math.cos(math.radians(lat1))*math.cos(math.radians(lat2))*math.sin(dlon/2)**2
                if 2 * 6371008.8 * math.asin(math.sqrt(h)) <= 150:
                    duplicate_values.append(value)
                    break
        for value in duplicate_values:
            node_id = value["osm_id"]
            rejected_node_ids.add(node_id)
            rejections_by_entity[code].append({"osm_id": node_id,
                "reason": "lower-quality stop-position/platform is within 150 m of the primary station node; treated as duplicate geometry"})
            record = audit_by_node.get(node_id)
            if record:
                record.update({"outcome": "unmatched", "reason": "nearby lower-quality stop-position/platform duplicate"})
        duplicate_ids = {value["osm_id"] for value in duplicate_values}
        top_values = [value for value in top_values if value["osm_id"] not in duplicate_ids]
        distant_pairs = []
        for index, left in enumerate(top_values):
            for right in top_values[index + 1:]:
                lon1, lat1 = left["coordinates"]
                lon2, lat2 = right["coordinates"]
                dlat, dlon = math.radians(lat2-lat1), math.radians(lon2-lon1)
                h = math.sin(dlat/2)**2 + math.cos(math.radians(lat1))*math.cos(math.radians(lat2))*math.sin(dlon/2)**2
                distance_m = 2 * 6371008.8 * math.asin(math.sqrt(h))
                if distance_m > 600:
                    distant_pairs.append({"osm_ids": [left["osm_id"], right["osm_id"]],
                                          "distance_m": round(distance_m, 1),
                                          "line_codes": [left["line_codes"], right["line_codes"]]})
        if distant_pairs:
            entity_conflicts[code] = {"candidate_osm_node_ids": sorted(value["osm_id"] for value in top_values),
                                      "distant_pairs": distant_pairs,
                                      "reason": "equally strong station-name matches are more than 600 m apart; no geometry selected"}
            for value in top_values:
                node_id = value["osm_id"]
                ambiguous_node_ids.add(node_id)
                record = audit_by_node.get(node_id)
                if record:
                    record.update({"outcome": "ambiguous", "reason": entity_conflicts[code]["reason"]})
            rejections_by_entity[code].append(entity_conflicts[code])
        else:
            selected_node_ids.update(value["osm_id"] for value in top_values)

    output = [feature for feature in output if feature.get("properties", {}).get("osm_id") in selected_node_ids]
    matching_node_ids = set(selected_node_ids)

    per_entity: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for code, entity in by_code.items():
        values = [value for value in candidates_by_entity.get(code, []) if value["osm_id"] in selected_node_ids]
        line_scope = entity_line_scope(entity)[1]
        matched_line_codes = sorted({line for value in values for line in value["line_codes"]})
        per_entity[code] = {
            "entity_type": entity["entity_type"], "status": entity.get("status"),
            "station_line": sorted(line_scope) if line_scope is not None else None,
            "matched_line_codes": matched_line_codes,
            "matched_osm_node_ids": sorted(x["osm_id"] for x in values),
            "matched_count": len(values), "outcome": "matched" if values else "unmatched",
            "rejected_candidates": rejections_by_entity.get(code, []) or ([] if values else [{
                "reason": "no exact normalized name/alias match in the supplied OSM station nodes, or no source point passed the line/lifecycle guards"
            }]),
        }
    per_line: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for code, entity in by_code.items():
        line_scope = entity_line_scope(entity)[1]
        keys = per_entity[code]["matched_line_codes"] if per_entity[code]["matched_count"] else (sorted(line_scope) if line_scope else [])
        if not keys:
            keys = ["UNSUPPORTED-LINE" if entity_line_scope(entity)[0] == set() else
                    "RAIL-TRAIN" if code.startswith("RAIL-") else "MODE-RESOLVED"]
        for key in keys:
            summary = per_line.setdefault(key, {"station_entities": 0, "matched_entities": 0, "matched_entity_codes": [],
                                                 "unmatched_entities": [], "matched_osm_nodes": 0,
                                                 "station_feature_count_before": 0, "station_feature_count_after": 0})
            summary["station_entities"] += 1
            if per_entity[code]["matched_count"]:
                summary["matched_entities"] += 1
                summary["matched_entity_codes"].append(code)
                summary["matched_osm_nodes"] += per_entity[code]["matched_count"]
            else:
                summary["unmatched_entities"].append(code)

    brt_seed_codes = sorted(code for code in by_code if code.startswith("STOP-BRT-"))
    brt_source_nodes = []
    for element in osm_data.get("elements", []):
        tags = element.get("tags", {})
        if element.get("type") != "node" or tags.get("bus") != "yes":
            continue
        raw_names = " ".join(str(tags.get(key, "")) for key in ("name", "name:ar", "name:en", "alt_name"))
        if "brt" in raw_names.casefold() or "ترددي" in raw_names:
            brt_source_nodes.append({"osm_id": int(element.get("id", 0) or 0), "name": tags.get("name"),
                                    "name_ar": tags.get("name:ar"), "name_en": tags.get("name:en"),
                                    "public_transport": tags.get("public_transport")})
    brt_matched = [code for code in brt_seed_codes if per_entity.get(code, {}).get("matched_count", 0)]
    audit = {
        "source_station_nodes_examined": examined,
        "source_nodes_with_seed_name_candidates": len(node_audit),
        "matched_source_nodes": len(matching_node_ids),
        "ambiguous_source_nodes": len(ambiguous_node_ids),
        "rejected_source_nodes": len(rejected_node_ids),
        "entity_level_ambiguous_entities": len(entity_conflicts),
        "entity_level_conflicts": entity_conflicts,
        "brt_round_two": {"seed_brt_station_entities": len(brt_seed_codes), "matched_brt_station_entities": len(brt_matched),
                          "matched_entity_codes": brt_matched,
                          "unmatched_entity_codes": sorted(set(brt_seed_codes) - set(brt_matched)),
                          "source_named_bus_yes_brt_nodes": brt_source_nodes},
        "station_entities": per_entity,
        "by_line": per_line,
        "source_node_audit": node_audit,
        "attribution": "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0",
        "license": "ODbL-1.0",
    }
    return output, audit


===== FILE: scripts/test_road_gap_fill.py =====
import unittest

from scripts.road_gap_fill import (
    build_road_graph,
    connect_seed_components,
    filter_graph_for_entity,
    normalize,
)


def way(way_id, nodes, longitudes, tags=None, version=1):
    return {
        "type": "way",
        "id": way_id,
        "version": version,
        "nodes": nodes,
        "geometry": [{"lon": lon, "lat": 30.0} for lon in longitudes],
        "tags": {"highway": "primary", **(tags or {})},
    }


class RoadGapFillTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.elements = [
            way(101, [1, 2], [31.000, 31.001], {"name": "Ring Road", "ref": "R1"}),
            way(102, [4, 5], [31.019, 31.020], {"name:en": "Ring Road", "ref": "R1"}),
            way(103, [2, 3, 4], [31.001, 31.010, 31.019]),
        ]
        self.ways, self.adjacency = build_road_graph(self.elements)

    def test_gap_fill_uses_only_real_osm_ways_and_records_provenance(self):
        graph = filter_graph_for_entity(
            self.ways, self.adjacency, {normalize("Ring Road")}, {normalize("R1")}
        )
        result = connect_seed_components(
            entity_code="ROAD-RING-ROAD", seed_way_ids={101, 102}, ways=self.ways,
            adjacency=graph, max_km=3.0,
        )
        self.assertEqual(result["gaps_before"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(result["gaps_after"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(result["filled_way_ids"], [103])
        self.assertEqual(result["paths"][0]["way_ids"], [103])
        self.assertEqual(result["paths"][0]["method"], "gap_fill_graph")
        self.assertEqual(len(result["paths"][0]["from_coordinate"]), 2)
        self.assertGreater(result["km_closed"], 1.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(result["km_closed"], 3.0)

    def test_maximum_path_length_is_enforced(self):
        graph = filter_graph_for_entity(
            self.ways, self.adjacency, {normalize("Ring Road")}, {normalize("R1")}
        )
        result = connect_seed_components(
            entity_code="ROAD-RING-ROAD", seed_way_ids={101, 102}, ways=self.ways,
            adjacency=graph, max_km=1.0,
        )
        self.assertEqual(result["gaps_after"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(result["filled_way_ids"], [])
        self.assertEqual(result["km_closed"], 0.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(result["unresolved_gaps"]), result["gaps_after"])
        self.assertIn("coordinates", result["unresolved_gaps"][0]["endpoint_a"])

    def test_named_other_road_is_never_used_as_a_connector(self):
        self.elements[2]["tags"]["name"] = "Different Expressway"
        self.ways, self.adjacency = build_road_graph(self.elements)
        graph = filter_graph_for_entity(
            self.ways, self.adjacency, {normalize("Ring Road")}, {normalize("R1")}
        )
        result = connect_seed_components(
            entity_code="ROAD-RING-ROAD", seed_way_ids={101, 102}, ways=self.ways,
            adjacency=graph, max_km=3.0,
        )
        self.assertEqual(result["gaps_after"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(result["filled_way_ids"], [])

    def test_matching_ref_permits_the_real_intermediate_way(self):
        self.elements[2]["tags"]["ref"] = "R1"
        self.elements[2]["tags"]["name"] = "Different Local Label"
        self.ways, self.adjacency = build_road_graph(self.elements)
        graph = filter_graph_for_entity(
            self.ways, self.adjacency, {normalize("Ring Road")}, {normalize("R1")}
        )
        result = connect_seed_components(
            entity_code="ROAD-RING-ROAD", seed_way_ids={101, 102}, ways=self.ways,
            adjacency=graph, max_km=3.0,
        )
        self.assertEqual(result["filled_way_ids"], [103])

    def test_secondary_link_can_connect_same_entity_components_when_enabled(self):
        self.elements[2]["tags"].update({"highway": "secondary_link", "name": "Interchange ramp"})
        self.ways, self.adjacency = build_road_graph(self.elements)
        graph = filter_graph_for_entity(
            self.ways, self.adjacency, {normalize("Ring Road")}, {normalize("R1")},
            allow_interchange_links=True,
        )
        result = connect_seed_components(
            entity_code="ROAD-RING-ROAD", seed_way_ids={101, 102}, ways=self.ways,
            adjacency=graph, max_km=3.0,
        )
        self.assertEqual(result["filled_way_ids"], [103])
        self.assertEqual(result["paths"][0]["interchange_link_way_ids"], [103])

    def test_secondary_way_is_supported_and_short_disconnected_endpoints_are_topology_gaps(self):
        self.elements = [
            way(201, [11, 12], [31.0000, 31.0010], {"name": "July Street", "highway": "secondary"}),
            way(202, [13, 14], [31.0011, 31.0020], {"name": "July Street", "highway": "secondary"}),
            way(203, [12, 13], [31.0010, 31.0011], {"highway": "secondary"}),
        ]
        self.ways, self.adjacency = build_road_graph(self.elements)
        graph = filter_graph_for_entity(
            self.ways, self.adjacency, {normalize("July Street")}, set()
        )
        result = connect_seed_components(
            entity_code="ROAD-26-JULY", seed_way_ids={201, 202}, ways=self.ways,
            adjacency=graph, max_km=3.0,
        )
        self.assertEqual(result["gaps_after"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(result["unresolved_gaps"][0]["gap_type"], "osm_topology_gap")
        self.assertEqual(result["unresolved_gaps"][0]["reason"], "osm_topology_gap")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


===== FILE: scripts/vercel-build.ts =====
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import path from "node:path";
import { executeVercelBuild } from "../src/lib/db/vercel-build";
import { runAutomaticAtlasImport } from "../src/lib/db/automatic-import";
import { redactSecrets } from "../src/lib/errors";

function runNextBuild(): void {
  const nextBin = path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    "node_modules",
    ".bin",
    process.platform === "win32" ? "next.cmd" : "next",
  );

  const result = spawnSync(nextBin, ["build"], {
    stdio: "inherit",
    env: process.env,
  });

  if (result.error) {
    throw result.error;
  }

  if (result.status !== 0) {
    process.exit(result.status ?? 1);
  }
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  await executeVercelBuild({
    env: process.env,
    runNextBuildFn: runNextBuild,
    runProductionImportFn: () => runAutomaticAtlasImport(),
    runPreviewValidationFn: () => runAutomaticAtlasImport(process.cwd(), { dryRun: true }),
  });
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  const message =
    error instanceof Error ? redactSecrets(error.message) : redactSecrets(String(error));
  console.error(`Build failed: ${message}`);
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: scripts/verify-static-assets.mjs =====
import { stat } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
const requiredAssets = [
  ".next/BUILD_ID",
  "public/maplibre-gl-worker.mjs",
  "public/favicon.ico",
  "public/icon.svg",
];

for (const relativePath of requiredAssets) {
  const filePath = path.join(root, relativePath);
  const info = await stat(filePath).catch(() => null);
  if (!info?.isFile() || info.size === 0) {
    throw new Error(`Post-build asset verification failed: ${relativePath} is missing or empty.`);
  }
}

console.info("Verified production build and required worker/favicon assets.");

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

/** BATCH 13 — GET /api/admin/audit?entity=&fact=&limit= — the append-only trail, newest first. */
export async function GET(request: Request) {

    resolveAdminIdentity(request);
    const params = new URL(request.url).searchParams;

    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }

===== FILE: src/app/api/admin/dashboard/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { resolveAdminIdentity } from "@/lib/admin/auth";
import { getAdminCounts } from "@/lib/admin/queries";
import { getAreaBoundaryCodes } from "@/lib/atlas/area-boundaries";
import { toApiErrorResponse } from "@/lib/errors";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — GET /api/admin/dashboard
 *
 * Boundaries are not a table: the count comes from the same boundary files the public map reads, so
 * the dashboard and the map can never disagree about how many official boundaries exist.
 */
export async function GET(request: Request) {
  try {
    resolveAdminIdentity(request);
    const counts = await getAdminCounts();
    const boundaryCodes = await getAreaBoundaryCodes();
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, counts: { ...counts, boundaries: boundaryCodes.size } },
      { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0" } });
  } catch (error) {
    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}


===== FILE: src/app/api/admin/entities/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { resolveAdminIdentity } from "@/lib/admin/auth";
import { getEntityFilterOptions, listEntities, listFactsForReview } from "@/lib/admin/queries";
import { getAreaBoundaryCodes } from "@/lib/atlas/area-boundaries";
import { toApiErrorResponse } from "@/lib/errors";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — GET /api/admin/entities?q=&type=&sector=&status=&geometry=&boundary=&page=&page_size=
 * and GET /api/admin/entities?facts=1&state=pending for the review queue.
 *
 * Read-only, but still behind admin auth: the queue exposes unpublished research state.
 */
export async function GET(request: Request) {
  try {
    resolveAdminIdentity(request);
    const params = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
    if (params.get("facts") === "1") {
      const page = Number.parseInt(params.get("page") ?? "1", 10);
      const pageSize = Number.parseInt(params.get("page_size") ?? "25", 10);
      const result = await listFactsForReview({
        state: params.get("state") ?? "pending",
        entity_code: params.get("entity_code") ?? undefined,
        q: params.get("q") ?? undefined,
        page: Number.isFinite(page) ? page : 1,
        page_size: Number.isFinite(pageSize) ? pageSize : 25,
      });
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, ...result }, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0" } });
    }

    if (params.get("options") === "1") {
      const options = await getEntityFilterOptions();
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, ...options }, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0" } });
    }

    const booleanParam = (value: string | null): boolean | undefined => (value === "true" ? true : value === "false" ? false : undefined);
    const page = Number.parseInt(params.get("page") ?? "1", 10);
    const pageSize = Number.parseInt(params.get("page_size") ?? "25", 10);
    const result = await listEntities({
      q: params.get("q") ?? undefined,
      entity_type: params.get("type") ?? undefined,
      sector: params.get("sector") ?? undefined,
      status: params.get("status") ?? undefined,
      has_geometry: booleanParam(params.get("geometry")),
      has_boundary: booleanParam(params.get("boundary")),
      page: Number.isFinite(page) ? page : 1,
      page_size: Number.isFinite(pageSize) ? pageSize : 25,
    }, );

    // Boundaries come from the boundary GeoJSON files, not a table, so they are joined here rather
    // than fabricated in SQL.
    const boundaryParam = booleanParam(params.get("boundary"));
    let rows = result.rows;
    let total = result.total;
    if (boundaryParam !== undefined) {
      const boundaryCodes = await getAreaBoundaryCodes();
      rows = rows.map((row) => ({ ...row, has_boundary: boundaryCodes.has(row.code) })).filter((row) => row.has_boundary === boundaryParam);
      if (total === rows.length) total = rows.length;
    } else {
      const boundaryCodes = await getAreaBoundaryCodes();
      rows = rows.map((row) => ({ ...row, has_boundary: boundaryCodes.has(row.code) }));
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, rows, total, page: result.page, page_size: result.page_size }, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0" } });
  } catch (error) {
    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}


===== FILE: src/app/api/admin/gaps/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { resolveAdminIdentity } from "@/lib/admin/auth";
import { getAdminCounts } from "@/lib/admin/queries";
import { toApiErrorResponse } from "@/lib/errors";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — GET /api/admin/gaps
 *
 * Every count here is measured. When a measurement is unavailable it is `null` and the UI shows «—»;
 * the endpoint never substitutes a zero it did not count.
 */
export async function GET(request: Request) {
  try {
    resolveAdminIdentity(request);
    const counts = await getAdminCounts();
    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,
      groups: [
        { kind: "open_conflicts", severity: "high", label_ar: "تعارضات مفتوحة", label_en: "Open conflicts", count: counts.open_conflicts,
          note_ar: "كيان واحد وسمة واحدة بقيمتين مختلفتين", note_en: "One entity, one attribute, two different values" },
        { kind: "single_source", severity: "medium", label_ar: "حقائق بمصدر واحد", label_en: "Facts with a single source", count: counts.facts_single_source,
          note_ar: "لا يمكن اعتبارها موثّقة بمصدر واحد", note_en: "A single source cannot carry a verified claim" },
        { kind: "without_evidence", severity: "high", label_ar: "حقائق بلا دليل", label_en: "Facts without evidence", count: counts.facts_without_evidence,
          note_ar: "أُدخلت دون رابط مصدر", note_en: "Entered with no source link" },
        { kind: "unknown_status", severity: "medium", label_ar: "حالات غير معروفة", label_en: "Unknown statuses", count: counts.unknown_statuses,
          note_ar: "لم يُتحقق من كونها قائمة أو قيد الإنشاء", note_en: "Not confirmed as open or under construction" },
        { kind: "without_geometry", severity: "medium", label_ar: "كيانات بلا هندسة", label_en: "Entities without geometry", count: counts.entities_without_geometry,
          note_ar: "لا تظهر على الخريطة", note_en: "They cannot appear on the map" },
        { kind: "unreviewed", severity: "low", label_ar: "حقائق بلا مراجعة", label_en: "Unreviewed facts", count: counts.unreviewed_facts,
          note_ar: "لم يطّلع عليها مراجع بعد", note_en: "No reviewer has looked at them yet" },
      ],
    }, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0" } });
  } catch (error) {
    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}


===== FILE: src/app/api/admin/geometry/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { resolveAdminIdentity } from "@/lib/admin/auth";
import { getSql } from "@/lib/db/client";
import { getPointOutsideBoundaryFindings } from "@/lib/atlas/area-boundaries";
import { toApiErrorResponse } from "@/lib/errors";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

interface GeometryRow {
  entity_code: string; name_ar: string; name_en: string; entity_type: string;
  feature_count: number; sources: string[]; licenses: string[];
  has_conflict: boolean; conflict_sources: string[]; point_outside_boundary: boolean;
}

/**
 * BATCH 13 — GET /api/admin/geometry?filter=missing|conflict|outside_boundary|all
 *
 * Sources and licences come from the stored feature rows; the "two sources disagree" flag comes from
 * the same comparison the public researcher view uses, so the admin screen cannot invent a conflict the
 * atlas does not show.
 */
export async function GET(request: Request) {
  try {
    resolveAdminIdentity(request);
    const filter = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("filter") ?? "missing";
    const sql = getSql();

    const entityRows = await sql.query(
      `SELECT e.code AS entity_code, e.name_ar, e.name_en, e.entity_type,
              COUNT(f.id)::int AS feature_count,
              COALESCE(array_agg(DISTINCT f.osm_type) FILTER (WHERE f.osm_type IS NOT NULL), '{}') AS osm_types,
              COALESCE(array_agg(DISTINCT f.license), '{}') AS licenses
         FROM entities e
         LEFT JOIN features f ON f.entity_id = e.id
        GROUP BY e.code, e.name_ar, e.name_en, e.entity_type
        ORDER BY e.code`) as Array<{
          entity_code: string; name_ar: string; name_en: string; entity_type: string;
          feature_count: number; osm_types: string[]; licenses: string[];
        }>;

    // Conflict detection reuses the boundary file comparison: an entity with both an official CAPMAS
    // boundary and an OSM boundary is drawn from two sources.
    const official = new Set<string>();
    const osm = new Set<string>();
    const readCodes = async (file: string): Promise<string[]> => {
      try {
        const data = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(process.cwd(), "data/features", file), "utf8")) as {
          features?: Array<{ properties?: { entity_code?: string } }>;
        };
        return (data.features ?? []).flatMap((feature) => feature.properties?.entity_code ? [feature.properties.entity_code] : []);
      } catch { return []; }
    };
    for (const code of await readCodes("codab-entity-boundaries.geojson")) official.add(code);
    for (const code of await readCodes("boundaries.geojson")) osm.add(code);
    const outside = await getPointOutsideBoundaryFindings();

    const rows: GeometryRow[] = entityRows.map((row) => {
      const conflictSources: string[] = [];
      if (official.has(row.entity_code)) conflictSources.push("CAPMAS COD-AB");
      if (osm.has(row.entity_code)) conflictSources.push("OpenStreetMap");
      return {
        entity_code: row.entity_code,
        name_ar: row.name_ar,
        name_en: row.name_en,
        entity_type: row.entity_type,
        feature_count: Number(row.feature_count) || 0,
        sources: [
          ...(Number(row.feature_count) > 0 ? ["OpenStreetMap"] : []),
          ...(official.has(row.entity_code) ? ["CAPMAS COD-AB"] : []),
        ],
        licenses: row.licenses ?? [],
        has_conflict: conflictSources.length > 1,
        conflict_sources: conflictSources.length > 1 ? conflictSources : [],
        point_outside_boundary: outside.has(row.entity_code),
      };
    });

    const filtered = filter === "missing" ? rows.filter((row) => row.feature_count === 0)
      : filter === "conflict" ? rows.filter((row) => row.has_conflict)
        : filter === "outside_boundary" ? rows.filter((row) => row.point_outside_boundary)
          : rows;

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, rows: filtered }, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0" } });
  } catch (error) {
    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

===== FILE: src/app/api/admin/import/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";
import { enforceAdminWriteRateLimit, resolveAdminIdentity } from "@/lib/admin/auth";
import { csvToImportPayload, planImport, type ExistingState } from "@/lib/admin/import-pipeline";
import { commitImportPlan } from "@/lib/admin/import-commit";
import { defaultExecutor } from "@/lib/admin/queries";
import { getSql } from "@/lib/db/client";
import { RequestValidationError, toApiErrorResponse } from "@/lib/errors";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const requestSchema = z.strictObject({
  /** Always processed as a dry run unless this is explicitly `"commit"`. */
  mode: z.enum(["dry_run", "commit"]).default("dry_run"),
  format: z.enum(["json", "csv"]).default("json"),
  /** For CSV: which of the three seed sections the file contains. */
  section: z.enum(["entities", "facts", "evidence"]).optional(),
  /** SHA-256 of the payload returned by a previous dry run. Required to commit. */
  preview_checksum: z.string().regex(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/u).optional(),
  payload: z.string().min(1).max(5_000_000),
});

/**
 * BATCH 13 — POST /api/admin/import
 *
 * Dry run by default. A commit requires the checksum of the plan that was previewed, so the person
 * approving is approving exactly those bytes rather than a recomputation.
 *
 * Committing inserts only rows the plan marked actionable, only as candidates, and only when the
 * evidence they cite already exists in the database or arrives in the same payload.
 */
export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const identity = resolveAdminIdentity(request);
    enforceAdminWriteRateLimit(identity);
    const parsed = requestSchema.safeParse(await request.json());
    if (!parsed.success) {
      throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid import request.", parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
        path: issue.path.join(".") || "body", message: issue.message,
      })));
    }
    const { mode, format, section, payload, preview_checksum: previewChecksum } = parsed.data;

    let payloadText = payload;
    if (format === "csv") {
      if (!section) throw new RequestValidationError("A CSV import must declare its section.", [{ path: "section", message: "Provide entities, facts or evidence." }]);
      const converted = csvToImportPayload(payload, section);
      if (converted.error) throw new RequestValidationError(converted.error, [{ path: "payload", message: converted.error }]);
      payloadText = converted.payload;
    }

    const sql = getSql();
    const existing = await loadExistingState(sql);
    const plan = planImport(payloadText, existing);

    if (mode === "dry_run") {
      // Record the preview so a later commit can be checked against it.
      await sql.query(
        `INSERT INTO admin_import_previews (created_by, source_filename, source_format, payload_checksum, summary, diff)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5::jsonb, $6::jsonb)`,
        [identity.actor, `upload.${format}`, format, plan.checksum,
          JSON.stringify({ ok: plan.ok, counts: plan.counts, duplicates: plan.duplicates.length, rejected: plan.rejected_rows.length }),
          JSON.stringify(plan.diff.slice(0, 500))],
      );
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, mode, plan }, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0" } });
    }

    if (previewChecksum !== plan.checksum) {
      throw new RequestValidationError("This payload is not the one that was previewed.", [{
        path: "preview_checksum",
        message: "Run the dry run again and pass the checksum it returned.",
      }]);
    }
    if (!plan.ok) {
      throw new RequestValidationError("The payload does not validate, so it was not committed.", plan.issues.slice(0, 20).map((issue) => ({
        path: `${issue.section}[${issue.index}].${issue.path}`, message: issue.message,
      })));
    }

    const committed = await commitImportPlan(defaultExecutor, plan);

    await sql.query(
      `INSERT INTO admin_audit_log (actor, action, reason, before_state, after_state, request_fingerprint)
       VALUES ($1, 'import.commit', $2, $3::jsonb, $4::jsonb, $5)`,
      [identity.actor, `commit of preview ${plan.checksum.slice(0, 12)}`,
        JSON.stringify({ preview_checksum: plan.checksum }),
        JSON.stringify({ inserted: committed }),
        identity.token_fingerprint]);

    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, mode, committed }, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0" } });
  } catch (error) {
    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

async function loadExistingState(sql: ReturnType<typeof getSql>): Promise<ExistingState> {
  const entities = await sql.query(
    `SELECT code, name_ar, name_en, aliases FROM entities ORDER BY code`) as Array<{ code: string; name_ar: string; name_en: string; aliases: string[] | null }>;
  const facts = await sql.query(`SELECT code, value FROM facts`) as Array<{ code: string; value: string }>;
  const evidence = await sql.query(`SELECT code, url, source_type FROM evidence`) as Array<{ code: string; url: string | null; source_type: string }>;
  const attributes = await sql.query(
    `SELECT e.code AS entity_code, f.attribute, f.value FROM facts f JOIN entities e ON e.id = f.entity_id`) as Array<{ entity_code: string; attribute: string; value: string }>;

  const attributesByEntity = new Map<string, Map<string, string>>();
  for (const row of attributes) {
    const map = attributesByEntity.get(row.entity_code) ?? new Map<string, string>();
    if (!map.has(row.attribute)) map.set(row.attribute, row.value);
    attributesByEntity.set(row.entity_code, map);
  }

  return {
    entity_codes: new Set(entities.map((row) => row.code)),
    fact_codes: new Set(facts.map((row) => row.code)),
    evidence_codes: new Set(evidence.map((row) => row.code)),
    entities: entities.map((row) => ({ code: row.code, name_ar: row.name_ar, name_en: row.name_en, aliases: row.aliases ?? [] })),
    fact_values: new Map(facts.map((row) => [row.code, row.value])),
    evidence_urls: new Map(evidence.map((row) => [row.code, row.url])),
    evidence_source_types: new Map(evidence.map((row) => [row.code, row.source_type])),
    fact_attributes_by_entity: attributesByEntity,
  };
}


===== FILE: src/app/api/admin/review/[factCode]/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { enforceAdminWriteRateLimit, resolveAdminIdentity } from "@/lib/admin/auth";
import { applyFactReview, attachEvidence, liveExecutor } from "@/lib/admin/mutations";
import { toApiErrorResponse } from "@/lib/errors";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — POST /api/admin/review/:factCode
 *
 * Body: `{ action: "approve" | "reject" | "dispute" | "reset", reason?, expected_state? }`
 * or   `{ attach: { stance, evidence_code, reason } }` to link evidence to the fact.
 *
 * Auth is enforced before the body is read; the token never leaves the server. Every successful call
 * writes an audit row (see lib/admin/mutations.ts).
 */
export async function POST(request: Request, context: { params: Promise<{ factCode: string }> }) {
  try {
    const identity = resolveAdminIdentity(request);
    enforceAdminWriteRateLimit(identity);
    const { factCode } = await context.params;
    const code = decodeURIComponent(factCode);
    const body = await request.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
    const context2 = { actor: identity.actor, token_fingerprint: identity.token_fingerprint };
    const result = typeof body.attach === "object" && body.attach !== null
      ? await attachEvidence(liveExecutor(), context2, code, body.attach)
      : await applyFactReview(liveExecutor(), context2, code, body);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, result }, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0" } });
  } catch (error) {
    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

===== FILE: src/app/api/atlas/entities/[code]/boundary/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getAreaBoundary } from "@/lib/atlas/area-boundaries";
import { atlasCodeSchema } from "@/lib/atlas/schema";
import { RequestValidationError, toApiErrorResponse, type ApiErrorBody } from "@/lib/errors";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(
  _request: Request,
  context: { params: Promise<{ code: string }> },
): Promise<NextResponse | NextResponse<ApiErrorBody>> {
  try {
    const { code: rawCode } = await context.params;
    const parsed = atlasCodeSchema.safeParse(rawCode);
    if (!parsed.success) {
      throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid entity code.", parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
        path: "code", message: issue.message,
      })));
    }
    const feature = await getAreaBoundary(parsed.data);
    if (!feature) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: {
        code: "BOUNDARY_NOT_AVAILABLE", message: "No source boundary is available for this entity.",
      } }, { status: 404, headers: { "Cache-Control": "public, s-maxage=60, stale-while-revalidate=300" } });
    }
    const attribution = feature.properties.properties.attribution;
    const headerAttribution = feature.properties.license === "CC BY-IGO"
      ? "(c) CAPMAS via OCHA/HDX, CC BY-IGO"
      : "(c) OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0";
    return NextResponse.json({ type: "FeatureCollection", metadata: {
      geometry_license: feature.properties.license,
      attribution,
    }, features: [feature] }, {
      headers: {
        "Cache-Control": "public, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400",
        "X-Atlas-Geometry-Attribution": headerAttribution,
        "Content-Type": "application/geo+json; charset=utf-8",
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}


===== FILE: src/app/api/atlas/entities/[code]/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getAtlasEntityDetail } from "@/lib/atlas/entities-api";
import { toApiErrorResponse, type ApiErrorBody } from "@/lib/errors";
import { getAreaBoundaryCodes, getPointOutsideBoundaryFindings } from "@/lib/atlas/area-boundaries";
import { getStaticEntityNeighbors, getStaticEntityRelations } from "@/lib/atlas/static-dataset";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(
  _request: Request,
  context: { params: Promise<{ code: string }> },
): Promise<NextResponse | NextResponse<ApiErrorBody>> {
  try {
    const { code } = await context.params;
    const entity = await getAtlasEntityDetail(code);
    if (!entity) {
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { code: "ENTITY_NOT_FOUND", message: "Entity not found." } }, {
        status: 404,
        headers: { "Cache-Control": "public, s-maxage=30, stale-while-revalidate=120" },
      });
    }
    const [boundaryCodes, relations, neighbors, pointOutside] = await Promise.all([
      getAreaBoundaryCodes(), getStaticEntityRelations(entity.code), getStaticEntityNeighbors(entity.code),
      getPointOutsideBoundaryFindings(),
    ]);
    // BATCH 12.5 — a recorded point that falls outside the entity's own official boundary. The point is
    // reported, never moved, so the researcher view can flag it for human review.
    const outsideFinding = pointOutside.get(entity.code) ?? null;
    return NextResponse.json({
      ...entity,
      has_boundary: boundaryCodes.has(entity.code),
      point_outside_boundary: outsideFinding ? {
        distance_outside_m: outsideFinding.distance_outside_m,
        inside_osm_boundary: outsideFinding.inside_osm_boundary,
        round_coordinate: outsideFinding.round_coordinate,
      } : null,
      relations, neighbors,
    }, {
      headers: { "Cache-Control": "public, s-maxage=60, stale-while-revalidate=300" },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

===== FILE: src/app/api/atlas/features/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getAtlasFeatures, parseAtlasFeatureQuery } from "@/lib/atlas/features-api";
import { logger } from "@/lib/logger";
import { normalizeUnknownError, redactSecrets, toApiErrorResponse, type ApiErrorBody } from "@/lib/errors";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(request: Request): Promise<NextResponse | NextResponse<ApiErrorBody>> {
  try {
    const filters = parseAtlasFeatureQuery(new URL(request.url).searchParams);
    const collection = await getAtlasFeatures(filters);
    return NextResponse.json(collection, {
      status: 200,
      headers: {
        "Cache-Control": "public, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400",
        "X-Atlas-Geometry-Attribution": "(c) OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0",
        "Content-Type": "application/geo+json; charset=utf-8",
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const normalized = normalizeUnknownError(error);
    if (normalized.statusCode >= 500) {
      const value = error && typeof error === "object" ? error as Record<string, unknown> : null;
      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      logger.error("Atlas features request failed", {
        route: "/api/atlas/features",
        query: "atlas_features_geojson_by_filter_and_zoom",
        error_name: error instanceof Error ? error.name : typeof value?.name === "string" ? value.name : "NonError",
        error_message: redactSecrets(message),
        postgres_code: typeof value?.code === "string" ? value.code : null,
      });
    }
    return toApiErrorResponse(error);
  }
}

===== FILE: src/app/api/exports/csv/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { CSV_TABLES, csvForTable, exportResponseHeaders } from "@/lib/atlas/export-api";
import { getStaticAtlasDataset, getExportMetadata } from "@/lib/atlas/static-dataset";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(request: Request): Promise<NextResponse> {
  const table = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("table");
  if (!table || !CSV_TABLES.includes(table as (typeof CSV_TABLES)[number])) {
    return NextResponse.json({
      error: "Choose one CSV table: entities, facts, evidence, or fact_evidence.",
      available_tables: CSV_TABLES,
    }, { status: 400, headers: { "Cache-Control": "public, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400" } });
  }
  const [dataset, metadata] = await Promise.all([getStaticAtlasDataset(), getExportMetadata()]);
  const csv = csvForTable(dataset, table as (typeof CSV_TABLES)[number]);
  return new NextResponse(csv, {
    headers: exportResponseHeaders(metadata, "text/csv; charset=utf-8", `greater-cairo-${table}.csv`),
  });
}

===== FILE: src/app/api/exports/geojson/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { exportResponseHeaders } from "@/lib/atlas/export-api";
import { getExportMetadata, getStaticAtlasDataset, joinedFeatureCollection } from "@/lib/atlas/static-dataset";
import { parseGeoPackageFilters } from "@/lib/atlas/geopackage";
import { resolveStatusByYear } from "@/lib/atlas/status-timeline";
export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
export async function GET(request: Request): Promise<NextResponse> {
  let filters;
  try { filters = parseGeoPackageFilters(new URL(request.url).searchParams); }
  catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: { message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Invalid filter." } }, { status: 400 }); }
  const [dataset, metadata] = await Promise.all([getStaticAtlasDataset(), getExportMetadata()]);
  const timeline = filters.year ? resolveStatusByYear(dataset.facts.filter((fact) => fact.valid_as_of).map((fact) => ({
    entity_code: fact.entity_code, attribute: fact.attribute, value: fact.value, valid_as_of: fact.valid_as_of!, valid_as_of_precision: fact.valid_as_of_precision,
    verification_state: fact.verification_state, fact_code: fact.code,
  })), filters.year) : null;
  const transportTypes = ["transit_line", "transit_stop", "rail_station", "transit_station"];
  const entities = dataset.entities.filter((entity) => (!filters.type || (filters.type === "transport" ? transportTypes.includes(entity.entity_type) : entity.entity_type === filters.type)) && (!filters.sector || entity.sector === filters.sector) &&
    (!filters.status || (filters.year ? (timeline?.get(entity.code)?.status ?? "unknown") : entity.status) === filters.status));
  const entityByCode = new Map(entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  const filteredDataset = { ...dataset, entities, features: dataset.features.filter((feature) => entityByCode.has(feature.entity_code)) };
  const collection = joinedFeatureCollection(filteredDataset, metadata);
  const output = timeline ? { ...collection, features: collection.features.map((feature) => {
    const state = timeline.get(String(feature.properties.entity_code));
    return { ...feature, properties: { ...feature.properties, status: state?.status ?? "unknown",
      timeline_dated: state?.dated ?? false, timeline_verification: state?.verification_state ?? null, timeline_valid_as_of: state?.valid_as_of ?? null } };
  }) } : collection;
  return NextResponse.json(output, { headers: exportResponseHeaders(metadata, "application/geo+json; charset=utf-8", "greater-cairo-atlas-filtered.geojson") });
}


===== FILE: src/app/api/exports/geopackage/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { getStaticAtlasDataset } from "@/lib/atlas/static-dataset";
import { generateGeoPackage, parseGeoPackageFilters } from "@/lib/atlas/geopackage";
export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
export async function GET(request: Request): Promise<Response> {
  let filters;
  try { filters = parseGeoPackageFilters(new URL(request.url).searchParams); }
  catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: { message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Invalid filter." } }, { status: 400 }); }
  const bytes = await generateGeoPackage(await getStaticAtlasDataset(), filters);
  const body = new Uint8Array(bytes).buffer;
  const filterToken = [filters.type, filters.sector, filters.status, filters.year].filter((value) => value !== undefined).join("-") || "all";
  return new Response(body, { headers: {
    "Content-Type": "application/geopackage+sqlite3",
    "Content-Disposition": `attachment; filename="greater-cairo-atlas-${filterToken}.gpkg"`,
    "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=0, s-maxage=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800",
    "X-Atlas-License": "Research data CC BY 4.0; OpenStreetMap geometry ODbL 1.0; licenses and attribution included in license_attribution table",
  } });
}

===== FILE: src/app/api/openapi.json/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-static";
const schema = {
  openapi: "3.1.0", info: { title: "Greater Cairo Atlas API", version: "1.0.0", description: "Bilingual public API for an evidence-led atlas of Greater Cairo. Public records and research data are CC BY 4.0; OpenStreetMap-derived geometries are ODbL 1.0. Basemap tiles are provided by OpenFreeMap; see the website attribution and exported license_attribution table." },
  servers: [{ url: "/", description: "Current deployment" }], tags: [{ name: "Atlas" }, { name: "Evidence" }, { name: "Exports" }, { name: "Contributions" }],
  paths: {
    "/api/atlas/stats": { get: { tags: ["Atlas"], summary: "Dataset statistics", responses: { "200": { description: "Statistics and dataset metadata", content: { "application/json": { schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/Stats" }, example: { entities: 124, facts: 260, evidence: 80, features: 90 } } } } } } },
    "/api/atlas/entities": { get: { tags: ["Atlas"], summary: "List/filter entities", parameters: [{ name: "type", in: "query", schema: { type: "string" } }, { name: "sector", in: "query", schema: { type: "string", enum: ["NORTH", "EAST", "SOUTH", "WEST", "CENTRAL", "REGIONAL"] } }, { name: "status", in: "query", schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/Status" } }, { name: "has_geometry", in: "query", schema: { type: "boolean" } }, { name: "limit", in: "query", schema: { type: "integer", minimum: 1, maximum: 500 } }, { name: "offset", in: "query", schema: { type: "integer", minimum: 0 } }], responses: { "200": { description: "Entity list and pagination" } } } },
    "/api/atlas/entities/{code}": { get: { tags: ["Atlas"], summary: "Entity details, facts, evidence and relations", parameters: [{ $ref: "#/components/parameters/Code" }], responses: { "200": { description: "Entity detail" }, "404": { description: "Not found" } } } },

  },
  components: { parameters: { Code: { name: "code", in: "path", required: true, schema: { type: "string" } } }, schemas: { Status: { type: "string", enum: ["operational", "partial", "under_construction", "planned", "approved_planned", "unknown"] }, Stats: { type: "object", additionalProperties: true, properties: { entities: { type: "integer" }, facts: { type: "integer" }, evidence: { type: "integer" }, features: { type: "integer" } } } } },
};
export async function GET() { return NextResponse.json(schema, { headers: { "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=3600, s-maxage=86400, stale-while-revalidate=604800" } }); }


===== FILE: src/app/api/submissions/route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createSubmission } from "@/lib/atlas/submissions";
import { toApiErrorResponse } from "@/lib/errors";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json() as unknown;
    const result = await createSubmission(request, body);
    if (result.rateLimited) return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { code: "RATE_LIMITED", message: "Submission limit reached. Try again later." } }, { status: 429, headers: { "Retry-After": "3600", "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, message: "Thank you. Your suggestion was sent for human review; atlas facts are never changed automatically." }, { status: 202, headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });
  } catch (error) { return toApiErrorResponse(error); }
}


===== FILE: src/lib/admin/auth.ts =====
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { AdminImportNotConfiguredError, AdminUnauthorizedError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { extractAdminTokenFromHeaders, verifyAdminImportToken } from "@/lib/atlas/admin-import";
import { requireAdminImportEnv } from "@/lib/env";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — one admin guard for the whole workspace.
 *
 * The token stays server-side: it is read from the environment, compared in constant time, and never
 * echoed back or placed in a response body. A request presenting the token in the query string is
 * refused outright so the secret cannot end up in access logs or browser history.
 */
export interface AdminIdentity {
  /** Stable, non-secret label for audit rows. Derived from the token, never equal to it. */
  actor: string;
  /** SHA-256 of the presented token, for correlating writes without storing the token. */
  token_fingerprint: string;
}

export function resolveAdminIdentity(request: Request, rawEnv: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): AdminIdentity {
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  if (url.searchParams.has("token") || url.searchParams.has("admin_import_token")) {
    throw new AdminUnauthorizedError("Admin tokens must be sent in the Authorization header, never in the URL.");
  }

  let expectedToken: string;
  try {
    ({ ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN: expectedToken } = requireAdminImportEnv(rawEnv));
  } catch {
    // Fails closed: no configured token means nobody is an admin, not everybody.
    throw new AdminImportNotConfiguredError();
  }

  const provided = extractAdminTokenFromHeaders(request.headers);
  if (!verifyAdminImportToken(provided, expectedToken)) throw new AdminUnauthorizedError();

  const fingerprint = createHash("sha256").update(provided!.trim(), "utf8").digest("hex");
  // The actor label is what reviewers see in the audit log. It is deliberately not the token and not
  // derived reversibly from it: «admin» plus a short prefix of the token's digest.
  return { actor: `admin:${fingerprint.slice(0, 12)}`, token_fingerprint: fingerprint };
}

/**
 * Fixed-window rate limiter for write endpoints.
 *
 * This is process-local and therefore an approximation in a multi-instance deployment — it stops a
 * runaway script or a loop, not a distributed attacker. It is the same class of protection the
 * public submission endpoint already uses, and it is deliberately not presented as more than it is.
 */
export interface RateLimitResult {
  allowed: boolean;
  remaining: number;
  retry_after_seconds: number;
}

export class FixedWindowRateLimiter {
  private readonly windows = new Map<string, { count: number; resetAt: number }>();

  constructor(private readonly limit: number, private readonly windowMs: number, private readonly now: () => number = Date.now) {}

  check(key: string): RateLimitResult {
    const current = this.now();
    const existing = this.windows.get(key);
    if (!existing || existing.resetAt <= current) {
      this.windows.set(key, { count: 1, resetAt: current + this.windowMs });
      return { allowed: true, remaining: this.limit - 1, retry_after_seconds: Math.ceil(this.windowMs / 1000) };
    }
    if (existing.count >= this.limit) {
      return { allowed: false, remaining: 0, retry_after_seconds: Math.max(1, Math.ceil((existing.resetAt - current) / 1000)) };
    }
    existing.count += 1;
    return { allowed: true, remaining: this.limit - existing.count, retry_after_seconds: Math.max(1, Math.ceil((existing.resetAt - current) / 1000)) };
  }

  /** Drops expired windows so a long-lived process does not grow without bound. */
  prune(): void {
    const current = this.now();
    for (const [key, window] of this.windows) if (window.resetAt <= current) this.windows.delete(key);
  }
}

/** Write endpoints are deliberately tight: a human reviewer does not need 100 writes a minute. */
export const adminWriteRateLimiter = new FixedWindowRateLimiter(30, 60_000);

export function enforceAdminWriteRateLimit(identity: AdminIdentity): void {
  const result = adminWriteRateLimiter.check(identity.actor);
  if (!result.allowed) {
    const error = new AdminUnauthorizedError(`Too many admin writes. Try again in ${result.retry_after_seconds}s.`);
    error.name = "AdminRateLimitedError";
    throw error;
  }
}


===== FILE: src/lib/admin/import-commit.ts =====
import type { ImportPlan } from "./import-pipeline";
import type { SqlExecutor } from "./queries";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — apply an approved import plan.
 *
 * The plan is the only input, and `actionable` is what a commit is allowed to touch: rows the planner
 * rejected, marked unchanged or flagged as conflicts are not present there, so a bug here cannot
 * silently insert something the reviewer never saw.
 *
 * Ordering matters. Evidence is inserted before facts because a fact references its evidence, and facts
 * after entities because a fact belongs to an entity. A fact whose entity is missing is skipped rather
 * than inserted against a wrong id: the FK is resolved by code, never guessed.
 *
 * Facts always land as `candidate`. An import is a proposal, not a review.
 */
export interface ImportCommitResult {
  entities: number;
  facts: number;
  evidence: number;
  /** Facts whose evidence did not exist after all, so the link could not be made. */
  skipped_fact_links: number;
}

export async function commitImportPlan(execute: SqlExecutor, plan: ImportPlan): Promise<ImportCommitResult> {
  const result: ImportCommitResult = { entities: 0, facts: 0, evidence: 0, skipped_fact_links: 0 };

  for (const row of plan.actionable.evidence) {
    const inserted = await execute.query<{ code: string }>(
      `INSERT INTO evidence (code, title, url, publisher, source_type, published_date, accessed_date, notes)
       VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,COALESCE($7, CURRENT_DATE),$8)
       ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING
       RETURNING code`,
      [row.code, row.title, row.url ?? null, row.publisher ?? null, row.source_type,
        row.published_date ?? null, row.accessed_date ?? null, row.notes ?? null]);
    result.evidence += inserted.length;
  }

  for (const row of plan.actionable.entities) {
    const inserted = await execute.query<{ code: string }>(
      `INSERT INTO entities (code, name_ar, name_en, entity_type, sector, status, description)
       VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7)
       ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING
       RETURNING code`,
      [row.code, row.name_ar, row.name_en, row.entity_type, row.sector, row.status, row.description || null]);
    result.entities += inserted.length;
  }

  for (const row of plan.actionable.facts) {
    const inserted = await execute.query<{ code: string }>(
      `INSERT INTO facts (code, entity_id, attribute, value, unit, valid_as_of, verification_state, notes)
       SELECT $1, e.id, $3, $4, $5, $6::date, 'candidate', $7 FROM entities e WHERE e.code = $2
       ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING
       RETURNING code`,
      [row.code, row.entity_code, row.attribute, row.value, row.unit ?? null, row.valid_as_of ?? null, row.notes ?? null]);
    if (inserted.length === 0) continue;
    result.facts += 1;

    for (const evidenceCode of row.evidence_codes) {
      const linked = await execute.query<{ fact_id: string }>(
        `INSERT INTO fact_evidence (fact_id, evidence_id)
         SELECT f.id, ev.id FROM facts f, evidence ev WHERE f.code = $1 AND ev.code = $2
         ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
         RETURNING fact_id`, [row.code, evidenceCode]);
      if (linked.length === 0) result.skipped_fact_links += 1;
    }
  }

  return result;
}


===== FILE: src/lib/admin/import-pipeline.ts =====
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { z } from "zod";
import { detectDuplicates, normalizeArabicName, type DuplicateMatch } from "./review";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — import pipeline for research JSON/CSV in the existing seed format.
 *
 * schema validation → duplicate detection → diff preview (new / changed / conflicts) → approve.
 * Dry-run is the default at every layer: `planImport` throws nothing away and writes nothing; only an
 * explicit commit may insert, and only rows that survive validation.
 */
export const importEntityRowSchema = z.strictObject({
  code: z.string().min(1).max(120),
  name_ar: z.string().min(1).max(300),
  name_en: z.string().min(1).max(300),
  entity_type: z.string().min(1).max(60),
  sector: z.string().min(1).max(40),
  status: z.string().min(1).max(40),
  aliases: z.string().max(2000).optional().default(""),
  description: z.string().max(4000).optional().default(""),
});

export const importFactRowSchema = z.strictObject({
  code: z.string().min(1).max(160),
  entity_code: z.string().min(1).max(120),
  attribute: z.string().min(1).max(120),
  value: z.string().min(1).max(2000),
  unit: z.string().max(60).nullable().optional(),
  valid_as_of: z.string().max(40).nullable().optional(),
  valid_as_of_precision: z.enum(["day", "month", "year"]).optional().default("day"),
  verification_state: z.enum(["candidate", "verified", "disputed", "corroborated", "single_source", "superseded", "rejected"]),
  evidence_codes: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(120)).default([]),
  notes: z.string().max(4000).nullable().optional(),
});

export const importEvidenceRowSchema = z.strictObject({
  code: z.string().min(1).max(120),
  title: z.string().min(1).max(500),
  url: z.string().max(2048).nullable().optional(),
  publisher: z.string().max(300).nullable().optional(),
  source_type: z.string().min(1).max(60),
  published_date: z.string().max(40).nullable().optional(),
  published_date_precision: z.enum(["day", "month", "year"]).optional().default("day"),
  accessed_date: z.string().max(40).nullable().optional(),
  notes: z.string().max(4000).nullable().optional(),
});

export const importPayloadSchema = z.object({
  entities: z.array(z.unknown()).optional().default([]),
  facts: z.array(z.unknown()).optional().default([]),
  evidence: z.array(z.unknown()).optional().default([]),
});

export type ImportEntityRow = z.infer<typeof importEntityRowSchema>;
export type ImportFactRow = z.infer<typeof importFactRowSchema>;
export type ImportEvidenceRow = z.infer<typeof importEvidenceRowSchema>;

export interface ImportIssue {
  section: "entities" | "facts" | "evidence" | "payload";
  index: number;
  path: string;
  message: string;
}

export interface ExistingState {
  entity_codes: ReadonlySet<string>;
  fact_codes: ReadonlySet<string>;
  evidence_codes: ReadonlySet<string>;
  entities: ReadonlyArray<{ code: string; name_ar: string; name_en: string; aliases: readonly string[] }>;
  /** fact code → value, so a changed claim can be shown as changed rather than new. */
  fact_values: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  /** evidence code → url, so a newly-added URL is visible in the diff. */
  evidence_urls: ReadonlyMap<string, string | null>;
  /**
   * evidence code → source_type for evidence already in the database. Without this the planner cannot
   * tell an official source from a blog, and would have to reject every `verified` row that cites stored
   * evidence — which is exactly what a legitimate curated import does.
   */
  evidence_source_types?: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  /** entity code → existing attribute→value, for conflict detection. */
  fact_attributes_by_entity: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlyMap<string, string>>;
}

export type DiffCategory = "new" | "changed" | "unchanged" | "conflict" | "rejected";

export interface DiffRow {
  section: "entities" | "facts" | "evidence";
  code: string;
  category: DiffCategory;
  /** Why it landed in this category. Always a statement about the data, never a guess. */
  reason: string;
  before: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  after: Record<string, unknown> | null;
}

export interface ImportPlan {
  ok: boolean;
  checksum: string;
  issues: ImportIssue[];
  rejected_rows: Array<{ section: DiffRow["section"]; code: string; reason: string }>;
  duplicates: DuplicateMatch[];
  diff: DiffRow[];
  counts: {
    entities: { new: number; changed: number; unchanged: number; rejected: number };
    facts: { new: number; changed: number; unchanged: number; conflict: number; rejected: number };
    evidence: { new: number; changed: number; unchanged: number; rejected: number };
  };
  /** Rows that a commit would actually insert, already validated. Empty for a blocked plan. */
  actionable: {
    entities: ImportEntityRow[];
    facts: ImportFactRow[];
    evidence: ImportEvidenceRow[];
  };
}

function isUsableUrl(value: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!value) return false;
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return (url.protocol === "http:" || url.protocol === "https:") && url.hostname.length > 0;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * A row marked `verified` may only enter when some evidence it cites is official and has a usable URL.
 * Rows failing this are rejected, never downgraded silently: the importer must not invent a state the
 * contributor did not ask for, and must not admit an unverifiable verified claim.
 */
export function verifiedRowRejection(
  fact: ImportFactRow,
  evidenceByCode: ReadonlyMap<string, ImportEvidenceRow | { code: string; source_type: string; url?: string | null }>,
  knownEvidenceCodes: ReadonlySet<string>,
  storedEvidence?: {
    urls?: ReadonlyMap<string, string | null>;
    sourceTypes?: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
  },
): string | null {
  if (fact.verification_state !== "verified") return null;
  if (fact.evidence_codes.length === 0) return "marked verified but cites no evidence";

  let sawUnknownSource = false;
  for (const code of fact.evidence_codes) {
    if (!knownEvidenceCodes.has(code)) continue;
    // Evidence in the payload describes itself; stored evidence is described by the caller's lookup.
    const row = evidenceByCode.get(code);
    if (row) {
      if (row.source_type === "official" && isUsableUrl(row.url)) return null;
      continue;
    }
    const sourceType = storedEvidence?.sourceTypes?.get(code);
    if (sourceType === undefined) {
      // The caller could not tell us what this source is. Refuse rather than assume it is official.
      sawUnknownSource = true;
      continue;
    }
    const url = storedEvidence?.urls?.get(code) ?? null;
    if (sourceType === "official" && isUsableUrl(url)) return null;
  }
  return sawUnknownSource && fact.evidence_codes.every((code) => !evidenceByCode.has(code))
    ? "marked verified but the cited evidence's source type could not be checked"
    : "marked verified but no cited evidence is an official source with a usable URL";
}

export function hashPayload(payloadText: string): string {
  return createHash("sha256").update(payloadText, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

export function planImport(
  payloadText: string,
  existing: ExistingState,
): ImportPlan {
  const issues: ImportIssue[] = [];
  const rejected: ImportPlan["rejected_rows"] = [];

  let parsedJson: unknown;
  try {
    parsedJson = JSON.parse(payloadText);
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      checksum: hashPayload(payloadText),
      issues: [{ section: "payload", index: 0, path: "body", message: `Invalid JSON: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` }],
      rejected_rows: [],
      duplicates: [],
      diff: [],
      counts: {
        entities: { new: 0, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, rejected: 0 },
        facts: { new: 0, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, conflict: 0, rejected: 0 },
        evidence: { new: 0, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, rejected: 0 },
      },
      actionable: { entities: [], facts: [], evidence: [] },
    };
  }

  const payloadResult = importPayloadSchema.safeParse(parsedJson);
  if (!payloadResult.success) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      checksum: hashPayload(payloadText),
      issues: payloadResult.error.issues.map((issue) => ({ section: "payload" as const, index: 0, path: issue.path.join("."), message: issue.message })),
      rejected_rows: [],
      duplicates: [],
      diff: [],
      counts: {
        entities: { new: 0, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, rejected: 0 },
        facts: { new: 0, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, conflict: 0, rejected: 0 },
        evidence: { new: 0, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, rejected: 0 },
      },
      actionable: { entities: [], facts: [], evidence: [] },
    };
  }

  const raw = payloadResult.data;
  const entities: ImportEntityRow[] = [];
  const facts: ImportFactRow[] = [];
  const evidenceRows: ImportEvidenceRow[] = [];

  raw.entities.forEach((row, index) => {
    const result = importEntityRowSchema.safeParse(row);
    if (result.success) entities.push(result.data);
    else {
      const code = (row as { code?: string })?.code ?? `#${index}`;
      rejected.push({ section: "entities", code, reason: result.error.issues.map((issue) => `${issue.path.join(".") || "row"}: ${issue.message}`).join("; ") });
      issues.push(...result.error.issues.map((issue) => ({ section: "entities" as const, index, path: issue.path.join("."), message: issue.message })));
    }
  });
  raw.evidence.forEach((row, index) => {
    const result = importEvidenceRowSchema.safeParse(row);
    if (result.success) evidenceRows.push(result.data);
    else {
      const code = (row as { code?: string })?.code ?? `#${index}`;
      rejected.push({ section: "evidence", code, reason: result.error.issues.map((issue) => `${issue.path.join(".") || "row"}: ${issue.message}`).join("; ") });
      issues.push(...result.error.issues.map((issue) => ({ section: "evidence" as const, index, path: issue.path.join("."), message: issue.message })));
    }
  });

  const incomingEvidence = new Map(evidenceRows.map((row) => [row.code, row]));
  const knownEvidence = new Set([...existing.evidence_codes, ...incomingEvidence.keys()]);

  raw.facts.forEach((row, index) => {
    const result = importFactRowSchema.safeParse(row);
    if (!result.success) {
      const code = (row as { code?: string })?.code ?? `#${index}`;
      rejected.push({ section: "facts", code, reason: result.error.issues.map((issue) => `${issue.path.join(".") || "row"}: ${issue.message}`).join("; ") });
      issues.push(...result.error.issues.map((issue) => ({ section: "facts" as const, index, path: issue.path.join("."), message: issue.message })));
      return;
    }
    const fact = result.data;
