    // A fact may only reference evidence that exists now or arrives in this same payload.
    const missingEvidence = fact.evidence_codes.filter((code) => !knownEvidence.has(code));
    if (missingEvidence.length > 0) {
      rejected.push({ section: "facts", code: fact.code, reason: `cites evidence that does not exist: ${missingEvidence.join(", ")}` });
      return;
    }
    const rejection = verifiedRowRejection(fact, incomingEvidence, knownEvidence, {
      urls: existing.evidence_urls,
      sourceTypes: existing.evidence_source_types,
    });
    if (rejection) {
      rejected.push({ section: "facts", code: fact.code, reason: rejection });
      return;
    }
    facts.push(fact);
  });

  // ---- duplicate detection against existing entities -------------------------------
  const duplicates = detectDuplicates(
    entities.map((row) => ({ code: row.code, name_ar: row.name_ar, name_en: row.name_en, aliases: row.aliases.split("|").map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean) })),
    existing.entities.map((entity) => ({ code: entity.code, name_ar: entity.name_ar, name_en: entity.name_en, aliases: entity.aliases })),
  );

  // ---- diff -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  const diff: DiffRow[] = [];
  const counts = {
    entities: { new: 0, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, rejected: rejected.filter((row) => row.section === "entities").length },
    facts: { new: 0, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, conflict: 0, rejected: rejected.filter((row) => row.section === "facts").length },
    evidence: { new: 0, changed: 0, unchanged: 0, rejected: rejected.filter((row) => row.section === "evidence").length },
  };

  const actionableEntities: ImportEntityRow[] = [];
  for (const row of entities) {
    const exists = existing.entity_codes.has(row.code);
    if (!exists) {
      counts.entities.new += 1;
      actionableEntities.push(row);
      diff.push({ section: "entities", code: row.code, category: "new", reason: "New entity code.", before: null, after: { ...row } });
    } else {
      // An existing entity is never rewritten by an import: only genuinely new entities are inserted,
      // anything else is surfaced for a human decision instead of being applied silently.
      counts.entities.unchanged += 1;
      diff.push({ section: "entities", code: row.code, category: "unchanged", reason: "Entity code already exists; not overwritten by an import.", before: { code: row.code }, after: { ...row } });
    }
  }

  const actionableEvidence: ImportEvidenceRow[] = [];
  for (const row of evidenceRows) {
    const existingUrl = existing.evidence_urls.get(row.code);
    if (!existing.evidence_codes.has(row.code)) {
      counts.evidence.new += 1;
      actionableEvidence.push(row);
      diff.push({ section: "evidence", code: row.code, category: "new", reason: "New evidence code.", before: null, after: { ...row } });
    } else if ((existingUrl ?? null) !== (row.url ?? null)) {
      counts.evidence.changed += 1;
      diff.push({ section: "evidence", code: row.code, category: "changed", reason: "Evidence URL differs from the stored row; review before applying.", before: { url: existingUrl ?? null }, after: { url: row.url ?? null } });
    } else {
      counts.evidence.unchanged += 1;
      diff.push({ section: "evidence", code: row.code, category: "unchanged", reason: "Identical to the stored evidence row.", before: { url: existingUrl ?? null }, after: { url: row.url ?? null } });
    }
  }

  const actionableFacts: ImportFactRow[] = [];
  for (const row of facts) {
    const existingValue = existing.fact_values.get(row.code);
    const entityAttributes = existing.fact_attributes_by_entity.get(row.entity_code);
    const conflictingValue = entityAttributes?.get(row.attribute);
    if (!existing.fact_codes.has(row.code)) {
      // The same attribute on the same entity with a different value is a conflict, not a new claim.
      if (conflictingValue !== undefined && normalizeArabicName(conflictingValue) !== normalizeArabicName(row.value)) {
        counts.facts.conflict += 1;
        diff.push({ section: "facts", code: row.code, category: "conflict", reason: `Entity already records ${row.attribute} = «${conflictingValue}»; this row says «${row.value}».`, before: { value: conflictingValue }, after: { ...row } });
      } else {
        counts.facts.new += 1;
        actionableFacts.push(row);
        diff.push({ section: "facts", code: row.code, category: "new", reason: "New fact code.", before: null, after: { ...row } });
      }
    } else if (existingValue !== row.value) {
      counts.facts.changed += 1;
      diff.push({ section: "facts", code: row.code, category: "changed", reason: "Same fact code with a different value; review before applying.", before: { value: existingValue ?? null }, after: { ...row } });
    } else {
      counts.facts.unchanged += 1;
      diff.push({ section: "facts", code: row.code, category: "unchanged", reason: "Identical to the stored fact.", before: { value: existingValue ?? null }, after: { ...row } });
    }
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    checksum: hashPayload(payloadText),
    issues,
    rejected_rows: rejected,
    duplicates,
    diff,
    counts,
    actionable: { entities: actionableEntities, facts: actionableFacts, evidence: actionableEvidence },
  };
}

/**
 * Minimal CSV reader for the seed's flat format. Handles quoted fields with embedded commas and
 * doubled quotes, which the seed files actually use. It is deliberately strict: a ragged row is an
 * error, not something to guess at.
 */
export function parseCsv(text: string): { rows: Record<string, string>[]; error: string | null } {
  const lines: string[][] = [];
  let field = "";
  let row: string[] = [];
  let quoted = false;
  for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index += 1) {
    const char = text[index]!;
    if (quoted) {
      if (char === '"') {
        if (text[index + 1] === '"') { field += '"'; index += 1; }
        else quoted = false;
      } else field += char;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === '"') { quoted = true; continue; }
    if (char === ",") { row.push(field); field = ""; continue; }
    if (char === "\n" || char === "\r") {
      if (char === "\r" && text[index + 1] === "\n") index += 1;
      row.push(field); field = "";
      if (row.some((cell) => cell.trim().length > 0)) lines.push(row);
      row = [];
      continue;
    }
    field += char;
  }
  if (field.length > 0 || row.length > 0) { row.push(field); if (row.some((cell) => cell.trim().length > 0)) lines.push(row); }
  if (quoted) return { rows: [], error: "Unterminated quoted field." };
  if (lines.length === 0) return { rows: [], error: "The CSV file is empty." };
  const header = lines[0]!.map((cell) => cell.trim());
  const rows = lines.slice(1).map((cells) => {
    const record: Record<string, string> = {};
    header.forEach((name, position) => { record[name] = cells[position] ?? ""; });
    return record;
  });
  return { rows, error: null };
}

/** Converts a seed-format CSV upload into the JSON shape `planImport` expects. */
export function csvToImportPayload(text: string, section: "entities" | "facts" | "evidence"): { payload: string; error: string | null } {
  const { rows, error } = parseCsv(text);
  if (error) return { payload: "", error };
  const normalized = rows.map((row) => {
    if (section === "facts") {
      return { ...row, evidence_codes: (row.evidence_codes ?? "").split("|").map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean) };
    }
    if (section === "entities" && row.aliases) {
      return { ...row, aliases: row.aliases.split("|").map((item) => item.trim()).join("|") };
    }
    return row;
  });
  return { payload: JSON.stringify({ [section]: normalized }), error: null };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/admin/mutations.ts =====
import { z } from "zod";
import { RequestValidationError } from "@/lib/errors";
import {
  decideFactReview, reviewRequestSchema, type EvidenceFacts, type FactReviewState, type ReviewFactInput,
} from "./review";
import { defaultExecutor, type SqlExecutor } from "./queries";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — write operations for the admin workspace.
 *
 * Every function here writes the data change AND its audit row inside one transaction, so a crash
 * cannot leave a decision without its history. Nothing issues a DELETE; the only destructive-looking
 * operation (attaching evidence) is an insert.
 */
export interface AdminWriteContext {
  actor: string;
  token_fingerprint: string;
}

export interface TransactionalExecutor extends SqlExecutor {
  transaction?<T>(work: (tx: SqlExecutor) => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
}

async function inTransaction<T>(execute: TransactionalExecutor, work: (tx: SqlExecutor) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  if (execute.transaction) return execute.transaction(work);
  return work(execute);
}

/** The executor a route uses when it does not inject one (the real database). */
export function liveExecutor(): TransactionalExecutor {
  return defaultExecutor;
}

export interface FactStateRow {
  code: string;
  entity_code: string;
  attribute: string;
  value: string;
  verification_state: string;
}

function asFactInput(row: FactStateRow, evidenceCodes: readonly string[]): ReviewFactInput {
  return {
    code: row.code, entity_code: row.entity_code, attribute: row.attribute, value: row.value,
    verification_state: row.verification_state, evidence_codes: evidenceCodes,
  };
}

export interface ReviewMutationResult {
  fact_code: string;
  review_state: FactReviewState;
  verification_state: string;
  verification_unchanged_reason: string | null;
  audit_id: string | number;
}

/**
 * Applies a review decision to one fact.
 *
 * The expected_state check is optimistic concurrency: if another reviewer already decided, the request
 * is refused with the current state instead of silently overwriting their decision.
 */
export async function applyFactReview(
  execute: TransactionalExecutor = liveExecutor(),
  context: AdminWriteContext,
  factCode: string,
  rawBody: unknown,
): Promise<ReviewMutationResult> {
  const parsed = reviewRequestSchema.safeParse(rawBody);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    const missingReason = parsed.error.issues.some((issue) => issue.path.join(".") === "reason");
    throw new RequestValidationError(
      missingReason ? "A written reason is required for this action." : "Invalid review request.",
      parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({ path: issue.path.join(".") || "body", message: issue.message })),
    );
  }
  const request = parsed.data;

  return inTransaction(execute, async (tx) => {
    const [factRow] = await tx.query<FactStateRow>(`
      SELECT f.code, e.code AS entity_code, f.attribute, f.value, f.verification_state
        FROM facts f JOIN entities e ON e.id = f.entity_id
       WHERE f.code = $1
       LIMIT 1`, [factCode]);
    if (!factRow) throw new RequestValidationError("Unknown fact code.", [{ path: "fact", message: `No fact with code ${factCode}.` }]);

    const evidenceRows = await tx.query<EvidenceFacts & { title: string | null; publisher: string | null }>(`
      SELECT ev.code, ev.source_type, ev.url, ev.title, ev.publisher
        FROM fact_evidence fe JOIN evidence ev ON ev.id = fe.evidence_id
       WHERE fe.fact_id = (SELECT id FROM facts WHERE code = $1)
       ORDER BY ev.code`, [factCode]);
    const evidenceByCode = new Map(evidenceRows.map((row) => [row.code, row]));

    const [currentReview] = await tx.query<{ review_state: FactReviewState; reason: string | null }>(
      "SELECT review_state, reason FROM fact_reviews WHERE fact_code = $1", [factCode]);
    const currentState: FactReviewState = currentReview?.review_state ?? "pending";

    if (request.expected_state && request.expected_state !== currentState) {
      throw new RequestValidationError("Someone else already reviewed this fact.", [{
        path: "expected_state",
        message: `Expected ${request.expected_state} but the fact is currently ${currentState}. Reload before deciding.`,
      }]);
    }

    const outcome = decideFactReview(
      asFactInput(factRow, evidenceRows.map((row) => row.code)),
      evidenceByCode,
      request,
      currentState,
    );

    // Review state: upsert on the fact code. The previous state lives on in the audit log.
    await tx.query(`
      INSERT INTO fact_reviews (fact_code, review_state, reason, reviewer, reviewed_at, updated_at)
      VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, NOW(), NOW())
      ON CONFLICT (fact_code) DO UPDATE
        SET review_state = EXCLUDED.review_state, reason = EXCLUDED.reason,
            reviewer = EXCLUDED.reviewer, reviewed_at = NOW(), updated_at = NOW()`,
      [factCode, outcome.next_review_state, outcome.audit.reason, context.actor]);

    // verification_state only moves when the decision says it should.
    if (outcome.next_verification_state !== factRow.verification_state) {
      await tx.query("UPDATE facts SET verification_state = $2, updated_at = NOW() WHERE code = $1",
        [factCode, outcome.next_verification_state]);
    }

    const [auditRow] = await tx.query<{ id: string | number }>(`
      INSERT INTO admin_audit_log
        (actor, action, entity_code, fact_code, reason, before_state, after_state, request_fingerprint)
      VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6::jsonb, $7::jsonb, $8)
      RETURNING id`,
      [context.actor, outcome.audit.action, factRow.entity_code, factCode, outcome.audit.reason,
        JSON.stringify(outcome.audit.before_state), JSON.stringify(outcome.audit.after_state), context.token_fingerprint]);

    return {
      fact_code: factCode,
      review_state: outcome.next_review_state,
      verification_state: outcome.next_verification_state,
      verification_unchanged_reason: outcome.verification_unchanged_reason,
      audit_id: auditRow!.id,
    };
  });
}

export const attachEvidenceSchema = z.strictObject({
  /** `supporting` confirms the claim; `contradicting` is recorded as a dispute, never as a silent edit. */
  stance: z.enum(["supporting", "contradicting"]),
  evidence_code: z.string().min(1).max(120),
  reason: z.string().trim().min(1).max(2000),
});

/**
 * Attaches existing evidence to a fact. Contradicting evidence moves the review to `disputed` — it does
 * not delete or overwrite the claim, because a contradiction is a fact about the record, not a verdict.
 */
export async function attachEvidence(
  execute: TransactionalExecutor = liveExecutor(),
  context: AdminWriteContext,
  factCode: string,
  rawBody: unknown,
): Promise<{ fact_code: string; review_state: FactReviewState; audit_id: string | number }> {
  const parsed = attachEvidenceSchema.safeParse(rawBody);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid evidence attachment.", parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
      path: issue.path.join(".") || "body", message: issue.message,
    })));
  }
  const { stance, evidence_code: evidenceCode, reason } = parsed.data;

  return inTransaction(execute, async (tx) => {
    const [factRow] = await tx.query<FactStateRow>(`
      SELECT f.code, e.code AS entity_code, f.attribute, f.value, f.verification_state
        FROM facts f JOIN entities e ON e.id = f.entity_id WHERE f.code = $1 LIMIT 1`, [factCode]);
    if (!factRow) throw new RequestValidationError("Unknown fact code.", [{ path: "fact", message: `No fact with code ${factCode}.` }]);

    const [evidenceRow] = await tx.query<EvidenceFacts & { title: string | null }>(
      "SELECT code, source_type, url, title FROM evidence WHERE code = $1 LIMIT 1", [evidenceCode]);
    if (!evidenceRow) throw new RequestValidationError("Unknown evidence code.", [{ path: "evidence", message: `No evidence with code ${evidenceCode}.` }]);

    const [current] = await tx.query<{ review_state: FactReviewState }>(
      "SELECT review_state FROM fact_reviews WHERE fact_code = $1", [factCode]);
    const currentState: FactReviewState = current?.review_state ?? "pending";

    // Already linked: the write is a no-op for the link itself, but the decision is still recorded.
    await tx.query(`
      INSERT INTO fact_evidence (fact_id, evidence_id)
      SELECT f.id, e.id FROM facts f, evidence e WHERE f.code = $1 AND e.code = $2
      ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, [factCode, evidenceCode]);

    const nextState: FactReviewState = stance === "contradicting" ? "disputed" : currentState;
    if (nextState !== currentState) {
      await tx.query(`
        INSERT INTO fact_reviews (fact_code, review_state, reason, reviewer, reviewed_at, updated_at)
        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, NOW(), NOW())
        ON CONFLICT (fact_code) DO UPDATE
          SET review_state = EXCLUDED.review_state, reason = EXCLUDED.reason,
              reviewer = EXCLUDED.reviewer, reviewed_at = NOW(), updated_at = NOW()`,
        [factCode, nextState, reason, context.actor]);
      await tx.query("UPDATE facts SET verification_state = 'disputed', updated_at = NOW() WHERE code = $1", [factCode]);
    }

    const [auditRow] = await tx.query<{ id: string | number }>(`
      INSERT INTO admin_audit_log
        (actor, action, entity_code, fact_code, evidence_code, reason, before_state, after_state, request_fingerprint)
      VALUES ($1, 'evidence.attach', $2, $3, $4, $5, $6::jsonb, $7::jsonb, $8)
      RETURNING id`,
      [context.actor, factRow.entity_code, factCode, evidenceCode, reason,
        JSON.stringify({ review_state: currentState }),
        JSON.stringify({
          review_state: nextState, stance, evidence: {
            code: evidenceRow.code, source_type: evidenceRow.source_type, url: evidenceRow.url, title: evidenceRow.title,
          },
        }), context.token_fingerprint]);

    return { fact_code: factCode, review_state: nextState, audit_id: auditRow!.id };
  });
}

/**
 * Accepts a community submission as a *candidate* fact with its evidence. It can never create a
 * verified fact: the source is whatever URL the submitter gave, which has not been checked officially.
 */
export async function acceptSubmission(
  execute: TransactionalExecutor = liveExecutor(),
  context: AdminWriteContext,
  submissionId: string,
): Promise<{ fact_code: string; submission_id: string; audit_id: string | number; verification_state: "candidate" }> {
  return inTransaction(execute, async (tx) => {
    const [submission] = await tx.query<{
      id: string; entity_code: string; attribute: string; proposed_value: string; source_url: string; status: string;
    }>("SELECT id, entity_code, attribute, proposed_value, source_url, status FROM submissions WHERE id = $1 LIMIT 1", [submissionId]);
    if (!submission) throw new RequestValidationError("Unknown submission.", [{ path: "submission", message: `No submission with id ${submissionId}.` }]);
    if (submission.status !== "pending") {
      throw new RequestValidationError("This submission was already reviewed.", [{ path: "submission", message: `Submission is ${submission.status}.` }]);
    }

    // Deterministic codes so re-accepting cannot create duplicates, and so the provenance is obvious.
    const evidenceCode = `EV-SUB-${submission.id.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`;
    const factCode = `F-${submission.entity_code}-${submission.attribute.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/gu, "-")}-SUB`;

    await tx.query(`
      INSERT INTO evidence (code, title, url, publisher, source_type, accessed_date, notes)
      VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'Community submission', 'secondary', CURRENT_DATE, $4)
      ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING`,
      [evidenceCode, `Community submission for ${submission.attribute}`, submission.source_url, "Submitted through the public form; not independently verified."]);

    await tx.query(`
      INSERT INTO facts (code, entity_id, attribute, value, verification_state, notes)
      SELECT $1, id, $3, $4, 'candidate', $5 FROM entities WHERE code = $2
      ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING`,
      [factCode, submission.entity_code, submission.attribute, submission.proposed_value,
        `Created from community submission ${submission.id}. Candidate only.`]);

    await tx.query(`
      INSERT INTO fact_evidence (fact_id, evidence_id)
      SELECT f.id, e.id FROM facts f, evidence e WHERE f.code = $1 AND e.code = $2
      ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, [factCode, evidenceCode]);

    await tx.query("UPDATE submissions SET status = 'accepted', updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1", [submissionId]);

    const [auditRow] = await tx.query<{ id: string | number }>(`
      INSERT INTO admin_audit_log
        (actor, action, entity_code, fact_code, evidence_code, submission_id, before_state, after_state, request_fingerprint)
      VALUES ($1, 'submission.accept', $2, $3, $4, $5, $6::jsonb, $7::jsonb, $8)
      RETURNING id`,
      [context.actor, submission.entity_code, factCode, evidenceCode, submissionId,
        JSON.stringify({ submission_status: "pending" }),
        JSON.stringify({ submission_status: "accepted", fact_code: factCode, verification_state: "candidate" }),
        context.token_fingerprint]);

    return { fact_code: factCode, submission_id: submissionId, audit_id: auditRow!.id, verification_state: "candidate" };
  });
}

export async function rejectSubmission(
  execute: TransactionalExecutor = liveExecutor(),
  context: AdminWriteContext,
  submissionId: string,
  rawBody: unknown,
): Promise<{ submission_id: string; audit_id: string | number }> {
  const parsed = z.strictObject({ reason: z.string().trim().min(1).max(1000) }).safeParse(rawBody);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    throw new RequestValidationError("A reason is required to reject a submission.", parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
      path: issue.path.join(".") || "body", message: issue.message,
    })));
  }
  return inTransaction(execute, async (tx) => {
    const [submission] = await tx.query<{ id: string; entity_code: string; status: string }>(
      "SELECT id, entity_code, status FROM submissions WHERE id = $1 LIMIT 1", [submissionId]);
    if (!submission) throw new RequestValidationError("Unknown submission.", [{ path: "submission", message: `No submission with id ${submissionId}.` }]);
    if (submission.status !== "pending") {
      throw new RequestValidationError("This submission was already reviewed.", [{ path: "submission", message: `Submission is ${submission.status}.` }]);
    }
    await tx.query("UPDATE submissions SET status = 'rejected', reviewer_note = $2, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1",
      [submissionId, parsed.data.reason]);
    const [auditRow] = await tx.query<{ id: string | number }>(`
      INSERT INTO admin_audit_log
        (actor, action, entity_code, submission_id, reason, before_state, after_state, request_fingerprint)
      VALUES ($1, 'submission.reject', $2, $3, $4, $5::jsonb, $6::jsonb, $7)
      RETURNING id`,
      [context.actor, submission.entity_code, submissionId, parsed.data.reason,
        JSON.stringify({ submission_status: "pending" }), JSON.stringify({ submission_status: "rejected" }),
        context.token_fingerprint]);
    return { submission_id: submissionId, audit_id: auditRow!.id };
  });
}

export interface AuditRow {
  id: string | number;
  occurred_at: string;
  actor: string;
  action: string;
  entity_code: string | null;
  fact_code: string | null;
  evidence_code: string | null;
  reason: string | null;
  before_state: unknown;
  after_state: unknown;
}

export async function listAuditRows(
  execute: SqlExecutor = defaultExecutor,
  options: { entity_code?: string; fact_code?: string; limit?: number } = {},
): Promise<AuditRow[]> {
  const limit = Math.min(200, Math.max(1, Math.trunc(options.limit ?? 50)));
  const conditions: string[] = [];
  const values: unknown[] = [];
  if (options.entity_code) { values.push(options.entity_code); conditions.push(`entity_code = $${values.length}`); }
  if (options.fact_code) { values.push(options.fact_code); conditions.push(`fact_code = $${values.length}`); }
  const where = conditions.length ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}` : "";
  return execute.query<AuditRow>(
    `SELECT id, occurred_at::text AS occurred_at, actor, action, entity_code, fact_code, evidence_code, reason, before_state, after_state
       FROM admin_audit_log ${where} ORDER BY occurred_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT $${values.length + 1}`,
    [...values, limit]);
}


===== FILE: src/lib/admin/queries.ts =====
import { getSql } from "@/lib/db/client";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — read queries behind the admin workspace.
 *
 * Every function takes its SQL executor as a parameter (defaulting to the real client) so the pages
 * can be tested against PGlite without a network database. Nothing here invents a number: a count that
 * cannot be computed is returned as `null`, and the UI renders "—" rather than a zero it did not observe.
 */
export interface SqlExecutor {
  query<Row = Record<string, unknown>>(text: string, values?: unknown[]): Promise<Row[]>;
}

/**
 * The production executor. Exported so the write layer can default to it too, instead of every caller
 * having to thread one through (and instead of a route passing a fake one by accident).
 *
 * `transaction` runs the statements on a single session so a data change and its audit row commit or
 * roll back together. Neon's HTTP client has no interactive transactions, so the session client is
 * used for those calls; a failure to open a session surfaces as an error rather than a partial write.
 */
export const defaultExecutor: SqlExecutor = {
  async query<Row>(text: string, values?: unknown[]): Promise<Row[]> {
    const sql = getSql();
    return (await sql.query(text, values)) as Row[];
  },
};

export interface AdminCounts {
  entities: number;
  facts: number;
  evidence: number;
  features: number;
  boundaries: number | null;
  unknown_statuses: number;
  facts_single_source: number;
  facts_without_evidence: number;
  entities_without_geometry: number;
  pending_submissions: number | null;
  open_conflicts: number;
  unreviewed_facts: number;
}

export async function getAdminCounts(execute: SqlExecutor = defaultExecutor): Promise<AdminCounts> {
  const [row] = await execute.query<Record<string, string | number | null>>(`
    SELECT
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM entities) AS entities,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM facts) AS facts,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM evidence) AS evidence,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM features) AS features,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM entities WHERE status = 'unknown') AS unknown_statuses,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
         SELECT f.id FROM facts f JOIN fact_evidence fe ON fe.fact_id = f.id
          GROUP BY f.id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT fe.evidence_id) = 1) AS single) AS facts_single_source,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM facts f
        WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM fact_evidence fe WHERE fe.fact_id = f.id)) AS facts_without_evidence,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM entities e
        WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM features ft WHERE ft.entity_id = e.id)) AS entities_without_geometry,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM facts f
        WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM fact_reviews fr WHERE fr.fact_code = f.code
                          AND fr.review_state <> 'pending')) AS unreviewed_facts,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
         SELECT e.id FROM facts f JOIN entities e ON e.id = f.entity_id
          GROUP BY e.id, f.attribute HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT f.value) > 1) AS conflicts) AS open_conflicts
  `);

  // Counted separately so a database that has not applied 010/011 yet degrades to null (rendered as
  // "—") instead of breaking the whole dashboard.
  const pendingSubmissions = await countOrNull(execute, "SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS n FROM submissions WHERE status = 'pending'");

  const toInt = (value: string | number | null | undefined): number =>
    typeof value === "number" ? value : Number.parseInt(String(value ?? "0"), 10) || 0;

  return {
    entities: toInt(row?.entities),
    facts: toInt(row?.facts),
    evidence: toInt(row?.evidence),
    features: toInt(row?.features),
    // Boundaries are derived from the boundary GeoJSON files, not stored in a table, so this count is
    // supplied by the page that reads those files; null here means "not counted by SQL".
    boundaries: null,
    unknown_statuses: toInt(row?.unknown_statuses),
    facts_single_source: toInt(row?.facts_single_source),
    facts_without_evidence: toInt(row?.facts_without_evidence),
    entities_without_geometry: toInt(row?.entities_without_geometry),
    pending_submissions: pendingSubmissions,
    open_conflicts: toInt(row?.open_conflicts),
    unreviewed_facts: toInt(row?.unreviewed_facts),
  };
}

async function countOrNull(execute: SqlExecutor, text: string, values?: unknown[]): Promise<number | null> {
  try {
    const [row] = await execute.query<{ n: number | string }>(text, values);
    return typeof row?.n === "number" ? row.n : Number.parseInt(String(row?.n ?? ""), 10) || 0;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export interface EntityListRow {
  code: string;
  name_ar: string;
  name_en: string;
  entity_type: string;
  sector: string;
  status: string;
  has_geometry: boolean;
  has_boundary: boolean;
}

export interface EntityListQuery {
  q?: string;
  entity_type?: string;
  sector?: string;
  status?: string;
  has_geometry?: boolean;
  has_boundary?: boolean;
  page?: number;
  page_size?: number;
}

export const ADMIN_PAGE_SIZES = [25, 50, 100] as const;
const DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 25;
const MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 100;

/**
 * Server-side pagination: the LIMIT/OFFSET happens in SQL, and the total is a separate COUNT, so the
 * page never loads the whole table to slice it in memory.
 */
export async function listEntities(
  filters: EntityListQuery,
  execute: SqlExecutor = defaultExecutor,
): Promise<{ rows: EntityListRow[]; total: number; page: number; page_size: number }> {
  const page = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(filters.page ?? 1));
  const pageSize = Math.min(MAX_PAGE_SIZE, Math.max(1, Math.trunc(filters.page_size ?? DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE)));

  const conditions: string[] = [];
  const values: unknown[] = [];
  const add = (sql: string, value: unknown) => { values.push(value); conditions.push(sql.replace("$?", `$${values.length}`)); };

  if (filters.q?.trim()) {
    values.push(`%${filters.q.trim()}%`);
    const placeholder = `$${values.length}`;
    conditions.push(`(e.name_ar ILIKE ${placeholder} OR e.name_en ILIKE ${placeholder} OR e.code ILIKE ${placeholder})`);
  }
  if (filters.entity_type) add("e.entity_type = $?", filters.entity_type);
  if (filters.sector) add("e.sector = $?", filters.sector);
  if (filters.status) add("e.status = $?", filters.status);
  if (filters.has_geometry === true) conditions.push("EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM features f WHERE f.entity_id = e.id)");
  if (filters.has_geometry === false) conditions.push("NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM features f WHERE f.entity_id = e.id)");
  // Boundaries live in the file-derived boundary set, which the database does not model; the filter is
  // applied by the caller that has that set, and is accepted here only to keep the signature explicit.
  const where = conditions.length ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}` : "";

  const countRows = await execute.query<{ total: string | number }>(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM entities e ${where}`, values);
  const total = Number.parseInt(String(countRows[0]?.total ?? "0"), 10) || 0;

  const rows = await execute.query<{
    code: string; name_ar: string; name_en: string; entity_type: string; sector: string; status: string;
    has_geometry: boolean;
  }>(
    `SELECT e.code, e.name_ar, e.name_en, e.entity_type, e.sector, e.status,
            EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM features f WHERE f.entity_id = e.id) AS has_geometry
       FROM entities e ${where}
      ORDER BY e.code
      LIMIT $${values.length + 1} OFFSET $${values.length + 2}`,
    [...values, pageSize, (page - 1) * pageSize],
  );

  return {
    rows: rows.map((row) => ({ ...row, has_geometry: Boolean(row.has_geometry), has_boundary: false })),
    total,
    page,
    page_size: pageSize,
  };
}

export interface FactReviewRow {
  code: string;
  entity_code: string;
  attribute: string;
  value: string;
  unit: string | null;
  valid_as_of: string | null;
  verification_state: string;
  review_state: string;
  review_reason: string | null;
  evidence: Array<{
    code: string; title: string | null; url: string | null; publisher: string | null;
    source_type: string; published_date: string | null; accessed_date: string | null;
  }>;
}

/** Facts queued for review, with every linked evidence row so the reviewer sees the whole case. */
export async function listFactsForReview(
  options: { state?: string; entity_code?: string; q?: string; page?: number; page_size?: number } = {},
  execute: SqlExecutor = defaultExecutor,
): Promise<{ rows: FactReviewRow[]; total: number; page: number; page_size: number }> {
  const page = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(options.page ?? 1));
  const pageSize = Math.min(MAX_PAGE_SIZE, Math.max(1, Math.trunc(options.page_size ?? DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE)));
  const conditions: string[] = [];
  const values: unknown[] = [];
  if (options.state && options.state !== "all") {
    values.push(options.state);
    conditions.push(options.state === "pending"
      ? `COALESCE(fr.review_state, 'pending') = $${values.length}`
      : `fr.review_state = $${values.length}`);
  }
  if (options.entity_code) { values.push(options.entity_code); conditions.push(`e.code = $${values.length}`); }
  if (options.q?.trim()) {
    values.push(`%${options.q.trim()}%`);
    conditions.push(`(f.code ILIKE $${values.length} OR e.name_ar ILIKE $${values.length} OR e.name_en ILIKE $${values.length})`);
  }
  const where = conditions.length ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}` : "";

  const countRows = await execute.query<{ total: string | number }>(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM facts f
       JOIN entities e ON e.id = f.entity_id
       LEFT JOIN fact_reviews fr ON fr.fact_code = f.code ${where}`, values);
  const total = Number.parseInt(String(countRows[0]?.total ?? "0"), 10) || 0;

  const rows = await execute.query<Record<string, unknown>>(
    `SELECT f.code, e.code AS entity_code, f.attribute, f.value, f.unit,
            f.valid_as_of::text AS valid_as_of, f.verification_state,
            COALESCE(fr.review_state, 'pending') AS review_state, fr.reason AS review_reason,
            COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
              'code', ev.code, 'title', ev.title, 'url', ev.url, 'publisher', ev.publisher,
              'source_type', ev.source_type, 'published_date', ev.published_date::text,
              'accessed_date', ev.accessed_date::text
            ) ORDER BY ev.code) FILTER (WHERE ev.id IS NOT NULL), '[]'::jsonb) AS evidence
       FROM facts f
       JOIN entities e ON e.id = f.entity_id
       LEFT JOIN fact_reviews fr ON fr.fact_code = f.code
       LEFT JOIN fact_evidence fe ON fe.fact_id = f.id
       LEFT JOIN evidence ev ON ev.id = fe.evidence_id
       ${where}
      GROUP BY f.id, f.code, e.code, f.attribute, f.value, f.unit, f.valid_as_of, f.verification_state,
               fr.review_state, fr.reason
      ORDER BY (COALESCE(fr.review_state, 'pending') = 'pending') DESC, f.code
      LIMIT $${values.length + 1} OFFSET $${values.length + 2}`,
    [...values, pageSize, (page - 1) * pageSize],
  );

  return { rows: rows as unknown as FactReviewRow[], total, page, page_size: pageSize };
}

/**
 * The filter options, read from the data rather than hardcoded.
 *
 * A hardcoded list drifts: it offers values that no longer exist (nothing to filter) and hides new ones
 * (`partial`, `approved_planned`), which makes a real status look absent. Distinct values from the table
 * are always exactly the values someone can filter by.
 */
export async function getEntityFilterOptions(execute: SqlExecutor = defaultExecutor): Promise<{
  types: string[]; sectors: string[]; statuses: string[];
}> {
  const rows = await execute.query<{ kind: string; value: string }>(
    `SELECT 'type' AS kind, entity_type AS value FROM entities
     UNION SELECT 'sector', sector FROM entities
     UNION SELECT 'status', status FROM entities`,
  );
  const collect = (kind: string) => rows.filter((row) => row.kind === kind).map((row) => row.value).sort();
  return { types: collect("type"), sectors: collect("sector"), statuses: collect("status") };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/admin/review.ts =====
import { z } from "zod";

/**
 * BATCH 13 — fact review actions.
 *
 * Rules this module enforces, regardless of what the UI sends:
 *   1. Nothing is deleted or overwritten. A decision writes a `fact_reviews` row and an audit row.
 *   2. `verified` still means an official evidence row with a non-empty URL (migration 008). An
 *      approval of a fact whose evidence does not meet that bar keeps the fact `candidate` and says
 *      so, instead of quietly upgrading the claim.
 *   3. Reject and dispute require a written reason.
 */

export const FACT_REVIEW_STATES = ["pending", "approved", "rejected", "disputed"] as const;
export type FactReviewState = (typeof FACT_REVIEW_STATES)[number];

export const REVIEW_ACTIONS = ["approve", "reject", "dispute", "reset"] as const;
export type ReviewAction = (typeof REVIEW_ACTIONS)[number];

export const reviewRequestSchema = z.strictObject({
  action: z.enum(REVIEW_ACTIONS),
  reason: z.string().trim().min(1).max(2000).optional(),
  /** The review state the caller believes the fact is in, to catch two reviewers racing. */
  expected_state: z.enum(FACT_REVIEW_STATES).optional(),
}).superRefine((value, ctx) => {
  if ((value.action === "reject" || value.action === "dispute") && !value.reason) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["reason"], message: `A written reason is required to ${value.action} a fact.` });
  }
});

export type ReviewRequest = z.infer<typeof reviewRequestSchema>;

/** The subset of an evidence row that decides whether a fact may be called verified. */
export interface EvidenceFacts {
  code: string;
  source_type: string;
  url: string | null;
}

export interface ReviewFactInput {
  code: string;
  entity_code: string;
  attribute: string;
  value: string;
  verification_state: string;
  evidence_codes: readonly string[];
}

/** Migration 008's rule, in one place: official source AND a non-empty URL. */
export function evidenceSupportsVerification(evidence: EvidenceFacts | undefined | null): boolean {
  if (!evidence) return false;
  return evidence.source_type === "official" && typeof evidence.url === "string" && evidence.url.trim().length > 0;
}

export function factMayBeVerified(fact: Pick<ReviewFactInput, "evidence_codes">, evidenceByCode: ReadonlyMap<string, EvidenceFacts>): boolean {
  if (fact.evidence_codes.length === 0) return false;
  return fact.evidence_codes.some((code) => evidenceSupportsVerification(evidenceByCode.get(code)));
}

export interface ReviewOutcome {
  action: ReviewAction;
  next_review_state: FactReviewState;
  /** What `verification_state` becomes. Unchanged unless the approval is actually backed. */
  next_verification_state: string;
  verification_unchanged_reason: string | null;
  audit: {
    action: "fact.approve" | "fact.reject" | "fact.dispute" | "fact.reset";
    before_state: Record<string, unknown>;
    after_state: Record<string, unknown>;
    reason: string | null;
  };
}

/**
 * Pure decision function: given the fact, its evidence and the requested action, decide the next
 * state and build the audit payload. Nothing here touches a database, so the rules can be tested
 * exhaustively.
 */
export function decideFactReview(
  fact: ReviewFactInput,
  evidenceByCode: ReadonlyMap<string, EvidenceFacts>,
  request: ReviewRequest,
  currentReviewState: FactReviewState = "pending",
): ReviewOutcome {
  const mayVerify = factMayBeVerified(fact, evidenceByCode);
  const beforeState = {
    review_state: currentReviewState,
    verification_state: fact.verification_state,
    evidence_codes: [...fact.evidence_codes],
  };

  const build = (
    nextReview: FactReviewState,
    nextVerification: string,
    unchangedReason: string | null,
    action: ReviewOutcome["audit"]["action"],
  ): ReviewOutcome => ({
    action: request.action,
    next_review_state: nextReview,
    next_verification_state: nextVerification,
    verification_unchanged_reason: unchangedReason,
    audit: {
      action,
      before_state: beforeState,
      after_state: { review_state: nextReview, verification_state: nextVerification, evidence_codes: [...fact.evidence_codes] },
      reason: request.reason ?? null,
    },
  });

  switch (request.action) {
    case "approve":
      // Approval is a human review decision, not a verification. It only promotes a claim when the
      // evidence actually satisfies migration 008; otherwise the claim stays a candidate and the
      // reviewer is told why.
      return mayVerify
        ? build("approved", "verified", null, "fact.approve")
        : build("approved", fact.verification_state === "verified" ? "verified" : "candidate",
          "approved, but not marked verified: no linked evidence is an official source with a URL", "fact.approve");
    case "reject":
      // Rejection never deletes the claim; it records that a human refused it.
      return build("rejected", fact.verification_state, null, "fact.reject");
    case "dispute":
      return build("disputed", "disputed", null, "fact.dispute");
    case "reset":
      return build("pending", fact.verification_state, null, "fact.reset");
  }
}

/** Human-readable Arabic/English label for the review state, used by the admin UI. */
export const REVIEW_STATE_LABELS: Record<FactReviewState, { ar: string; en: string }> = {
  pending: { ar: "بانتظار المراجعة", en: "Pending review" },
  approved: { ar: "معتمدة", en: "Approved" },
  rejected: { ar: "مرفوضة", en: "Rejected" },
  disputed: { ar: "متنازع عليها", en: "Disputed" },
};

/**
 * Duplicate detection for the import pipeline: by exact code, by normalised Arabic name, and by
 * alias. Returns every existing entity the incoming row would collide with.
 */
export interface DuplicateCandidate {
  code: string;
  name_ar: string;
  name_en: string;
  aliases: readonly string[];
}

export interface IncomingRow {
  code: string;
  name_ar: string;
  name_en: string;
  aliases?: readonly string[];
}

export interface DuplicateMatch {
  match_type: "code" | "normalised_name_ar" | "normalised_name_en" | "alias";
  incoming: IncomingRow;
  existing_code: string;
  detail: string;
}

export function normalizeArabicName(value: string): string {
  return value
    .normalize("NFKC")
    .replace(/[\u0640\u200c\u200d\u00a0]/gu, "")
    .replace(/[\u064b-\u0652]/gu, "")
    .replace(/[أإآٱ]/gu, "ا")
    .replace(/ى/gu, "ي")
    .replace(/ة/gu, "ه")
    .replace(/\s+/gu, " ")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

export function detectDuplicates(
  incoming: readonly IncomingRow[],
  existing: readonly DuplicateCandidate[],
): DuplicateMatch[] {
  const byCode = new Map(existing.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  const byNameAr = new Map<string, DuplicateCandidate[]>();
  const byNameEn = new Map<string, DuplicateCandidate[]>();
  const byAlias = new Map<string, DuplicateCandidate[]>();
  const push = (map: Map<string, DuplicateCandidate[]>, key: string, entity: DuplicateCandidate) => {
    if (!key) return;
    map.set(key, [...(map.get(key) ?? []), entity]);
  };
  for (const entity of existing) {
    push(byNameAr, normalizeArabicName(entity.name_ar), entity);
    push(byNameEn, normalizeArabicName(entity.name_en), entity);
    for (const alias of entity.aliases) push(byAlias, normalizeArabicName(alias), entity);
  }

  const matches: DuplicateMatch[] = [];
  for (const row of incoming) {
    const exact = byCode.get(row.code);
    if (exact) {
      matches.push({ match_type: "code", incoming: row, existing_code: exact.code, detail: `Entity code ${row.code} already exists.` });
      // A code match already identifies the row; name checks would only add noise.
      continue;
    }
    const nameAr = normalizeArabicName(row.name_ar);
    const nameEn = normalizeArabicName(row.name_en);
    for (const entity of byNameAr.get(nameAr) ?? []) {
      if (entity.code === row.code) continue;
      matches.push({ match_type: "normalised_name_ar", incoming: row, existing_code: entity.code, detail: `Arabic name «${row.name_ar}» matches ${entity.code}.` });
    }
    for (const entity of byNameEn.get(nameEn) ?? []) {
      if (entity.code === row.code) continue;
      matches.push({ match_type: "normalised_name_en", incoming: row, existing_code: entity.code, detail: `English name «${row.name_en}» matches ${entity.code}.` });
    }
    for (const alias of row.aliases ?? []) {
      for (const entity of byAlias.get(normalizeArabicName(alias)) ?? []) {
        if (entity.code === row.code) continue;
        matches.push({ match_type: "alias", incoming: row, existing_code: entity.code, detail: `Alias «${alias}» is already attached to ${entity.code}.` });
      }
    }
  }
  return matches;
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/admin-import.ts =====
import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";
import path from "node:path";
import { z } from "zod";
import {
  runSeedImport,
  type ImportSyncReport,
  type SeedBundleReport,
  type SeedDbSession,
} from "@/lib/atlas/seed";
import { requireAdminImportEnv } from "@/lib/env";
import {
  AdminUnauthorizedError,
  RequestValidationError,
} from "@/lib/errors";

const adminImportRequestBodySchema = z.strictObject({
  dryRun: z.boolean().optional().default(true),
});

export interface AdminSeedImportResponseBody {
  ok: true;
  mode: "dry_run" | "import";
  dryRun: boolean;
  summary: {
    entitiesCount: number;
    evidenceCount: number;
    factsCount: number;
    relationsCount: number;
    factEvidenceLinksCount: number;
    featuresCount: number;
    importSync: ImportSyncReport;
  };
  report: SeedBundleReport;
  timestamp: string;
}

export interface ExecuteAdminSeedImportOptions {
  request: Request;
  rawEnv?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
  seedDir?: string;
  featuresPath?: string | null;
  sessionFactory?: () => SeedDbSession;
}

/**
 * Compares two tokens in constant time by hashing both with SHA-256 first,
 * preventing timing side-channels from leaking token length or prefix characters.
 */
export function verifyAdminImportToken(
  providedToken: string | null,
  expectedToken: string,
): boolean {
  if (!providedToken || providedToken.trim().length === 0) {
    return false;
  }

  const providedDigest = createHash("sha256")
    .update(providedToken.trim(), "utf8")
    .digest();
  const expectedDigest = createHash("sha256")
    .update(expectedToken.trim(), "utf8")
    .digest();

  return timingSafeEqual(providedDigest, expectedDigest);
}

export function extractAdminTokenFromHeaders(headers: Headers): string | null {
  const authHeader = headers.get("authorization");
  if (authHeader) {
    const match = /^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i.exec(authHeader.trim());
    if (match?.[1]) {
      return match[1].trim();
    }
    return null;
  }

  const customHeader = headers.get("x-admin-import-token");
  if (customHeader && customHeader.trim().length > 0) {
    return customHeader.trim();
  }

  return null;
}

export function parseAdminImportRequestBody(rawText: string): {
  dryRun: boolean;
} {
  const trimmed = rawText.trim();
  if (trimmed.length === 0) {
    return { dryRun: true };
  }

  let parsedJson: unknown;
  try {
    parsedJson = JSON.parse(trimmed);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new RequestValidationError(
      `Invalid JSON request body: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
      [
        {
          path: "body",
          message: "Request body must be empty or valid JSON (e.g. {\"dryRun\": true}).",
        },
      ],
    );
  }

  const validated = adminImportRequestBodySchema.safeParse(parsedJson);
  if (!validated.success) {
    const issues = validated.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
      path: issue.path.length > 0 ? `body.${issue.path.join(".")}` : "body",
      message: issue.message,
    }));
    throw new RequestValidationError(
      `Invalid request parameters: ${issues.map((i) => `${i.path}: ${i.message}`).join("; ")}`,
      issues,
    );
  }

  return validated.data;
}

/**
 * Protected server-side handler for `POST /api/admin/import-seed`.
 * - Fails closed (503) if ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN is not configured on the server.
 * - Rejects query-string tokens (400) so secrets never leak in access logs.
 * - Verifies Bearer / x-admin-import-token in constant time (401 on failure).
 * - Defaults to `dryRun: true` unless explicitly passed `{"dryRun": false}`.
 * - Executes inside Vercel using server-side DATABASE_URL without ever exposing it.
 */
export async function executeAdminSeedImport(
  options: ExecuteAdminSeedImportOptions,
): Promise<AdminSeedImportResponseBody> {
  const { ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN } = requireAdminImportEnv(
    options.rawEnv ?? process.env,
  );

  const parsedUrl = new URL(options.request.url);
  if (
    parsedUrl.searchParams.has("token") ||
    parsedUrl.searchParams.has("admin_import_token")
  ) {
    throw new RequestValidationError(
      "Do not pass ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN in URL query parameters. Supply `Authorization: Bearer <token>` header instead.",
      [
        {
          path: "query.token",
          message:
            "Admin token must be sent in the Authorization header, never in the URL.",
        },
      ],
    );
  }

  const providedToken = extractAdminTokenFromHeaders(options.request.headers);
  if (!verifyAdminImportToken(providedToken, ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN)) {
    throw new AdminUnauthorizedError();
  }

  const rawBodyText = await options.request.text();
  const { dryRun } = parseAdminImportRequestBody(rawBodyText);

  const result = await runSeedImport({
    ...(options.seedDir ? { seedDir: options.seedDir } : {}),
    featuresPath: options.featuresPath !== undefined
      ? options.featuresPath
      : options.seedDir
        ? null
        : path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "features", "features.geojson"),
    dryRun,
    ...(options.sessionFactory
      ? { sessionFactory: options.sessionFactory }
      : {}),
  });

  return {
    ok: true,
    mode: result.dryRun ? "dry_run" : "import",
    dryRun: result.dryRun,
    summary: {
      entitiesCount: result.entitiesCount,
      evidenceCount: result.evidenceCount,
      factsCount: result.factsCount,
      relationsCount: result.relationsCount,
      factEvidenceLinksCount: result.factEvidenceLinksCount,
      featuresCount: result.featuresCount,
      importSync: result.importSync,
    },
    report: result.report,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/area-boundaries.ts =====
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import type { EntitySeed } from "@/lib/atlas/schema";
import type { PointOutsideBoundaryFinding } from "./point-outside-boundary";

export type { PointOutsideBoundaryFinding } from "./point-outside-boundary";

export interface AreaBoundaryFeature {
  type: "Feature";
  id: string;
  geometry: { type: "Polygon" | "MultiPolygon"; coordinates: unknown };
  properties: {
    code: string;
    entity_code: string;
    osm_type?: "way" | "relation";
    osm_id?: number;
    osm_version?: number;
    license: "ODbL-1.0" | "CC BY-IGO";
    properties: {
      role: "boundary";
      entity_type: string;
      boundary_kind: "administrative" | "place" | "landuse";
      source_kind: "administrative" | "place" | "landuse";
      area_km2: number;
      display_label_ar: string | null;
      display_label_en: string | null;
      admin_level: string | null;
      name: string | null;
      name_ar: string | null;
      name_en: string | null;
      osm_tags?: Record<string, unknown>;
      source_ids?: { osm_type: string; osm_id: number; osm_version: number };
      source: string;
      attribution: string;
      license: "ODbL-1.0" | "CC BY-IGO";
      retrieval_date: string;
    };
  };
}

const boundaryFile = (root: string) => path.join(root, "data/features/boundaries.geojson");
const officialBoundaryFile = (root: string) => path.join(root, "data/features/codab-entity-boundaries.geojson");
const cache = new Map<string, Promise<AreaBoundaryFeature[]>>();
const officialCache = new Map<string, Promise<AreaBoundaryFeature[]>>();

function isFinitePosition(value: unknown): value is number[] {
  return Array.isArray(value) && value.length >= 2 && typeof value[0] === "number" && Number.isFinite(value[0]) &&
    typeof value[1] === "number" && Number.isFinite(value[1]);
}

function validRing(ring: unknown): boolean {
  if (!Array.isArray(ring) || ring.length < 4 || !ring.every(isFinitePosition)) return false;
  const first = ring[0] as number[];
  const last = ring.at(-1) as number[];
  return first[0] === last[0] && first[1] === last[1] &&
    new Set(ring.map((point) => `${(point as number[])[0]},${(point as number[])[1]}`)).size >= 3;
}

function validPolygonGeometry(geometry: unknown): geometry is AreaBoundaryFeature["geometry"] {
  if (!geometry || typeof geometry !== "object") return false;
  const value = geometry as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (value.type === "Polygon") return Array.isArray(value.coordinates) && value.coordinates.length > 0 && value.coordinates.every(validRing);
  if (value.type === "MultiPolygon") return Array.isArray(value.coordinates) && value.coordinates.length > 0 &&
    value.coordinates.every((polygon) => Array.isArray(polygon) && polygon.length > 0 && polygon.every(validRing));
  return false;
}

export function isAreaBoundaryFeature(value: unknown): value is AreaBoundaryFeature {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return false;
  const feature = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const props = feature.properties;
  if (feature.type !== "Feature" || typeof feature.id !== "string" || !validPolygonGeometry(feature.geometry) ||
      !props || typeof props !== "object") return false;
  const metadata = (props as Record<string, unknown>).properties;
  if (!metadata || typeof metadata !== "object") return false;
  const p = props as Record<string, unknown>;
  const m = metadata as Record<string, unknown>;
  const ids = m.source_ids as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  return typeof p.code === "string" && typeof p.entity_code === "string" &&
    (p.osm_type === "way" || p.osm_type === "relation") && Number.isInteger(p.osm_id) && Number(p.osm_id) > 0 &&
    Number.isInteger(p.osm_version) && Number(p.osm_version) > 0 && p.license === "ODbL-1.0" &&
    m.role === "boundary" && ["administrative", "place", "landuse"].includes(String(m.boundary_kind)) &&
    ["administrative", "place", "landuse"].includes(String(m.source_kind)) &&
    m.source === "OpenStreetMap" && m.attribution === "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0" &&
    m.license === "ODbL-1.0" && typeof m.retrieval_date === "string" &&
    Boolean(ids && ids.osm_type === p.osm_type && ids.osm_id === p.osm_id && ids.osm_version === p.osm_version);
}

async function loadBoundaries(root: string): Promise<AreaBoundaryFeature[]> {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(await readFile(boundaryFile(root), "utf8")) as { features?: unknown } | null;
    return parsed && Array.isArray(parsed.features) ? parsed.features.filter(isAreaBoundaryFeature) : [];
  } catch (error) {
    if (error && typeof error === "object" && "code" in error && error.code === "ENOENT") return [];
    throw error;
  }
}

function getCachedBoundaries(root: string): Promise<AreaBoundaryFeature[]> {
  let pending = cache.get(root);
  if (!pending) {
    pending = loadBoundaries(root);
    cache.set(root, pending);
  }
  return pending;
}

async function loadOfficialBoundaries(root: string): Promise<AreaBoundaryFeature[]> {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(await readFile(officialBoundaryFile(root), "utf8")) as { features?: Array<Record<string, unknown>> };
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed.features)) return [];
    return parsed.features.flatMap((raw) => {
      const props = raw.properties as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
      const geometry = raw.geometry as AreaBoundaryFeature["geometry"] | undefined;
      if (!props || !geometry || typeof props.entity_code !== "string" || typeof props.code !== "string") return [];
      const feature = {
        type: "Feature" as const, id: String(raw.id ?? props.code), geometry,
        properties: {
          code: props.code, entity_code: props.entity_code, license: "CC BY-IGO" as const,
          properties: {
            role: "boundary" as const, entity_type: "administrative_unit", boundary_kind: "administrative" as const,
            source_kind: "administrative" as const, area_km2: Number(props.area_sqkm) || 0,
            display_label_ar: typeof props.name_ar === "string" ? props.name_ar : null,
            display_label_en: typeof props.name_en === "string" ? props.name_en : null,
            admin_level: String(props.level ?? "2"), name: null,
            name_ar: typeof props.name_ar === "string" ? props.name_ar : null,
            name_en: typeof props.name_en === "string" ? props.name_en : null,
            source: String(props.source ?? "CAPMAS COD-AB 2017 (reviewed 2024)"),
            attribution: String(props.attribution ?? "حدود إدارية © الجهاز المركزي للتعبئة العامة والإحصاء عبر OCHA/HDX (CC BY-IGO)"),
            license: "CC BY-IGO" as const, retrieval_date: "2026-11-15",
          },
        },
      };
      return [feature as AreaBoundaryFeature];
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error && typeof error === "object" && "code" in error && error.code === "ENOENT") return [];
    throw error;
  }
}

function getCachedOfficialBoundaries(root: string): Promise<AreaBoundaryFeature[]> {
  let pending = officialCache.get(root);
  if (!pending) { pending = loadOfficialBoundaries(root); officialCache.set(root, pending); }
  return pending;
}

export async function getAreaBoundaryCodes(root = process.cwd()): Promise<Set<string>> {
  const [official, osm] = await Promise.all([getCachedOfficialBoundaries(root), getCachedBoundaries(root)]);
  return new Set([...official, ...osm].map((feature) => feature.properties.entity_code));
}

export async function getAreaBoundary(entityCode: string, root = process.cwd()): Promise<AreaBoundaryFeature | null> {
  const official = await getCachedOfficialBoundaries(root);
  return official.find((feature) => feature.properties.entity_code === entityCode) ??
    (await getCachedBoundaries(root)).find((feature) => feature.properties.entity_code === entityCode) ?? null;
}

export async function getOsmAreaBoundary(entityCode: string, root = process.cwd()): Promise<AreaBoundaryFeature | null> {
  return (await getCachedBoundaries(root)).find((feature) => feature.properties.entity_code === entityCode) ?? null;
}

export function isBoundaryEligibleEntity(entity: Pick<EntitySeed, "entity_type">): boolean {
  return ["city", "district", "neighborhood", "authority"].includes(entity.entity_type);
}

const normalize = (value: string) => {
  const normalized = value.normalize("NFKC").toLocaleLowerCase("und")
    .replace(/[أإآٱ]/gu, "ا").replace(/ة/gu, "ه").replace(/ى/gu, "ي");
  const generic = new Set(["حي", "الحي", "قسم", "القسم", "مدينه", "المدينه", "منطقه", "المنطقه", "district", "city"]);
  return normalized.match(/[\p{L}\p{N}]+/gu)?.filter((token) => !generic.has(token)).join("") ?? "";
};
const adminLevels: Record<string, Set<string>> = {
  city: new Set(["4", "6", "8"]), district: new Set(["6", "8", "9", "10"]),
  neighborhood: new Set(["8", "9", "10"]), authority: new Set(["4", "6", "8", "9", "10"]),
  development_zone: new Set(["6", "8", "9", "10"]),
};

export function matchOsmBoundaryElement(
  element: Record<string, unknown>, entity: EntitySeed,
  bbox: readonly [number, number, number, number] = [30.2, 28.8, 32.5, 31.0],
): AreaBoundaryFeature | null {
  if (!isBoundaryEligibleEntity(entity) || !["way", "relation"].includes(String(element.type))) return null;
  const tags = element.tags;
  if (!tags || typeof tags !== "object") return null;
  const tagMap = tags as Record<string, unknown>;
  const kind: "administrative" | "place" | "landuse" | null = tagMap.boundary === "administrative" ? "administrative" :
    ["city", "town", "village", "suburb", "neighbourhood", "neighborhood", "quarter", "district", "borough"].includes(String(tagMap.place)) ? "place" :
      tagMap.landuse === "residential" && ["name", "name:ar", "name:en"].some((key) => typeof tagMap[key] === "string") ? "landuse" : null;
  if (!kind) return null;
  const adminLevel = typeof tagMap.admin_level === "string" ? tagMap.admin_level : null;
  if (kind === "administrative" && (!adminLevel || !adminLevels[entity.entity_type]?.has(adminLevel))) return null;
  const labels = ["name", "name:ar", "name:en", "official_name", "alt_name", "old_name", "short_name"]
    .flatMap((key) => typeof tagMap[key] === "string" ? String(tagMap[key]).split(";") : []);
  const entityNames = [entity.name_ar, entity.name_en, ...entity.aliases].map(normalize).filter(Boolean);
  if (!labels.some((label) => entityNames.includes(normalize(label)))) return null;
  const coordinates = element.geometry;
  if (!Array.isArray(coordinates) || coordinates.length < 4 || !coordinates.every((position) =>
    position && typeof position === "object" && typeof (position as Record<string, unknown>).lon === "number" && typeof (position as Record<string, unknown>).lat === "number")) return null;
  const ring = (coordinates as Array<{ lon: number; lat: number }>).map((position) => [position.lon, position.lat]);
  if (ring[0]?.[0] !== ring.at(-1)?.[0] || ring[0]?.[1] !== ring.at(-1)?.[1]) return null;
  const [west, south, east, north] = bbox;
  if (ring.some(([lon, lat]) => lon! < west || lon! > east || lat! < south || lat! > north)) return null;
  const id = Number(element.id); const version = Number(element.version);
  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0 || !Number.isInteger(version) || version <= 0) return null;
  const osmType = element.type as "way" | "relation";
  const code = `OSM-BOUNDARY-${osmType.toUpperCase()}-${id}-${entity.code}`;
  const centerLat = ring.reduce((sum, position) => sum + (position[1] ?? 0), 0) / ring.length;
  let twiceArea = 0;
  for (let index = 0; index < ring.length - 1; index += 1) {
    const left = ring[index]!;
    const right = ring[index + 1]!;
    twiceArea += (left[0]! * right[1]!) - (right[0]! * left[1]!);
  }
  const areaKm2 = Math.abs(twiceArea / 2) * 111.32 * 110.574 * Math.max(0.1, Math.abs(Math.cos(centerLat * Math.PI / 180)));
  const props = {
    role: "boundary" as const, entity_type: entity.entity_type, boundary_kind: kind, source_kind: kind,
    area_km2: areaKm2, display_label_ar: null, display_label_en: null, admin_level: adminLevel,
    name: typeof tagMap.name === "string" ? tagMap.name : null,
    name_ar: typeof tagMap["name:ar"] === "string" ? tagMap["name:ar"] : null,
    name_en: typeof tagMap["name:en"] === "string" ? tagMap["name:en"] : null,
    osm_tags: tagMap, source_ids: { osm_type: osmType, osm_id: id, osm_version: version }, source: "OpenStreetMap" as const,
    attribution: "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0" as const, license: "ODbL-1.0" as const,
    retrieval_date: "2026-11-14",
  };
  const feature: AreaBoundaryFeature = {
    type: "Feature", id: code,
    geometry: { type: "Polygon", coordinates: [ring] },
    properties: { code, entity_code: entity.code, osm_type: osmType, osm_id: id, osm_version: version,
      license: "ODbL-1.0", properties: props },
  };
  return isAreaBoundaryFeature(feature) ? feature : null;
}

/**
 * BATCH 12.5 — entities whose recorded point falls outside their own official boundary.
 *
 * The report is produced by `scripts/point_outside_boundary_report.py` and is deliberately a *report*,
 * not a correction: no point is moved. This loader only tells the researcher view that a finding
 * exists, so the panel can say «النقطة خارج الحدود الرسمية» without recomputing geometry at render time.
 */
const pointOutsideCache = new Map<string, Promise<Map<string, PointOutsideBoundaryFinding>>>();

export async function getPointOutsideBoundaryFindings(
  root = process.cwd(),
): Promise<Map<string, PointOutsideBoundaryFinding>> {
  const cached = pointOutsideCache.get(root);
  if (cached) return cached;
  const pending = readFile(path.join(root, "data/features/point-outside-boundary-report.json"), "utf8")
    .then((text) => {
      const report = JSON.parse(text) as { findings?: PointOutsideBoundaryFinding[] };
      const byCode = new Map<string, PointOutsideBoundaryFinding>();
      // One row per entity is enough for the panel note; the report keeps every point occurrence.
      for (const finding of report.findings ?? []) if (!byCode.has(finding.entity_code)) byCode.set(finding.entity_code, finding);
      return byCode;
    })
    .catch(() => new Map<string, PointOutsideBoundaryFinding>());
  pointOutsideCache.set(root, pending);
  return pending;
}

export async function hasPointOutsideBoundary(entityCode: string, root = process.cwd()): Promise<boolean> {
  return (await getPointOutsideBoundaryFindings(root)).has(entityCode);
}
