===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/display-settings.ts =====
/**
 * BATCH 14.2 — saved display settings.
 *
 * The map has accumulated enough switches (layer groups, live modes, line toggles, reference networks,
 * theme, opacity) that losing them on every reload is a real annoyance, and saving them badly is worse
 * than not saving them. This module is the whole contract, and it is deliberately paranoid:
 *
 * - **Versioned.** A stored blob from an older build has a different shape; it is discarded, not merged.
 *   Merging unknown history is how a settings file starts silently changing what a user sees.
 * - **Strict.** Every field is validated on read. An unknown key is dropped; a wrong type falls back to
 *   the default for that field rather than poisoning the whole object.
 * - **Corrupt-safe.** `JSON.parse` throws on hand-edited or truncated storage, so every read is wrapped.
 *   A browser with storage disabled must behave exactly like a browser with no saved settings.
 * - **URL wins.** A shared link carries the sender's intent; it must beat whatever the recipient saved.
 *   That precedence lives in `resolveDisplaySettings`, so it is testable rather than buried in a component.
 *
 * Nothing here touches `window`: the parse/serialize halves are pure and the storage wrapper takes the
 * `Storage` object as an argument. That is what makes the failure modes testable at all.
 */

export const DISPLAY_SETTINGS_VERSION = 2;
export const DISPLAY_SETTINGS_KEY = "gca-display-settings-v2";

/** The layer groups the map draws, in the order the panel lists them. */
export const LAYER_GROUPS = ["infrastructure", "transport", "areas", "projects", "reference"] as const;
export type LayerGroup = (typeof LAYER_GROUPS)[number];
export type LayerGroupVisibility = Record<LayerGroup, boolean>;

/** Opacity is stored per group as 0–1 and clamped on read, never trusted as stored. */
export type LayerOpacity = Record<LayerGroup, number>;

export interface DisplaySettings {
  version: typeof DISPLAY_SETTINGS_VERSION;
  layerGroups: LayerGroupVisibility;
  layerOpacity: LayerOpacity;
  showRoadNetwork: boolean;
  showRailNetwork: boolean;
  showDisusedRail: boolean;
  showInterchanges: boolean;
  theme: "day" | "night";
}

export const DEFAULT_LAYER_GROUPS: LayerGroupVisibility = {
  infrastructure: true, transport: true, areas: true, projects: true, reference: true,
};

/**
 * Defaults are 1 for every group: "opacity 100%" must render byte-identically to the map before this
 * feature existed. A default of 0.9 would have subtly changed every existing view.
 */
export const DEFAULT_LAYER_OPACITY: LayerOpacity = {
  infrastructure: 1, transport: 1, areas: 1, projects: 1, reference: 1,
};

export const DEFAULT_DISPLAY_SETTINGS: DisplaySettings = {
  version: DISPLAY_SETTINGS_VERSION,
  layerGroups: { ...DEFAULT_LAYER_GROUPS },
  layerOpacity: { ...DEFAULT_LAYER_OPACITY },
  showRoadNetwork: true,
  showRailNetwork: false,
  showDisusedRail: false,
  showInterchanges: true,
  theme: "night",
};

const isRecord = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> => typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
const isBoolean = (value: unknown): value is boolean => typeof value === "boolean";

/** Opacity is clamped rather than rejected, so a corrupt 0 or 5 still yields a usable map. */
export function clampOpacity(value: unknown): number {
  if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) return 1;
  return Math.min(1, Math.max(0.1, Math.round(value * 100) / 100));
}

function readLayerGroups(value: unknown): LayerGroupVisibility {
  const groups = { ...DEFAULT_LAYER_GROUPS };
  if (!isRecord(value)) return groups;
  for (const group of LAYER_GROUPS) {
    const stored = value[group];
    if (isBoolean(stored)) groups[group] = stored;
  }
  return groups;
}

function readLayerOpacity(value: unknown): LayerOpacity {
  const opacity = { ...DEFAULT_LAYER_OPACITY };
  if (!isRecord(value)) return opacity;
  for (const group of LAYER_GROUPS) {
    if (group in value) opacity[group] = clampOpacity(value[group]);
  }
  return opacity;
}

/**
 * Parse a stored blob.
 *
 * Returns `null` — not defaults — when the blob is unusable, so the caller can tell "nothing saved" from
 * "saved settings that happen to equal the defaults". A version mismatch is unusable: the shapes differ,
 * and guessing how to migrate a preference file is how people end up with a map they cannot reset.
 */
export function parseDisplaySettings(raw: string | null | undefined): DisplaySettings | null {
  if (typeof raw !== "string" || raw.trim() === "") return null;
  let parsed: unknown;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  if (!isRecord(parsed)) return null;
  if (parsed.version !== DISPLAY_SETTINGS_VERSION) return null;

  // Field by field, so one bad value cannot discard an otherwise good set of preferences.
  return {
    version: DISPLAY_SETTINGS_VERSION,
    layerGroups: readLayerGroups(parsed.layerGroups),
    layerOpacity: readLayerOpacity(parsed.layerOpacity),
    showRoadNetwork: isBoolean(parsed.showRoadNetwork) ? parsed.showRoadNetwork : DEFAULT_DISPLAY_SETTINGS.showRoadNetwork,
    showRailNetwork: isBoolean(parsed.showRailNetwork) ? parsed.showRailNetwork : DEFAULT_DISPLAY_SETTINGS.showRailNetwork,
    showDisusedRail: isBoolean(parsed.showDisusedRail) ? parsed.showDisusedRail : DEFAULT_DISPLAY_SETTINGS.showDisusedRail,
    showInterchanges: isBoolean(parsed.showInterchanges) ? parsed.showInterchanges : DEFAULT_DISPLAY_SETTINGS.showInterchanges,
    theme: parsed.theme === "day" || parsed.theme === "night" ? parsed.theme : DEFAULT_DISPLAY_SETTINGS.theme,
  };
}

/** Serialize for storage. Keys are written in a fixed order so the value is stable and diffable. */
export function serializeDisplaySettings(settings: DisplaySettings): string {
  return JSON.stringify({
    version: DISPLAY_SETTINGS_VERSION,
    layerGroups: Object.fromEntries(LAYER_GROUPS.map((group) => [group, settings.layerGroups[group]])),
    layerOpacity: Object.fromEntries(LAYER_GROUPS.map((group) => [group, clampOpacity(settings.layerOpacity[group])])),
    showRoadNetwork: settings.showRoadNetwork,
    showRailNetwork: settings.showRailNetwork,
    showDisusedRail: settings.showDisusedRail,
    showInterchanges: settings.showInterchanges,
    theme: settings.theme,
  });
}

/**
 * Read from a Storage-like object. Any thrown error (private mode, disabled storage, quota) becomes
 * `null`: a browser that cannot store preferences must still work, just without remembering them.
 */
export function readDisplaySettings(storage: Pick<Storage, "getItem"> | null | undefined): DisplaySettings | null {
  if (!storage) return null;
  try {
    return parseDisplaySettings(storage.getItem(DISPLAY_SETTINGS_KEY));
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** Write to a Storage-like object. Returns whether it actually stuck, so a caller can stay quiet on failure. */
export function writeDisplaySettings(storage: Pick<Storage, "setItem"> | null | undefined, settings: DisplaySettings): boolean {
  if (!storage) return false;
  try {
    storage.setItem(DISPLAY_SETTINGS_KEY, serializeDisplaySettings(settings));
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function clearDisplaySettings(storage: Pick<Storage, "removeItem"> | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!storage) return false;
  try {
    storage.removeItem(DISPLAY_SETTINGS_KEY);
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/** What a URL is allowed to override. Deliberately narrow: only what a shared link is meant to convey. */
export interface UrlDisplayIntent {
  layerGroups?: Partial<LayerGroupVisibility>;
  showRoadNetwork?: boolean;
  showRailNetwork?: boolean;
  showDisusedRail?: boolean;
}

/**
 * URL beats saved settings, per-key.
 *
 * If the link says `layers=transport`, the recipient sees only transport — not their saved layers merged
 * with it, which would quietly show more than the sender was pointing at. Keys the URL is silent about
 * keep the saved value, so a link about one thing does not reset everything else.
 */
export function resolveDisplaySettings(saved: DisplaySettings | null, url: UrlDisplayIntent | null): DisplaySettings {
  const base = saved ?? DEFAULT_DISPLAY_SETTINGS;
  if (!url) return base;
  const merged: DisplaySettings = {
    ...base,
    layerGroups: { ...base.layerGroups, ...(url.layerGroups ?? {}) },
    layerOpacity: { ...base.layerOpacity },
  };
  if (isBoolean(url.showRoadNetwork)) merged.showRoadNetwork = url.showRoadNetwork;
  if (isBoolean(url.showRailNetwork)) merged.showRailNetwork = url.showRailNetwork;
  if (isBoolean(url.showDisusedRail)) merged.showDisusedRail = url.showDisusedRail;
  return merged;
}

/** True when the settings are the defaults, so the UI can disable «إعادة الضبط» instead of no-oping. */
export function isDefaultSettings(settings: DisplaySettings): boolean {
  return serializeDisplaySettings(settings) === serializeDisplaySettings(DEFAULT_DISPLAY_SETTINGS);
}

/**
 * Parse `?layers=` into an intent. An unknown group name is ignored, never invented as `false`.
 *
 * `?layers=` with an empty value means "no intent", not "hide everything": an empty parameter is what a
 * query-string builder leaves behind when every group is on, and reading it as "hide all" would hand the
 * recipient a blank map from a link whose sender was looking at a full one.
 */
export function parseLayersParam(value: string | null): Partial<LayerGroupVisibility> | undefined {
  if (value === null) return undefined;
  const requested = new Set(value.split(",").map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean));
  if (requested.size === 0) return undefined;
  return Object.fromEntries(LAYER_GROUPS.map((group) => [group, requested.has(group)])) as Partial<LayerGroupVisibility>;
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/entities-api.ts =====
import { z } from "zod";
import { getSql } from "@/lib/db/client";
import { RequestValidationError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { atlasCodeSchema, ENTITY_SECTORS, ENTITY_STATUSES, ENTITY_TYPES } from "@/lib/atlas/schema";

const entityListQuerySchema = z.strictObject({
  q: z.string().trim().max(120).optional(),
  type: z.enum(ENTITY_TYPES).optional(),
  sector: z.enum(ENTITY_SECTORS).optional(),
  status: z.enum(ENTITY_STATUSES).optional(),
  has_geometry: z.enum(["true", "false"]).transform((value) => value === "true").optional(),
  limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(500).default(250),
  offset: z.coerce.number().int().min(0).max(10000).default(0),
});

export interface AtlasEntityListQuery {
  q?: string;
  type?: string;
  sector?: string;
  status?: string;
  has_geometry?: boolean;
  limit: number;
  offset: number;
}

export interface AtlasEntitySummary {
  code: string;
  name_ar: string;
  name_en: string;
  aliases: string[];
  entity_type: string;
  sector: string;
  status: string;
  has_geometry: boolean;
  has_boundary?: boolean;
}

export interface AtlasEntityListResult {
  entities: AtlasEntitySummary[];
  total: number;
  limit: number;
  offset: number;
}

export function parseAtlasEntityListQuery(params: URLSearchParams): AtlasEntityListQuery {
  const input: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const key of ["q", "type", "sector", "status", "has_geometry", "limit", "offset"] as const) {
    const value = params.get(key);
    if (value !== null) input[key] = value;
  }
  const parsed = entityListQuerySchema.safeParse(input);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid entity query parameters.", parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
      path: issue.path.join("."), message: issue.message,
    })));
  }
  return { ...parsed.data, ...(parsed.data.q ? { q: parsed.data.q } : {}) };
}

export type EntityListExecutor = (query: AtlasEntityListQuery) => Promise<AtlasEntityListResult>;

const defaultEntityListExecutor: EntityListExecutor = async (filters) => {
  const sql = getSql();
  const q = filters.q || null;
  const values = [q, filters.type ?? null, filters.sector ?? null, filters.status ?? null, filters.has_geometry ?? null];
  const where = `
    WHERE ($1::text IS NULL OR
      strpos(lower(e.name_ar), lower($1)) > 0 OR
      strpos(lower(e.name_en), lower($1)) > 0 OR
      EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM unnest(e.aliases) AS alias WHERE strpos(lower(alias), lower($1)) > 0))
      AND ($2::text IS NULL OR e.entity_type = $2)
      AND ($3::text IS NULL OR e.sector = $3)
      AND ($4::text IS NULL OR e.status = $4)
      AND ($5::boolean IS NULL OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM features f WHERE f.entity_id = e.id) = $5)
  `;
  const [countRows, rows] = await Promise.all([
    sql.query(`SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS total FROM entities e ${where}`, values),
    sql.query(
      `SELECT e.code, e.name_ar, e.name_en, e.aliases, e.entity_type, e.sector, e.status,
              EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM features f WHERE f.entity_id = e.id) AS has_geometry
         FROM entities e ${where}
        ORDER BY e.entity_type, e.name_ar, e.code
        LIMIT $6 OFFSET $7`,
      [...values, filters.limit, filters.offset],
    ),
  ]);
  const total = Number((countRows as Array<{ total: number | string }>)[0]?.total ?? 0);
  return { entities: rows as AtlasEntitySummary[], total, limit: filters.limit, offset: filters.offset };
};

export async function getAtlasEntities(
  filters: AtlasEntityListQuery,
  execute: EntityListExecutor = defaultEntityListExecutor,
): Promise<AtlasEntityListResult> {
  return execute(filters);
}

export interface AtlasEvidenceItem {
  code: string;
  url: string | null;
  publisher: string;
  title: string;
  source_type: string;
  published_date: string | null;
  accessed_date: string | null;
}

export interface AtlasEntityFact {
  code: string;
  attribute: string;
  value: string;
  unit: string | null;
  valid_as_of: string | null;
  valid_as_of_precision: "day" | "month" | "year";
  verification_state: string;
  notes: string | null;
  evidence: AtlasEvidenceItem[];
}

export interface AtlasEntityDetail extends AtlasEntitySummary {
  description: string | null;
  facts: AtlasEntityFact[];
}

export interface AtlasEntityDetailExecutor {
  entity(code: string): Promise<Omit<AtlasEntityDetail, "facts"> | null>;
  facts(code: string): Promise<AtlasEntityFact[]>;
}

const defaultEntityDetailExecutor: AtlasEntityDetailExecutor = {
  async entity(code) {
    const sql = getSql();
    const rows = await sql.query(
      `SELECT e.code, e.name_ar, e.name_en, e.aliases, e.entity_type, e.sector, e.status, e.description,
              EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM features f WHERE f.entity_id = e.id) AS has_geometry
         FROM entities e WHERE e.code = $1 LIMIT 1`,
      [code],
    );
    return (rows as Array<Omit<AtlasEntityDetail, "facts">>)[0] ?? null;
  },
  async facts(code) {
    const sql = getSql();
    const rows = await sql.query(
      `SELECT f.code, f.attribute, f.value, f.unit, f.valid_as_of::text AS valid_as_of,
              f.valid_as_of_precision, f.verification_state, f.notes,
              COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'code', ev.code, 'url', ev.url, 'publisher', ev.publisher, 'title', ev.title,
                'source_type', ev.source_type, 'published_date', ev.published_date::text,
                'accessed_date', ev.accessed_date::text
              ) ORDER BY ev.code) FILTER (WHERE ev.id IS NOT NULL), '[]'::jsonb) AS evidence
         FROM facts f
         JOIN entities e ON e.id = f.entity_id
         LEFT JOIN fact_evidence fe ON fe.fact_id = f.id
         LEFT JOIN evidence ev ON ev.id = fe.evidence_id
        WHERE e.code = $1
        GROUP BY f.id, f.code, f.attribute, f.value, f.unit, f.valid_as_of,
                 f.valid_as_of_precision, f.verification_state, f.notes
        ORDER BY f.attribute, CASE f.verification_state
          WHEN 'verified' THEN 0 WHEN 'corroborated' THEN 1 WHEN 'disputed' THEN 2
          WHEN 'candidate' THEN 3 WHEN 'single_source' THEN 4 WHEN 'superseded' THEN 5
          WHEN 'rejected' THEN 6 ELSE 7 END, f.code`,
      [code],
    );
    return rows as AtlasEntityFact[];
  },
};

export async function getAtlasEntityDetail(
  rawCode: string,
  execute: AtlasEntityDetailExecutor = defaultEntityDetailExecutor,
): Promise<AtlasEntityDetail | null> {
  const parsed = atlasCodeSchema.safeParse(rawCode);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid entity code.", parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
      path: "code", message: issue.message,
    })));
  }
  const entity = await execute.entity(parsed.data);
  if (!entity) return null;
  const facts = await execute.facts(parsed.data);
  return { ...entity, facts };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/entity-presentation.ts =====
import { ENTITY_TYPE_LABELS, SECTOR_LABELS, translateLabel } from "./labels";
import { transitModeLabel } from "./transit-modes";

export type PresentationLanguage = "ar" | "en";

/** The minimum an entity must expose to be presented in a list or search row. */
export interface PresentableEntity {
  code: string;
  name_ar: string;
  name_en: string;
  entity_type: string;
  sector?: string | null;
  /** The seed stores aliases as a `|`-joined string; the static dataset normalises them to an array. */
  aliases?: string | readonly string[] | null;
}

/** Normalises the two alias shapes the codebase uses into a list. */
export function aliasList(aliases: string | readonly string[] | null | undefined): string[] {
  if (!aliases) return [];
  if (Array.isArray(aliases)) return aliases.map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return (aliases as string).split("|").map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

const STATION_TYPES = new Set(["transit_stop", "rail_station", "transit_station"]);

export function isStationType(entityType: string): boolean {
  return STATION_TYPES.has(entityType);
}

/**
 * BATCH 12.2 — REGIONAL-sector entities are regional connection points, not Greater Cairo entities.
 *
 * CITY-ABU-SIMBEL and CITY-ALEXANDRIA are real registered entities that other facts and relations
 * point at, so they are never deleted or retyped; they are *presented* differently. This is the single
 * predicate every surface (directory, search, counts, the «المدن» layer) must use, so the atlas can
 * never drift into claiming Alexandria is inside Greater Cairo.
 */
export function isRegionalLink(entity: Pick<PresentableEntity, "sector">): boolean {
  return (entity.sector ?? "").trim().toUpperCase() === "REGIONAL";
}

export function regionalLinkLabel(language: PresentationLanguage): string {
  return language === "ar" ? "نقطة ربط إقليمية" : "Regional link";
}

export function regionalLinkNote(language: PresentationLanguage): string {
  return language === "ar"
    ? "خارج نطاق القاهرة الكبرى — مذكورة كنقطة ربط لأن حقائق أو علاقات تشير إليها."
    : "Outside Greater Cairo — listed as a regional link because facts or relations refer to it.";
}

/**
 * The type label used in a list row. A REGIONAL entity must never read as a plain «مدينة» inside a
 * Greater Cairo atlas, so its type label is replaced by the regional-link label.
 */
export function entityTypeLabel(entity: Pick<PresentableEntity, "entity_type" | "sector">, language: PresentationLanguage): string {
  if (isRegionalLink(entity)) return regionalLinkLabel(language);
  return translateLabel(ENTITY_TYPE_LABELS, entity.entity_type, language);
}

/** The extra qualifier a row needs after the name: mode/line for stations, nothing otherwise. */
export function entityQualifier(
  entity: Pick<PresentableEntity, "code" | "entity_type">,
  language: PresentationLanguage,
): string | null {
  if (!isStationType(entity.entity_type)) return null;
  const mode = transitModeLabel(entity.code, language);
  if (!mode) return null;
  return language === "ar" ? `محطة ${mode}` : mode;
}

/**
 * BATCH 12.3 — the one visible label a list/search row renders.
 *
 * Two different entities can share a name («6 أكتوبر» the city and «6 أكتوبر» the monorail station),
 * so the row is always name + type/mode. The type is part of the label rather than a separate cell so
 * that two rows can never render identical visible text.
 */
export function entityListLabel(entity: PresentableEntity, language: PresentationLanguage): string {
  const name = language === "ar" ? entity.name_ar : entity.name_en;
  const qualifier = entityQualifier(entity, language);
  const type = entityTypeLabel(entity, language);
  const detail = qualifier ?? type;
  return `${name} · ${detail}`;
}

function normalizeName(value: string): string {
  return value
    .normalize("NFKC")
    .replace(/[\u0640\u200c\u200d\u00a0]/gu, "")
    .replace(/[\u064b-\u0652]/gu, "")
    .replace(/[أإآٱ]/gu, "ا")
    .replace(/ى/gu, "ي")
    .replace(/ة/gu, "ه")
    .replace(/\s+/gu, " ")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

export function normalizeEntityName(value: string): string {
  return normalizeName(value);
}

export interface DisambiguationInput {
  code: string;
  displayLabel: string;
  /** Every name the entity is known by, including aliases. */
  names: readonly string[];
}

/**
 * BATCH 12.3 — when an alias exactly equals another entity's name (after normalisation), the row gets
 * a small line saying which entity that alias normally refers to. Nothing is renamed: the seed's names
 * and aliases are evidence, and the disambiguation lives in presentation only.
 */
export function disambiguationNote(
  entity: DisambiguationInput,
  all: readonly DisambiguationInput[],
  language: PresentationLanguage,
): string | null {
  const mine = new Set(entity.names.map(normalizeName).filter(Boolean));
  const colliding = all.find((other) => {
    if (other.code === entity.code) return false;
    const otherNames = new Set([...other.names, other.displayLabel].map(normalizeName).filter(Boolean));
    for (const name of mine) if (otherNames.has(name)) return true;
    return false;
  });
  if (!colliding) return null;
  const otherLabel = colliding.displayLabel;
  return language === "ar"
    ? `اسم يتكرر: يوجد أيضًا «${otherLabel}»، وهو كيان مختلف.`
    : `Shared name: «${otherLabel}» is a different entity.`;
}

/**
 * Ensures no two rows render the same visible label. Returns the codes that still collide, so the
 * caller (and the test) can prove the list is unambiguous instead of hoping it is.
 */
export function duplicateVisibleLabels(rows: ReadonlyArray<{ code: string; label: string }>): string[][] {
  const byLabel = new Map<string, string[]>();
  for (const row of rows) {
    const key = row.label.trim();
    byLabel.set(key, [...(byLabel.get(key) ?? []), row.code]);
  }
  return [...byLabel.values()].filter((codes) => codes.length > 1);
}

/**
 * Splits a directory/search list into the Greater Cairo group and the regional-link group.
 * `sectorOf` lets a caller pass already-rendered rows instead of raw entities.
 */
export function groupByRegionalScope<T>(
  entities: readonly T[],
  sectorOf: (item: T) => string | null | undefined = (item) => (item as Pick<PresentableEntity, "sector">).sector,
): { local: T[]; regional: T[] } {
  const local: T[] = [];
  const regional: T[] = [];
  for (const entity of entities) (isRegionalLink({ sector: sectorOf(entity) }) ? regional : local).push(entity);
  return { local, regional };
}

/**
 * BATCH 12.2 — the «المدن» layer and city counts must ignore regional links, so Alexandria can never be
 * counted as a Greater Cairo city. Deleting it is not an option: relations and facts reference it.
 */
export function isGreaterCairoCity(entity: Pick<PresentableEntity, "entity_type" | "sector">): boolean {
  return entity.entity_type === "city" && !isRegionalLink(entity);
}

export function sectorLabel(sector: string | null | undefined, language: PresentationLanguage): string | null {
  if (!sector) return null;
  if (isRegionalLink({ sector })) return regionalLinkLabel(language);
  return translateLabel(SECTOR_LABELS, sector, language);
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/entity-relations.ts =====
import type { EntityRelationSeed, EntitySeed, FactSeed } from "@/lib/atlas/schema";

export interface OsmRouteRelation {
  type: "relation";
  id: number;
  tags?: { route?: string };
  members: Array<{ type: string; ref: number; role: string }>;
}

export interface RelationDerivationResult {
  relations: EntityRelationSeed[];
  unresolved: Array<{ fact_code: string; entity_code: string; attribute: string; value: string; reason: string }>;
  osmRouteRelationsChecked: number;
  osmRouteRelationsMatched: number;
  osmRouteRelationMatchedIds: number[];
  osmRouteRelationsUnmatched: Array<{ relation_id: number; route: string; reason: string }>;
  osmOrdinalsAdded: number;
}

const DIRECT_RELATION_ATTRIBUTES = new Set<EntityRelationSeed["predicate"]>([
  "interchange_with", "part_of", "crosses", "connects", "located_in", "operated_by", "developed_by",
]);
const INACTIVE_STATES = new Set(["superseded", "rejected"]);
const keyOf = (relation: Pick<EntityRelationSeed, "subject_code" | "predicate" | "object_code">) =>
  `${relation.subject_code}\u0000${relation.predicate}\u0000${relation.object_code}`;

/** Build only explicit, entity-to-entity assertions; never use proximity or name similarity. */
export function deriveEntityRelations(entities: readonly EntitySeed[], facts: readonly FactSeed[], osmRelations: readonly OsmRouteRelation[] = []): RelationDerivationResult {
  const entityByCode = new Map(entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  const relations = new Map<string, EntityRelationSeed>();
  const unresolved: RelationDerivationResult["unresolved"] = [];
  const activeFacts = facts.filter((fact) => !INACTIVE_STATES.has(fact.verification_state));
  const lineMemberships = activeFacts.filter((fact) => fact.attribute === "line_membership");
  const lineOrders = activeFacts.filter((fact) => fact.attribute === "line_order");

  for (const membership of lineMemberships) {
    const station = entityByCode.get(membership.entity_code);
    const line = entityByCode.get(membership.value);
    if (!line || line.entity_type !== "transit_line") {
      unresolved.push({ fact_code: membership.code, entity_code: membership.entity_code, attribute: membership.attribute, value: membership.value,
        reason: "line_membership value is not an exact code for an existing transit_line entity" });
      continue;
    }
    if (!station || !["transit_stop", "rail_station", "transit_station"].includes(station.entity_type)) {
      unresolved.push({ fact_code: membership.code, entity_code: membership.entity_code, attribute: membership.attribute, value: membership.value,
        reason: "line membership subject is not an existing station entity" });
      continue;
    }

    const matchingOrders = lineOrders.flatMap((order) => {
      if (order.entity_code !== station.code || !/^\d+$/.test(order.value)) return [];
      const commonEvidence = order.evidence_codes.filter((code) => membership.evidence_codes.includes(code)).sort();
      return commonEvidence.length ? [{ order, evidence: commonEvidence[0]!, ordinal: Number(order.value) }] : [];
    }).sort((a, b) => a.evidence.localeCompare(b.evidence) || a.ordinal - b.ordinal || a.order.code.localeCompare(b.order.code));
    const ordered = matchingOrders[0];
    const relation: EntityRelationSeed = {
      subject_code: line.code,
      predicate: "has_station",
      object_code: station.code,
      ordinal: ordered?.ordinal ?? null,
      valid_as_of: ordered?.order.valid_as_of ?? membership.valid_as_of,
      evidence_ref: ordered?.evidence ?? membership.evidence_codes[0] ?? null,
    };
    const key = keyOf(relation);
    const previous = relations.get(key);
    if (!previous || (previous.ordinal === null && relation.ordinal !== null)) relations.set(key, relation);
  }

  for (const fact of activeFacts) {
    if (!DIRECT_RELATION_ATTRIBUTES.has(fact.attribute as EntityRelationSeed["predicate"])) continue;
    const predicate = fact.attribute as EntityRelationSeed["predicate"];
    if (!entityByCode.has(fact.value)) {
      unresolved.push({ fact_code: fact.code, entity_code: fact.entity_code, attribute: fact.attribute, value: fact.value,
        reason: "relation value is prose or a non-entity identifier; no entity-code relationship was inferred" });
      continue;
    }
    if (!entityByCode.has(fact.entity_code)) continue;
    const relation: EntityRelationSeed = {
      subject_code: fact.entity_code,
      predicate,
      object_code: fact.value,
      ordinal: null,
      valid_as_of: fact.valid_as_of,
      evidence_ref: fact.evidence_codes[0] ?? null,
    };
    relations.set(keyOf(relation), relation);
  }

  // OSM route relations contribute order only when their stop nodes map by exact OSM IDs
  // to existing entities and all mapped stops agree on one fact-backed transit line.
  const osmNodeEntities = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  for (const fact of activeFacts) if (fact.attribute === "osm_node_id" && entityByCode.has(fact.entity_code)) {
    const codes = osmNodeEntities.get(fact.value) ?? new Set<string>();
    codes.add(fact.entity_code);
    osmNodeEntities.set(fact.value, codes);
  }
  const membershipByStation = new Map<string, FactSeed[]>();
  for (const fact of lineMemberships) membershipByStation.set(fact.entity_code, [...(membershipByStation.get(fact.entity_code) ?? []), fact]);
  const routeRecords = osmRelations.filter((relation) => ["subway", "monorail", "train"].includes(relation.tags?.route ?? ""))
    .slice().sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);
  const osmOrdinalSourceByRelation = new Map<string, number>();
  let osmRouteRelationsMatched = 0;
  const osmRouteRelationMatchedIds: number[] = [];
  let osmOrdinalsAdded = 0;
  const osmRouteRelationsUnmatched: RelationDerivationResult["osmRouteRelationsUnmatched"] = [];
  for (const route of routeRecords) {
    const stops = route.members.filter((member) => member.type === "node" && ["stop", "stop_entry_only", "stop_exit_only"].includes(member.role));
    const stationLines = stops.map((member) => {
      const stationCodes = osmNodeEntities.get(String(member.ref)) ?? new Set<string>();
      return new Set([...stationCodes].flatMap((stationCode) => (membershipByStation.get(stationCode) ?? [])
        .map((fact) => fact.value).filter((lineCode) => entityByCode.get(lineCode)?.entity_type === "transit_line")));
    });
    if (!stationLines.length || stationLines.some((lines) => lines.size === 0)) {
      osmRouteRelationsUnmatched.push({ relation_id: route.id, route: route.tags?.route ?? "unknown",
        reason: "At least one stop member has no exact entity-code and fact-backed line-membership match." });
      continue;
    }
    const commonLines = [...stationLines[0]!].filter((lineCode) => stationLines.every((lines) => lines.has(lineCode)));
    if (commonLines.length !== 1) {
      osmRouteRelationsUnmatched.push({ relation_id: route.id, route: route.tags?.route ?? "unknown",
        reason: `Mapped stops do not share exactly one fact-backed transit_line (candidate count: ${commonLines.length}).` });
      continue;
    }
    const lineCode = commonLines[0]!;
    osmRouteRelationsMatched++;
    osmRouteRelationMatchedIds.push(route.id);
    let ordinal = 0;
    for (const member of stops) {
      const stationCodes = osmNodeEntities.get(String(member.ref)) ?? new Set<string>();
      const stationCode = [...stationCodes].sort().find((candidate) =>
        (membershipByStation.get(candidate) ?? []).some((fact) => fact.value === lineCode));
      if (!stationCode) continue;
      ordinal++;
      const relation: EntityRelationSeed = { subject_code: lineCode, predicate: "has_station", object_code: stationCode,
        ordinal: null, valid_as_of: null, evidence_ref: null };
      const key = keyOf(relation);
      const existing = relations.get(key);
      if (!existing || existing.ordinal !== null) continue;
      const membershipFact = (membershipByStation.get(stationCode) ?? []).find((fact) => fact.value === lineCode);
      if (!membershipFact?.evidence_codes[0]) continue;
      const previousRouteId = osmOrdinalSourceByRelation.get(key);
      if (previousRouteId !== undefined && previousRouteId <= route.id) continue;
      relations.set(key, { ...existing, ordinal, evidence_ref: existing.evidence_ref ?? membershipFact.evidence_codes[0] });
      osmOrdinalSourceByRelation.set(key, route.id);
      osmOrdinalsAdded++;
    }
  }

  return {
    relations: [...relations.values()].sort((a, b) => a.subject_code.localeCompare(b.subject_code) ||
      a.predicate.localeCompare(b.predicate) || (a.ordinal ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) - (b.ordinal ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) ||
      a.object_code.localeCompare(b.object_code)),
    unresolved: unresolved.sort((a, b) => a.fact_code.localeCompare(b.fact_code)),
    osmRouteRelationsChecked: routeRecords.length,
    osmRouteRelationsMatched,
    osmRouteRelationMatchedIds,
    osmRouteRelationsUnmatched,
    osmOrdinalsAdded,
  };
}

/**
 * BATCH 12.4 — governorate parentage from CAPMAS admin codes.
 *
 * Every read of the COD-AB extract states the governorate each adm2 unit belongs to (`adm1_pcode`,
 * `adm1_ar`, `adm1_en`). This maps those published codes onto the governorate entities that already
 * exist in the seed, so a district/city gets «تابع لمحافظة …» without any geometric guessing and
 * without inventing an entity. A code with no matching entity is reported, never silently linked.
 */
export interface CodabGovernorateRelation {
  entity_code: string;
  relation: "governorate";
  adm1_pcode: string;
  adm1_name_ar: string;
  adm1_name_en: string | null;
  pcodes: string[];
  evidence_code: string;
  source: string;
  license: string;
}

export interface GovernorateDerivationResult {
  relations: EntityRelationSeed[];
  unresolved: Array<{ entity_code: string; adm1_pcode: string; reason: string }>;
}

export function deriveGovernorateRelations(
  codabRelations: readonly CodabGovernorateRelation[],
  entities: ReadonlyArray<{ code: string; entity_type: string }>,
): GovernorateDerivationResult {
  const known = new Set(entities.map((entity) => entity.code));
  const governoratesByCode = new Set(
    entities.filter((entity) => entity.entity_type === "authority" && entity.code.startsWith("AUTH-") && entity.code.endsWith("-GOVERNORATE"))
      .map((entity) => entity.code),
  );
  // adm1 pcode → the seed entity that represents that governorate. Only the three whose authority
  // entity is actually in the seed are mapped; anything else stays unresolved on purpose.
  const expectedGovernorateEntity: Record<string, string> = {
    EG01: "AUTH-CAIRO-GOVERNORATE",
    EG21: "AUTH-GIZA-GOVERNORATE",
    EG14: "AUTH-QALYUBIA-GOVERNORATE",
  };

  const relations: EntityRelationSeed[] = [];
  const unresolved: GovernorateDerivationResult["unresolved"] = [];
  for (const relation of codabRelations) {
    if (relation.relation !== "governorate") continue;
    if (!known.has(relation.entity_code)) {
      unresolved.push({ entity_code: relation.entity_code, adm1_pcode: relation.adm1_pcode, reason: "subject entity is not in the seed" });
      continue;
    }
    const objectCode = expectedGovernorateEntity[relation.adm1_pcode];
    if (!objectCode) {
      unresolved.push({ entity_code: relation.entity_code, adm1_pcode: relation.adm1_pcode, reason: "no governorate entity is mapped for this adm1 pcode" });
      continue;
    }
    if (!governoratesByCode.has(objectCode)) {
      unresolved.push({ entity_code: relation.entity_code, adm1_pcode: relation.adm1_pcode, reason: `governorate entity ${objectCode} is not in the seed` });
      continue;
    }
    if (objectCode === relation.entity_code) {
      unresolved.push({ entity_code: relation.entity_code, adm1_pcode: relation.adm1_pcode, reason: "entity is its own governorate" });
      continue;
    }
    relations.push({
      subject_code: relation.entity_code, predicate: "located_in", object_code: objectCode,
      ordinal: null, valid_as_of: null, evidence_ref: relation.evidence_code,
    });
  }
  return { relations, unresolved };
}

/** The one short simple-panel line: «يتبع: محافظة الجيزة» / "Part of: Giza Governorate". */
export function governorateParentLabel(
  objectNameAr: string,
  objectNameEn: string,
  language: "ar" | "en",
): string {
  return language === "ar" ? `يتبع: ${objectNameAr}` : `Part of: ${objectNameEn}`;
}

    "X-Atlas-Research-Data-Copyright": "Mustafa Aboalazm",

  };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/features-api.ts =====
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { z } from "zod";
import { getSql } from "@/lib/db/client";
import { RequestValidationError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { ENTITY_SECTORS, ENTITY_STATUSES, ENTITY_TYPES } from "@/lib/atlas/schema";
import { isTransitInterchangeEntityCode, transitModesForEntityCode, type TransitMode } from "@/lib/atlas/transit-modes";
import { stationDisplayName } from "@/lib/atlas/labels";

const featureQuerySchema = z.strictObject({
  type: z.union([z.enum(ENTITY_TYPES), z.literal("admin_unit")]).optional(),
  level: z.enum(["2", "3"]).optional(),
  sector: z.enum(ENTITY_SECTORS).optional(),
  status: z.enum(ENTITY_STATUSES).optional(),
  zoom: z.coerce.number().int().min(4).max(20).default(12),
});

export interface AtlasFeatureQuery {
  type?: string;
  level?: "2" | "3";
  sector?: string;
  status?: string;
  zoom: number;
}

export interface FeatureCollectionRow {
  code: string;
  entity_code: string;
  name_ar: string;
  name_en: string;
  entity_type: string;
  status: string;
  geometry: unknown;
  license: string;
  attribution: string;
  source: string;
  retrieval_date: string;
  source_ids: unknown;
  line_codes?: unknown;
}

export interface AtlasFeatureCollection {
  type: "FeatureCollection";
  metadata: {
    data_license: string;
    geometry_license: string;
    contact: string;
  };
  features: Array<{
    type: "Feature";
    id: string;
    geometry: unknown;
    properties: {
      code: string;
      entity_code: string;
      name_ar: string;
      name_en: string;
      type: string;
      status: string;
      license: string;
      attribution: string;
      source: string;
      retrieval_date: string;
      source_ids: unknown;
      modes: string[];
      interchange: boolean;
    };
  }>;
}

export type FeatureRowsExecutor = (filters: AtlasFeatureQuery) => Promise<FeatureCollectionRow[]>;

export function parseAtlasFeatureQuery(params: URLSearchParams): AtlasFeatureQuery {
  const input: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const key of ["type", "level", "sector", "status", "zoom"] as const) {
    const value = params.get(key);
    if (value !== null) input[key] = value;
  }
  const parsed = featureQuerySchema.safeParse(input);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid feature query parameters.", parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
      path: issue.path.join("."), message: issue.message,
    })));
  }
  return parsed.data;
}

export const ATLAS_FEATURES_SQL = `
  SELECT f.code, e.code AS entity_code, e.name_ar, e.name_en, e.entity_type, e.status,
         ST_AsGeoJSON(
           CASE WHEN $4::double precision > 0
             THEN ST_SimplifyPreserveTopology(f.geometry, $4::double precision)
             ELSE f.geometry END,
           6
         )::jsonb AS geometry,
         f.license,
         CASE WHEN f.license = 'ODbL-1.0' THEN '© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0'
              ELSE f.properties ->> 'attribution' END AS attribution,
         f.properties ->> 'source' AS source,
         f.properties ->> 'retrieval_date' AS retrieval_date,
         jsonb_build_array(f.properties -> 'source_ids') AS source_ids,
         COALESCE(f.properties -> 'matched_line_codes', '[]'::jsonb) AS line_codes
    FROM features f
    JOIN entities e ON e.id = f.entity_id
   WHERE ($1::text IS NULL OR e.entity_type = $1)
     AND ($2::text IS NULL OR e.sector = $2)
     AND ($3::text IS NULL OR e.status = $3)
   ORDER BY e.entity_type, e.code, f.code
   LIMIT 5000
`;

export function featureSimplificationTolerance(zoom: number): number {
  return zoom <= 7 ? 0.004 : zoom <= 9 ? 0.001 : zoom <= 11 ? 0.0002 : 0;
}

const defaultFeatureRowsExecutor: FeatureRowsExecutor = async (filters) => {
  const sql = getSql();
  const tolerance = featureSimplificationTolerance(filters.zoom);
  const rows = await sql.query(
    ATLAS_FEATURES_SQL,
    [filters.type ?? null, filters.sector ?? null, filters.status ?? null, tolerance],
  );
  return rows as FeatureCollectionRow[];
};

export async function getAtlasFeatures(
  filters: AtlasFeatureQuery,
  executeRows: FeatureRowsExecutor = defaultFeatureRowsExecutor,
): Promise<AtlasFeatureCollection> {
  if (filters.type === "admin_unit") {
    const file = path.join(process.cwd(), "data/features/codab-admin-units.geojson");
    const collection = JSON.parse(await readFile(file, "utf8")) as { features: Array<{ id: string; geometry: unknown; properties: Record<string, unknown> }> };
    const features = collection.features.filter((feature) => !filters.level || String(feature.properties.level) === filters.level);
    return {
      type: "FeatureCollection",
      metadata: {
        data_license: "CAPMAS COD-AB 2017 (reviewed 2024), CC BY-IGO",
        geometry_license: "CC BY-IGO",
        contact: "CAPMAS / OCHA HDX",
      },
      features: features.map((feature) => ({
        type: "Feature", id: feature.id, geometry: feature.geometry,
        properties: {
          code: String(feature.properties.code), entity_code: String(feature.properties.pcode),
          name_ar: String(feature.properties.name_ar), name_en: String(feature.properties.name_en ?? ""),
          pcode: String(feature.properties.pcode), level: Number(feature.properties.level),
          parent_pcodes: Array.isArray(feature.properties.parent_pcodes) ? feature.properties.parent_pcodes.map(String) : [],
          parent_name_ar: String(feature.properties.parent_name_ar ?? ""), area_sqkm: Number(feature.properties.area_sqkm),
          type: "admin_unit", status: "unknown", license: "CC BY-IGO",
          attribution: String(feature.properties.attribution), source: String(feature.properties.source),
          retrieval_date: "2026-11-15", source_ids: { pcode: feature.properties.pcode, level: feature.properties.level,
            parent_pcodes: feature.properties.parent_pcodes, area_sqkm: feature.properties.area_sqkm },
          modes: [], interchange: false,
        },
      })),
    };
  }
  const rows = await executeRows(filters);
  return {
    type: "FeatureCollection",
    metadata: {
      data_license: "© Mustafa Aboalazm (مصطفى أبوالعزم), licensed under CC BY 4.0",
      geometry_license: "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0",
      contact: "01092500454 · 3boalazm@gmail.com",
    },
    features: rows.map((row) => {
      const lineCodes = Array.isArray(row.line_codes) ? row.line_codes.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string") : row.entity_code.startsWith("TRANSIT-") ? [row.entity_code] : [];
      const inferredModes = transitModesForEntityCode(row.entity_code, lineCodes);
      const isStation = row.entity_type === "transit_stop" || row.entity_type === "rail_station";
      const modes: TransitMode[] = inferredModes.length ? inferredModes : isStation ? ["unclassified"] : [];
      return {
      type: "Feature",
      id: row.code,
      geometry: typeof row.geometry === "string" ? JSON.parse(row.geometry) : row.geometry,
      properties: {
        code: row.code,
        entity_code: row.entity_code,
        name_ar: stationDisplayName(row.entity_code, row.name_ar),
        name_en: row.name_en,
        type: row.entity_type,
        status: row.status,
        license: row.license,
        attribution: row.license.split(",").includes("ODbL-1.0")
          ? "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0"
          : row.attribution,
        source: row.source,
        retrieval_date: row.retrieval_date,
        source_ids: row.source_ids,
        line_codes: lineCodes,
        primary_line_code: lineCodes[0] ?? (row.entity_code.startsWith("TRANSIT-") ? row.entity_code : null),
        modes,
        interchange: modes.length > 1 || isTransitInterchangeEntityCode(row.entity_code),
      },
    }; }),
  };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/features.ts =====
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import {
  featureSeedSchema,
  type EntitySeed,
  type EvidenceSeed,
  type FeatureSeed,
} from "@/lib/atlas/schema";
import { SeedValidationError, type ValidationIssueDetail } from "@/lib/errors";

/** Inclusive Greater Cairo WGS84 envelope: west, south, east, north. */
export const GREATER_CAIRO_BBOX = {
  west: 30.5,
  south: 29.2,
  east: 32.1,
  north: 30.8,
} as const;

/** Road-only corridor envelope; extended north/east without widening non-road geometry acceptance. */
export const ROAD_GEOMETRY_BBOX = {
  west: 30.5,
  south: 29.2,
  east: 32.35,
  north: 30.9,
} as const;

/** Narrow westward exception for the explicitly scoped Regional Ring patch. */
export const REGIONAL_RING_ROAD_GEOMETRY_BBOX = {
  west: 30.25,
  south: 29.2,
  east: 32.35,
  north: 30.9,
} as const;

function positions(geometry: unknown): number[][] {
  if (!geometry || typeof geometry !== "object") return [];
  const record = geometry as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (record.type === "GeometryCollection") {
    return Array.isArray(record.geometries)
      ? record.geometries.flatMap(positions)
      : [];
  }
  const output: number[][] = [];
  const visit = (value: unknown): void => {
    if (!Array.isArray(value)) return;
    if (value.length >= 2 && typeof value[0] === "number" && typeof value[1] === "number") {
      output.push(value as number[]);
      return;
    }
    value.forEach(visit);
  };
  visit(record.coordinates);
  return output;
}

function ringIsValid(ring: number[][]): boolean {
  if (ring.length < 4) return false;
  const first = ring[0];
  const last = ring[ring.length - 1];
  if (!first || !last || first[0] !== last[0] || first[1] !== last[1]) return false;
  let twiceArea = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < ring.length - 1; i++) {
    const a = ring[i]; const b = ring[i + 1];
    if (!a || !b) return false;
    twiceArea += a[0]! * b[1]! - b[0]! * a[1]!;
  }
  if (Math.abs(twiceArea) < 1e-12) return false;
  const orient = (a: number[], b: number[], c: number[]) =>
    (b[0]! - a[0]!) * (c[1]! - a[1]!) - (b[1]! - a[1]!) * (c[0]! - a[0]!);
  const intersects = (a: number[], b: number[], c: number[], d: number[]) => {
    const abC = orient(a, b, c); const abD = orient(a, b, d);
    const cdA = orient(c, d, a); const cdB = orient(c, d, b);
    return abC * abD <= 0 && cdA * cdB <= 0;
  };
  const edgeCount = ring.length - 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < edgeCount; i++) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < edgeCount; j++) {
      if (j === i + 1 || (i === 0 && j === edgeCount - 1)) continue;
      const a = ring[i]; const b = ring[i + 1]; const c = ring[j]; const d = ring[j + 1];
      if (a && b && c && d && intersects(a, b, c, d)) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

function hasValidPolygonRings(geometry: unknown): boolean {
  if (!geometry || typeof geometry !== "object") return false;
  const record = geometry as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (record.type === "GeometryCollection") {
    return Array.isArray(record.geometries) && record.geometries.every(hasValidPolygonRings);
  }
  if (record.type === "Polygon") {
    const rings = record.coordinates;
    return Array.isArray(rings) && rings.every((ring) => Array.isArray(ring) && ringIsValid(ring as number[][]));
  }
  if (record.type === "MultiPolygon") {
    const polygons = record.coordinates;
    return Array.isArray(polygons) && polygons.every((polygon) =>
      Array.isArray(polygon) && polygon.every((ring) => Array.isArray(ring) && ringIsValid(ring as number[][])),
    );
  }
  return true;
}

type Wgs84Bounds = { west: number; south: number; east: number; north: number };

export function isGeometryInsideGreaterCairoBbox(
  geometry: unknown,
  bbox: Wgs84Bounds = GREATER_CAIRO_BBOX,
): boolean {
  const points = positions(geometry);
  return points.length > 0 && points.every(([longitude, latitude]) =>
    longitude !== undefined && latitude !== undefined &&
    longitude >= bbox.west && longitude <= bbox.east &&
    latitude >= bbox.south && latitude <= bbox.north,
  );
}

export async function loadAndValidateFeatureCollection(
  featurePath: string,
  entities: readonly EntitySeed[],
  evidence: readonly EvidenceSeed[] = [],
): Promise<FeatureSeed[]> {
  let parsed: unknown;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(await readFile(/*turbopackIgnore: true*/ featurePath, "utf8"));
  } catch (error) {
    throw new SeedValidationError(
      `Cannot read feature GeoJSON "${featurePath}": ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
      [{ path: featurePath, message: "Feature GeoJSON is missing, unreadable, or invalid JSON." }],
    );
  }

  const issues: ValidationIssueDetail[] = [];
  if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object" || (parsed as Record<string, unknown>).type !== "FeatureCollection" || !Array.isArray((parsed as Record<string, unknown>).features)) {
    throw new SeedValidationError("Feature input must be a GeoJSON FeatureCollection.", [
      { path: featurePath, message: "Expected GeoJSON FeatureCollection with a features array." },
    ]);
  }

  const entityByCode = new Map(entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  const evidenceByCode = new Map(evidence.map((item) => [item.code, item]));
  const features: FeatureSeed[] = [];
  const seenCodes = new Set<string>();
  const rawFeatures = (parsed as { features: unknown[] }).features;
  for (let index = 0; index < rawFeatures.length; index++) {
    const raw = rawFeatures[index];
    const label = `features[${index}]`;
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object" || Array.isArray(raw)) {
      issues.push({ path: label, message: "Expected GeoJSON Feature object." });
      continue;
    }
    const record = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
    const props = record.properties;
    if (!props || typeof props !== "object" || Array.isArray(props)) {
      issues.push({ path: `${label}.properties`, message: "Expected feature metadata properties object." });
      continue;
    }
    const propsRecord = props as Record<string, unknown>;
    const candidate = {
      code: propsRecord.code,
      entity_code: propsRecord.entity_code,
      evidence_code: propsRecord.evidence_code ?? null,
      geometry: record.geometry,
      srid: propsRecord.srid,
      osm_type: propsRecord.osm_type ?? null,
      osm_id: propsRecord.osm_id ?? null,
      osm_version: propsRecord.osm_version ?? null,
      license: propsRecord.license,
      properties: propsRecord.properties,
    };
    const result = featureSeedSchema.safeParse(candidate);
    if (!result.success) {
      for (const issue of result.error.issues) {
        issues.push({ path: `${label}.${issue.path.join(".")}`, message: issue.message });
      }
      continue;
    }
    const item = result.data;
    if (seenCodes.has(item.code)) {
      issues.push({ path: `${label}.code`, message: `Duplicate feature code "${item.code}".` });
      continue;
    }
    seenCodes.add(item.code);
    const entity = entityByCode.get(item.entity_code);
    if (!entity) {
      issues.push({ path: `${label}.entity_code`, message: `Unknown entity code "${item.entity_code}"; feature imports never create entities.` });
      continue;
    }
    if (item.evidence_code !== null && !evidenceByCode.has(item.evidence_code)) {
      issues.push({ path: `${label}.evidence_code`, message: `Unknown evidence code "${item.evidence_code}".` });
      continue;
    }
    if (!hasValidPolygonRings(item.geometry)) {
      issues.push({ path: `${label}.geometry`, message: "Polygon rings must be closed, non-degenerate, and non-self-intersecting." });
      continue;
    }
    const bbox = entity.entity_type === "road"
      ? item.entity_code === "ROAD-REGIONAL-RING" ? REGIONAL_RING_ROAD_GEOMETRY_BBOX : ROAD_GEOMETRY_BBOX
      : GREATER_CAIRO_BBOX;
    if (!isGeometryInsideGreaterCairoBbox(item.geometry, bbox)) {
      issues.push({ path: `${label}.geometry`, message: `Geometry must lie wholly within ${entity?.entity_type === "road" ? "road-only" : "Greater Cairo"} WGS84 bbox ${JSON.stringify(bbox)}.` });
      continue;
    }
    const metadata = item.properties;
    if (typeof metadata.source !== "string" || metadata.source.trim() === "" ||
        typeof metadata.attribution !== "string" || metadata.attribution.trim() === "" ||
        typeof metadata.retrieval_date !== "string" || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(metadata.retrieval_date)) {
      issues.push({ path: `${label}.properties.properties`, message: "Every feature must include source, attribution, and ISO retrieval_date metadata." });
      continue;
    }
    const alignmentEvidence = item.evidence_code ? evidenceByCode.get(item.evidence_code) : undefined;
    if (["planned", "approved_planned", "under_construction"].includes(entity.status) &&
        (metadata.official_alignment !== true || alignmentEvidence?.source_type !== "official" || alignmentEvidence.url === null)) {
      issues.push({ path: `${label}.entity_code`, message: `Entity status "${entity.status}" requires official_alignment metadata and linked official evidence with a URL before geometry can be imported.` });
      continue;
    }
    features.push(item);
  }

  if (issues.length > 0) {
    throw new SeedValidationError(
      `Feature validation failed with ${issues.length} issue(s):\n${issues.map((i) => `  - [${i.path}] ${i.message}`).join("\n")}`,
      issues,
    );
  }
  return features;
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/geometry-comparisons.ts =====
import type { AtlasFeature } from "@/components/atlas/atlas-client";
export interface GeometryAlternative { feature_code: string; source_label: string; retrieval_date: string; }

/** Flag source-backed line and area alternatives only when distinct source geometries actually disagree. */
export function findGeometryAlternatives(features: readonly AtlasFeature[]): Map<string, GeometryAlternative[]> {
  const grouped = new Map<string, Array<{ feature: AtlasFeature; sourceType: string; sourceId: number; geometryKey: string }>>();
  for (const feature of features) {
    const geometryType = feature.geometry && typeof feature.geometry === "object" && "type" in feature.geometry ? String(feature.geometry.type) : "";
    const isRoute = feature.properties.type === "transit_line" && geometryType.includes("Line");
    const isBoundary = ["Polygon", "MultiPolygon"].includes(geometryType) && ["city", "district", "neighborhood", "authority"].includes(feature.properties.type);
    if (!isRoute && !isBoundary) continue;
    const rawSources = feature.properties.source_ids;
    const sources = Array.isArray(rawSources) ? rawSources : [rawSources];
    const source = sources.find((item): item is { osm_type: string; osm_id: number } => Boolean(item && typeof item === "object" && "osm_type" in item && "osm_id" in item));
    if (!source || typeof source.osm_id !== "number") continue;
    const sourceType = String(source.osm_type);
    if (isRoute && sourceType !== "relation") continue;
    if (isBoundary && !["way", "relation"].includes(sourceType)) continue;
    const entries = grouped.get(feature.properties.entity_code) ?? [];
    entries.push({ feature, sourceType, sourceId: source.osm_id, geometryKey: JSON.stringify(feature.geometry) });
    grouped.set(feature.properties.entity_code, entries);
  }
  const result = new Map<string, GeometryAlternative[]>();
  for (const [code, entries] of grouped) {
    const bySource = new Map(entries.map((entry) => [`${entry.sourceType}:${entry.sourceId}`, entry]));
    const alternatives = [...bySource.values()];
    if (alternatives.length < 2 || new Set(alternatives.map((entry) => entry.geometryKey)).size < 2) continue;
    result.set(code, alternatives.map(({ feature, sourceType, sourceId }) => ({
      feature_code: feature.id,
      source_label: `OpenStreetMap ${sourceType} ${sourceId}`,
      retrieval_date: feature.properties.retrieval_date,
    })));
  }
  return result;
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/geopackage.ts =====
import initSqlJs from "sql.js";
import path from "node:path";
import { ENTITY_SECTORS, ENTITY_STATUSES, ENTITY_TYPES, type EntitySeed, type EntityStatus, type EntityType, type EntitySector } from "@/lib/atlas/schema";
import type { StaticAtlasDataset } from "@/lib/atlas/static-dataset";
import { resolveStatusByYear } from "@/lib/atlas/status-timeline";

export interface GeoPackageFilters { type?: EntityType | "transport"; sector?: EntitySector; status?: EntityStatus; year?: number; }
let sqlPromise: ReturnType<typeof initSqlJs> | null = null;
async function getSqlJs() {
  if (!sqlPromise) sqlPromise = initSqlJs({ locateFile: () => path.join(process.cwd(), "node_modules/sql.js/dist/sql-wasm.wasm") });
  return sqlPromise;
}

export function parseGeoPackageFilters(params: URLSearchParams): GeoPackageFilters {
  const result: GeoPackageFilters = {};
  const type = params.get("type"); if (type) { if (type !== "transport" && !(ENTITY_TYPES as readonly string[]).includes(type)) throw new Error("Invalid entity type filter."); result.type = type as EntityType | "transport"; }
  const sector = params.get("sector"); if (sector) { if (!(ENTITY_SECTORS as readonly string[]).includes(sector)) throw new Error("Invalid sector filter."); result.sector = sector as EntitySector; }
  const status = params.get("status"); if (status) { if (!(ENTITY_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(status)) throw new Error("Invalid status filter."); result.status = status as EntityStatus; }
  const year = params.get("year"); if (year) { const n = Number(year); if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1000 || n > 9999) throw new Error("Invalid timeline year."); result.year = n; }
  return result;
}

function wkb(geometry: { type: string; coordinates?: unknown; geometries?: unknown[] }): Buffer {
  const u32 = (n: number) => { const b = Buffer.alloc(4); b.writeUInt32LE(n); return b; };
  const f64 = (n: number) => { const b = Buffer.alloc(8); b.writeDoubleLE(n); return b; };
  const coord = (point: unknown) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(point) || point.length < 2) throw new Error("Invalid coordinate in GeoPackage export.");
    return Buffer.concat([f64(Number(point[0])), f64(Number(point[1]))]);
  };
  const line = (points: unknown) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(points)) throw new Error("Invalid line coordinates.");
    return Buffer.concat([u32(points.length), ...points.map(coord)]);
  };
  const polygon = (rings: unknown) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(rings)) throw new Error("Invalid polygon coordinates.");
    return Buffer.concat([u32(rings.length), ...rings.map(line)]);
  };
  const typeMap: Record<string, number> = { Point: 1, LineString: 2, Polygon: 3, MultiPoint: 4, MultiLineString: 5, MultiPolygon: 6, GeometryCollection: 7 };
  const type = typeMap[geometry.type];
  if (!type) throw new Error(`Unsupported GeoPackage geometry type: ${geometry.type}`);
  const header = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([1]), u32(type)]);
  const coordinates = geometry.coordinates;
  if (type === 1) return Buffer.concat([header, coord(coordinates)]);
  if (type === 2) return Buffer.concat([header, line(coordinates)]);
  if (type === 3) return Buffer.concat([header, polygon(coordinates)]);
  if (type >= 4 && type <= 6) {
    if (!Array.isArray(coordinates)) throw new Error("Invalid multi-geometry coordinates.");
    const childType = type - 3;
    const parts = coordinates.map((part) => wkb({ type: ["", "Point", "LineString", "Polygon"][childType]!, coordinates: part }));
    return Buffer.concat([header, u32(parts.length), ...parts]);
  }
  const geometries = geometry.geometries ?? [];
  const parts = geometries.map((part) => wkb(part as { type: string; coordinates?: unknown; geometries?: unknown[] }));
  return Buffer.concat([header, u32(parts.length), ...parts]);
}

function gpkgGeometry(geometry: { type: string; coordinates?: unknown; geometries?: unknown[] }): Buffer {
  const srs = Buffer.alloc(4); srs.writeInt32LE(4326);
  return Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([0x47, 0x50, 0, 1]), srs, wkb(geometry)]);
}

const entityRows = (entities: readonly EntitySeed[]) => entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity.name_ar, entity.name_en, entity.entity_type, entity.sector, entity.status, entity.description, JSON.stringify(entity.aliases)]);

export async function generateGeoPackage(dataset: StaticAtlasDataset, filters: GeoPackageFilters = {}): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  const timeline = filters.year ? resolveStatusByYear(dataset.facts.filter((fact) => fact.valid_as_of).map((fact) => ({
    entity_code: fact.entity_code, attribute: fact.attribute, value: fact.value, valid_as_of: fact.valid_as_of!,
    valid_as_of_precision: fact.valid_as_of_precision, verification_state: fact.verification_state, fact_code: fact.code,
  })), filters.year) : null;
  const transportTypes = ["transit_line", "transit_stop", "rail_station", "transit_station"];
  const entities = dataset.entities.filter((entity) =>
    (!filters.type || (filters.type === "transport" ? transportTypes.includes(entity.entity_type) : entity.entity_type === filters.type)) && (!filters.sector || entity.sector === filters.sector) &&
    (!filters.status || (filters.year ? (timeline?.get(entity.code)?.status ?? "unknown") : entity.status) === filters.status));
  const entityByCode = new Map(entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  const features = dataset.features.filter((feature) => entityByCode.has(feature.entity_code));
  const SQL = await getSqlJs();
  const db = new SQL.Database();
  try {
    db.run("PRAGMA application_id = 1196444487; PRAGMA user_version = 10300;");
    db.run(`CREATE TABLE gpkg_spatial_ref_sys (srs_name TEXT NOT NULL, srs_id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, organization TEXT NOT NULL, organization_coordsys_id INTEGER NOT NULL, definition TEXT NOT NULL, description TEXT);
      CREATE TABLE gpkg_contents (table_name TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, data_type TEXT NOT NULL, identifier TEXT UNIQUE, description TEXT DEFAULT '', last_change DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now')), min_x DOUBLE, min_y DOUBLE, max_x DOUBLE, max_y DOUBLE, srs_id INTEGER);
      CREATE TABLE gpkg_geometry_columns (table_name TEXT NOT NULL, column_name TEXT NOT NULL, geometry_type_name TEXT NOT NULL, srs_id INTEGER NOT NULL, z TINYINT NOT NULL, m TINYINT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (table_name, column_name));
      INSERT INTO gpkg_spatial_ref_sys VALUES ('Undefined Cartesian',-1,'NONE',-1,'undefined','undefined Cartesian coordinate reference system');
      INSERT INTO gpkg_spatial_ref_sys VALUES ('Undefined Geographic',0,'NONE',0,'undefined','undefined geographic coordinate reference system');
      INSERT INTO gpkg_spatial_ref_sys VALUES ('WGS 84 geodetic',4326,'EPSG',4326,'GEOGCS[\"WGS 84\",DATUM[\"World Geodetic System 1984\",SPHEROID[\"WGS 84\",6378137,298.257223563]],PRIMEM[\"Greenwich\",0],UNIT[\"degree\",0.0174532925199433]]','longitude/latitude coordinates in decimal degrees');
      CREATE TABLE entities (code TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name_ar TEXT NOT NULL, name_en TEXT NOT NULL, entity_type TEXT NOT NULL, sector TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, description TEXT, aliases_json TEXT NOT NULL);
      CREATE TABLE features (fid INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, geom BLOB NOT NULL, feature_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, entity_code TEXT NOT NULL, name_ar TEXT NOT NULL, name_en TEXT NOT NULL, entity_type TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, license TEXT NOT NULL, attribution TEXT NOT NULL, source TEXT NOT NULL, retrieval_date TEXT NOT NULL, source_ids_json TEXT NOT NULL);
      CREATE TABLE admin_units (fid INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, geom BLOB NOT NULL, pcode TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, level INTEGER NOT NULL, name_ar TEXT NOT NULL, name_en TEXT, parent_pcode TEXT, parent_name_ar TEXT, area_sqkm REAL NOT NULL, license TEXT NOT NULL, attribution TEXT NOT NULL, source TEXT NOT NULL, evidence_code TEXT NOT NULL);
      CREATE TABLE license_attribution (kind TEXT NOT NULL, license TEXT NOT NULL, attribution TEXT NOT NULL, note TEXT NOT NULL);
      INSERT INTO gpkg_contents (table_name,data_type,identifier,description,srs_id) VALUES ('entities','attributes','Greater Cairo Atlas entities','Entity records matched to the selected GeoPackage feature filter.',NULL);
      INSERT INTO gpkg_contents (table_name,data_type,identifier,description,srs_id) VALUES ('features','features','Greater Cairo Atlas features','WGS 84 source geometries; see license_attribution table.',4326);
      INSERT INTO gpkg_contents (table_name,data_type,identifier,description,srs_id) VALUES ('admin_units','features','CAPMAS COD-AB administrative units','WGS 84 official administrative units; see license_attribution table.',4326);
      INSERT INTO gpkg_contents (table_name,data_type,identifier,description,srs_id) VALUES ('license_attribution','attributes','License and attribution','Preserve these attributions when redistributing this GeoPackage.',NULL);
      INSERT INTO gpkg_geometry_columns VALUES ('features','geom','GEOMETRY',4326,0,0);
      INSERT INTO gpkg_geometry_columns VALUES ('admin_units','geom','GEOMETRY',4326,0,0);
    `);
    const entityInsert = db.prepare("INSERT INTO entities VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)");
    for (const row of entityRows(entities)) entityInsert.run(row);
    entityInsert.free();
    const featureInsert = db.prepare("INSERT INTO features (geom,feature_code,entity_code,name_ar,name_en,entity_type,status,license,attribution,source,retrieval_date,source_ids_json) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)");
    for (const feature of features) {
      const entity = entityByCode.get(feature.entity_code)!;
      const props = (feature.properties?.properties ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
      const at = timeline?.get(entity.code);
      const status = filters.year ? at?.status ?? "unknown" : entity.status;
      const attribution = feature.license === "ODbL-1.0" ? "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0" : String(props.attribution ?? "© Mustafa Aboalazm, CC BY 4.0");
      featureInsert.run([gpkgGeometry(feature.geometry as { type: string; coordinates?: unknown; geometries?: unknown[] }), feature.code, entity.code, entity.name_ar, entity.name_en,
        entity.entity_type, status, feature.license, attribution, String(props.source ?? ""), String(props.retrieval_date ?? ""), JSON.stringify(props.source_ids ?? [])]);
    }
    featureInsert.free();
    const adminUnitInsert = db.prepare("INSERT INTO admin_units (geom,pcode,level,name_ar,name_en,parent_pcode,parent_name_ar,area_sqkm,license,attribution,source,evidence_code) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)");
    for (const feature of dataset.adminUnits) {
      const props = feature.properties;
      const parents = Array.isArray(props.parent_pcodes) ? props.parent_pcodes : [];
      adminUnitInsert.run([
        gpkgGeometry(feature.geometry as { type: string; coordinates?: unknown; geometries?: unknown[] }),
        String(props.pcode), Number(props.level), String(props.name_ar), props.name_en == null ? null : String(props.name_en),
        parents.length ? String(parents[0]) : null, String(props.parent_name_ar ?? ""), Number(props.area_sqkm ?? 0),
        "CC BY-IGO", String(props.attribution ?? ""), String(props.source ?? "CAPMAS COD-AB 2017 (reviewed 2024)"), String(props.evidence_code ?? "EV-CODAB-ADMIN-2026"),
      ]);
    }
    adminUnitInsert.free();
    const attrInsert = db.prepare("INSERT INTO license_attribution VALUES (?,?,?,?)");
    attrInsert.run(["Research data and evidence", "CC BY 4.0", "© Mustafa Aboalazm (مصطفى أبوالعزم)", "Attribution required."]);
    attrInsert.run(["OpenStreetMap geometry", "ODbL 1.0", "© OpenStreetMap contributors", "Keep ODbL attribution and comply with the ODbL when sharing derived databases."]);
    attrInsert.run(["Official administrative boundaries", "CC BY-IGO", "© CAPMAS (الجهاز المركزي للتعبئة العامة والإحصاء) via OCHA/HDX", "Source: https://data.humdata.org/dataset/cod-ab-egy. Preserve this attribution."]);
    attrInsert.run(["Basemap", "OpenFreeMap", "OpenFreeMap", "Basemap tiles are not embedded in this export."]);
    attrInsert.free();
    db.run("CREATE INDEX features_entity_code_idx ON features(entity_code); CREATE INDEX features_type_sector_idx ON features(entity_type,status);");
    return db.export();
  } finally { db.close(); }
}
