}

function parseEntitiesCsv(
  entitiesCsv: string,
  issues: ValidationIssueDetail[],
): EntitySeed[] {
  const rows = parseCsvRows(entitiesCsv, issues, "entities.csv");
  if (rows.length === 0) {
    issues.push({
      path: "entities.csv",
      message:
        "entities.csv is empty; expected header row: code,name_ar,name_en,entity_type,sector,status[,aliases][,description].",
    });
    return [];
  }

  const headerRow = rows[0];
  if (!headerRow) {
    return [];
  }

  const headers = headerRow.cells.map((h) => h.trim());
  const seenHeaders = new Set<string>();

  for (const header of headers) {
    if (seenHeaders.has(header)) {
      issues.push({
        path: "entities.csv:header",
        message: `Duplicate CSV column header "${header}".`,
      });
    }
    seenHeaders.add(header);

    if (!ALLOWED_ENTITY_CSV_HEADERS.has(header)) {
      issues.push({
        path: "entities.csv:header",
        message: `Unknown CSV column header "${header}". Allowed headers: ${Array.from(ALLOWED_ENTITY_CSV_HEADERS).join(", ")}.`,
      });
    }
  }

  for (const requiredHeader of REQUIRED_ENTITY_CSV_HEADERS) {
    if (!seenHeaders.has(requiredHeader)) {
      issues.push({
        path: "entities.csv:header",
        message: `Missing required CSV column header "${requiredHeader}".`,
      });
    }
  }

  // Cannot reliably map cells if header definitions themselves are invalid
  const hasHeaderErrors = issues.some((i) =>
    i.path.startsWith("entities.csv:header"),
  );
  if (hasHeaderErrors) {
    return [];
  }

  const validEntities: EntitySeed[] = [];
  const seenEntityCodes = new Map<string, number>();

  for (let rowIdx = 1; rowIdx < rows.length; rowIdx++) {
    const row = rows[rowIdx];
    if (!row) {
      continue;
    }

    if (row.cells.length !== headers.length) {
      issues.push({
        path: `entities.csv:line ${row.lineNumber}`,
        message: `Expected ${headers.length} columns but found ${row.cells.length}.`,
      });
      continue;
    }

    const record: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    let aliasParseFailed = false;

    for (let colIdx = 0; colIdx < headers.length; colIdx++) {
      const colName = headers[colIdx];
      const cellValue = row.cells[colIdx]?.trim() ?? "";
      if (!colName) {
        continue;
      }
      if (colName === "description") {
        record[colName] = cellValue.length === 0 ? undefined : cellValue;
      } else if (colName === "aliases") {
        const parsedAliases = parseCanonicalAliasesCell(
          cellValue,
          row.lineNumber,
          issues,
        );
        if (parsedAliases === null) {
          aliasParseFailed = true;
        } else {
          record[colName] = parsedAliases;
        }
      } else {
        record[colName] = cellValue;
      }
    }

    if (aliasParseFailed) {
      continue;
    }

    const parsed = entitySeedSchema.safeParse(record);
    if (!parsed.success) {
      appendZodIssues(
        issues,
        `entities.csv:line ${row.lineNumber}`,
        parsed.error.issues,
      );
      continue;
    }

    const prevLine = seenEntityCodes.get(parsed.data.code);
    if (prevLine !== undefined) {
      issues.push({
        path: `entities.csv:line ${row.lineNumber}.code`,
        message: `Duplicate entity code "${parsed.data.code}" (already defined at line ${prevLine}).`,
      });
      continue;
    }

    seenEntityCodes.set(parsed.data.code, row.lineNumber);
    validEntities.push(parsed.data);
  }

  return validEntities;
}

function parseJsonArrayFile<T>(
  rawText: string,
  fileLabel: string,
  itemSchema: z.ZodType<T>,
  getCode: (item: T) => string,
  issues: ValidationIssueDetail[],
): T[] {
  let parsedJson: unknown;
  try {
    parsedJson = JSON.parse(rawText);
  } catch (error) {
    issues.push({
      path: fileLabel,
      message: `Invalid JSON syntax: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
    });
    return [];
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(parsedJson)) {
    issues.push({
      path: fileLabel,
      message: `Expected top-level JSON array in ${fileLabel}.`,
    });
    return [];
  }

  const validItems: T[] = [];
  const seenCodes = new Map<string, number>();

  for (let index = 0; index < parsedJson.length; index++) {
    const candidate = parsedJson[index];
    const parsed = itemSchema.safeParse(candidate);

    if (!parsed.success) {
      appendZodIssues(issues, `${fileLabel}[${index}]`, parsed.error.issues);
      continue;
    }

    const code = getCode(parsed.data);
    const prevIndex = seenCodes.get(code);
    if (prevIndex !== undefined) {
      issues.push({
        path: `${fileLabel}[${index}].code`,
        message: `Duplicate code "${code}" in ${fileLabel} (already defined at index ${prevIndex}).`,
      });
      continue;
    }

    seenCodes.set(code, index);
    validItems.push(parsed.data);
  }

  return validItems;
}

/**
 * Produces a structured breakdown & non-blocking warning report for a validated seed bundle.
 */
export function summarizeValidatedSeedBundle(
  bundle: ValidatedSeedBundle,
): SeedBundleReport {
  const entitiesByStatus: Partial<Record<EntityStatus, number>> = {};
  const entitiesBySector: Partial<Record<EntitySector, number>> = {};
  const entitiesByType: Partial<Record<EntityType, number>> = {};
  let entitiesWithAliasesCount = 0;

  for (const entity of bundle.entities) {
    entitiesByStatus[entity.status] = (entitiesByStatus[entity.status] ?? 0) + 1;
    entitiesBySector[entity.sector] = (entitiesBySector[entity.sector] ?? 0) + 1;
    entitiesByType[entity.entity_type] =
      (entitiesByType[entity.entity_type] ?? 0) + 1;
    if (entity.aliases.length > 0) {
      entitiesWithAliasesCount++;
    }
  }

  const evidenceBySourceType: Partial<Record<EvidenceSourceType, number>> = {};
  const warnings: SeedBundleWarning[] = [];
  const unresolvedOfficialEvidenceCodes: string[] = [];
  let evidenceMissingUrlCount = 0;

  for (let idx = 0; idx < bundle.evidence.length; idx++) {
    const ev = bundle.evidence[idx];
    if (!ev) {
      continue;
    }
    evidenceBySourceType[ev.source_type] =
      (evidenceBySourceType[ev.source_type] ?? 0) + 1;
    if (ev.url === null) {
      evidenceMissingUrlCount++;
      if (ev.source_type === "official") {
        unresolvedOfficialEvidenceCodes.push(ev.code);
        warnings.push({
          code: "OFFICIAL_SOURCE_MISSING_URL",
          path: `evidence[${idx}] (${ev.code})`,
          message: `Unresolved official source "${ev.code}" (publisher: "${ev.publisher}", title: "${ev.title ?? ""}") has url = null.`,
        });
      } else {
        warnings.push({
          code: "EVIDENCE_MISSING_URL",
          path: `evidence[${idx}] (${ev.code})`,
          message: `Evidence "${ev.code}" (${ev.source_type}, publisher: "${ev.publisher}") has url = null and relies on title "${ev.title ?? ""}".`,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  const factsByVerificationState: Partial<Record<FactVerificationState, number>> =
    {};
  const factsByDatePrecision: Record<DatePrecision | "null", number> = {
    day: 0,
    month: 0,
    year: 0,
    null: 0,
  };
  let factEvidenceLinksCount = 0;

  for (const fact of bundle.facts) {
    factsByVerificationState[fact.verification_state] =
      (factsByVerificationState[fact.verification_state] ?? 0) + 1;
    factEvidenceLinksCount += fact.evidence_codes.length;
    if (fact.valid_as_of === null) {
      factsByDatePrecision.null++;
    } else {
      factsByDatePrecision[fact.valid_as_of_precision]++;
    }
  }

  return {
    entitiesCount: bundle.entities.length,
    entitiesWithAliasesCount,
    evidenceCount: bundle.evidence.length,
    evidenceMissingUrlCount,
    unresolvedOfficialEvidenceCount: unresolvedOfficialEvidenceCodes.length,
    unresolvedOfficialEvidenceCodes,
    factsCount: bundle.facts.length,
    relationsCount: bundle.relations.length,
    factEvidenceLinksCount,
    breakdowns: {
      entitiesByStatus,
      entitiesBySector,
      entitiesByType,
      evidenceBySourceType,
      factsByVerificationState,
      factsByDatePrecision,
    },
    warnings,
  };
}

/**
 * Validates entities.csv, evidence.json, and facts.json together and reports
 * ALL schema, enum, duplicate-code, and cross-file reference errors at once.
 */
export function validateSeedSources(
  sources: RawSeedSources,
): ValidatedSeedBundle {
  const issues: ValidationIssueDetail[] = [];

  const entities = parseEntitiesCsv(sources.entitiesCsv, issues);
  const evidence = parseJsonArrayFile(
    sources.evidenceJsonText,
    "evidence.json",
    evidenceSeedSchema,
    (item) => item.code,
    issues,
  );
  const facts = parseJsonArrayFile(
    sources.factsJsonText,
    "facts.json",
    factSeedSchema,
    (item) => item.code,
    issues,
  );
  const relations = parseJsonArrayFile(
    sources.relationsJsonText ?? "[]",
    "relations.json",
    entityRelationSeedSchema,
    (item) => `${item.subject_code}:${item.predicate}:${item.object_code}`,
    issues,
  );

  // Cross-file referential & epistemic checks against known entity & evidence codes
  const knownEntityCodes = new Set(entities.map((e) => e.code));
  const evidenceByCode = new Map(evidence.map((ev) => [ev.code, ev]));

  for (let idx = 0; idx < facts.length; idx++) {
    const fact = facts[idx];
    if (!fact) {
      continue;
    }

    if (!knownEntityCodes.has(fact.entity_code)) {
      issues.push({
        path: `facts.json[${idx}].entity_code`,
        message: `Unknown entity_code "${fact.entity_code}" referenced by fact "${fact.code}" (not found in entities.csv).`,
      });
    }

    const resolvedSources: EvidenceSeed[] = [];
    let hasUnknownEvidence = false;

    for (let evIdx = 0; evIdx < fact.evidence_codes.length; evIdx++) {
      const evCode = fact.evidence_codes[evIdx];
      if (!evCode) {
        continue;
      }
      const evItem = evidenceByCode.get(evCode);
      if (!evItem) {
        hasUnknownEvidence = true;
        issues.push({
          path: `facts.json[${idx}].evidence_codes.${evIdx}`,
          message: `Unknown evidence_code "${evCode}" referenced by fact "${fact.code}" (not found in evidence.json).`,
        });
      } else {
        resolvedSources.push(evItem);
      }
    }

    if (
      fact.verification_state === "verified" &&
      !hasUnknownEvidence &&
      !canVerifyFactWithSources(resolvedSources)
    ) {
      issues.push({
        path: `facts.json[${idx}].verification_state`,
        message: `Fact "${fact.code}" cannot have verification_state "verified" without linked official evidence with a URL (${fact.evidence_codes.join(", ")}).`,
      });
    }
  }

  const entityByCode = new Map(entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  const relationTriplets = new Set<string>();
  for (let idx = 0; idx < relations.length; idx++) {
    const relation = relations[idx];
    if (!relation) continue;
    const triplet = `${relation.subject_code}\\u0000${relation.predicate}\\u0000${relation.object_code}`;
    if (relationTriplets.has(triplet)) issues.push({ path: `relations.json[${idx}]`, message: "Duplicate subject-predicate-object relation." });
    relationTriplets.add(triplet);
    const subject = entityByCode.get(relation.subject_code);
    const object = entityByCode.get(relation.object_code);
    if (!subject) issues.push({ path: `relations.json[${idx}].subject_code`, message: `Unknown relation subject "${relation.subject_code}".` });
    if (!object) issues.push({ path: `relations.json[${idx}].object_code`, message: `Unknown relation object "${relation.object_code}".` });
    if (relation.evidence_ref && !evidenceByCode.has(relation.evidence_ref)) {
      issues.push({ path: `relations.json[${idx}].evidence_ref`, message: `Unknown evidence_ref "${relation.evidence_ref}".` });
    }
    const isSupportedByFact = relation.evidence_ref !== null && facts.some((fact) => {
      const direct = fact.entity_code === relation.subject_code && fact.attribute === relation.predicate && fact.value === relation.object_code;
      const membership = relation.predicate === "has_station" && fact.attribute === "line_membership" &&
        fact.entity_code === relation.object_code && fact.value === relation.subject_code;
      return (direct || membership) && fact.evidence_codes.includes(relation.evidence_ref!);
    });
    if (!relation.evidence_ref || !isSupportedByFact) {
      issues.push({ path: `relations.json[${idx}].evidence_ref`, message: "Every seeded relation must cite evidence linked to a supporting fact." });
    }
    if (relation.predicate === "has_station" && subject && object &&
        (subject.entity_type !== "transit_line" || !["transit_stop", "rail_station", "transit_station"].includes(object.entity_type))) {
      issues.push({ path: `relations.json[${idx}].predicate`, message: "has_station must connect a transit line to a station entity." });
    }
  }

  if (issues.length > 0) {
    throw new SeedValidationError(
      `Seed validation failed with ${issues.length} error(s):\n` +
        issues.map((i) => `  - [${i.path}] ${i.message}`).join("\n"),
      issues,
    );
  }

  return {
    entities,
    evidence,
    facts,
    relations,
  };
}

export async function loadAndValidateSeedDir(
  seedDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data/seed"),
): Promise<ValidatedSeedBundle> {
  const entitiesPath = path.join(seedDir, "entities.csv");
  const evidencePath = path.join(seedDir, "evidence.json");
  const factsPath = path.join(seedDir, "facts.json");
  const relationsPath = path.join(seedDir, "relations.json");

  const fileReadIssues: ValidationIssueDetail[] = [];

  const readRequired = async (
    fullPath: string,
    label: string,
  ): Promise<string | null> => {
    try {
      return await readFile(fullPath, "utf8");
    } catch (error) {
      fileReadIssues.push({
        path: label,
        message: `Cannot read required seed file at ${fullPath}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
      });
      return null;
    }
  };

  const readOptionalRelations = async (): Promise<string> => {
    try {
      return await readFile(relationsPath, "utf8");
    } catch (error) {
      if (error && typeof error === "object" && "code" in error && error.code === "ENOENT") return "[]";
      fileReadIssues.push({ path: "relations.json", message: `Cannot read relation seed at ${relationsPath}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });
      return "[]";
    }
  };
  const [entitiesCsv, evidenceJsonText, factsJsonText, relationsJsonText] = await Promise.all([
    readRequired(entitiesPath, "entities.csv"),
    readRequired(evidencePath, "evidence.json"),
    readRequired(factsPath, "facts.json"),
    readOptionalRelations(),
  ]);

  if (
    fileReadIssues.length > 0 ||
    entitiesCsv === null ||
    evidenceJsonText === null ||
    factsJsonText === null
  ) {
    throw new SeedValidationError(
      `Missing or unreadable seed file(s) in ${seedDir}:\n` +
        fileReadIssues.map((i) => `  - [${i.path}] ${i.message}`).join("\n"),
      fileReadIssues,
    );
  }

  return validateSeedSources({
    entitiesCsv,
    evidenceJsonText,
    factsJsonText,
    relationsJsonText,
  });
}

const defaultSessionFactory = (): SeedDbSession => {
  const client = createNeonSessionClient();
  return {
    async connect() {
      await client.connect();
    },
    async query<Row = Record<string, unknown>>(
      text: string,
      values?: unknown[],
    ) {
      const result = values
        ? await client.query(text, values)
        : await client.query(text);
      return { rows: result.rows as Row[] };
    },
    async end() {
      await client.end();
    },
  };
};

async function getImportSyncPreview(
  bundle: ValidatedSeedBundle,
  features: readonly FeatureSeed[],
  sessionFactory: () => SeedDbSession,
  syncFeatures: boolean,
): Promise<ImportSyncReport> {
  const session = sessionFactory();
  await session.connect();
  try {
    await session.query("BEGIN READ ONLY");
    const report = await readImportSyncCounts(session, bundle, features, syncFeatures);
    await session.query("COMMIT");
    return { ...report, databasePreviewAvailable: true };
  } catch (error) {
    await session.query("ROLLBACK").catch(() => undefined);
    throw error;
  } finally {
    await session.end().catch(() => undefined);
  }
}

async function readImportSyncCounts(
  session: SeedDbSession,
  bundle: ValidatedSeedBundle,
  features: readonly FeatureSeed[],
  syncFeatures: boolean,
): Promise<Omit<ImportSyncReport, "databasePreviewAvailable">> {
  const count = async (table: "entities" | "facts" | "evidence" | "features", codes: readonly string[]) => {
    const result = await session.query<{ count: number | string }>(
      `SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS count FROM ${table} WHERE NOT (code = ANY($1::text[]))`,
      [codes],
    );
    return Number(result.rows[0]?.count ?? 0);
  };
  const factCodes = bundle.facts.map((fact) => fact.code);
  const evidenceCodes = bundle.evidence.map((item) => item.code);
  const factPairCodes = bundle.facts.flatMap((fact) => fact.evidence_codes.map((evidenceCode) => [fact.code, evidenceCode]));
  const pairFactCodes = factPairCodes.map(([factCode]) => factCode);
  const pairEvidenceCodes = factPairCodes.map(([, evidenceCode]) => evidenceCode);
  const linkCount = await session.query<{ count: number | string }>(
    `SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS count
     FROM fact_evidence fe
     JOIN facts f ON f.id = fe.fact_id
     JOIN evidence e ON e.id = fe.evidence_id
     WHERE NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM unnest($1::text[], $2::text[]) AS desired(fact_code, evidence_code)
       WHERE desired.fact_code = f.code AND desired.evidence_code = e.code
     )`,
    [pairFactCodes, pairEvidenceCodes],
  );
  const relationCount = await session.query<{ count: number | string }>(
    `SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS count FROM entity_relations er
     WHERE NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM unnest($1::text[], $2::text[], $3::text[]) AS desired(subject_code, predicate, object_code)
       WHERE desired.subject_code = er.subject_code AND desired.predicate = er.predicate AND desired.object_code = er.object_code
     )`,
    [bundle.relations.map((relation) => relation.subject_code), bundle.relations.map((relation) => relation.predicate), bundle.relations.map((relation) => relation.object_code)],
  );
  return {
    featuresDeletedCount: syncFeatures ? await count("features", features.map((feature) => feature.code)) : null,
    entityRelationsDeletedCount: Number(relationCount.rows[0]?.count ?? 0),
    factEvidenceLinksDeletedCount: Number(linkCount.rows[0]?.count ?? 0),
    entitiesNotInSeedCount: await count("entities", bundle.entities.map((item) => item.code)),
    factsNotInSeedCount: await count("facts", factCodes),
    evidenceNotInSeedCount: await count("evidence", evidenceCodes),
  };
}

/**
 * Upserts a validated seed bundle idempotently by `code` within one PostgreSQL
 * transaction. Features and fact/evidence associations are fully synchronized;
 * stale entities, facts and evidence are reported but never deleted.
 */
export async function upsertValidatedSeedBundle(
  bundle: ValidatedSeedBundle,
  sessionFactory: () => SeedDbSession = defaultSessionFactory,
  features: readonly FeatureSeed[] = [],
  importRun?: ImportRunChecksum,
  syncFeatures = true,
): Promise<{
  entitiesCount: number;
  evidenceCount: number;
  factsCount: number;
  relationsCount: number;
  factEvidenceLinksCount: number;
  featuresCount: number;
  importSync: ImportSyncReport;
  importSkipped?: boolean;
}> {
  const session = sessionFactory();
  await session.connect();

  try {
    await session.query("BEGIN");
    const importSyncCounts = await readImportSyncCounts(session, bundle, features, syncFeatures);

    if (importRun) {
      if (!/^[a-z0-9_-]{1,80}$/.test(importRun.datasetKey)) {
        throw new Error("Invalid automatic import dataset key.");
      }
      if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(importRun.checksum)) {
        throw new Error("Invalid automatic import checksum.");
      }
      await session.query("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock($1)", [72940132]);
      const previous = await session.query<{ checksum: string }>(
        "SELECT checksum FROM import_runs WHERE dataset_key = $1 FOR UPDATE",
        [importRun.datasetKey],
      );
      if (
        previous.rows[0]?.checksum === importRun.checksum &&
        importSyncCounts.featuresDeletedCount === 0 &&
        importSyncCounts.entityRelationsDeletedCount === 0 &&
        importSyncCounts.factEvidenceLinksDeletedCount === 0
      ) {
        await session.query("COMMIT");
        return {
          entitiesCount: bundle.entities.length,
          evidenceCount: bundle.evidence.length,
          factsCount: bundle.facts.length,
          relationsCount: bundle.relations.length,
          factEvidenceLinksCount: bundle.facts.reduce((count, fact) => count + fact.evidence_codes.length, 0),
          featuresCount: features.length,
          importSync: { ...importSyncCounts, databasePreviewAvailable: true },
          importSkipped: true,
        };
      }
    }

    const entityIdByCode = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const entity of bundle.entities) {
      const res = await session.query<{ id: string; code: string }>(
        `
        INSERT INTO entities (code, name_ar, name_en, entity_type, sector, status, description, aliases)
        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8)
        ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE SET
          name_ar = EXCLUDED.name_ar,
          name_en = EXCLUDED.name_en,
          entity_type = EXCLUDED.entity_type,
          sector = EXCLUDED.sector,
          status = EXCLUDED.status,
          description = EXCLUDED.description,
          aliases = EXCLUDED.aliases
        RETURNING id, code
        `,
        [
          entity.code,
          entity.name_ar,
          entity.name_en,
          entity.entity_type,
          entity.sector,
          entity.status,
          entity.description,
          entity.aliases,
        ],
      );
      const row = res.rows[0];
      if (!row?.id) {
        throw new Error(`Upsert did not return id for entity "${entity.code}".`);
      }
      entityIdByCode.set(entity.code, row.id);
    }

    const evidenceIdByCode = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const ev of bundle.evidence) {
      const res = await session.query<{ id: string; code: string }>(
        `
        INSERT INTO evidence (code, title, url, publisher, source_type, published_date, accessed_date, notes, published_date_precision)
        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9)
        ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE SET
          title = EXCLUDED.title,
          url = EXCLUDED.url,
          publisher = EXCLUDED.publisher,
          source_type = EXCLUDED.source_type,
          published_date = EXCLUDED.published_date,
          accessed_date = EXCLUDED.accessed_date,
          notes = EXCLUDED.notes,
          published_date_precision = EXCLUDED.published_date_precision
        RETURNING id, code
        `,
        [
          ev.code,
          ev.title,
          ev.url,
          ev.publisher,
          ev.source_type,
          ev.published_date,
          ev.accessed_date,
          ev.notes,
          ev.published_date_precision,
        ],
      );
      const row = res.rows[0];
      if (!row?.id) {
        throw new Error(`Upsert did not return id for evidence "${ev.code}".`);
      }
      evidenceIdByCode.set(ev.code, row.id);
    }

    for (const relation of bundle.relations) {
      await session.query(
        `INSERT INTO entity_relations (subject_code, predicate, object_code, ordinal, valid_as_of, evidence_ref)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
         ON CONFLICT (subject_code, predicate, object_code) DO UPDATE SET
           ordinal = EXCLUDED.ordinal,
           valid_as_of = EXCLUDED.valid_as_of,
           evidence_ref = EXCLUDED.evidence_ref,
           updated_at = NOW()`,
        [relation.subject_code, relation.predicate, relation.object_code, relation.ordinal, relation.valid_as_of, relation.evidence_ref],
      );
    }
    const staleRelations = await session.query<{ id: string }>(
      `DELETE FROM entity_relations er
       WHERE NOT EXISTS (
         SELECT 1 FROM unnest($1::text[], $2::text[], $3::text[]) AS desired(subject_code, predicate, object_code)
         WHERE desired.subject_code = er.subject_code AND desired.predicate = er.predicate AND desired.object_code = er.object_code
       ) RETURNING id`,
      [bundle.relations.map((relation) => relation.subject_code), bundle.relations.map((relation) => relation.predicate), bundle.relations.map((relation) => relation.object_code)],
    );
    const entityRelationsDeletedCount = staleRelations.rows.length;

    let factEvidenceLinksCount = 0;
    let factEvidenceLinksDeletedCount = 0;

    for (const fact of bundle.facts) {
      const entityId = entityIdByCode.get(fact.entity_code);
      if (!entityId) {
        throw new Error(
          `Missing resolved entity UUID for code "${fact.entity_code}".`,
        );
      }

      const factRes = await session.query<{ id: string; code: string }>(
        `
        INSERT INTO facts (code, entity_id, attribute, value, unit, valid_as_of, verification_state, notes, valid_as_of_precision)
        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9)
        ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE SET
          entity_id = EXCLUDED.entity_id,
          attribute = EXCLUDED.attribute,
          value = EXCLUDED.value,
          unit = EXCLUDED.unit,
          valid_as_of = EXCLUDED.valid_as_of,
          verification_state = EXCLUDED.verification_state,
          notes = EXCLUDED.notes,
          valid_as_of_precision = EXCLUDED.valid_as_of_precision
        RETURNING id, code
        `,
        [
          fact.code,
          entityId,
          fact.attribute,
          fact.value,
          fact.unit,
          fact.valid_as_of,
          fact.verification_state,
          fact.notes,
          fact.valid_as_of_precision,
        ],
      );

      const factRow = factRes.rows[0];
      if (!factRow?.id) {
        throw new Error(`Upsert did not return id for fact "${fact.code}".`);
      }

      const resolvedEvidenceIds: string[] = [];
      for (const evCode of fact.evidence_codes) {
        const evId = evidenceIdByCode.get(evCode);
        if (!evId) {
          throw new Error(
            `Missing resolved evidence UUID for code "${evCode}".`,
          );
        }
        resolvedEvidenceIds.push(evId);
      }

      const obsoleteFactLinks = await session.query<{ fact_id: string; evidence_id: string }>(
        `DELETE FROM fact_evidence WHERE fact_id = $1 AND NOT (evidence_id = ANY($2::uuid[])) RETURNING fact_id, evidence_id`,
        [factRow.id, resolvedEvidenceIds],
      );
      factEvidenceLinksDeletedCount += obsoleteFactLinks.rows.length;

      for (const evId of resolvedEvidenceIds) {
        await session.query(
          `
          INSERT INTO fact_evidence (fact_id, evidence_id)
          VALUES ($1, $2)
          ON CONFLICT (fact_id, evidence_id) DO NOTHING
          `,
          [factRow.id, evId],
        );
        factEvidenceLinksCount++;
      }
    }

    const seedFactCodes = bundle.facts.flatMap((fact) => fact.evidence_codes.map(() => fact.code));
    const seedEvidenceCodes = bundle.facts.flatMap((fact) => fact.evidence_codes);
    const orphanFactEvidenceLinks = await session.query<{ fact_id: string; evidence_id: string }>(
      `DELETE FROM fact_evidence fe
       WHERE NOT EXISTS (
         SELECT 1 FROM facts f
         JOIN evidence e ON e.id = fe.evidence_id
         JOIN unnest($1::text[], $2::text[]) AS desired(fact_code, evidence_code)
           ON desired.fact_code = f.code AND desired.evidence_code = e.code
         WHERE f.id = fe.fact_id
       )
       RETURNING fe.fact_id, fe.evidence_id`,
      [seedFactCodes, seedEvidenceCodes],
    );
    factEvidenceLinksDeletedCount += orphanFactEvidenceLinks.rows.length;

    let featuresDeletedCount = 0;
    if (syncFeatures) {
      const staleFeatures = await session.query<{ code: string }>(
        `DELETE FROM features WHERE NOT (code = ANY($1::text[])) RETURNING code`,
        [features.map((feature) => feature.code)],
      );
      featuresDeletedCount = staleFeatures.rows.length;
    }

    for (const feature of features) {
      const entityId = entityIdByCode.get(feature.entity_code);
      if (!entityId) {
        throw new Error(`Missing resolved entity UUID for feature entity "${feature.entity_code}".`);
      }
      const evidenceId = feature.evidence_code
        ? evidenceIdByCode.get(feature.evidence_code)
        : null;
      if (feature.evidence_code && !evidenceId) {
        throw new Error(`Missing resolved evidence UUID for feature evidence "${feature.evidence_code}".`);
      }
      await session.query(
        `
        INSERT INTO features (code, entity_id, evidence_id, geometry, osm_type, osm_id, osm_version, license, properties)
        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, ST_SetSRID(ST_GeomFromGeoJSON($4), 4326), $5, $6, $7, $8, $9::jsonb)
        ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE SET
          entity_id = EXCLUDED.entity_id,
          evidence_id = EXCLUDED.evidence_id,
          geometry = EXCLUDED.geometry,
          osm_type = EXCLUDED.osm_type,
          osm_id = EXCLUDED.osm_id,
          osm_version = EXCLUDED.osm_version,
          license = EXCLUDED.license,
          properties = EXCLUDED.properties
        `,
        [
          feature.code,
          entityId,
          evidenceId,
          JSON.stringify(feature.geometry),
          feature.osm_type,
          feature.osm_id,
          feature.osm_version,
          feature.license,
          JSON.stringify(feature.properties),
        ],
      );
    }

    if (importRun) {
      await session.query(
        `INSERT INTO import_runs (dataset_key, checksum, imported_at)
         VALUES ($1, $2, NOW())
         ON CONFLICT (dataset_key) DO UPDATE
           SET checksum = EXCLUDED.checksum, imported_at = EXCLUDED.imported_at`,
        [importRun.datasetKey, importRun.checksum],
      );
    }
    await session.query("COMMIT");

    return {
      entitiesCount: bundle.entities.length,
      evidenceCount: bundle.evidence.length,
      factsCount: bundle.facts.length,
      relationsCount: bundle.relations.length,
      factEvidenceLinksCount,
      featuresCount: features.length,
      importSync: {
        featuresDeletedCount: syncFeatures ? featuresDeletedCount : null,
        entityRelationsDeletedCount,
        factEvidenceLinksDeletedCount,
        entitiesNotInSeedCount: importSyncCounts.entitiesNotInSeedCount,
        factsNotInSeedCount: importSyncCounts.factsNotInSeedCount,
        evidenceNotInSeedCount: importSyncCounts.evidenceNotInSeedCount,
        databasePreviewAvailable: true,
      },
      ...(importRun ? { importSkipped: false } : {}),
    };
  } catch (error) {
    await session.query("ROLLBACK").catch(() => undefined);
    throw error;
  } finally {
    await session.end().catch(() => undefined);
  }
}

export async function runSeedImport(
  options: RunSeedImportOptions = {},
): Promise<SeedImportSummary> {
  const seedDir =
    options.seedDir ?? path.resolve(process.cwd(), "data/seed");
  const dryRun = options.dryRun ?? false;

  const bundle = await loadAndValidateSeedDir(seedDir);
  const featurePath = options.featuresPath ?? null;
  const features = featurePath
    ? await loadAndValidateFeatureCollection(featurePath, bundle.entities, bundle.evidence)
    : [];
  const syncFeatures = Boolean(featurePath);
  const report = summarizeValidatedSeedBundle(bundle);

  if (dryRun) {
    const hasDatabaseConfig = Boolean(process.env.DATABASE_URL?.trim() || process.env.DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED?.trim());
    const previewSessionFactory = options.sessionFactory ?? (hasDatabaseConfig ? defaultSessionFactory : null);
    const importSync: ImportSyncReport = previewSessionFactory
      ? { ...(await getImportSyncPreview(bundle, features, previewSessionFactory, syncFeatures)), databasePreviewAvailable: true }
        : {
          featuresDeletedCount: null,
          entityRelationsDeletedCount: null,
          factEvidenceLinksDeletedCount: null,
          entitiesNotInSeedCount: null,
          factsNotInSeedCount: null,
          evidenceNotInSeedCount: null,
          databasePreviewAvailable: false,
        };
    report.importSync = importSync;
    logger.info("Validated seed bundle in dry-run mode", {
      seedDir,
      entitiesCount: report.entitiesCount,
      evidenceCount: report.evidenceCount,
      factsCount: report.factsCount,
      relationsCount: report.relationsCount,
      factEvidenceLinksCount: report.factEvidenceLinksCount,
      featuresCount: features.length,
      ...importSync,
      warningsCount: report.warnings.length,
    });
    return {
      dryRun: true,
      seedDir,
      entitiesCount: report.entitiesCount,
      evidenceCount: report.evidenceCount,
      factsCount: report.factsCount,
      relationsCount: report.relationsCount,
      factEvidenceLinksCount: report.factEvidenceLinksCount,
      featuresCount: features.length,
      importSync,
      report,
    };
  }

  const counts = await upsertValidatedSeedBundle(
    bundle,
    options.sessionFactory,
    features,
    options.importRun,
    syncFeatures,
  );

  logger.info(counts.importSkipped
    ? "Seed bundle checksum unchanged; automatic import skipped"
    : "Seed bundle imported idempotently", {
    seedDir,
    ...counts,
    ...counts.importSync,
    warningsCount: report.warnings.length,
  });

  return {
    dryRun: false,
    seedDir,
    ...counts,
    report,
  };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/service-area.ts =====
export function approximateRadiusPolygon(longitude: number, latitude: number, radiusMeters: number, segments = 64) {
  const earthRadius = 6_371_000;
  const angularRadius = radiusMeters / earthRadius;
  const lat = latitude * Math.PI / 180;
  const lon = longitude * Math.PI / 180;
  const ring: number[][] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index <= segments; index += 1) {
    const bearing = 2 * Math.PI * index / segments;
    const nextLat = Math.asin(Math.sin(lat) * Math.cos(angularRadius) + Math.cos(lat) * Math.sin(angularRadius) * Math.cos(bearing));
    const nextLon = lon + Math.atan2(Math.sin(bearing) * Math.sin(angularRadius) * Math.cos(lat), Math.cos(angularRadius) - Math.sin(lat) * Math.sin(nextLat));
    ring.push([nextLon * 180 / Math.PI, nextLat * 180 / Math.PI]);
  }
  return { type: "Polygon" as const, coordinates: [ring] };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/static-dataset.ts =====
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { readdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { loadAndValidateFeatureCollection } from "@/lib/atlas/features";
import { loadAndValidateSeedDir } from "@/lib/atlas/seed";
import type { EntityRelationSeed, EntitySeed, EvidenceSeed, FactSeed, FeatureSeed } from "@/lib/atlas/schema";
import { getAreaBoundaryCodes } from "@/lib/atlas/area-boundaries";

export interface StaticAtlasDataset {
  entities: EntitySeed[];
  evidence: EvidenceSeed[];
  facts: FactSeed[];
  relations: EntityRelationSeed[];
  factEvidence: Array<{ fact_code: string; evidence_code: string }>;
  features: FeatureSeed[];
  adminUnits: Array<{ id: string; geometry: FeatureSeed["geometry"]; properties: Record<string, unknown> }>;
}

let datasetPromise: Promise<StaticAtlasDataset> | null = null;

async function collectFiles(directory: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const entries = await readdir(directory, { withFileTypes: true });
  const nested = await Promise.all(entries.map(async (entry) => {
    const absolute = path.join(directory, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) return collectFiles(absolute);
    return entry.isFile() ? [absolute] : [];
  }));
  return nested.flat();
}

async function computeStaticDatasetChecksum(root: string): Promise<string> {
  const hash = createHash("sha256");
  for (const relativeDir of ["data/seed", "data/features"] as const) {
    const directory = path.join(/*turbopackIgnore: true*/ root, relativeDir);
    const files = (await collectFiles(directory)).sort();
    if (!files.length) throw new Error(`Atlas export source is empty: ${relativeDir}`);
    for (const file of files) {
      hash.update(path.relative(root, file).split(path.sep).join("/"), "utf8").update("\0");
      hash.update(await readFile(file)).update("\0");
    }
  }
  return hash.digest("hex");
}

export async function getStaticAtlasDataset(root = process.cwd()): Promise<StaticAtlasDataset> {
  if (!datasetPromise || root !== process.cwd()) {
    datasetPromise = loadStaticAtlasDataset(root);
  }
  return datasetPromise;
}

async function loadStaticAtlasDataset(root: string): Promise<StaticAtlasDataset> {
  const seedDir = path.join(root, "data/seed");
  const featurePath = path.join(root, "data/features/features.geojson");
  const bundle = await loadAndValidateSeedDir(seedDir);
  const features = await loadAndValidateFeatureCollection(featurePath, bundle.entities, bundle.evidence);
  let adminUnits: StaticAtlasDataset["adminUnits"] = [];
  try {
    const source = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(root, "data/features/codab-admin-units.geojson"), "utf8")) as { features?: Array<{ id?: unknown; geometry?: unknown; properties?: unknown }> };
    adminUnits = (source.features ?? []).flatMap((feature) => feature.geometry && feature.properties && typeof feature.properties === "object"
      ? [{ id: String(feature.id ?? ""), geometry: feature.geometry as FeatureSeed["geometry"], properties: feature.properties as Record<string, unknown> }] : []);
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error && typeof error === "object" && "code" in error && error.code === "ENOENT")) throw error;
  }
  return {
    ...bundle,
    factEvidence: bundle.facts.flatMap((fact) => fact.evidence_codes.map((evidence_code) => ({
      fact_code: fact.code,
      evidence_code,
    }))),
    features,
    adminUnits,
  };
}

interface EntityMapGeometryRecord {
  geometry: FeatureSeed["geometry"];
  license: string[];
  attribution: string[];
  sources: string[];
  retrieval_dates: string[];
}

export interface StaticEvidenceCatalogItem extends EvidenceSeed {
  linked_facts_count: number;
  linked_entities_count: number;
}

export interface StaticEvidenceFact extends FactSeed {
  entity_name_ar: string;
  entity_name_en: string;
  is_contradicted: boolean;
}

export interface StaticEvidenceDetail {
  evidence: StaticEvidenceCatalogItem;
  facts: StaticEvidenceFact[];
}

export interface StaticEntityRelation {
  subject_code: string;
  predicate: EntityRelationSeed["predicate"];
  object_code: string;
  ordinal: number | null;
  valid_as_of: string | null;
  evidence_ref: string | null;
  subject: Pick<EntitySeed, "code" | "name_ar" | "name_en" | "entity_type" | "status">;
  object: Pick<EntitySeed, "code" | "name_ar" | "name_en" | "entity_type" | "status">;
  evidence: EvidenceSeed | null;
}

export interface StaticEntityNeighbor extends StaticEntityRelation {
  direction: "previous" | "next";
}

export interface StaticEntityPageData extends EntitySeed {
  has_boundary: boolean;
  facts: Array<FactSeed & { evidence: EvidenceSeed[] }>;
  relations: StaticEntityRelation[];
  neighbors: StaticEntityNeighbor[];
  mapGeometry: FeatureSeed["geometry"] | null;
  mapLicense: string;
  mapAttribution: string;
}

const entityMapGeometryPromises = new Map<string, Promise<Record<string, EntityMapGeometryRecord>>>();

async function loadEntityMapGeometries(root: string): Promise<Record<string, EntityMapGeometryRecord>> {
  let pending = entityMapGeometryPromises.get(root);
  if (!pending) {
    pending = readFile(path.join(root, "data/derived/entity-map-geometries.json"), "utf8")
      .then((text) => (JSON.parse(text) as { geometries: Record<string, EntityMapGeometryRecord> }).geometries)
      .catch(() => ({}));
    entityMapGeometryPromises.set(root, pending);
  }
  return pending;
}

function staticRelationRows(dataset: StaticAtlasDataset, code?: string): StaticEntityRelation[] {
  const entities = new Map(dataset.entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  const evidence = new Map(dataset.evidence.map((item) => [item.code, item]));
  return dataset.relations.flatMap((relation) => {
    if (code && relation.subject_code !== code && relation.object_code !== code) return [];
    const subject = entities.get(relation.subject_code);
    const object = entities.get(relation.object_code);
    if (!subject || !object) return [];
    const source = relation.evidence_ref ? evidence.get(relation.evidence_ref) ?? null : null;
    return [{
      ...relation,
      subject: { code: subject.code, name_ar: subject.name_ar, name_en: subject.name_en, entity_type: subject.entity_type, status: subject.status },
      object: { code: object.code, name_ar: object.name_ar, name_en: object.name_en, entity_type: object.entity_type, status: object.status },
      evidence: source,
    }];
  });
}

export async function getStaticEntityRelations(code?: string, root = process.cwd()): Promise<StaticEntityRelation[]> {
  return staticRelationRows(await getStaticAtlasDataset(root), code);
}

export async function getCodabLocatedWithin(code: string, root = process.cwd()): Promise<{ relation: "located_within"; pcodes: string[]; names_ar: string[]; evidence_code: string; source: string; license: string } | null> {
  try {
    const file = path.join(root, "data/features/codab-relations.json");
    const relations = JSON.parse(await readFile(file, "utf8")) as Array<{ entity_code: string; relation: "located_within"; pcodes: string[]; names_ar: string[]; evidence_code: string; source: string; license: string }>;
    return relations.find((item) => item.entity_code === code) ?? null;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error && typeof error === "object" && "code" in error && error.code === "ENOENT") return null;
    throw error;
  }
}

export function staticEntityNeighbors(dataset: StaticAtlasDataset, code: string): StaticEntityNeighbor[] {
  const relations = staticRelationRows(dataset);
  const stationRelations = relations.filter((relation) => relation.predicate === "has_station" && relation.object_code === code && relation.ordinal !== null);
  const neighbors = new Map<string, StaticEntityNeighbor>();
  for (const stationRelation of stationRelations) {
    for (const direction of ["previous", "next"] as const) {
      const ordinal = stationRelation.ordinal! + (direction === "previous" ? -1 : 1);
      const neighbor = relations.find((relation) => relation.predicate === "has_station" &&
        relation.subject_code === stationRelation.subject_code && relation.ordinal === ordinal);
      if (!neighbor || neighbor.object_code === code) continue;
      neighbors.set(`${neighbor.subject_code}:${neighbor.object_code}`, { ...neighbor, direction });
    }
  }
  return [...neighbors.values()].sort((a, b) => a.subject_code.localeCompare(b.subject_code) || a.ordinal! - b.ordinal!);
}

export async function getStaticEntityNeighbors(code: string, root = process.cwd()): Promise<StaticEntityNeighbor[]> {
  return staticEntityNeighbors(await getStaticAtlasDataset(root), code);
}

export async function getStaticEvidenceCatalog(root = process.cwd()): Promise<StaticEvidenceCatalogItem[]> {
  const dataset = await getStaticAtlasDataset(root);
  const factsByEvidence = new Map<string, FactSeed[]>();
  for (const fact of dataset.facts) for (const evidenceCode of fact.evidence_codes) {
    factsByEvidence.set(evidenceCode, [...(factsByEvidence.get(evidenceCode) ?? []), fact]);
  }
  return dataset.evidence.map((evidence) => {
    const facts = factsByEvidence.get(evidence.code) ?? [];
    return { ...evidence, linked_facts_count: facts.length, linked_entities_count: new Set(facts.map((fact) => fact.entity_code)).size };
  });
}

export async function getStaticEvidenceDetail(code: string, root = process.cwd()): Promise<StaticEvidenceDetail | null> {
  const dataset = await getStaticAtlasDataset(root);
  const evidence = await getStaticEvidenceCatalog(root).then((items) => items.find((item) => item.code === code));
  if (!evidence) return null;
  const entities = new Map(dataset.entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  const facts = dataset.facts.filter((fact) => fact.evidence_codes.includes(code));
  return {
    evidence,
    facts: facts.map((fact) => {
      const entity = entities.get(fact.entity_code)!;
      const contradictingClaim = fact.verification_state === "disputed" || dataset.facts.some((other) =>
        other.entity_code === fact.entity_code && other.attribute === fact.attribute && other.value !== fact.value &&
        !["superseded", "rejected"].includes(other.verification_state) && !["superseded", "rejected"].includes(fact.verification_state));
      return { ...fact, entity_name_ar: entity.name_ar, entity_name_en: entity.name_en, is_contradicted: contradictingClaim };
    }),
  };
}

export async function getStaticEntityPageData(code: string, root = process.cwd()): Promise<StaticEntityPageData | null> {
  const dataset = await getStaticAtlasDataset(root);
  const entity = dataset.entities.find((item) => item.code === code);
  if (!entity) return null;
  const evidenceByCode = new Map(dataset.evidence.map((item) => [item.code, item]));
  const mapGeometries = await loadEntityMapGeometries(root);
  const boundaryCodes = await getAreaBoundaryCodes(root);
  return {
    ...entity,
    has_boundary: boundaryCodes.has(code),
    facts: dataset.facts.filter((fact) => fact.entity_code === code).map((fact) => ({
      ...fact,
      evidence: fact.evidence_codes.flatMap((evidenceCode) => {
        const source = evidenceByCode.get(evidenceCode);
        return source ? [source] : [];
      }),
    })),
    relations: staticRelationRows(dataset, code),
    neighbors: staticEntityNeighbors(dataset, code),
    mapGeometry: mapGeometries[code]?.geometry ?? null,
    mapLicense: (mapGeometries[code]?.license ?? []).join(" + "),
    mapAttribution: (mapGeometries[code]?.attribution ?? []).join(" · "),
  };
}

export async function getExportMetadata(root = process.cwd()): Promise<{
  dataset_version: string;
  git_short_sha: string;
  import_checksum: string;
  generated_at: string;
  counts: { entities: number; facts: number; evidence: number; fact_evidence: number; features: number };
  licenses: {
    geometry: string;
    administrative_boundaries: string;
    research_data: string;
    code: string;
    contact: string;
    basemap: string;
  };
}> {
  const dataset = await getStaticAtlasDataset(root);
  const { execFileSync } = await import("node:child_process");
  let sha = process.env.VERCEL_GIT_COMMIT_SHA?.slice(0, 7) ?? process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GIT_COMMIT_SHA?.slice(0, 7);
  if (!sha) {
    try {
      sha = execFileSync("git", ["rev-parse", "--short=7", "HEAD"], { cwd: root, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] }).toString().trim();
    } catch {
      sha = "unknown";
    }
  }
  const checksum = await computeStaticDatasetChecksum(root);
  return {
    dataset_version: `${sha}+${checksum.slice(0, 12)}`,
    git_short_sha: sha,
    import_checksum: checksum,
    generated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    counts: {
      entities: dataset.entities.length,
      facts: dataset.facts.length,
      evidence: dataset.evidence.length,
      fact_evidence: dataset.factEvidence.length,
      features: dataset.features.length + dataset.adminUnits.length,
    },
    licenses: {
      geometry: "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0. Derived geometry exports must retain this ODbL attribution notice.",
      administrative_boundaries: "© CAPMAS (الجهاز المركزي للتعبئة العامة والإحصاء) via OCHA/HDX, CC BY-IGO. Source: https://data.humdata.org/dataset/cod-ab-egy",
      research_data: "© Mustafa Aboalazm (مصطفى أبوالعزم), licensed under CC BY 4.0",
      code: "No code license has been specified by the owner; code licensing remains for the owner to decide.",
      contact: "01092500454 · 3boalazm@gmail.com",
      basemap: "OpenFreeMap basemap; see https://openfreemap.org/ for its terms and attribution.",
    },
  };
}

export function serializeCsv(headers: readonly string[], rows: readonly Record<string, unknown>[]): string {
  const cell = (value: unknown): string => {
    let text: string;
    if (value === null || value === undefined) text = "";
    else if (Array.isArray(value)) text = value.join("|");
    else if (typeof value === "object") text = JSON.stringify(value);
    else text = String(value);
    return /[",\r\n]/.test(text) || text !== text.trim() ? `"${text.replaceAll('"', '""')}"` : text;
  };
  return `${headers.join(",")}\r\n${rows.map((row) => headers.map((header) => cell(row[header])).join(",")).join("\r\n")}\r\n`;
}

export function joinedFeatureCollection(dataset: StaticAtlasDataset, metadata: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getExportMetadata>>) {
  const entities = new Map(dataset.entities.map((entity) => [entity.code, entity]));
  return {
    type: "FeatureCollection" as const,
    name: "Greater Cairo Atlas geometry export",
    metadata,
    features: [...dataset.features.map((feature) => {
      const entity = entities.get(feature.entity_code)!;
      const props = (feature.properties.properties && typeof feature.properties.properties === "object"
        ? feature.properties.properties
        : {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
      return {
        type: "Feature" as const,
        id: feature.code,
        geometry: feature.geometry,
        properties: {
          code: feature.code,
          entity_code: entity.code,
          name_ar: entity.name_ar,
          name_en: entity.name_en,
          entity_type: entity.entity_type,
          status: entity.status,
          sector: entity.sector,
          aliases: entity.aliases,
          license: feature.license,
          attribution: feature.license.startsWith("ODbL")
            ? "© OpenStreetMap contributors, ODbL 1.0"
            : props.attribution,
          source: props.source,
          retrieval_date: props.retrieval_date,
          source_ids: props.source_ids,
          osm_type: feature.osm_type,
          osm_id: feature.osm_id,
          osm_version: feature.osm_version,
        },
      };
    }), ...dataset.adminUnits.map((feature) => ({
      type: "Feature" as const, id: feature.id, geometry: feature.geometry,
      properties: {
        ...feature.properties, code: feature.properties.code, entity_code: feature.properties.pcode,
        level: feature.properties.level, name_ar: feature.properties.name_ar, name_en: feature.properties.name_en ?? "",
        entity_type: "admin_unit", status: "unknown", sector: null, aliases: [],
        license: "CC BY-IGO", attribution: feature.properties.attribution,
        source: feature.properties.source, retrieval_date: "2026-11-15",
        source_ids: { pcode: feature.properties.pcode, level: feature.properties.level,
          parent_pcodes: feature.properties.parent_pcodes, area_sqkm: feature.properties.area_sqkm },
      },
    }))],
  };
}



===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/station-layer-visibility.ts =====
import { TRANSIT_MODE_ORDER, type TransitMode } from "./transit-modes";

/**
 * Rail-network reference layers (`public/rail-network*.geojson`) tag every element with
 * `mode: "enr"`, so the ENR transit-mode toggle governs both the reference network and its
 * station symbols. These are the layer ids that carry station symbols/labels.
 */
export const RAIL_REFERENCE_STATION_LAYERS = ["atlas-rail-stations", "atlas-rail-station-labels"] as const;

export type StationLayerGroup = "entity" | "overview" | "rail-reference";

export interface StationLayerVisibilityInput {
  /** Transit modes currently enabled in the layers panel (`النقل` → «وسائل النقل والفلترة»). */
  visibleModes: readonly TransitMode[];
  /** The «المحطات التبادلية» toggle. */
  showInterchanges: boolean;
  /** The `reference` layer group, which owns the OSM rail-network reference layers. */
  showRailReference: boolean;
  /** «إظهار المسارات المهجورة والمحافظة عليها». */
  showDisusedRail?: boolean;
  /** `compact` maps (entity pages, boundary previews) never create the overview/rail layers. */
  compact?: boolean;
}

export interface StationLayerVisibility {
  /** Whether the transit mode filter currently selects any mode at all. */
  anyModeVisible: boolean;
  /** Modes passed to the MapLibre `in` expression for `modes`. */
  modes: TransitMode[];
  /** Interchange rings are hidden entirely when the interchange toggle is off. */
  showInterchangeRings: boolean;
  /** Extra property filter for the rail reference station layers (`rail_status`). */
  railStatuses: string[] | null;
  groups: Record<StationLayerGroup, boolean>;
  /** Per-layer id → visible. Every station layer must appear here. */
  layers: Record<string, boolean>;
}

const ENTITY_STATION_LAYERS = [
  "atlas-station-entity-interchange-ring",
  "atlas-station-entity-icons",
  "atlas-station-entity-labels",
];

const OVERVIEW_STATION_LAYERS = [
  "atlas-transit-interchange-ring",
  "atlas-transit-station-icons",
  "atlas-transit-station-labels",
];

/**
 * Single source of truth for station symbol visibility.
 *
 * FIX D: a station layer may never outlive the filter state of its line/mode. Turning a mode off
 * (or the whole «النقل» group) removes that mode from `modes`, so both the line layers and the
 * station layers resolve to "no match" from the same expression. Keeping this in one pure function
 * makes the line/station pairing testable without a WebGL context.
 */
export function stationLayerVisibility(input: StationLayerVisibilityInput): StationLayerVisibility {
  const requested = [...new Set(input.visibleModes)];
  // Preserve the canonical order so the generated MapLibre expression is deterministic.
  const modes = TRANSIT_MODE_ORDER.filter((mode) => requested.includes(mode));
  const anyModeVisible = modes.length > 0;
  const showInterchangeRings = input.showInterchanges;
  const railStatuses = input.showDisusedRail ? null : ["operational", "under_construction"];
  const groups: Record<StationLayerGroup, boolean> = {
    entity: anyModeVisible,
    overview: anyModeVisible && !input.compact,
    "rail-reference": input.showRailReference && !input.compact && modes.includes("enr"),
  };
  const layers: Record<string, boolean> = {};
  for (const id of ENTITY_STATION_LAYERS) {
    layers[id] = groups.entity && (id.includes("interchange-ring") ? showInterchangeRings : true);
  }
  for (const id of OVERVIEW_STATION_LAYERS) {
    layers[id] = groups.overview && (id.includes("interchange-ring") ? showInterchangeRings : true);
  }
  for (const id of RAIL_REFERENCE_STATION_LAYERS) {
    layers[id] = groups["rail-reference"];
  }
  return { anyModeVisible, modes, showInterchangeRings, railStatuses, groups, layers };
}

/** MapLibre expression that matches station features against the enabled modes. */
export function stationModeFilter(modes: readonly TransitMode[]): unknown[] {
  if (!modes.length) return ["==", ["get", "osm_node_id"], -1];
  return ["any", ...modes.map((mode) => ["in", mode, ["get", "modes"]])];
}

/** Interchange ring expression; an off toggle removes the ring from the same mode filter. */
export function stationRingFilter(modes: readonly TransitMode[], showInterchanges: boolean): unknown[] {
  if (!showInterchanges) return ["==", ["get", "osm_node_id"], -1];
  return ["all", stationModeFilter(modes), ["==", ["get", "interchange"], true]];
}


/** Rail reference station expression: `rail_status` gate only (the mode gate is layer visibility). */
export function railReferenceStationFilter(railStatuses: readonly string[] | null): unknown[] {
  const base: unknown[] = ["all", ["==", ["geometry-type"], "Point"], ["in", ["get", "railway"], ["literal", ["station", "halt"]]]];
  if (!railStatuses) return base;
  return [...base, ["in", ["get", "rail_status"], ["literal", [...railStatuses]]]];
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/stats.ts =====
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { ATLAS_PHASE } from "@/lib/atlas/constants";
import { getSql } from "@/lib/db/client";
import {
  DatabaseNotConfiguredError,
  EnvValidationError,
  redactSecrets,
  type ValidationIssueDetail,
} from "@/lib/errors";
import { logger } from "@/lib/logger";

export const ATLAS_TABLE_COUNTS_SQL = `
SELECT
  (SELECT COUNT(*)::int FROM entities) AS entities,
  (SELECT COUNT(*)::int FROM facts) AS facts,
  (SELECT COUNT(*)::int FROM evidence) AS evidence,
  (SELECT COUNT(*)::int FROM features) AS features,
  (SELECT COUNT(*)::int FROM fact_evidence) AS fact_evidence
` as const;

export interface AtlasTableCountsRow {
  entities: number | string;
  facts: number | string;
  evidence: number | string;
  features: number | string;
  fact_evidence: number | string;
}

export interface AtlasTableCounts {
  entities: number;
  facts: number;
  evidence: number;
  features: number;
  fact_evidence: number;
}

export type StatsQueryExecutor = () => Promise<readonly AtlasTableCountsRow[]>;

export type AtlasStatsResponseBody =
  | {
      ok: true;
      status: "ok";
      service: "greater-cairo-atlas";
      phase: typeof ATLAS_PHASE;
      timestamp: string;
      counts: AtlasTableCounts;
      boundary_counts: { source: string; license: string; total: number; adm2: number; adm3: number };
    }
  | {
      ok: false;
      status:
        | "not_configured"
        | "misconfigured"
        | "schema_not_migrated"
        | "unhealthy";
      service: "greater-cairo-atlas";
      phase: typeof ATLAS_PHASE;
      timestamp: string;
      error: {
        code: string;
        message: string;
        issues?: ValidationIssueDetail[];
      };
    };

export interface AtlasStatsOutcome {
  httpStatus: number;
  body: AtlasStatsResponseBody;
}

const defaultStatsExecutor: StatsQueryExecutor = async () => {
  const sql = getSql();
  const rows = await sql.query(ATLAS_TABLE_COUNTS_SQL);
  return rows as AtlasTableCountsRow[];
};

function isMissingCoreTableError(error: unknown): boolean {
  if (!(error instanceof Error)) {
    return false;
  }
  const pgCode =
    "code" in error && typeof error.code === "string" ? error.code : undefined;
  return pgCode === "42P01";
}

function toNonNegativeInt(val: number | string | undefined): number | null {
  const num = Number(val);
  if (!Number.isInteger(num) || num < 0) {
    return null;
  }
  return num;
}

async function getCodabBoundaryCounts(): Promise<{ source: string; license: string; total: number; adm2: number; adm3: number }> {
  const file = path.join(process.cwd(), "data/features/codab-admin-units.geojson");
  try {
    const data = JSON.parse(await readFile(file, "utf8")) as { features?: Array<{ properties?: { level?: number } }> };
    const levels = (data.features ?? []).map((feature) => feature.properties?.level);
    return { source: "CAPMAS COD-AB 2017 (reviewed 2024)", license: "CC BY-IGO", total: levels.length,
      adm2: levels.filter((level) => level === 2).length, adm3: levels.filter((level) => level === 3).length };
  } catch {
    return { source: "CAPMAS COD-AB 2017 (reviewed 2024)", license: "CC BY-IGO", total: 0, adm2: 0, adm3: 0 };
  }
}

export async function getAtlasTableStats(
  executeQuery: StatsQueryExecutor = defaultStatsExecutor,
): Promise<AtlasStatsOutcome> {
  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();

  try {
    const rows = await executeQuery();
    const row = rows[0];

    const entities = toNonNegativeInt(row?.entities);
    const facts = toNonNegativeInt(row?.facts);
    const evidence = toNonNegativeInt(row?.evidence);
    const features = toNonNegativeInt(row?.features);
    const factEvidence = toNonNegativeInt(row?.fact_evidence);

    if (
      entities === null ||
      facts === null ||
      evidence === null ||
      features === null ||
      factEvidence === null
    ) {
      logger.error("Atlas stats query returned unexpected counts row", {
        rowCount: rows.length,
      });
      return {
        httpStatus: 503,
        body: {
          ok: false,
          status: "unhealthy",
          service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
          phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
          timestamp,
          error: {
            code: "INVALID_STATS_RESULT",
            message:
              "Database query succeeded, but table count payload was invalid.",
          },
        },
      };
    }

    return {
      httpStatus: 200,
      body: {
        ok: true,
        status: "ok",
        service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
        phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
        timestamp,
        counts: {
          entities,
          facts,
          evidence,
          features,
          fact_evidence: factEvidence,
        },
        boundary_counts: await getCodabBoundaryCounts(),
      },
    };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof DatabaseNotConfiguredError) {
      logger.warn("Atlas stats requested while DATABASE_URL is not configured", {
        code: error.code,
      });
      return {
        httpStatus: 503,
        body: {
          ok: false,
          status: "not_configured",
          service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
          phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
          timestamp,
          error: {
            code: error.code,
            message: error.message,
            ...(error.issues ? { issues: error.issues } : {}),
          },
        },
      };
    }

    if (error instanceof EnvValidationError) {
      logger.error("Atlas stats failed due to invalid DATABASE_URL format", {
        code: error.code,
        issues: error.issues,
      });
      return {
        httpStatus: 500,
        body: {
          ok: false,
          status: "misconfigured",
          service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
          phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
          timestamp,
          error: {
            code: error.code,
            message: error.message,
            ...(error.issues ? { issues: error.issues } : {}),
          },
        },
      };
    }

    if (isMissingCoreTableError(error)) {
      logger.warn("Atlas stats requested before 001_core_schema.sql migration", {
        error,
      });
      return {
        httpStatus: 503,
        body: {
          ok: false,
          status: "schema_not_migrated",
          service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
          phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
          timestamp,
          error: {
            code: "SCHEMA_NOT_MIGRATED",
            message:
              "Core Atlas tables are not migrated yet. Redeploy on Vercel (vercel-build) or run 'npm run db:migrate' to apply SQL migrations.",
          },
        },
      };
    }

    logger.error("Atlas stats query failed", { error });
    const safeCause =
      error instanceof Error ? redactSecrets(error.message) : "Unknown error";

    return {
      httpStatus: 503,
      body: {
        ok: false,
        status: "unhealthy",
        service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
        phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
        timestamp,
        error: {
          code: "DATABASE_UNAVAILABLE",
          message: `Failed to query Atlas table counts: ${safeCause}`,
        },
      },
    };
  }
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/status-timeline.ts =====
import { ENTITY_STATUSES, type EntityStatus, type FactVerificationState } from "@/lib/atlas/schema";

/**
 * BATCH 14.1 — «الحالة في تاريخ»: what did the atlas know about an entity in a given year?
 *
 * ## What this is allowed to use, and why it is narrower than it looks
 *
 * The only admissible input is a *dated status assertion*: an `operational_status` fact whose
 * `valid_as_of` says when that status was true. That is the whole vocabulary. Nothing here infers a
 * transition from an opening date, from a phase schedule, or from the fact that a later status exists.
 *
 * In particular the seed's `opening` facts are **not** usable as a status source. Their `valid_as_of` is
 * the date the claim was recorded (mostly 2026-11-14), while the opening date itself sits in the free-text
 * `value` — sometimes as prose («المترو أغسطس 2020؛ LRT ومجمع النقل يوليو 2022»). Parsing that text would
 * be guessing, and a guess is exactly what this feature must not do. They remain a research aid in the
 * panel, never an input to a rendered status.
 *
 * ## Why "today" can differ from the entity's stored status
 *
 * The map draws `entities.status`, a curated column. This resolver answers a narrower question — "what is
 * the latest dated status claim on or before this year?" — so where the two disagree it is because a dated
 * claim exists that the curated column does not follow (or a claim is marked disputed). That is reported,
 * not smoothed over: the UI says how many entities are dated and how many are unknown at the chosen year,
 * and the researcher panel shows the deciding fact. Any disagreement is a finding for the data review, not
 * something to be hidden by making the resolver copy the column.
 */

/**
 * The weakest verification state that may determine what the map *draws*.
 *
 * `candidate` means nobody has reviewed the claim yet. Letting an unreviewed row repaint the map is how a
 * timeline starts contradicting the published dataset in public — measured on the seed, 16 of 18
 * disagreements with the curated column came from a candidate-only claim. So a candidate may still be
 * reported as context (the panel shows it), but it does not get to change a colour.
 *
 * `corroborated` or better is required. This is the same bar the rest of the codebase already uses when it
 * ranks sources, not a new threshold invented for this feature.
 */
const DRAWABLE_STATES: ReadonlySet<FactVerificationState> = new Set(["verified", "corroborated"]);

export interface TimelineStatusFact {
  entity_code: string;
  attribute: string;
  value: string;
  valid_as_of: string;
  valid_as_of_precision: "day" | "month" | "year";
  verification_state: FactVerificationState;
  fact_code: string;
  /** Present only when a claim is explicitly unbounded; the seed has none, but the resolver honours it. */
  valid_to?: string | null;
}

/** `unknown` is not an entity status: it is this resolver's word for "no dated claim said anything". */
export type TimelineResolvedStatus = EntityStatus;

export interface TimelineStatusResolution {
  /** The status the map may draw. `unknown` means no drawing-grade dated claim exists for the year. */
  status: TimelineResolvedStatus;
  /**
   * The status the panel may *report* even when it is not drawing-grade — e.g. an unreviewed candidate.
   * Null when nothing beyond `status` is worth saying.
   */
  reported_status: EntityStatus | null;
  /** Why a reported claim was not allowed to colour the map. Null when it was. */
  not_drawn_reason: "unreviewed" | null;
  /** False when the entity had no dated claim at all at this year (drawn as «غير معروف في هذا التاريخ»). */
  dated: boolean;
  verification_state: FactVerificationState | "conflicted" | null;
  valid_as_of: string | null;
  valid_as_of_precision: TimelineStatusFact["valid_as_of_precision"] | null;
  /** The fact that decided the state, for the researcher panel. Null when nothing decided it. */
  fact_code: string | null;
  /** Every claim still in force at the chosen year, so a panel can show what was weighed. */
  candidates: Array<{ fact_code: string; value: string; valid_as_of: string; verification_state: FactVerificationState }>;
}

/** Claims the atlas has withdrawn are not evidence of a past state. */
const WITHDRAWN_STATES: ReadonlySet<FactVerificationState> = new Set(["rejected", "superseded"]);

const VERIFICATION_RANK: Record<FactVerificationState, number> = {
  verified: 0, corroborated: 1, single_source: 2, candidate: 3, disputed: 4, superseded: 5, rejected: 6,
};

/**
 * A year is the unit the user moves in, so a fact is compared by calendar year. `2015-05-31` is inside
 * 2015; `2016-01-01` is not. Using the year prefix rather than a parsed Date keeps this independent of
 * the runtime's timezone, which is the kind of bug that makes a timeline disagree with itself.
 */
export function factYear(fact: Pick<TimelineStatusFact, "valid_as_of">): number {
  return Number.parseInt(fact.valid_as_of.slice(0, 4), 10);
}

/** The year on or before which a claim stops applying, if the claim declares one. */
function endYear(fact: TimelineStatusFact): number | null {
  if (!fact.valid_to) return null;
  const year = Number.parseInt(fact.valid_to.slice(0, 4), 10);
  return Number.isFinite(year) ? year : null;
}

/** Only dated, non-withdrawn `operational_status` claims with a status the map can draw are admissible. */
export function isAdmissibleStatusFact(fact: TimelineStatusFact): boolean {
  return fact.attribute === "operational_status"
    && Boolean(fact.valid_as_of)
    && !WITHDRAWN_STATES.has(fact.verification_state)
    && ENTITY_STATUSES.includes(fact.value as EntityStatus);
}

/**
 * The claims that were in force during `year`: recorded on or before it, and (when the claim declares an
 * end) not yet ended. A claim that ends in year Y is still true *during* Y, so the comparison is
 * `endYear >= year`, not `>`.
 */
function inForceAt(facts: readonly TimelineStatusFact[], year: number): TimelineStatusFact[] {
  return facts.filter((fact) => isAdmissibleStatusFact(fact) && factYear(fact) <= year && (endYear(fact) === null || endYear(fact)! >= year));
}

/**
 * Resolve every entity's status for a year.
 *
 * Rules, in order:
 * 1. Ignore undated, withdrawn, or non-status claims.
 * 2. Keep the claims in force at the year (see `inForceAt`).
 * 3. The *latest* `valid_as_of` wins — not the most severe status, not the most recently added row.
 * 4. If two or more claims share that latest date and disagree, the result is `unknown` with
 *    `verification_state: "conflicted"`. The atlas does not pick a winner between two sources that
 *    contradict each other on the same day.
 * 5. If they agree, the best-supported one is reported, but a `disputed` claim on the winning date keeps
 *    the result marked disputed. Disagreement is never flattened away.
 * 6. An entity with no admissible claim in force is present in the result with `status: "unknown"` and
 *    `dated: false`. It is *not* assumed operational, and it is not silently omitted.
 */
export function resolveStatusByYear(facts: readonly TimelineStatusFact[], year: number): Map<string, TimelineStatusResolution> {
  const grouped = new Map<string, TimelineStatusFact[]>();
  for (const fact of inForceAt(facts, year)) {
    const rows = grouped.get(fact.entity_code);
    if (rows) rows.push(fact);
    else grouped.set(fact.entity_code, [fact]);
  }

  const results = new Map<string, TimelineStatusResolution>();
  for (const [code, candidates] of grouped) {
    const latestDate = candidates.map((fact) => fact.valid_as_of).sort().at(-1)!;
    const atLatest = candidates.filter((fact) => fact.valid_as_of === latestDate);
    const candidatesView = atLatest
      .slice()
      .sort((a, b) => a.fact_code.localeCompare(b.fact_code))
      .map((fact) => ({ fact_code: fact.fact_code, value: fact.value, valid_as_of: fact.valid_as_of, verification_state: fact.verification_state }));

    const distinctStatuses = new Set(atLatest.map((fact) => fact.value));
    if (distinctStatuses.size > 1) {
      results.set(code, {
        status: "unknown", reported_status: null, not_drawn_reason: null, dated: true,
        verification_state: "conflicted",
        valid_as_of: latestDate,
        // The coarsest precision on the winning date is the honest one to show.
        valid_as_of_precision: byCoarsestPrecision(atLatest),
        fact_code: null,
        candidates: candidatesView,
      });
      continue;
    }

    const selected = atLatest.slice().sort((a, b) => VERIFICATION_RANK[a.verification_state] - VERIFICATION_RANK[b.verification_state]
      || a.fact_code.localeCompare(b.fact_code))[0]!;
    // If any claim on the winning date is disputed, the status is disputed — a better-supported twin that
    // happens to say the same thing does not make the dispute go away. The value still stands (they agree),
    // but the caveat travels with it.
    const disputed = atLatest.some((fact) => fact.verification_state === "disputed");
    const drawable = DRAWABLE_STATES.has(selected.verification_state) && !(disputed && selected.verification_state !== "disputed");
    const agreedStatus = selected.value as EntityStatus;
    results.set(code, {
      status: drawable ? agreedStatus : "unknown",
      // An unreviewed claim is still worth reporting in the panel; it just may not repaint the map.
      reported_status: drawable ? null : agreedStatus,
      not_drawn_reason: drawable ? null : "unreviewed",
      dated: true,
      verification_state: disputed ? "disputed" : selected.verification_state,
      valid_as_of: selected.valid_as_of,
      valid_as_of_precision: selected.valid_as_of_precision,
      fact_code: selected.fact_code,
      candidates: candidatesView,
    });
  }
  return results;
}

function byCoarsestPrecision(facts: readonly TimelineStatusFact[]): TimelineStatusFact["valid_as_of_precision"] {
  const order: Array<TimelineStatusFact["valid_as_of_precision"]> = ["year", "month", "day"];
  return order.find((precision) => facts.some((fact) => fact.valid_as_of_precision === precision)) ?? "day";
}

/**
 * The range the slider may move through: the earliest dated status claim in the seed through this year.
 * The upper bound is never the latest claim — a project opening next decade must still be reachable, and
 * a sparsely dated dataset must not silently stop the timeline in the past.
 */
export function timelineYearRange(facts: readonly TimelineStatusFact[], now = new Date()): { min: number; max: number; latest_fact_year: number | null } {
  const years = facts.filter(isAdmissibleStatusFact).map(factYear).filter((year) => Number.isFinite(year));
  const thisYear = now.getUTCFullYear();
  const latestFactYear = years.length ? Math.max(...years) : null;
  return {
    min: years.length ? Math.min(...years) : thisYear,
    max: Math.max(thisYear, latestFactYear ?? thisYear),
    latest_fact_year: latestFactYear,
  };
}

/** How many entities the year changes relative to a base map, so the UI can be honest about the movement. */
export function timelineSummary(facts: readonly TimelineStatusFact[], year: number): {
  entities_with_dated_claim: number;
  entities_drawn: number;
  entities_unreviewed: number;
  entities_conflicted: number;
  entities_disputed: number;
} {
  let dated = 0; let drawn = 0; let unreviewed = 0; let conflicted = 0; let disputed = 0;
  for (const resolution of resolveStatusByYear(facts, year).values()) {
    if (resolution.dated) dated += 1;
    if (resolution.status !== "unknown") drawn += 1;
    if (resolution.not_drawn_reason === "unreviewed") unreviewed += 1;
    if (resolution.verification_state === "conflicted") conflicted += 1;
    if (resolution.verification_state === "disputed") disputed += 1;
  }
  return { entities_with_dated_claim: dated, entities_drawn: drawn, entities_unreviewed: unreviewed, entities_conflicted: conflicted, entities_disputed: disputed };
}

/** The label for an entity the timeline cannot speak about. */
export function undatedStatusLabel(language: "ar" | "en"): string {
  return language === "ar" ? "غير معروف في هذا التاريخ" : "Unknown at this date";
}

/** Why a status the panel reports was not allowed to colour the map. */
export function notDrawnReasonLabel(language: "ar" | "en", reason: nonNullableReason): string {
  return reason === "unreviewed"
    ? (language === "ar" ? "الادعاء المؤرخ لم يُراجَع بعد، فلا يغيّر الخريطة." : "The dated claim is unreviewed, so it does not change the map.")
    : "";
}
type nonNullableReason = NonNullable<TimelineStatusResolution["not_drawn_reason"]>;

/** The reason, shown in the researcher panel, for why a drawn status may differ from the curated column. */
export function timelineDisagreementNote(language: "ar" | "en"): string {
  return language === "ar"
    ? "الحالة المعروضة تتغير حسب السنة المختارة، وقد تختلف عن الحالة المنشورة لأن آخر ادعاء مؤرخ لا يطابق العمود المنقح. الاثنان ظاهران ولم يُرجَّح أحدهما آليًا."
    : "The status shown changes with the selected year and may differ from the published status because the latest dated claim does not match the curated column. Both remain visible; neither is resolved automatically.";
}

/**
 * BATCH 14.1 — decide the timeline state a URL asks for.
 *
 * This exists as a pure function because the bug it fixes was in exactly this glue: a shared
 * `?year=2019` link applied the year but left the control collapsed, so the recipient saw a map that
 * looked wrong with no way to tell why or to return to today. The rule is simple and worth pinning:
 *
 * - a valid year in the URL opens the control and selects that year;
 * - `today` in the URL, or no year at all, closes the control and returns to the range's maximum;
 * - a year outside the range is ignored rather than clamped, because silently showing 2015 for `?year=1800`
 *   would misrepresent what the sender asked for. The control stays closed, at today.
 */
export interface TimelineUrlState {
  year: number;
  enabled: boolean;
}

export function resolveTimelineUrlState(yearParam: string | null, range: { min: number; max: number }): TimelineUrlState {
  if (yearParam === null || yearParam.trim() === "") return { year: range.max, enabled: false };
  const parsed = Number.parseInt(yearParam, 10);
  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || String(parsed) !== yearParam.trim()) return { year: range.max, enabled: false };
  if (parsed < range.min || parsed > range.max) return { year: range.max, enabled: false };
  // Today's own year is the same state the control calls "closed", so treat it as closed to keep URLs clean.
  return { year: parsed, enabled: parsed !== range.max };
}

/** The query-string value for the current state: omitted when the map is showing today. */
export function timelineYearParam(state: TimelineUrlState, max: number): string | null {
  if (!state.enabled || state.year >= max) return null;
  return String(state.year);
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/submissions.ts =====
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { z } from "zod";
import { getSql } from "@/lib/db/client";
import { requireAdminImportEnv } from "@/lib/env";
import { AdminUnauthorizedError, RequestValidationError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { extractAdminTokenFromHeaders, verifyAdminImportToken } from "@/lib/atlas/admin-import";

const plainText = (max: number, min = 0) => z.string().trim().min(min).max(max).refine((value) =>
  !/[<>]/.test(value) && !/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/.test(value),
"Use plain text only; HTML and control characters are not accepted.");

export const submissionInputSchema = z.strictObject({
  entity_code: z.string().regex(/^[A-Z0-9]+(?:-[A-Z0-9]+)*$/).max(80),
  attribute: z.string().trim().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,119}$/),
  proposed_value: plainText(1000, 1),
  source_url: z.string().trim().min(1).max(2048).url().refine((value) => {
    try { const url = new URL(value); return ["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol) && !url.username && !url.password; }
    catch { return false; }
  }, "Provide a public HTTP or HTTPS source URL."),
  note: plainText(1000).optional().default(""),
  email: z.union([z.literal(""), z.string().trim().email().max(254)]).optional().default(""),
  website: z.string().max(500).optional().default(""),
});

export type SubmissionInput = z.infer<typeof submissionInputSchema>;
export type SubmissionStatus = "pending" | "accepted" | "rejected";
export interface SubmissionRow {
  id: string; entity_code: string; attribute: string; proposed_value: string; source_url: string;
  note: string; email: string | null; status: SubmissionStatus; reviewer_note: string | null; created_at: string;
}

export function parseSubmissionInput(value: unknown): SubmissionInput {
