  const parsed = submissionInputSchema.safeParse(value);
  if (!parsed.success) throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid suggestion form.", parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({ path: issue.path.join("."), message: issue.message })));
  return parsed.data;
}

export function submissionFingerprint(request: Request, salt = process.env.SUBMISSION_RATE_LIMIT_SALT ?? process.env.ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN ?? "gca-submission-limit") {
  const forwarded = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")?.split(",")[0]?.trim();
  const ip = forwarded || request.headers.get("x-real-ip")?.trim() || "unknown";
  return createHash("sha256").update(`${salt}\0${ip}`).digest("hex");
}

export async function createSubmission(request: Request, raw: unknown): Promise<{ id?: string; acceptedForReview: true; rateLimited?: true }> {
  const input = parseSubmissionInput(raw);
  // A filled honeypot is silently accepted to avoid revealing the spam control.
  if (input.website.trim()) return { acceptedForReview: true };
  const fingerprint = submissionFingerprint(request);
  const sql = getSql();
  const rows = await sql.query(
    `WITH request_lock AS MATERIALIZED (
       SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended($1::text, 0))
     ), recent AS MATERIALIZED (
       SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS attempts
         FROM submissions s CROSS JOIN request_lock
        WHERE s.request_fingerprint = $1 AND s.created_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '1 hour'
     )
     INSERT INTO submissions (entity_code, attribute, proposed_value, source_url, note, email, request_fingerprint)
     SELECT $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, NULLIF($7, ''), $1
       FROM request_lock
      WHERE (SELECT attempts FROM recent) < 5
     RETURNING id`,
    [fingerprint, input.entity_code, input.attribute, input.proposed_value, input.source_url, input.note, input.email],
  ) as Array<{ id: string }>;
  if (!rows[0]?.id) return { acceptedForReview: true, rateLimited: true };
  return { id: rows[0].id, acceptedForReview: true };
}

function assertAdminHeader(request: Request): void {
  const url = new URL(request.url);
  if (["token", "admin_import_token"].some((key) => url.searchParams.has(key))) {
    throw new RequestValidationError("Admin credentials are accepted only in an Authorization header.", [{ path: "query", message: "Remove token query parameters; use Authorization: Bearer …" }]);
  }
  const { ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN } = requireAdminImportEnv(process.env);
  if (!verifyAdminImportToken(extractAdminTokenFromHeaders(request.headers), ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN)) throw new AdminUnauthorizedError();
}

export async function listSubmissions(request: Request): Promise<SubmissionRow[]> {
  assertAdminHeader(request);
  const status = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("status");
  if (status && !["pending", "accepted", "rejected"].includes(status)) throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid submission status.");
  const rows = await getSql().query(
    `SELECT id::text, entity_code, attribute, proposed_value, source_url, note, email, status, reviewer_note, created_at::text
       FROM submissions WHERE ($1::text IS NULL OR status = $1) ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 500`,
    [status],
  );
  return rows as unknown as SubmissionRow[];
}

export async function reviewSubmission(request: Request, id: string, raw: unknown): Promise<SubmissionRow> {
  assertAdminHeader(request);
  if (!/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(id)) throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid submission ID.");
  const body = z.strictObject({ status: z.enum(["accepted", "rejected"]), reviewer_note: plainText(1000).optional().default("") }).safeParse(raw);
  if (!body.success) throw new RequestValidationError("Invalid review update.", body.error.issues.map((issue) => ({ path: issue.path.join("."), message: issue.message })));
  const rows = await getSql().query(
    `UPDATE submissions SET status = $2, reviewer_note = NULLIF($3, ''), updated_at = NOW()
      WHERE id = $1::uuid
      RETURNING id::text, entity_code, attribute, proposed_value, source_url, note, email, status, reviewer_note, created_at::text`,
    [id, body.data.status, body.data.reviewer_note],
  );
  const row = (rows as unknown as SubmissionRow[])[0];
  if (!row) throw new RequestValidationError("Submission not found.");
  // Accepted suggestions are still only research review records; this code never writes facts.
  return row;
}

export async function exportAcceptedSubmissions(request: Request): Promise<{ schema_version: 1; exported_at: string; warning: string; submissions: SubmissionRow[] }> {
  assertAdminHeader(request);
  const rows = await getSql().query(
    `SELECT id::text, entity_code, attribute, proposed_value, source_url, note, email, status, reviewer_note, created_at::text
       FROM submissions WHERE status = 'accepted' ORDER BY created_at ASC`,
  );
  return { schema_version: 1, exported_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    warning: "Research review export only. Do not auto-apply suggestions to canonical facts.", submissions: rows as unknown as SubmissionRow[] };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/atlas/transit-feature-filters.ts =====
import { transitModesForEntityCode, type TransitMode } from "./transit-modes";

const TRANSIT_FEATURE_TYPES = new Set(["transit_line", "transit_stop", "rail_station", "transit_station"]);

export function isTransitFeatureType(featureType: string): boolean {
  return TRANSIT_FEATURE_TYPES.has(featureType);
}

/**
 * Resolves the modes that a transit feature is drawn under.
 *
 * The map data comes from two places: `/api/atlas/features` (a database row with explicit
 * `modes`) and the static home-page dataset. Both may leave `modes` empty for a station whose line
 * is not documented, so the entity code is used as the last resort — exactly like the MapLibre
 * symbol layers do. Returning `["unclassified"]` keeps such a station visible under the
 * «محطة — الخط غير موثق» mode instead of making it unconditionally immune to every filter.
 */
export function resolvedTransitModes(
  entityCode: string,
  featureModes: readonly string[] | undefined,
  lineCodes: readonly string[] = [],
): TransitMode[] {
  if (featureModes?.length) return featureModes as TransitMode[];
  const inferred = transitModesForEntityCode(entityCode, lineCodes);
  if (inferred.length) return inferred;
  return ["unclassified"];
}

/** FIX D: a transit feature is visible only when at least one of its modes is enabled. */
export function transitFeatureVisible(
  entityCode: string,
  featureModes: readonly string[] | undefined,
  visibleModes: readonly TransitMode[],
  lineCodes: readonly string[] = [],
): boolean {
  const modes = resolvedTransitModes(entityCode, featureModes, lineCodes);
  // Never let an unresolved mode become an unfilterable station: with no modes enabled nothing shows.
  if (!visibleModes.length) return false;
  return modes.some((mode) => visibleModes.includes(mode));
}

/** FIX D: a transit feature is visible only when at least one of its lines is enabled. */
export function transitLineEnabled(
  entityCode: string,
  featureLineCodes: readonly string[] | undefined,
  visibleLines: readonly string[],
): boolean {
  const lineCodes = featureLineCodes?.length ? featureLineCodes : entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-") ? [entityCode] : [];
  if (!lineCodes.length) return true;
  return lineCodes.some((line) => visibleLines.includes(line));
}

const MULTI_MODE_STATIONS: Record<string, TransitMode[]> = {
  "STOP-ADLY-MANSOUR": ["metro", "lrt"],
  "STOP-CENTRAL-CAPITAL": ["lrt", "hsr"],
  "STOP-ARTS-CULTURE-CITY": ["lrt", "monorail_east"],
  "STOP-WADI-EL-NIL": ["metro", "monorail_west"],
  "STOP-MONORAIL-MOSHIR-TANTAWY": ["monorail_east"],
};

const INTERCHANGE_STATION_CODES = new Set([
  "STOP-SADAT", "STOP-SHUHADA", "STOP-MONORAIL-MOSHIR-TANTAWY", "STOP-HADAEK-OCTOBER", "STOP-6-OCTOBER-INTERCHANGE", "RAIL-RAMSES",
]);

const METRO_MISC_STATIONS = new Set([
  "STOP-SADAT", "STOP-DEMERDASH", "STOP-ABBASSIA", "STOP-KIT-KAT", "STOP-CAIRO-STADIUM",
  "STOP-M2-SHUBRA-ELKHEIMA", "STOP-M2-KHALAFAWY", "STOP-M2-ST-TERESA",
  "STOP-M3-ROD-ELFARAG-AXIS", "STOP-M3-RING-ROAD", "STOP-M1-EL-MARG", "STOP-M1-NEW-EL-MARG",
]);

export function transitModesForEntityCode(entityCode: string, lineCodes: readonly string[] = []): TransitMode[] {
  const modesFromLines = new Set<TransitMode>();
  for (const line of lineCodes) {
    if (line.startsWith("TRANSIT-METRO-L")) modesFromLines.add(line.endsWith("L4") ? "metro_l4" : "metro");
    else if (line.startsWith("TRANSIT-LRT")) modesFromLines.add("lrt");
    else if (line.includes("MONORAIL-EAST")) modesFromLines.add("monorail_east");
    else if (line.includes("MONORAIL-WEST")) modesFromLines.add("monorail_west");
    else if (line.includes("BRT")) modesFromLines.add("brt");
    else if (line.includes("HSR")) modesFromLines.add("hsr");
    else if (line.includes("ENR") || line.includes("SUEZ-TRAIN")) modesFromLines.add("enr");
  }
  const explicit = MULTI_MODE_STATIONS[entityCode];
  explicit?.forEach((mode) => modesFromLines.add(mode));
  if (modesFromLines.size) return [...modesFromLines];
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-METRO-L4")) return ["metro_l4"];
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-METRO-L") || /^STOP-M[123]-/.test(entityCode) || METRO_MISC_STATIONS.has(entityCode)) return ["metro"];
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-LRT") || entityCode.startsWith("STOP-LRT-")) return ["lrt"];
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-MONORAIL-EAST") || entityCode.startsWith("STOP-MRE-") || entityCode === "STOP-MONORAIL-MOSHIR-TANTAWY") return ["monorail_east"];
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-MONORAIL-WEST") || entityCode.startsWith("STOP-MRW-")) return ["monorail_west"];
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-BRT") || entityCode.startsWith("STOP-BRT-")) return ["brt"];
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-HSR") || entityCode.startsWith("STOP-HSR-")) return ["hsr"];
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-SUEZ-TRAIN") || entityCode.startsWith("RAIL-")) return ["enr"];
  if (entityCode === "STOP-ADLY-MANSOUR") return ["metro", "lrt"];
  if (entityCode === "STOP-ADALA-CITY") return ["monorail_east"];
  if (entityCode === "STOP-AHMED-OMAR-HASHEM") return ["hsr"];
  return [];
}

export function isTransitInterchangeEntityCode(entityCode: string): boolean {
  return transitModesForEntityCode(entityCode).length > 1 || INTERCHANGE_STATION_CODES.has(entityCode);
}

export function transitModeForEntityCode(entityCode: string, lineCodes: readonly string[] = []): TransitMode | null {
  return transitModesForEntityCode(entityCode, lineCodes)[0] ?? null;
}

export function transitModeLabel(entityCode: string, language: "ar" | "en", lineCodes: readonly string[] = []): string | null {
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-METRO-L1") || entityCode.startsWith("STOP-M1-")) return language === "ar" ? "مترو · الخط الأول" : "Metro · Line 1";
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-METRO-L2") || entityCode.startsWith("STOP-M2-")) return language === "ar" ? "مترو · الخط الثاني" : "Metro · Line 2";
  if (entityCode.startsWith("TRANSIT-METRO-L3") || entityCode.startsWith("STOP-M3-")) return language === "ar" ? "مترو · الخط الثالث" : "Metro · Line 3";
  if (entityCode.includes("METRO-L4")) return language === "ar" ? "مترو · الخط الرابع" : "Metro · Line 4";
  const modes = transitModesForEntityCode(entityCode, lineCodes);
  if (modes.length > 1) return language === "ar" ? "محطة تبادلية" : "Interchange station";
  const mode = modes[0];
  if (mode) return TRANSIT_MODE_LABELS[mode][language];
  return entityCode.startsWith("STOP-") || entityCode.startsWith("RAIL-")
    ? TRANSIT_MODE_LABELS.unclassified[language]
    : null;
}


===== FILE: src/lib/db/automatic-import.ts =====
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { readdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { runSeedImport, type SeedImportSummary } from "@/lib/atlas/seed";

async function collectFiles(directory: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const entries = await readdir(directory, { withFileTypes: true });
  const nested = await Promise.all(entries.map(async (entry) => {
    const absolute = path.join(directory, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) return collectFiles(absolute);
    if (entry.isFile()) return [absolute];
    return [];
  }));
  return nested.flat();
}

/** Hash every committed artifact under data/seed and data/features deterministically. */
export async function computeAtlasDatasetChecksum(
  root = process.cwd(),
): Promise<string> {
  const hash = createHash("sha256");
  for (const relativeDir of ["data/seed", "data/features"]) {
    const absoluteDir = path.join(root, relativeDir);
    const files = (await collectFiles(absoluteDir)).sort();
    if (files.length === 0) throw new Error(`Automatic import source is empty: ${relativeDir}`);
    for (const file of files) {
      const relativePath = path.relative(root, file).split(path.sep).join("/");
      hash.update(relativePath, "utf8").update("\0");
      hash.update(await readFile(file)).update("\0");
    }
  }
  return hash.digest("hex");
}

/** Validate all source data before opening the atomic import transaction. */
export async function runAutomaticAtlasImport(
  root = process.cwd(),
  options: { dryRun?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<SeedImportSummary> {
  const checksum = await computeAtlasDatasetChecksum(root);
  return runSeedImport({
    seedDir: path.join(root, "data/seed"),
    featuresPath: path.join(root, "data/features/features.geojson"),
    // New key forces one full reconciliation on the first deployment of sync-v2,
    // even when the source checksum already matches a prior upsert-only import.
    importRun: options.dryRun ? undefined : { datasetKey: "atlas-seed-features-full-sync-v2", checksum },
    dryRun: options.dryRun ?? false,
  });
}


===== FILE: src/lib/db/client.ts =====
import {
  Client,
  neon,
  neonConfig,
  type NeonQueryFunction,
} from "@neondatabase/serverless";
import ws from "ws";
import {
  requireDatabaseEnv,
  requireMigrationDatabaseEnv,
} from "@/lib/env";

function assertServerRuntime(): void {
  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
    throw new Error(
      "Database client cannot be imported or executed in a browser environment.",
    );
  }
}

let cachedConnectionString: string | null = null;
let cachedSqlClient: NeonQueryFunction<false, false> | null = null;

/**
 * Returns a stateless Neon HTTP query function configured exclusively
 * from process.env.DATABASE_URL. Ideal for Next.js Route Handlers,
 * Server Components, and Vercel Serverless / Preview functions.
 */
export function getSql(): NeonQueryFunction<false, false> {
  assertServerRuntime();
  const { DATABASE_URL } = requireDatabaseEnv();

  if (cachedSqlClient && cachedConnectionString === DATABASE_URL) {
    return cachedSqlClient;
  }

  cachedConnectionString = DATABASE_URL;
  cachedSqlClient = neon(DATABASE_URL);
  return cachedSqlClient;
}

/**
 * Creates a session-capable Neon PostgreSQL WebSocket client for CLI / build
 * workloads requiring advisory locks and interactive transactions (e.g. migrations
 * and seed imports). Prefers DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED over pooled DATABASE_URL.
 */
export function createNeonSessionClient(
  rawEnv: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
): Client {
  assertServerRuntime();
  const { connectionString } = requireMigrationDatabaseEnv(rawEnv);

  if (typeof WebSocket === "undefined") {
    neonConfig.webSocketConstructor = ws;
  }

  return new Client({
    connectionString,
  });
}


===== FILE: src/lib/db/health.ts =====
import { ATLAS_PHASE } from "@/lib/atlas/constants";
import { getSql } from "@/lib/db/client";
import {
  DatabaseNotConfiguredError,
  EnvValidationError,
  redactSecrets,
  type ValidationIssueDetail,
} from "@/lib/errors";
import { logger } from "@/lib/logger";

export const HEALTH_CHECK_SQL =
  "SELECT PostGIS_Version() AS postgis_version, ST_AsGeoJSON(ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint(31.2357,30.0444),4326)) AS point_geojson, (SELECT COUNT(*)::int FROM schema_migrations) AS applied_migrations" as const;

export const HEALTH_CHECK_FALLBACK_SQL =
  "SELECT PostGIS_Version() AS postgis_version, ST_AsGeoJSON(ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint(31.2357,30.0444),4326)) AS point_geojson, 0::int AS applied_migrations" as const;

export interface HealthSpatialPointGeoJson {
  type: "Point";
  coordinates: [number, number];
}

export interface HealthProbeRow {
  postgis_version: string;
  point_geojson: string;
  applied_migrations: number | string;
}

export type HealthQueryExecutor = () => Promise<readonly HealthProbeRow[]>;

export type HealthCheckResponseBody =
  | {
      ok: true;
      status: "healthy";
      service: "greater-cairo-atlas";
      phase: typeof ATLAS_PHASE;
      timestamp: string;
      latencyMs: number;
      database: {
        configured: true;
        connected: true;
        appliedMigrationsCount: number;
        postgisVersion: string;
        spatialProbe: {
          srid: 4326;
          geoJsonRaw: string;
          geometry: HealthSpatialPointGeoJson | Record<string, unknown>;
        };
      };
    }
  | {
      ok: false;
      status: "not_configured" | "misconfigured" | "unhealthy";
      service: "greater-cairo-atlas";
      phase: typeof ATLAS_PHASE;
      timestamp: string;
      database: {
        configured: boolean;
        connected: false;
        appliedMigrationsCount: number | null;
      };
      error: {
        code: string;
        message: string;
        issues?: ValidationIssueDetail[];
      };
    };

export interface HealthCheckOutcome {
  httpStatus: number;
  body: HealthCheckResponseBody;
}

function isMissingSchemaMigrationsTableError(error: unknown): boolean {
  if (!(error instanceof Error)) {
    return false;
  }
  const pgCode =
    "code" in error && typeof error.code === "string" ? error.code : undefined;
  return (
    pgCode === "42P01" &&
    error.message.toLowerCase().includes("schema_migrations")
  );
}

function isMissingPostgisError(error: unknown): boolean {
  if (!(error instanceof Error)) {
    return false;
  }
  const pgCode =
    "code" in error && typeof error.code === "string" ? error.code : undefined;
  return (
    pgCode === "42883" &&
    error.message.toLowerCase().includes("postgis_version")
  );
}

const defaultExecutor: HealthQueryExecutor = async () => {
  const sql = getSql();
  try {
    const rows = await sql.query(HEALTH_CHECK_SQL);
    return rows as HealthProbeRow[];
  } catch (error) {
    if (isMissingSchemaMigrationsTableError(error)) {
      const fallbackRows = await sql.query(HEALTH_CHECK_FALLBACK_SQL);
      return fallbackRows as HealthProbeRow[];
    }
    throw error;
  }
};

export async function runDatabaseHealthCheck(
  executeProbe: HealthQueryExecutor = defaultExecutor,
): Promise<HealthCheckOutcome> {
  const startedAt = performance.now();
  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();

  try {
    const rows = await executeProbe();
    const firstRow = rows[0];
    const parsedMigrationCount =
      firstRow !== undefined ? Number(firstRow.applied_migrations) : NaN;

    if (
      !firstRow ||
      typeof firstRow.postgis_version !== "string" ||
      typeof firstRow.point_geojson !== "string" ||
      !Number.isInteger(parsedMigrationCount) ||
      parsedMigrationCount < 0
    ) {
      logger.error("Health check returned unexpected row shape", {
        rowCount: rows.length,
      });
      return {
        httpStatus: 503,
        body: {
          ok: false,
          status: "unhealthy",
          service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
          phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
          timestamp,
          database: {
            configured: true,
            connected: false,
            appliedMigrationsCount: null,
          },
          error: {
            code: "INVALID_HEALTH_PROBE_RESULT",
            message:
              "Database query succeeded, but PostGIS health probe returned an unexpected payload.",
          },
        },
      };
    }

    let parsedGeometry: HealthSpatialPointGeoJson | Record<string, unknown>;
    try {
      parsedGeometry = JSON.parse(firstRow.point_geojson) as
        | HealthSpatialPointGeoJson
        | Record<string, unknown>;
    } catch {
      parsedGeometry = { raw: firstRow.point_geojson };
    }

    const latencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt);

    return {
      httpStatus: 200,
      body: {
        ok: true,
        status: "healthy",
        service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
        phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
        timestamp,
        latencyMs,
        database: {
          configured: true,
          connected: true,
          appliedMigrationsCount: parsedMigrationCount,
          postgisVersion: firstRow.postgis_version,
          spatialProbe: {
            srid: 4326,
            geoJsonRaw: firstRow.point_geojson,
            geometry: parsedGeometry,
          },
        },
      },
    };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof DatabaseNotConfiguredError) {
      logger.warn("Health check requested while DATABASE_URL is not configured", {
        code: error.code,
      });
      return {
        httpStatus: 503,
        body: {
          ok: false,
          status: "not_configured",
          service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
          phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
          timestamp,
          database: {
            configured: false,
            connected: false,
            appliedMigrationsCount: null,
          },
          error: {
            code: error.code,
            message: error.message,
            ...(error.issues ? { issues: error.issues } : {}),
          },
        },
      };
    }

    if (error instanceof EnvValidationError) {
      logger.error("Health check failed due to invalid DATABASE_URL format", {
        code: error.code,
        issues: error.issues,
      });
      return {
        httpStatus: 500,
        body: {
          ok: false,
          status: "misconfigured",
          service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
          phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
          timestamp,
          database: {
            configured: false,
            connected: false,
            appliedMigrationsCount: null,
          },
          error: {
            code: error.code,
            message: error.message,
            ...(error.issues ? { issues: error.issues } : {}),
          },
        },
      };
    }

    if (isMissingPostgisError(error)) {
      logger.error("Connected to PostgreSQL, but PostGIS extension is missing", {
        error,
      });
      return {
        httpStatus: 503,
        body: {
          ok: false,
          status: "unhealthy",
          service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
          phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
          timestamp,
          database: {
            configured: true,
            connected: false,
            appliedMigrationsCount: 0,
          },
          error: {
            code: "POSTGIS_NOT_ENABLED",
            message:
              "Connected to Neon PostgreSQL, but PostGIS is not enabled yet. Redeploy on Vercel (vercel-build) or run 'npm run db:migrate' to apply SQL migrations.",
          },
        },
      };
    }

    logger.error("Database health check failed", { error });
    const safeCause =
      error instanceof Error ? redactSecrets(error.message) : "Unknown error";

    return {
      httpStatus: 503,
      body: {
        ok: false,
        status: "unhealthy",
        service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
        phase: ATLAS_PHASE,
        timestamp,
        database: {
          configured: true,
          connected: false,
          appliedMigrationsCount: null,
        },
        error: {
          code: "DATABASE_UNAVAILABLE",
          message: `Failed to query Neon PostgreSQL + PostGIS: ${safeCause}`,
        },
      },
    };
  }
}


===== FILE: src/lib/db/migrate.ts =====
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { readdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { createNeonSessionClient } from "@/lib/db/client";
import { MigrationError } from "@/lib/errors";
import { logger } from "@/lib/logger";

const MIGRATION_FILENAME_PATTERN = /^(\d{3})_([a-z0-9_]+)\.sql$/;
const MIGRATION_ADVISORY_LOCK_KEY = 72940131;

export interface MigrationFile {
  version: string;
  name: string;
  filename: string;
  fullPath: string;
  sql: string;
  checksum: string;
}

export interface AppliedMigrationRecord {
  version: string;
  name: string;
  checksum: string;
  applied_at: string | Date;
}

export interface MigrationDbSession {
  connect(): Promise<void>;
  query<Row = Record<string, unknown>>(
    text: string,
    values?: unknown[],
  ): Promise<{ rows: Row[] }>;
  end(): Promise<void>;
}

export interface RunMigrationsOptions {
  migrationsDir?: string;
  dryRun?: boolean;
  env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
  sessionFactory?: () => MigrationDbSession;
}

export interface RunMigrationsSummary {
  dryRun: boolean;
  discovered: MigrationFile[];
  appliedNow: string[];
  alreadyApplied: string[];
}

export function computeMigrationChecksum(sqlContent: string): string {
  const normalized = sqlContent.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n");
  return createHash("sha256").update(normalized, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

function stripSqlComments(sql: string): string {
  return sql
    .replace(/--.*$/gm, "")
    .replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, "")
    .trim();
}

export async function discoverMigrations(
  migrationsDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "db/migrations"),
): Promise<MigrationFile[]> {
  let entries;
  try {
    entries = await readdir(migrationsDir, { withFileTypes: true });
  } catch (error) {
    throw new MigrationError(
      `Cannot read migrations directory at ${migrationsDir}`,
      error,
    );
  }

  const migrations: MigrationFile[] = [];
  const seenVersions = new Map<string, string>();

  for (const entry of entries) {
    if (entry.name.startsWith(".")) {
      continue;
    }
    if (!entry.isFile()) {
      continue;
    }

    const match = MIGRATION_FILENAME_PATTERN.exec(entry.name);
    if (!match) {
      throw new MigrationError(
        `Invalid migration filename "${entry.name}". Expected NNN_snake_case_description.sql (e.g. 000_enable_postgis.sql).`,
      );
    }

    const version = match[1];
    const name = match[2];
    if (!version || !name) {
      throw new MigrationError(`Invalid migration filename "${entry.name}".`);
    }

    const existingFilename = seenVersions.get(version);
    if (existingFilename) {
      throw new MigrationError(
        `Duplicate migration version "${version}" found in "${existingFilename}" and "${entry.name}".`,
      );
    }
    seenVersions.set(version, entry.name);

    const fullPath = path.join(migrationsDir, entry.name);
    const sql = await readFile(fullPath, "utf8");

    if (stripSqlComments(sql).length === 0) {
      throw new MigrationError(
        `Migration file "${entry.name}" contains no executable SQL statements.`,
      );
    }

    migrations.push({
      version,
      name,
      filename: entry.name,
      fullPath,
      sql,
      checksum: computeMigrationChecksum(sql),
    });
  }

  migrations.sort((a, b) => a.version.localeCompare(b.version));
  return migrations;
}

const createDefaultSessionFactory =
  (rawEnv: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env) =>
  (): MigrationDbSession => {
    const client = createNeonSessionClient(rawEnv);
    return {
      async connect() {
        await client.connect();
      },
      async query<Row = Record<string, unknown>>(
        text: string,
        values?: unknown[],
      ) {
        const result = values
          ? await client.query(text, values)
          : await client.query(text);
        return { rows: result.rows as Row[] };
      },
      async end() {
        await client.end();
      },
    };
  };

export async function runMigrations(
  options: RunMigrationsOptions = {},
): Promise<RunMigrationsSummary> {
  const migrationsDir =
    options.migrationsDir ?? path.resolve(process.cwd(), "db/migrations");
  const dryRun = options.dryRun ?? false;
  const discovered = await discoverMigrations(migrationsDir);

  if (dryRun) {
    logger.info("Validated SQL migrations in dry-run mode", {
      count: discovered.length,
      migrations: discovered.map((m) => ({
        filename: m.filename,
        checksum: m.checksum.slice(0, 12),
      })),
    });
    return {
      dryRun: true,
      discovered,
      appliedNow: [],
      alreadyApplied: [],
    };
  }

  const createSession =
    options.sessionFactory ?? createDefaultSessionFactory(options.env);
  const session = createSession();
  let lockAcquired = false;

  await session.connect();

  try {
    await session.query("SELECT pg_advisory_lock($1)", [
      MIGRATION_ADVISORY_LOCK_KEY,
    ]);
    lockAcquired = true;

    await session.query(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
        version TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        name TEXT NOT NULL,
        checksum TEXT NOT NULL,
        applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
      );
    `);

    const existingResult = await session.query<AppliedMigrationRecord>(`
      SELECT version, name, checksum, applied_at
      FROM schema_migrations
      ORDER BY version ASC;
    `);

    const appliedByVersion = new Map<string, AppliedMigrationRecord>();
    for (const row of existingResult.rows) {
      appliedByVersion.set(row.version, row);
    }

    const alreadyApplied: string[] = [];
    const appliedNow: string[] = [];

    for (const migration of discovered) {
      const recorded = appliedByVersion.get(migration.version);
      if (recorded) {
        if (recorded.checksum !== migration.checksum) {
          throw new MigrationError(
            `Checksum mismatch for already-applied migration "${migration.filename}" (expected ${recorded.checksum}, found ${migration.checksum}). Applied migrations are immutable.`,
          );
        }
        alreadyApplied.push(migration.filename);
        continue;
      }

      logger.info(`Applying migration ${migration.filename}`);
      await session.query("BEGIN");
      try {
        await session.query(migration.sql);
        await session.query(
          `INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version, name, checksum) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)`,
          [migration.version, migration.name, migration.checksum],
        );
        await session.query("COMMIT");
        appliedNow.push(migration.filename);
        logger.info(`Applied migration ${migration.filename}`);
      } catch (error) {
        await session.query("ROLLBACK").catch(() => undefined);
        throw new MigrationError(
          `Failed applying migration "${migration.filename}": ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
          error,
        );
      }
    }

    return {
      dryRun: false,
      discovered,
      appliedNow,
      alreadyApplied,
    };
  } finally {
    if (lockAcquired) {
      await session
        .query("SELECT pg_advisory_unlock($1)", [MIGRATION_ADVISORY_LOCK_KEY])
        .catch(() => undefined);
    }
    await session.end().catch(() => undefined);
  }
}


===== FILE: src/lib/db/vercel-build.ts =====
import {
  requireMigrationDatabaseEnv,
  type MigrationDatabaseEnv,
} from "@/lib/env";
import {
  runMigrations,
  type RunMigrationsOptions,
  type RunMigrationsSummary,
} from "@/lib/db/migrate";
import { redactSecrets } from "@/lib/errors";
import { logger, type Logger } from "@/lib/logger";

export interface VercelBuildOptions {
  env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
  log?: Logger;
  runMigrationsFn?: (
    options: RunMigrationsOptions,
  ) => Promise<RunMigrationsSummary>;
  runNextBuildFn?: () => Promise<void> | void;
  runProductionImportFn?: () => Promise<{
    importSkipped?: boolean;
    entitiesCount: number;
    evidenceCount: number;
    factsCount: number;
    factEvidenceLinksCount: number;
    featuresCount: number;
  }>;
  runPreviewValidationFn?: () => Promise<{
    entitiesCount: number;
    evidenceCount: number;
    factsCount: number;
    factEvidenceLinksCount: number;
    featuresCount: number;
  }>;
}

export interface VercelBuildResult {
  migrated: boolean;
  skipReason?: "not_vercel" | "missing_database_url";
  connectionSource?: MigrationDatabaseEnv["source"];
  migrationSummary?: RunMigrationsSummary;
  autoImported?: boolean;
  previewValidated?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Runs SQL migrations before `next build` on Vercel. Production builds run the validated,
 * checksummed seed + feature import; preview builds run the same validation in dry-run mode
 * before compiling, without writing seed data. Database URLs are never included in logs.
 *
 * Outside Vercel build-time database work is skipped. Missing database configuration is fatal
 * in production and a warning-only migration skip in preview (preview data validation still runs).
 */
export async function executeVercelBuild(
  options: VercelBuildOptions & { runNextBuildFn: () => Promise<void> | void },
): Promise<VercelBuildResult> {
  const env = options.env ?? process.env;
  const log = options.log ?? logger;
  const migrate = options.runMigrationsFn ?? runMigrations;

  if (env.VERCEL !== "1") {
    log.info(
      "VERCEL is not set to '1'; skipping build-time SQL migrations and running next build.",
    );
    await options.runNextBuildFn();
    return {
      migrated: false,
      skipReason: "not_vercel",
    };
  }

  let previewValidated = false;
  if (env.VERCEL_ENV === "preview") {
    if (!options.runPreviewValidationFn) {
      throw new Error("Preview build is missing its atlas seed + features dry-run validator.");
    }
    try {
      const summary = await options.runPreviewValidationFn();
      previewValidated = true;
      log.info("Preview atlas seed + features passed full dry-run validation (no data writes)", {
        entitiesCount: summary.entitiesCount,
        evidenceCount: summary.evidenceCount,
        factsCount: summary.factsCount,
        factEvidenceLinksCount: summary.factEvidenceLinksCount,
        featuresCount: summary.featuresCount,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      const safeMessage = error instanceof Error ? redactSecrets(error.message) : "Unknown validation error";
      log.error(`Vercel preview build aborted due to atlas seed + features validation failure: ${safeMessage}`);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  const hasDatabaseUrl = Boolean(env.DATABASE_URL?.trim());
  const hasUnpooledUrl = Boolean(env.DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED?.trim());

  if (!hasDatabaseUrl && !hasUnpooledUrl) {
    if (env.VERCEL_ENV === "production") {
      log.error("Vercel production build aborted: database configuration is required for migrations and automatic data import.");
      throw new Error("Production build requires database configuration for migrations and automatic data import.");
    }
    log.warn(
      "VERCEL=1 detected without a database URL; skipping preview migrations and running next build.",
      { vercelEnv: env.VERCEL_ENV ?? "unknown" },
    );
    await options.runNextBuildFn();
    return { migrated: false, skipReason: "missing_database_url", ...(previewValidated ? { previewValidated: true } : {}) };
  }

  let resolvedDbEnv: MigrationDatabaseEnv;
  try {
    resolvedDbEnv = requireMigrationDatabaseEnv(env);
  } catch (error) {
    const safeMessage =
      error instanceof Error ? redactSecrets(error.message) : "Invalid database URL";
    log.error(
      `Vercel build aborted: invalid database configuration (${safeMessage})`,
      { error },
    );
    throw error;
  }

  log.info("Running SQL migrations during Vercel build", {
    connectionSource: resolvedDbEnv.source,
    vercelEnv: env.VERCEL_ENV ?? "unknown",
  });

  let migrationSummary: RunMigrationsSummary;
  try {
    migrationSummary = await migrate({
      dryRun: false,
      env,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const safeMessage =
      error instanceof Error ? redactSecrets(error.message) : String(error);
    log.error(
      `Vercel build aborted due to SQL migration failure: ${safeMessage}`,
      {
        connectionSource: resolvedDbEnv.source,
        error,
      },
    );
    throw error;
  }

  log.info("Vercel build-time SQL migrations succeeded", {
    connectionSource: resolvedDbEnv.source,
    appliedCount: migrationSummary.appliedNow.length,
    alreadyAppliedCount: migrationSummary.alreadyApplied.length,
  });

  let autoImported = false;
  if (env.VERCEL_ENV === "production") {
    if (!options.runProductionImportFn) {
      throw new Error("Production build is missing its automatic atlas data import handler.");
    }
    log.info("Validating and importing production atlas seed + features");
    try {
      const summary = await options.runProductionImportFn();
      autoImported = true;
      log.info(summary.importSkipped
        ? "Production atlas import skipped; data checksum is unchanged"
        : "Production atlas seed + features imported transactionally", {
        entitiesCount: summary.entitiesCount,
        evidenceCount: summary.evidenceCount,
        factsCount: summary.factsCount,
        factEvidenceLinksCount: summary.factEvidenceLinksCount,
        featuresCount: summary.featuresCount,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      const safeMessage = error instanceof Error ? redactSecrets(error.message) : "Unknown import error";
      log.error(`Vercel production build aborted due to atlas data import failure: ${safeMessage}`);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  await options.runNextBuildFn();

  return {
    migrated: true,
    connectionSource: resolvedDbEnv.source,
    migrationSummary,
    ...(autoImported ? { autoImported: true } : {}),
    ...(previewValidated ? { previewValidated: true } : {}),
  };
}


===== FILE: src/lib/env.ts =====
import { z } from "zod";
import {
  AdminImportNotConfiguredError,
  DatabaseNotConfiguredError,
  EnvValidationError,
  type ValidationIssueDetail,
} from "@/lib/errors";

const MISSING_DATABASE_URL_MESSAGE =
  "DATABASE_URL is missing. Set a Neon PostgreSQL connection string (postgresql://...) in .env.local or Vercel Environment Variables.";

const MISSING_ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN_MESSAGE =
  "ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN is missing. Configure a high-entropy secret (>= 16 characters) in Vercel Environment Variables.";

function isValidPostgresConnectionUrl(value: string): boolean {
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(value);
    return (
      (parsed.protocol === "postgres:" || parsed.protocol === "postgresql:") &&
      parsed.hostname.length > 0
    );
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export const runtimeEnvSchema = z.object({
  NODE_ENV: z
    .enum(["development", "test", "production"])
    .default("development"),
  LOG_LEVEL: z
    .enum(["debug", "info", "warn", "error", "silent"])
    .optional(),
  VERCEL_ENV: z
    .enum(["production", "preview", "development"])
    .optional(),
});

export const databaseEnvSchema = z.object({
  DATABASE_URL: z
    .string({
      error: MISSING_DATABASE_URL_MESSAGE,
    })
    .trim()
    .min(1, {
      error: MISSING_DATABASE_URL_MESSAGE,
    })
    .refine(isValidPostgresConnectionUrl, {
      error:
        "DATABASE_URL must be a valid PostgreSQL connection string starting with postgres:// or postgresql:// and containing a host.",
    }),
});

const unpooledDatabaseEnvSchema = z.object({
  DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .min(1)
    .refine(isValidPostgresConnectionUrl, {
      error:
        "DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED must be a valid PostgreSQL connection string starting with postgres:// or postgresql:// and containing a host.",
    }),
});

export const adminImportEnvSchema = z.object({
  ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN: z
    .string({
      error: MISSING_ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN_MESSAGE,
    })
    .trim()
    .min(16, {
      error:
        "ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN must be at least 16 characters (e.g. generated with `openssl rand -hex 32`).",
    }),
});

export type RuntimeEnv = z.infer<typeof runtimeEnvSchema>;
export type DatabaseEnv = z.infer<typeof databaseEnvSchema>;
export type AdminImportEnv = z.infer<typeof adminImportEnvSchema>;

export interface MigrationDatabaseEnv {
  connectionString: string;
  source: "DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED" | "DATABASE_URL";
}

function formatZodIssues(issues: z.core.$ZodIssue[]): ValidationIssueDetail[] {
  return issues.map((issue) => ({
    path: issue.path.length > 0 ? issue.path.join(".") : "env",
    message: issue.message,
  }));
}

/**
 * Validates non-database runtime environment variables.
 * Safe to call during build, render, and serverless cold starts without a live DB.
 */
export function getRuntimeEnv(
  rawEnv: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
): RuntimeEnv {
  const parsed = runtimeEnvSchema.safeParse({
    NODE_ENV: rawEnv.NODE_ENV,
    LOG_LEVEL: rawEnv.LOG_LEVEL || undefined,
    VERCEL_ENV: rawEnv.VERCEL_ENV || undefined,
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    const issues = formatZodIssues(parsed.error.issues);
    throw new EnvValidationError(
      `Invalid runtime environment configuration: ${issues.map((i) => `${i.path}: ${i.message}`).join("; ")}`,
      issues,
    );
  }

  return parsed.data;
}

/**
 * Validates and returns DATABASE_URL strictly from environment variables.
 * Throws DatabaseNotConfiguredError (HTTP 503) when DATABASE_URL is absent/blank,
 * or EnvValidationError (HTTP 500) when DATABASE_URL is present but malformed.
 */
export function requireDatabaseEnv(
  rawEnv: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
): DatabaseEnv {
  const rawDatabaseUrl = rawEnv.DATABASE_URL;
  const isMissingOrBlank =
    rawDatabaseUrl === undefined || rawDatabaseUrl.trim().length === 0;

  const parsed = databaseEnvSchema.safeParse({
    DATABASE_URL: rawDatabaseUrl,
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    const issues = formatZodIssues(parsed.error.issues);

    if (isMissingOrBlank) {
      throw new DatabaseNotConfiguredError(
        "Database connection is not configured: DATABASE_URL is missing. Provision a Neon PostgreSQL database, run migrations (npm run db:migrate), and set DATABASE_URL.",
        issues,
      );
    }

    throw new EnvValidationError(
      `Invalid database environment configuration: ${issues.map((i) => i.message).join("; ")}`,
      issues,
    );
  }

  return parsed.data;
}

/**
 * Resolves the PostgreSQL connection string for session-stateful workloads
 * (migrations with pg_advisory_lock and multi-statement transactions).
 *
 * Neon's PgBouncer pooled URL can interfere with session-scoped advisory locks;
 * therefore DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED (injected automatically by the Neon Vercel
 * integration) is preferred whenever present, falling back to DATABASE_URL.
 */
export function requireMigrationDatabaseEnv(
  rawEnv: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
): MigrationDatabaseEnv {
  const rawUnpooled = rawEnv.DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED;
  if (rawUnpooled !== undefined && rawUnpooled.trim().length > 0) {
    const parsedUnpooled = unpooledDatabaseEnvSchema.safeParse({
      DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED: rawUnpooled,
    });
    if (!parsedUnpooled.success) {
      const issues = formatZodIssues(parsedUnpooled.error.issues);
      throw new EnvValidationError(
        `Invalid unpooled database environment configuration: ${issues.map((i) => i.message).join("; ")}`,
        issues,
      );
    }
    return {
      connectionString: parsedUnpooled.data.DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED,
      source: "DATABASE_URL_UNPOOLED",
    };
  }

  const { DATABASE_URL } = requireDatabaseEnv(rawEnv);
  return {
    connectionString: DATABASE_URL,
    source: "DATABASE_URL",
  };
}

/**
 * Validates and returns ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN strictly from server environment variables.
 * Throws AdminImportNotConfiguredError (HTTP 503) when missing/blank so unconfigured
 * deployments fail closed, or EnvValidationError (HTTP 500) when too short (< 16 chars).
 */
export function requireAdminImportEnv(
  rawEnv: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
): AdminImportEnv {
  const rawToken = rawEnv.ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN;
  const isMissingOrBlank =
    rawToken === undefined || rawToken.trim().length === 0;

  if (isMissingOrBlank) {
    throw new AdminImportNotConfiguredError(
      "Admin seed import is not configured: ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN is missing. Add ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN (>= 16 characters) in Vercel Environment Variables.",
      [
        {
          path: "ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN",
          message: MISSING_ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN_MESSAGE,
        },
      ],
    );
  }

  const parsed = adminImportEnvSchema.safeParse({
    ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN: rawToken,
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    const issues = formatZodIssues(parsed.error.issues);
    throw new EnvValidationError(
      `Invalid ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN configuration: ${issues.map((i) => i.message).join("; ")}`,
      issues,
    );
  }

  return parsed.data;
}


===== FILE: src/lib/errors.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

export interface ValidationIssueDetail {
  path: string;
  message: string;
}

const POSTGRES_URI_PATTERN =
  /\bpostgres(?:ql)?:\/\/(?![.]{3})[^\s"'`<>)]+/gi;

/**
 * Redacts PostgreSQL connection strings so credentials or host tokens
 * never leak into logs or error payloads.
 */
export function redactSecrets(input: string): string {
  return input.replace(POSTGRES_URI_PATTERN, (match) => {
    try {
      const parsed = new URL(match);
      return `${parsed.protocol}//[REDACTED]@${parsed.hostname}`;
    } catch {
      return "postgresql://[REDACTED]";
    }
  });
}

export interface AppErrorOptions {
  code: string;
  statusCode: number;
  expose?: boolean;
  issues?: ValidationIssueDetail[];
  cause?: unknown;
}

export class AppError extends Error {
  public readonly code: string;
  public readonly statusCode: number;
  public readonly expose: boolean;
  public readonly issues?: ValidationIssueDetail[];

  constructor(message: string, options: AppErrorOptions) {
    super(redactSecrets(message), options.cause ? { cause: options.cause } : undefined);
    this.name = new.target.name;
    this.code = options.code;
    this.statusCode = options.statusCode;
    this.expose = options.expose ?? false;
    this.issues = options.issues;
  }
}

export class DatabaseNotConfiguredError extends AppError {
  constructor(
    message = "Database connection is not configured: DATABASE_URL is missing. Provision a Neon PostgreSQL database, enable PostGIS, and set DATABASE_URL.",
    issues?: ValidationIssueDetail[],
  ) {
    super(message, {
      code: "DATABASE_NOT_CONFIGURED",
      statusCode: 503,
      expose: true,
      issues,
    });
  }
}

export class AdminImportNotConfiguredError extends AppError {
  constructor(
    message = "Admin seed import is not configured: ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN is missing. Configure ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN in Vercel Environment Variables.",
    issues?: ValidationIssueDetail[],
  ) {
    super(message, {
      code: "ADMIN_IMPORT_NOT_CONFIGURED",
      statusCode: 503,
      expose: true,
      issues,
    });
  }
}

export class AdminUnauthorizedError extends AppError {
  constructor(
    message = "Unauthorized: missing or invalid ADMIN_IMPORT_TOKEN.",
  ) {
    super(message, {
      code: "ADMIN_UNAUTHORIZED",
      statusCode: 401,
      expose: true,
    });
  }
}

export class RequestValidationError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, issues?: ValidationIssueDetail[]) {
    super(message, {
      code: "REQUEST_VALIDATION_ERROR",
      statusCode: 400,
      expose: true,
      issues,
    });
  }
}

export class EnvValidationError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, issues: ValidationIssueDetail[]) {
    super(message, {
      code: "ENV_VALIDATION_ERROR",
      statusCode: 500,
      expose: true,
      issues,
    });
  }
}

export class DatabaseUnavailableError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, cause?: unknown) {
    super(message, {
      code: "DATABASE_UNAVAILABLE",
      statusCode: 503,
      expose: true,
      cause,
    });
  }
}

export class MigrationError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, cause?: unknown) {
    super(message, {
      code: "MIGRATION_ERROR",
      statusCode: 500,
      expose: false,
      cause,
    });
  }
}

export class SeedValidationError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, issues: ValidationIssueDetail[]) {
    super(message, {
      code: "SEED_VALIDATION_ERROR",
      statusCode: 400,
      expose: true,
      issues,
    });
  }
}

export interface ApiErrorBody {
  ok: false;
  error: {
    code: string;
    message: string;
    issues?: ValidationIssueDetail[];
  };
  timestamp: string;
}

export function normalizeUnknownError(error: unknown): {
  statusCode: number;
  code: string;
  publicMessage: string;
  internalMessage: string;
  issues?: ValidationIssueDetail[];
} {
  if (error instanceof AppError) {
    return {
      statusCode: error.statusCode,
      code: error.code,
      publicMessage: error.expose
        ? error.message
        : "An internal server error occurred.",
      internalMessage: error.message,
      issues: error.issues,
    };
  }

  if (error instanceof Error) {
    return {
      statusCode: 500,
      code: "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
      publicMessage: "An unexpected server error occurred.",
      internalMessage: redactSecrets(error.message),
    };
  }

  return {
    statusCode: 500,
    code: "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
    publicMessage: "An unexpected server error occurred.",
    internalMessage: redactSecrets(String(error)),
  };
}

export function toApiErrorResponse(error: unknown): NextResponse<ApiErrorBody> {
  const normalized = normalizeUnknownError(error);
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0",
  };
  if (normalized.statusCode === 401) {
    headers["WWW-Authenticate"] = 'Bearer realm="greater-cairo-atlas-admin"';
  }
  return NextResponse.json(
    {
      ok: false,
      error: {
        code: normalized.code,
        message: normalized.publicMessage,
        ...(normalized.issues ? { issues: normalized.issues } : {}),
      },
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    },
    {
      status: normalized.statusCode,
      headers,
    },
  );
}


===== FILE: src/lib/logger.ts =====
import { redactSecrets } from "@/lib/errors";

export type LogLevel = "debug" | "info" | "warn" | "error" | "silent";

const LEVEL_PRIORITY: Record<LogLevel, number> = {
  debug: 10,
  info: 20,
  warn: 30,
  error: 40,
  silent: 100,
};

const SENSITIVE_KEY_PATTERN =
  /^(database_url|database_url_unpooled|databaseurl|databaseurlunpooled|postgres_url|postgres_url_non_pooling|connectionstring|connection_string|admin_import_token|adminimporttoken|x-admin-import-token|password|secret|token|authorization|api_key|apikey|.*_token|.*_secret|.*_password)$/i;

function resolveConfiguredLevel(): LogLevel {
  const raw = process.env.LOG_LEVEL?.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (
    raw === "debug" ||
    raw === "info" ||
    raw === "warn" ||
    raw === "error" ||
    raw === "silent"
  ) {
    return raw;
  }
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "test") {
    return "silent";
  }
  return "info";
}

export function sanitizeLogValue(value: unknown, depth = 0): unknown {
  if (depth > 5) {
    return "[MaxDepth]";
  }
  if (value === null || value === undefined) {
    return value;
  }
  if (typeof value === "string") {
    return redactSecrets(value);
  }
  if (
    typeof value === "number" ||
    typeof value === "boolean" ||
    typeof value === "bigint"
  ) {
    return typeof value === "bigint" ? value.toString() : value;
  }
  if (value instanceof Error) {
    const serialized: Record<string, unknown> = {
      name: value.name,
      message: redactSecrets(value.message),
    };
    if ("code" in value && typeof value.code === "string") {
      serialized.code = value.code;
    }
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production" && value.stack) {
      serialized.stack = redactSecrets(value.stack);
    }
    if (value.cause !== undefined) {
      serialized.cause = sanitizeLogValue(value.cause, depth + 1);
    }
    return serialized;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value.map((item) => sanitizeLogValue(item, depth + 1));
  }
  if (typeof value === "object") {
    const sanitizedRecord: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const [key, entryValue] of Object.entries(value)) {
      if (SENSITIVE_KEY_PATTERN.test(key)) {
        sanitizedRecord[key] = "[REDACTED]";
      } else {
        sanitizedRecord[key] = sanitizeLogValue(entryValue, depth + 1);
      }
    }
    return sanitizedRecord;
  }
  return String(value);
}

export interface LogContext {
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

export interface Logger {
  debug(message: string, context?: LogContext): void;
  info(message: string, context?: LogContext): void;
  warn(message: string, context?: LogContext): void;
  error(message: string, context?: LogContext): void;
  child(bindings: LogContext): Logger;
}

function shouldEmit(level: Exclude<LogLevel, "silent">): boolean {
  const configured = resolveConfiguredLevel();
  return LEVEL_PRIORITY[level] >= LEVEL_PRIORITY[configured];
}

function isJsonOutputMode(): boolean {
  return (
    process.env.NODE_ENV === "production" || Boolean(process.env.VERCEL)
  );
}

function writeLog(
  level: Exclude<LogLevel, "silent">,
  message: string,
  context?: LogContext,
): void {
  if (!shouldEmit(level)) {
    return;
  }

  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
  const safeMessage = redactSecrets(message);
  const safeContext = context
    ? (sanitizeLogValue(context) as Record<string, unknown>)
    : undefined;

  const writer =
    level === "error"
      ? console.error
      : level === "warn"
        ? console.warn
        : console.info;

  if (isJsonOutputMode()) {
    writer(
      JSON.stringify({
        timestamp,
        level,
        service: "greater-cairo-atlas",
        message: safeMessage,
        ...(safeContext ? { context: safeContext } : {}),
      }),
    );
    return;
  }

  const contextSuffix =
    safeContext && Object.keys(safeContext).length > 0
      ? ` ${JSON.stringify(safeContext)}`
      : "";
  writer(`[${timestamp}] ${level.toUpperCase()} ${safeMessage}${contextSuffix}`);
}

export function createLogger(baseBindings: LogContext = {}): Logger {
  const mergeContext = (context?: LogContext): LogContext | undefined => {
    const hasBase = Object.keys(baseBindings).length > 0;
    const hasContext = Boolean(context && Object.keys(context).length > 0);
    if (!hasBase && !hasContext) {
      return undefined;
    }
    return { ...baseBindings, ...context };
  };

  return {
    debug(message, context) {
      writeLog("debug", message, mergeContext(context));
    },
    info(message, context) {
      writeLog("info", message, mergeContext(context));
    },
    warn(message, context) {
      writeLog("warn", message, mergeContext(context));
    },
    error(message, context) {
      writeLog("error", message, mergeContext(context));
    },
    child(bindings) {
      return createLogger({ ...baseBindings, ...bindings });
    },
  };
}

export const logger = createLogger();

}


===== FILE: src/proxy.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";

const LOCALES = new Set(["ar", "en"]);
export function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname, searchParams } = request.nextUrl;
  const parts = pathname.split("/").filter(Boolean);
  const first = parts[0];
  if (first && LOCALES.has(first)) {
    const internal = request.nextUrl.clone();
    const remainder = `/${parts.slice(1).join("/")}` || "/";
    internal.pathname = remainder === "/" ? "/" : remainder;
    internal.searchParams.set("lang", first);
    return NextResponse.rewrite(internal);
  }
  const legacyLanguage = searchParams.get("lang");
  const language = legacyLanguage === "en" ? "en" : "ar";
  const redirect = request.nextUrl.clone();
  redirect.pathname = `/${language}${pathname === "/" ? "" : pathname}`;
  redirect.searchParams.delete("lang");
  return NextResponse.redirect(redirect, 301);
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/((?!api/|_next/|favicon\\.ico$|icon\\.svg$|robots\\.txt$|sitemap\\.xml$|.*\\.[^/]+$).*)"],
};
